### [1] [Beyond the Bochner Method: Optimal Spectral Gaps for Block Dynamics of Spin Systems like the $O(N)$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05228)
*Jonathan Leake,Anqi Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了块 Glauber 动力学（block Glauber dynamics）的局部到全局谱隙界（local-to-global spectral-gap bounds），并借助扩展的 Bochner 方法（Bochner method）给出 O(N) 模型、交换游走（swap walks）、部分单纯复形（partite simplicial complexes）及随机森林（random forests）等多项应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是建立由局部谱信息推得全局谱隙的一般框架，并给出紧的（sharp）界，以刻画自旋系统与块动力学（block dynamics）的混合性质。

Method: 给定对称、逐元素非负且对角为零、满足 λ_max(A)<1 的矩阵 A（编码局部谱界），推导其边缘分布上块动力学的全局谱隙界；证明通过希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）上正交投影（orthogonal projection）的抽象不等式扩展 Bochner 方法，并将之与辅助实稳定多项式（real stable polynomial）分析结合。

Result: 所得的界是紧的：协方差为 (I-A)^{-1} 的高斯系统（Gaussian system）对任意不相交分块取等号；O(N) 模型（N≥2，任意外场、任意符号耦合）的 Glauber 动力学谱隙至少为 (1-||J_abs||_op/N)/n；对 F_q 上随机子空间旗（random flags of subspaces）中不相交秩组构成的复形，得到单侧局部谱扩张界 2√q/(q+1)；对任意有限图、任意正边权的随机森林，证明 Pr(e,f∈F) ≤ (4/3)Pr(e∈F)Pr(f∈F)，优于一般对数凹性（log-concavity）界给出的因子 2。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个局部到全局谱隙的统一工具，其界在若干情形下为紧，并在自旋模型、交换游走、单纯复形与随机森林等问题上给出改进或新的定量结果。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be a probability distribution on a spin system with $n$ sites and possibly infinite spin spaces. We prove local-to-global spectral-gap bounds for block Glauber dynamics. Given a symmetric, entrywise nonnegative matrix $A$ with zero diagonal and $λ_{\max}(A)<1$ encoding local spectral bounds for a spin system, we derive global spectral-gap bounds for block dynamics on its marginal distributions. These bounds are sharp: the Gaussian system with covariance $(I-A)^{-1}$ attains equality for every choice of disjoint blocks. Our proof extends the Bochner method through an abstract inequality for orthogonal projections on Hilbert spaces.
  As an application, we prove that Glauber dynamics for the $O(N)$ model, with $N\geq 2$, arbitrary external fields, and couplings of either sign, has spectral gap at least $(1-\|J_{\mathrm{abs}}\|_{\mathrm{op}}/N)/n$, where $J$ is the interaction matrix and $J_{\mathrm{abs}}=(|J_{ij}|)$.
  We also obtain spectral bounds for swap walks and local spectral expansion of partite simplicial complexes. For complexes formed from disjoint groups of ranks in random flags of subspaces over $\mathbb F_q$, we obtain a one-sided local spectral expansion bound of $2\sqrt q/(q+1)$. This bound is independent of the number and sizes of each group, and is nontrivial for every prime power $q$.
  Finally, for random forests $F$ of any finite graph with arbitrary positive edge weights, we prove that
  \[
  \Pr(e,f\in F)\le \frac{4}{3} \Pr(e\in F)\Pr(f\in F)
  \]
  for distinct edges $e,f$, improving the factor 2 supplied by general log-concavity bounds for matroid independent sets. The argument combines our extended Bochner method with the analysis of an auxiliary real stable polynomial.

</details>

### [2] [BGRem: A background noise remover for astronomical images based on a diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04718)
*Rodney Nicolaas,Sascha Caron,Fiorenzo Stoppa,Saptashwa Bhattacharyya,Roberto Ruiz de Austri,Paul J. Groot,Andrew J. Levan*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: BGRem 是一种基于扩散模型（diffusion model）与注意力 U-Net（attention U-Net）的天文图像背景噪声去除工具，作为预处理步骤可提升传统源检测流程的准确性，并在光学与伽马射线数据上展现了跨波段泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文成像需最大化信号并抑制噪声，而在探测器层面直接提升信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）困难且昂贵，因此后处理技术受到广泛关注；背景噪声去除对星表构建（catalog-building）等任务具有重要预处理价值。

Method: BGRem 采用以注意力 U-Net 为骨干的扩散模型，在 MeerLICHT 与 Fermi-LAT 望远镜的模拟光学及伽马（γ）射线图像上以监督方式训练，通过多个扩散步骤学习去噪。

Result: 与常用源提取工具 SourceExtractor（SExtractor）对比，使用 BGRem 预处理后 MeerLICHT 数据的真实正源（true positive sources）数量提升约 7%；在其他望远镜的光学图像及代表性 Fermi-LAT 模拟 γ 射线数据上，源检测效率亦得到改善。

Conclusion: BGRem 通过去除复杂背景噪声提升传统基于像素（pixel-based）方法的源检测精度；借助零样本（zero-shot）方式可泛化至多种光学图像，并在 γ 射线数据上成功应用，显示其作为多波段天文巡天（multi-wavelength astronomical surveys）通用背景去除框架的潜力。

Abstract: Context: Astronomical imaging aims to maximize signal capture while minimizing noise. Enhancing the signal-to-noise ratio directly on detectors is difficult and expensive, leading to extensive research in advanced post-processing techniques.
  Aims: Removing background noise from images is a valuable pre-processing step catalog-building tasks. We introduce BGRem, a machine learning (ML) based tool to remove background noise from astronomical images.
  Methods: BGRem uses a diffusion-based model with an attention U-Net as backbone, trained on simulated images for optical and gamma (γ)-ray data from the MeerLICHT and Fermi-LAT telescopes. In a supervised manner, BGRem learns to denoise astronomical images over several diffusion steps.
  Results: BGRem performance was compared with a widely used tool for cataloging astronomical sources, SourceExtractor (SExtractor). It was shown that the amount of true positive sources using SExtractor increased by about 7% for MeerLICHT data when BGRem was used as a pre-processing step. We also show the generalizability of BGRem by testing it with optical images from different telescopes and also on simulated γ-ray data representative of the Fermi-LAT telescope. We show that in both cases, BGRem improves the source detection efficiency.
  Conclusions: BGRem can improve the accuracy in source detection of traditional pixel-based methods by removing complex background noise. Using zero-shot approach, BGRem can generalize well to a wide range of optical images. The successful application of BGRem to simulated γ-ray images, alongside optical data, demonstrates its adaptability to distinct noise characteristics and observational domains. This cross-wavelength performance highlights its potential as a general-purpose background removal framework for multi-wavelength astronomical surveys.

</details>


### [3] [Latin hypercube sampling to reduce the variance in Monte Carlo integration: a practical guide and an application to gravitational-wave astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04045)
*Matteo Boschini,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 论文介绍拉丁超立方抽样（Latin hypercube sampling, LHS）与基于正交表（orthogonal arrays, OA）的 OA-LHS，作为通过分层抽样降低蒙特卡洛求和数值方差的手段，并以引力波族群分层推断中的选择函数为案例进行实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用统计中涉及高维积分的似然常以蒙特卡洛求和（Monte Carlo summation）近似，控制由此产生的数值方差（numerical variance）对可靠推断至关重要，因此需要实用的方差缩减抽样策略。

Method: 提供 LHS 与 strength-2 OA-LHS 的实用入门介绍，二者均通过分层抽样（stratifying the sampling distribution）降低方差；并将其应用于引力波族群分层推断（hierarchical inference of gravitational-wave populations）中的选择函数（selection function），在约一百万次注入（injections）规模下考察方差表现。

Result: LHS 渐近消除单坐标（individual coordinates）的方差贡献，strength-2 OA-LHS 还额外消除成对交互（pairwise interactions）的贡献，对合适被积函数可比普通蒙特卡洛估计量提升若干数量级；但在引力波案例中，约一百万次注入仅带来边际方差缩减，统计显著性较低。

Conclusion: 构造此类设计的计算成本相对引力波注入的评估成本可忽略，故即使增益边际也值得在未来应用中考虑；该实用介绍可为物理与天文学中其他计算昂贵的蒙特卡洛应用提供起点。

Abstract: In applied statistics, likelihoods involving high-dimensional integrals are often approximated via Monte Carlo summation. Controlling the resulting numerical variance is crucial for reliable inference. We provide a practical introduction to Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and its extension based on orthogonal arrays (OA-LHS) as strategies to reduce this variance by stratifying the sampling distribution. LHS asymptotically removes variance contributions from individual coordinates, while strength-2 OA-LHS also removes those from pairwise interactions, yielding orders-of-magnitude improvements over plain Monte Carlo estimators for suitable integrands. As a case study, we investigate their application to the selection function entering hierarchical inference of gravitational-wave populations. At approximately one million injections, we find only a marginal reduction in variance, with low statistical significance. Nevertheless, the computational cost of constructing these designs is negligible relative to evaluating gravitational-wave injections, making even marginal gains worth considering for future applications. This practical introduction to LHS and OA-LHS offers a starting point for other applications in physics and astronomy involving computationally expensive Monte Carlo calculations.

</details>

### [4] [The integer spectrum of a superintegrable quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05015)
*Marine De Clerck,Oleg Evnin*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文给出了量子 Gérard-Grellier 哈密顿量（quantum Gérard-Grellier Hamiltonian）的初等精确解：本征态由分拆（partition）标记，本征值可用主钩长（principal hook length）显式表达，从而证明了此前仅由数值研究观察到的整数谱（integer spectrum）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作（CMP 407 (2026) 131）研究的玻色型四次量子多体哈密顿量源自 Gérard 与 Grellier 关于 Lax 可积（Lax-integrable）框架下经典湍流能量转移的研究，其量子版本尊重多项式守恒律（polynomial conservation laws）层级，且哈密顿量及守恒量均具纯整数谱；但该整数谱特征此前仅见于数值研究、一直未获证明，本文即针对这一空白。

Method: 构造受经典多孤子相干态（multisoliton coherent states）启发的特定基底，在该基底下哈密顿量呈上三角（upper-triangular）形式；本征值表达式由此直接读出，完整本征基（eigenbasis）则通过 Gram-Schmidt 正交化过程恢复。所用方法被作者称为初等的（elementary）。

Result: 本征态以分拆（partition）为标签，对应本征值由主钩长（principal hook length）给出简单显式公式，由此证明哈密顿量具有纯整数谱。

Conclusion: 量子 Gérard-Grellier 哈密顿量可被初等地精确求解，其整数谱性质得到严格证明；所引入的基底与分拆—钩长结构为同类可积量子多体模型的谱分析提供了可操作的代数途径。

Abstract: In our previous article CMP 407 (2026) 131, we explored the rich algebraic structures arising from a specific quantum many-body Hamiltonian quartic in the bosonic creation-annihilation operators. This Hamiltonian originated from the works of Gérard and Grellier on classical turbulent energy transfer in a Lax-integrable setting, and its quantum version respects hierarchies of polynomial conservation laws all of which possess purely integer spectra, as does the Hamiltonian itself. The latter feature, known from numerical studies, has previously remained unproved. Here, we present an elementary solution of the quantum Gérard-Grellier Hamiltonian. The eigenstates are labelled by partitions and the corresponding eigenvalues admit simple explicit expressions in terms of the principal hook lengths. The key ingredient in this construction is a specific basis we introduce, inspired by the classical multisoliton coherent states, in which the Hamiltonian is upper-triangular. The expressions for the eigenvalues then immediately follow, while the full eigenbasis can be recovered via a Gram-Schmidt procedure.

</details>

### [5] [A Conditional Quantum Kac-Bernstein Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04363)
*Hesam Hosseini,Jinpei Zhao,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子记忆存在下建立条件量子 Kac-Bernstein 定理，精确刻画两个独立有限模玻色输入混合后输出在给定双记忆时条件独立的条件，并给出正交记忆扇区分解结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Kac-Bernstein 定理刻画混合后独立性的结构含义，作者将其推广到带量子记忆的有限模玻色系统，以确定条件独立输出的精确结构条件。

Method: 针对两个独立、有限模玻色输入（finite-mode bosonic inputs）与量子记忆（quantum memories）的混合，给出输出在给定双方记忆下条件独立（conditionally independent）的充要刻画，导出输入的分解结构。

Result: 每个输入可分解为正交记忆扇区（orthogonal memory sectors），各扇区共享物理系统上的同一高斯态（Gaussian state）与一个玻色记忆因子，差异仅在于依赖扇区的物理位移（displacements）。

Conclusion: 公共玻色因子的平凡性蕴含输入的可分性（separability），当任一记忆具有有限维支撑时该情形即出现，从而将 Kac-Bernstein 型结论推广至量子记忆场景。

Abstract: We establish a conditional quantum Kac-Bernstein theorem for mixing of two independent, finite-mode bosonic inputs with quantum memories. We characterize exactly when the outputs are conditionally independent given both memories. Each input then decomposes into orthogonal memory sectors sharing a common Gaussian state on the physical system and a bosonic memory factor, up to sector-dependent physical displacements. In particular, triviality of the common bosonic factor implies separability of the inputs, as occurs whenever either memory has finite-dimensional support.

</details>


### [6] [Motional Decoherence of the internal state of an atom by vacuum and thermal fluctuations of a quantum field: Necessity of a fully relativistic treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04512)
*Jen-Tsung Hsiang,Bei-Lok Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对在量子场中以恒定速度运动的大质量物体（如中性原子或 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器），给出完全相对论协变的内禀动力学计算，并与忽略协变原理的非相对论处理直接对比，发现在低原子速度下二者仍存在显著差异，主张先在完全协变的相对论框架内推导并正则化，再取低速极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有两个目的：一是获得量子退散（quantum dispersion）与退相干（decoherence）的正确结果，这对依赖原子内禀自由度量子相干性的量子信息处理方案设计至关重要；二是揭示常见做法的谬误，即先做非相对论计算再补充相对论性"修正"。

Method: 先基于协变原理（covariance principles）给出内禀动力学的完全相对论计算，再给出一种忽略这些原理、常以原子运动缓慢为由辩护的处理，并对两种方法的结果进行直接比较。

Result: 两种方法的对比表明，即便在低原子速度下，非相对论处理也会产生显著差异（significant discrepancies）。

Conclusion: 推荐的做法是：先在完全协变的相对论框架内推导动力学与必要的正则化（regularizations），待建立精确且内部自洽的运动方程后，再从中提取低速极限。唯有如此，时间膨胀（time dilation）、像差（aberration）、非惯性质量移动（non-inertial mass shifts）等所有起作用的相对论效应才能被系统地保留，并获得准确结果。

Abstract: We consider a massive object, such as a neutral atom or an Unruh-DeWitt detector, moving at constant speed in a quantum field and investigate how motion affects the quantum coherence of its internal degrees of freedom, manifesting as quantum dispersion in the absence of a quantum field, and decoherence in its presence. Our investigation aims to serve two purposes: One is to obtain the correct results for these effects, which are essential for the design of quantum information processing schemes where the quantum coherence of an atom's internal degrees of freedom is of major concern. The other is to expose the fallacies in commonplace approaches, which start with a non-relativistic calculation and then add on relativistic ``corrections". We first show a fully relativistic calculation of the internal dynamics based on covariance principles, and then one which ignores these principles, often justified by the atom's slow motion. By a direct comparison of results from these two approaches, we identify {significant discrepancies} in a non-relativistic treatment, even at low atomic speeds. The proper procedure, and a much safer one in terms of accuracy, is recommended here: first derive the dynamics and the necessary regularizations within a fully covariant relativistic framework. Only after the exact, internally consistent equations of motion are established should low-velocity limits be extracted from them. Only in this way can all the relativistic effects which matter: time dilation, aberration, non-inertial mass shifts, be systematically preserved, and accurate results obtained.

</details>


### [7] [Cooling more while dissipating less: anomalous tradeoffs from thermodynamic geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27948)
*Andrea Canzio,Vasco Cavina,Salvatore Raia,Vito Vetrano,Matteo Scandi,Paolo Abiuso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示在开放量子系统（open quantum system）慢驱动（slow-driving）区间存在一种反直觉现象：从同一初态出发，最优驱动可同时实现更冷（或更热）的目标与更少的能量耗散，作者将其命名为近平衡反常权衡（Near-Equilibrium Anomalous Tradeoff, NEAT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究驱动过程中“速度—耗散—目标温度”三者之间是否存在突破常规权衡的可能性，并厘清该现象与已知姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）的关系。

Method: 在开放量子系统的慢驱动区间中定义并分析 NEAT，给出其发生的必要条件与几何起源，并通过精确可解的 qutrit 模型以及能量层级可独立控制的 d 维生灭过程（birth-death dynamics）进行演示。

Result: 结果表明：从同一状态出发，最优驱动可在相同时内达到更冷或更热的目标且耗散更少，或等耗散下用时更短；NEAT 与姆潘巴效应虽相似但一般相互区别；该现象还能提升卡诺热机（Carnot engine）的性能。

Conclusion: NEAT 是开放量子系统慢驱动中的一种具有几何根源的反常热力学权衡，其存在需满足特定必要条件，并可用于改善热机等量子热力学器件的性能。

Abstract: We show that, starting from the same state, optimal driving can sometimes reach a colder (or hotter) target in the same amount of time while dissipating less energy, or equivalently dissipate the same amount of energy in a shorter time. We study this counterintuitive phenomenon in the slow-driving regime of an open quantum system, where we name it Near-Equilibrium Anomalous Tradeoff (NEAT). We establish necessary conditions for its occurrence and show its underlying geometric origin. While NEAT is reminiscent of the well-known Mpemba effect, the two phenomena are distinct in general. We demonstrate NEAT in an exactly solvable qutrit model, as well as for d-dimensional birth-death dynamics with independently controlled energy levels. Finally, we show how NEAT can improve the performance of Carnot engines.

</details>


### [8] [Long-range entangled criticality from coherent errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03811)
*Amir-Reza Negari,Subhayan Sahu,Jan Behrends,Benjamin Béri,Timothy H. Hsieh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 相干错误（coherent error）可使干净表面码相（clean surface code phase）转变为具有发散马尔可夫长度（Markov length）的扩展长程纠缠临界区（long-range entangled critical regime）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相干错误对拓扑码相的影响，特别是其能否诱导超出传统阈值图像的新型相变与长程纠缠结构。

Within this framework, we physically justify the consistency of the right-right formalism (as opposed to the biorthogonal one) for calculating expectation values.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用统计物理方法，构建共纠缠渗流的随机推广；在具有跨尺度解析可控性的层次 (U,V)-flower 网络上，推导端到端纠缠连接存活概率的精确重整化递推，并对端到端共纠缠的完整分布进行数值传播。

Abstract: Quantum networks distribute entanglement between distant nodes by combining many short entangled links using local quantum operations. We approach the problem through statistical physics, using a stochastic generalization of concurrence percolation to determine how reliable local operations must be for long-range entanglement to remain possible. On hierarchical $(U,V)$-flower networks, which permit full analytical control across network scales, we derive an exact renormalization recursion for the probability that an end-to-end entangled connection survives. For the $(2,2)$-flower this yields a closed-form operational phase boundary, $η_p = 2 - \frac{32}{27}η_s$, where $η_s$ and $η_p$ are the success probabilities of the series (entanglement swapping) and parallel (purification) operations, respectively. At this boundary the survival probability jumps discontinuously from $0$ to $S_\infty = 3/(4η_s)$. Above both thresholds the large-network state is binary: the connection either fails outright or approaches a perfect Bell pair. Our results thus reveal a two-threshold structure for long-range entanglement, in which operational reliability and initial link quality can compensate one another. Operational success rates therefore emerge alongside initial link quality as a primary design constraint for scalable quantum networks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相变中缺陷的完整统计刻画多局限于可精确求解模型，对长程相互作用自旋链等非可积系统（nonintegrable systems）研究不足，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Abstract: The complete statistical characterization of defects generated in a quantum phase transition has mostly been studied in exactly solvable models, and remains little explored in nonintegrable systems such as long-range interacting spin chains. We characterize the full defect counting statistics in a nonintegrable long-range spin chain, the fractional Ising model, by time-dependent variational-principle simulations within the matrix-product-state and matrix-product-operator formalism. Fractional interactions capture emergent multiscale correlations and long-range effects in nature, governed by the order of the underlying fractional derivative. Such couplings can be approximated on existing quantum simulation platforms, where the full defect statistics can be measured. The defect number cumulants follow power laws in the driving time, with exponents that vary continuously with the fractional order. At low fractional order, deep in the long-range regime, the mean defect number decays more slowly than predicted by the Kibble-Zurek mechanism (KZM), while at intermediate order the KZM holds with the numerically extracted many-body critical exponents. At large fractional order, the exactly solvable free-fermion limit of the model becomes progressively more accurate towards the short-range limit. We derive exact bounds on the kink-number cumulants and on the kink-kink correlations, implying an exact Gaussian limiting distribution for large system sizes. In the many-body simulations, however, kinks are more correlated, consistent with the confinement of domain walls, and these bounds are violated in the long-range regime, also in the bulk of the chain. Nevertheless, the kink distribution still approaches a Gaussian for large defect numbers, with proportionality partially broken between the cumulants.

Abstract: A defining feature of quantum materials is that multiple orders govern low-energy physics and are not independent. We study Bi$_2$Se$_3$ by combining DFT with a renormalized correlated honeycomb model in which frustrated magnetic order and helical topological surface states are coupled via one control parameter. Exact diagonalization up to 20 sites shows the Néel structure factor and valence-bond-solid order parameter cross at $J_2/J_1 \approx 0.22$, agreeing with QMC/DMRG, and a dynamical spin structure factor broadens near the crossover, as expected for a spinon continuum. Band inversion of the renormalized tight-binding model occurs on the same critical locus, producing jumps in the Chern number and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant mirrored by the skyrmion charge $Q$. This gives a microscopic link: $Q$ selects the axion angle and thus the topological magnetoelectric coupling. We distinguish axion coupling, independent of skyrmion helicity, from the helicity-dependent magnetoelectric multipole coupling, whose toroidal component scales as $\sinγ$ and vanishes for Néel-type textures. We derive the $Q$-topological magnetoelectric energy and estimate $\sim 10^{-20}\,\mathrm{J}$ per skyrmion per cycle for few-tesla fields. The cycle is thermodynamically bounded: constant-$θ$ cycles extract no net work, and efficiency is limited by axionic entropy change.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 有限温度正交金属物理可作为三维及层间跃迁不可忽略的准二维层状材料中赝能隙型行为（pseudogap-like behavior）的可能机制，并可能用于解释 Sr2VO3FeAs 的近期实验。

Abstract: Orthogonal metals provide a paradigmatic example of strongly entangled metallic states whose transport and thermodynamic properties resemble those of conventional Fermi liquids, while their single-electron excitations remain fundamentally distinct. Despite considerable interest in their unusual ground-state properties, orthogonal metals in three spatial dimensions and their finite-temperature behavior remain relatively unexplored. Here, we investigate a three-dimensional orthogonal metal and characterize the thermodynamic and spectroscopic signatures associated with its finite-temperature phase transition. Starting from a simple model of fermions coupled to spin degrees of freedom, we perturbatively demonstrate that the thermal transition known to occur in the purely bosonic limit persists in the presence of coupling to a gapless Fermi surface. We then examine physical observables that change prominently between the coherent and orthogonal-metal regimes. In particular, we calculate the evolution of the physical-electron spectral function and identify characteristic signatures in angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. We find a pronounced contrast in low-energy spectral weight between the coherent and orthogonal-metal regimes without requiring conventional symmetry-breaking order. Our results establish finite-temperature orthogonal-metal physics as a possible mechanism for pseudogap-like behavior in three-dimensional and quasi-two-dimensional layered materials with non-negligible interlayer hopping, which may be applied to the recent experiments in $\mathrm{Sr_2 VO_3 Fe As}$.

</details>

### [19] [Korringa factor in Dirac-nodal-line systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03994)
*Takahiko Sekine,Kanoda Kazushi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以态密度（DOS）为基础，理论研究了有限节点线色散的狄拉克节点线（Dirac nodal-line, DNL）体系中无能电子关联的Korringa因子K*(α)，揭示其本征温度依赖、朗道量子化增强效应及两类磁振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核磁共振（NMR）研究中的Korringa因子通常以无关联值K(α)=1作为参照来度量金属中的电子关联（electron-correlation）效应。但狄拉克费米（Dirac-fermion）体系的无关联参考值依赖态密度且不同于常规金属，因此需要为DNL材料建立定量的无关联基准以正确识别真实的关联效应。

Method: 采用基于态密度（DOS-based）的方法，对具有有限节点线色散（nodal-line dispersion）的DNL体系中无能关联的Korringa因子K*(α)进行理论计算与分析。

Result: K*(α)表现出本征温度依赖，从高温下狄拉克费米值1.71连续过渡到低温下金属值1；转变温度与节点线色散宽度成正比，该宽度即为支配K*(α)的特征能量尺度。朗道量子化（Landau quantization）产生奇异DOS结构，即使无电子关联也强烈增强K*(α)并形成显著峰。此外K*(α)呈现两类磁振荡，分别来自能级内（intra-level）与能级间（inter-level）贡献，后者源于正、负量子数朗道子带奇异点在费米能级处的重叠，是DNL有限能量色散的独特结果。

Conclusion: 该结果为DNL材料的NMR实验解释以及区分DOS诱导贡献之外的真正关联效应提供了定量参考。

Abstract: The Korringa factor $K(α)$ in the nuclear magnetic resonance studies is a widely used measure of electron-correlation effects in metals and is conventionally interpreted using the non-correlated value $K(α)$ = 1. In Dirac-fermion systems, however, the non-correlated reference value depends on the electronic density of states (DOS) and differs from that of conventional metals. Here, we theoretically investigate the non-correlated Korringa factor $K^*(α)$ in a Dirac nodal-line (DNL) system with finite nodal-line dispersion using a DOS-based approach. We show that $K^*(α)$ exhibits an intrinsic temperature dependence, continuously evolving from the Dirac-fermion value of 1.71 at high temperatures to the metallic value of 1 at low temperatures. The crossover temperature is found to be proportional to the nodal-line dispersion width, identified as the characteristic energy scale governing the non-correlated Korringa factor in DNL systems. We further demonstrate that Landau quantization generates singular DOS structures that strongly enhance $K^*(α)$, producing pronounced peaks even in the absence of electron correlations. In addition, $K^*(α)$ exhibits two distinct types of magnetic oscillations arising from intra-level and inter-level contributions. The latter originates from the overlap of singular points belonging to positive and negative quantum-number Landau subbands at the Fermi level and represents a unique consequence of the finite energy dispersion of a DNL. These results provide a quantitative reference for interpreting NMR experiments in DNL materials and for identifying genuine correlation effects beyond DOS-induced contributions.

</details>

TL;DR: 该综述系统阐述了纵向极化分析（longitudinal polarization analysis, LPA）在中子散射中分离磁性与非磁性散射、约束小磁矩分量及解析磁激发自旋空间各向异性的原理与实践方法，并展望其在交错磁体、非常规超导体与笼目磁体中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数中子散射实验使用非极化中子（unpolarized neutrons），未充分利用中子自旋所携带的信息，导致弱磁信号易被非磁背景掩盖、小磁矩分量难以约束。因此有必要推广自Moon、Riste与Koehler于20世纪60年代末在橡树岭国家实验室（Oak Ridge National Laboratory）建立的LPA方法。

Method: 综述性工作：介绍将实测极化通道（polarization channels）与磁学量相联系的形式体系（formalism），并讨论散射几何（scattering geometry）、晶体学投影（crystallographic projections）以及有限极化度（finite polarization）的修正方法，形成面向实践的操作指南。

Result: 依据已有文献，LPA能够区分弱磁散射（weak magnetic scattering）与非磁贡献，约束小磁矩分量（small moment components），并解析磁激发的自旋空间各向异性（spin-space anisotropy）。

Conclusion: LPA为确定量子材料（quantum materials）的磁结构（magnetic structures）与表征自旋激发（spin excitations）提供了实用流程；未来机遇包括交错磁体（altermagnets）、非常规超导体（unconventional superconductors）与笼目磁体（kagome magnets）等方向。

Abstract: Neutron scattering provides microscopic information on magnetic order and spin dynamics. Most experiments, however, use unpolarized neutrons and do not fully exploit the information carried by the neutron spin. Longitudinal polarization analysis (LPA), established in the pioneering work of Moon, Riste, and Koehler at Oak Ridge National Laboratory in the late 1960s, separates magnetic and nonmagnetic scattering contributions through their different effects on the neutron spin. In this review, we illustrate how LPA can distinguish weak magnetic scattering from nonmagnetic contributions, constrain small moment components, and resolve the spin-space anisotropy of magnetic excitations. We introduce the formalism connecting measured polarization channels to magnetic quantities and discuss scattering geometry, crystallographic projections, and corrections for finite polarization. Our aim is to provide a practical guide for neutron scatterers using LPA to determine magnetic structures and characterize spin excitations in quantum materials. We conclude with opportunities in altermagnets, unconventional superconductors, and kagome magnets.

</details>

*Yuntian Gu,Chaohui Fan,Ruichen Li,Haoxiang Chen,Weizhong Fu,Yixiao Chen,Dingshun Lv,Weiluo Ren,Tao Xiang,Liwei Wang,Mingpu Qin,Xiang Li*

TL;DR: LaQX 是一个基于 JAX 的开源神经量子态（neural quantum state）库，通过统一的函数式接口整合哈密顿量、采样、优化、可观测量与加速器并行，并支持基态、激发态、格林函数蒙特卡洛、绝热演化与实时演化等多种计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态虽能为相互作用格点费米子与自旋体系提供精确的变分表示，但将新拟设（ansatz）转化为可复现计算，需要哈密顿量、采样、优化、可观测量的协同实现与加速器并行，缺乏统一工具是主要障碍。

Method: 提出基于 JAX 的开源库 LaQX，以公共函数式接口（functional interfaces）组织各组件；除基态变分蒙特卡洛外，还支持激发态计算、格林函数蒙特卡洛（Green's Function Monte Carlo）精修、绝热演化以及基于含时变分蒙特卡洛（time-dependent variational Monte Carlo）的实时演化，并整合了近期哈伯德模型与阻挫磁体高精度研究中的方法。

Result: 该库已用于研究三角格子 Hofstadter-Hubbard 模型，并以可复现计算记录了在 Hubbard、J1-J2 Heisenberg、交错磁（altermagnetic）Hubbard 及相互作用 Hofstadter 模型上的能力；面向函数的模块划分隔离了科学组件，降低了人类开发者与大语言模型（large language models）进行扩展所需的源码上下文。

Conclusion: LaQX 为神经量子态研究提供了可复现、易扩展的开源框架，源代码、文档与可执行示例已公开于 https://github.com/bytedance/laqx。

Abstract: Neural quantum states provide accurate variational representations for interacting lattice fermions and spins. Turning a new ansatz into a reproducible calculation, however, requires coordinated implementations of the Hamiltonian, sampling, optimization, observables, and accelerator parallelism. We present LaQX (Lattice Quantum simulation based on jaX), an open-source neural quantum state library based on JAX that organizes these components through common functional interfaces. Beyond ground-state variational Monte Carlo, LaQX supports excited-state calculations, Green's Function Monte Carlo refinement, adiabatic evolution, and real-time evolution with time-dependent variational Monte Carlo. It consolidates methods developed in recent high-accuracy neural quantum state studies of the Hubbard model and frustrated magnets, and the package has been used to investigate the triangular-lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model. We document these capabilities with reproducible calculations for Hubbard, $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg, altermagnetic Hubbard, and interacting Hofstadter models. Function-oriented modules separate the scientific components and limit the source context needed for extensions by human developers and large language models. Source code, documentation, and executable examples are available at https://github.com/bytedance/laqx.

</details>


### [22] [Why strong spin-orbit coupling does not destabilize the Dirac spin liquid in the Yb delafossites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04607)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文论证三角晶格 Yb 三角铁石（delafossite）中强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）所诱导的 XXZ 各向异性（anisotropy）与键依赖交换（bond-dependent couplings）在单层稳定 Heisenberg 狄拉克自旋液体（Dirac spin liquid, DSL）框架内不产生相关算符，从而保护 U(1) DSL 的稳定性，并讨论层间与向 120° 序转变时的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NaYbSe$_2$、KYbSe$_2$、CsYbSe$_2$ 与 NaYbO$_2$ 被视为三角晶格 U(1) 狄拉克自旋液体的候选材料，但其强自旋轨道耦合引入的 XXZ 各向异性与键依赖耦合是否破坏 DSL 尚不明确，需系统检验这些微扰的相关性（relevance）。

Method: 在单层稳定 Heisenberg DSL 的有效理论框架下，对自旋轨道耦合诱导的各类算符做对称性与量纲分析：考察 Lorentz 标量（Lorentz-scalar）算符、四费米子（four-fermion）算符的 SU(4) 多重态归属与大 $N$ 标度维数，并结合键中心反演（inversion）对称性讨论 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 通道；同时分析菱面体堆叠（rhombohedral stacking）的层间效应，并用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）研究向 120° 序的转变。

Result: 单层中这些耦合不产生自身的相关 Lorentz 标量算符；键中心反演消除了使 kagome DSL 能隙化的 DM 通道，任意阶均禁止质量、密度与流，并在连续性假设下至电荷三不出现新单极子（monopole）项。自旋轨道耦合诱导的 Lorentz 标量四费米子算符均与 Heisenberg 允许的算符共享 SU(4) 多重态及标度维数；九个无对应伙伴的非标量四次项仅出现在二阶且在大 $N$ 下无关。Heisenberg DSL 本身存在大 $N$ 维数 2.92 的 Lorentz 矢量四次项，稳定相亦须使其无关。层间方面，菱面体堆叠下自旋轨道耦合开启层间单极子通道；若 Heisenberg DSL 为多临界（multicritical），则小至 KYbSe$_2$ 量级的各向异性仅在数十 mK 以下起作用；在向 120° 序的转变处各向异性相关，精确对角化显示易面各向异性选择手性 Ising（chiral-Ising）普适类。

Conclusion: 三角晶格 Yb 三角铁石的单层 U(1) DSL 在对称性上对自旋轨道耦合诱导的微扰具有显著鲁棒性，但层间单极子通道与向 120° 序转变处各向异性的相关性仍需关注；现有 KYbSe$_2$ 中子数据尚不能确定 K 点指数，K 点无能隙面外自旋涨落可作为手性 Ising 选择的检验。

Abstract: The Yb delafossites NaYbSe$_2$, KYbSe$_2$, CsYbSe$_2$ and NaYbO$_2$ are candidates for the U(1) Dirac spin liquid (DSL) of the triangular lattice. Strong spin-orbit coupling gives their exchange an XXZ anisotropy $Δ$ and bond-dependent couplings $J_{\pm\pm}$, $J_{z\pm}$ that lock the spin frame to the lattice. We show that, within a single layer of a stable Heisenberg DSL, these couplings generate no relevant Lorentz-scalar operator of their own. Inversion at the bond centers removes the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya channel that gaps the kagome DSL, so the locked symmetry forbids a mass, density or current at any order and, assuming continuity, any new monopole term through charge three. Every Lorentz-scalar four-fermion operator induced by spin-orbit coupling shares an SU(4) multiplet, and hence its scaling dimension, with a Heisenberg-allowed one, and the nine non-scalar quartics without such a partner arise only at second order and are irrelevant at large $N$. The Heisenberg DSL itself allows a Lorentz-vector quartic of large-$N$ dimension 2.92, which a stable phase must also render irrelevant. Beyond a single layer, spin-orbit coupling opens an interlayer monopole channel in rhombohedral stacking. If the Heisenberg DSL is instead multicritical, an anisotropy as small as that of KYbSe$_2$ matters only below tens of mK. At the transition to 120$^\circ$ order the anisotropy is relevant, and exact diagonalization indicates that easy-plane anisotropy selects the chiral-Ising class. Existing KYbSe$_2$ neutron data below $J$ do not yet fix the K-point exponent, and gapless out-of-plane spin fluctuations at the K point would test the chiral-Ising selection.

</details>

*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 针对三角晶格 Yb 蓝铜矿（delafossite）候选 U(1) Dirac 自旋液体，系统分析自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）下各向异性与键依赖交换（bond-dependent exchange）所诱导算符的对称性与量纲，并讨论其相变与可观测信号。

Solving the model in real time using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism, we treat closed and open systems within a unified framework and, in both cases, consider a thermal steady state to map their phase diagrams. Coupling the boson with a thermal environment induces a mass shift that deforms the boundaries between different regimes and exhausts the criticality budget, thus imposing a bound on temperature and dissipation rate compatible with self-tuned criticality. Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用大规模神经网络量子态变分蒙特卡洛方法，为spin-1/2 kagome Heisenberg反铁磁体的基态提供了U(1) Dirac自旋液体（Dirac spin liquid）的证据。

  <summary>Details</summary>

We perform large-scale variational Monte-Carlo calculations using neural-network quantum states (NQSs) with kagome-geometry-aware message-passing convolution on two-dimensional tori containing up to 300 spins, and achieve exceptionally low variational energies for the kagome model. The spin and dimer structure factors show no evidence of magnetic or valence-bond long-range order. Moreover, the dominant symmetry-resolved spin and dimer correlations exhibit a common scaling behavior consistent with the emergent $SO(6)$ monopole multiplet of the Dirac spin liquid. Finally, the fidelity of the optimized NQSs with Gutzwiller-projected mean-field states increasingly favors the Dirac state over the competing $Z_{2}$ candidates as the system size grows. Together, these complementary results provide evidence for a $U(1)$ Dirac spin-liquid ground state in the kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet.

</details>

*Yong-Huan Wang,Shuai Chen,Xin-Chi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，密度辅助自旋轨道耦合（density-assisted spin-orbit coupling）可作为在强关联体系中实现外尔超流的一种机制。

Abstract: We propose a scheme to realize Weyl superfluidity from topologically trivial bands in the strongly correlated regime of a three-dimensional optical Raman lattice. The scheme builds on a pairing mechanism arising from the interplay between a large Zeeman splitting and strong Hubbard interaction. Spin-flip hopping suppressed by the large Zeeman detuning is resonantly restored when the attractive Hubbard interaction compensates the energy mismatch, yielding a correlated spin-orbit coupling that depends on the local particle configuration. Combining this density-dependent in-plane spin-orbit coupling with density-dependent spin-conserving interlayer tunneling extends the pairing mechanism to three dimensions. A self-consistent mean-field treatment of the microscopic model yields Weyl superfluids with two, four, or eight nodes, together with fully gapped weak topological superfluids. Tuning the interaction strength and particle filling drives transitions between these phases and controls the number of Weyl nodes. We further propose an experimental implementation with ultracold $^{40}\mathrm{K}$ atoms. Our results highlight density-assisted spin-orbit coupling as a mechanism for realizing Weyl superfluidity in strongly correlated systems.

Conclusion: 整个应用为单一自包含 HTML 文件，每个模型配有面向研究生的“Learn”面板，涵盖方程、核心物理、解析预测与实验配对活动及教材参考文献。

Abstract: Condensate is an interactive simulator of two-dimensional quantum fluids that runs in a web browser on phones, tablets and computers, with no installation. It solves eight models built on the Gross-Pitaevskii equation: a `vanilla' condensate, a dipolar gas with tilted dipoles, a binary mixture with quantum droplets, Rabi and spin--orbit coupled gases, a self-gravitating (Schrödinger-Poisson) condensate, a driven-dissipative polariton fluid, a rotating condensate and a finite-temperature stochastic projected Gross--Pitaevskii equation. A split-step Fourier solver runs on the graphics processor through WebGPU at up to $1024^2$ grid points, with a double-precision CPU engine as a fallback and reference. Users stir the fluid with a laser-like beam, imprint quantised vortices and change parameters while the fluid evolves. Each model comes with a ``Learn'' panel written for graduate students, giving the equations, core physics, and activities that pair an analytic prediction with an experiment in the app, and references keyed to a standard textbook. The whole application is one self-contained HTML file. In this accompanying text, we describe the models, the numerical method and the teaching material.

</details>

Abstract: We study the superfluid phases of a two-dimensional degenerate Fermi gas in an optical lattice subject to a $d_{xy}$-wave altermagnetic term that couples to the in-plane spin component. The single-particle Hamiltonian generates anisotropic Fermi surfaces for the two pseudo-spin species while maintaining zero net polarization. Using the Bogoliubov-de Gennes approach, we show that this anisotropy favors Cooper pairing at a finite center-of-mass momentum, producing a competition between Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) and Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) pairing and leading to FFLO superfluids. We further examine the effect of a Zeeman-induced spin polarization and find that triplet and singlet pairing channels coexist and compete, resulting in a momentum-selective pairing pattern. Remarkably, this triplet pairing emerges exclusively in the FFLO phase and is entirely absent in the BCS phase. The model is readily realizable in ultracold atoms with current experimental techniques, providing a feasible route for quantum emulation and for studying unconventional superfluid phases.

Abstract: Magnetic field detection is of great significance in modern fields such as medical diagnostics, geological exploration, inertial navigation, and space physics. In this work, we present the parameter dependence of this scheme and perform a systematic optimization. We introduce a Bayesian optimization algorithm to automatically and efficiently search for the optimal combination of key parameters, thereby significantly enhancing the sensitivity of the magnetic field gradient measurement. The results show that the optimized gradiometer improves the sensitivity by a factor of five. This work not only provides a new approach for magnetic field gradient measurement but also serves as a reference for machine learning-empowered quantum precision measurement.

</details>


### [34] [Rotating two-dimensional Bose--Einstein condensates in power-law traps: collapse, Thomas--Fermi scaling, and the inverse-square endpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04849)

TL;DR: 本文在二维情形下追踪动能与接触能（contact energy）在标度变换下的同阶平衡，沿幂律势族 $-2\le s\le4$（$s\neq0$）、两种耦合符号与旋转情形，系统刻画吸引塌缩、排斥展开及涡旋态的能量误差结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维中动能与接触能在伸缩下同阶标度，使陷阱 $V=r^s/s$ 的能量随 $λ^{-s}$ 变化，这一平衡可能统一描述不同幂律陷阱中的塌缩与展开行为，但缺乏贯穿整族势的系统分析。

Result: 吸引相互作用使云在 Townes 耦合（Townes coupling）$g_*$ 处塌缩，宽度 $\ell\propto\delta^{1/(s+2)}$，$\delta=1-|g|/g_*$；排斥使半径按 Thomas–Fermi（TF）标度 $\propto g^{1/(s+2)}$。旋转塌缩在 $Ω$ 超过偶极频率后离开轴线，该频率仅在 $s>2$ 时软化，四极陷阱中发生于 $\delta\simeq0.32\,Ω^6$，与 Bogoliubov 阈值相差 3% 以内；随后在旋转系势的极小环上塌缩，并在 $Ω=(3g/\pi)^{1/6}$ 处清空排斥 TF 云。排斥涡旋态的 TF 能量误差精确分解为密度梯度项与流动项，二者量级相当或后者更大。$s=-2$ 时仅截断（cutoff）设定长度，能量与其严格协变，凝聚体对两种符号均落向中心，排斥留下密度 $\propto1/(gr^2)$、范围随 $g$ 指数增长的晕（halo）。近塌缩时 split-step 虚时方法存在一阶定态偏差，残差检验无法察觉。

Conclusion: 二维动能—接触能标度平衡贯穿整个幂律陷阱族，可为吸引塌缩、旋转失稳、排斥 TF 云与 $s=-2$ 情形的标度行为提供统一描述，并揭示虚时数值方法在近塌缩区的隐蔽偏差。

Abstract: In two dimensions, the kinetic and contact energies scale identically under dilation, a trap $V=r^s/s$ as $λ^{-s}$. We follow this balance through the power-law family $-2\le s\le4$, $s\neq0$, for both signs of the coupling $g$, with rotation, using scaling bounds, radial solutions, Bogoliubov spectra, and two-dimensional imaginary-time states. Attraction collapses the cloud at the Townes coupling $g_{*}$ in every trap, with width $\ell\proptoδ^{1/(s+2)}$, $δ=1-|g|/g_{*}$; repulsion spreads it to a Thomas--Fermi (TF) radius $\propto g^{1/(s+2)}$. A rotating collapse leaves the axis once the rotation frequency $Ω$ exceeds its dipole frequency, which softens only for $s>2$; in a quartic trap this happens at $δ\simeq0.32\,Ω^6$, within $3\%$ of the Bogoliubov threshold; it then collapses on the minimum ring of the rotating-frame potential, which also empties a repulsive TF cloud at $Ω=(3g/π)^{1/6}$. For repulsive vortex states the TF energy error splits exactly into a density-gradient and a flow part of comparable or larger size. At $s=-2$ only the cutoff sets a length: the energy is exactly covariant with it, the condensate falls to the center for both signs, and repulsion leaves a halo of density $\propto1/(gr^2)$ whose extent grows exponentially with $g$. Near collapse, split-step imaginary time has a first-order stationary bias invisible to residual tests.

</details>


### [35] [Single-anchor Delta-learning with the cluster Gutzwiller approximation: predicting phase boundaries of strongly correlated bosons in optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04936)

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出单锚点 Delta 学习（single-anchor Delta-learning, SA-Delta），仅用目标集团的一个高成本临界点即可重建完整相边界，显著降低集团 Gutzwiller 方法研究强关联玻色系统的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集团 Gutzwiller 方法（cluster Gutzwiller method）可显式处理团簇内量子涨落，但计算成本随团簇尺寸指数增长，导致大团簇相图计算代价高昂；此前的多点回归 Delta 学习（multipoint regression Delta-learning, MPR-Delta）仍需目标团簇的四个高成本临界点，对大团簇而言依然昂贵。

Method: 将 Delta 学习引入集团 Gutzwiller 计算，提出单锚点 Delta 学习（SA-Delta）：学习小团簇与大团簇结果之间的差异，仅依赖目标团簇的单个高成本临界点来重建完整的目标相边界。

Result: 在若干类 Bose-Hubbard 模型上测试，SA-Delta 在所考察情形中以更少的高成本数据点取得比 MPR-Delta 更低的重建误差。

Conclusion: SA-Delta 与集团 Gutzwiller 近似结合，为复杂光晶格中强关联玻色系统的相变研究提供了高效框架，并有望推广至更复杂的量子多体系统。

Abstract: The cluster Gutzwiller method is widely used to study strongly correlated bosonic systems in optical lattices because it explicitly treats quantum fluctuations within a cluster. However, its computational cost grows exponentially with the cluster size, making large-cluster phase diagrams computationally expensive. To reduce this cost, we recently introduced Delta-learning into cluster Gutzwiller calculations and proposed multipoint regression Delta-learning (MPR-Delta), which learns the difference between small- and large-cluster results from four high-cost critical points of the target cluster and thereby predicts the complete target phase boundary. For sufficiently large target clusters, however, even computing one critical point on the phase boundary is already expensive, so obtaining four such points remains highly costly. A natural question then arises: can the complete target phase boundary be predicted with fewer high-cost critical points of the target cluster? To address this question, we propose single-anchor Delta-learning (SA-Delta), which requires only a single high-cost critical point of the target cluster to reconstruct the complete target phase boundary, thereby greatly reducing the computational cost. We test this framework on several classes of Bose-Hubbard models and find that, in the cases considered, it achieves lower reconstruction errors with fewer high-cost points than MPR-Delta. The combination of SA-Delta with the cluster Gutzwiller approximation thus provides an efficient framework for studying phase transitions of strongly correlated bosonic systems in complex optical lattices, and may be extended to more complex quantum many-body systems.

</details>

*Klaas Luyckx,Michiel Yzewyn,Michiel Wouters*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在势梯度（potential gradient）存在时，耦合光子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（photonic Bose-Einstein condensates）阵列的同步（synchronization）行为及失同步（desynchronization）阈值的物理起源与参数依赖。

Method: 针对染料填充微腔阵列（dye-filled microcavity arrays）建立耦合光子BEC模型；在无噪声情形下分析动力学不稳定性（dynamical instability）；对两站点系统解析推导允许同步解的最大势差，并推广至N站点系统；进一步引入量子涨落（quantum fluctuations）考察随机相位滑移（stochastic phase slips）的影响。

Result: 无噪声时失同步阈值源于动力学不稳定性；两站点系统中最大可同步势差强烈依赖热化速率（thermalization rate）；N站点系统临界势差服从N^{-3}幂律标度（power law scaling）。含量子涨落时，随机相位滑移使尖锐转变展宽，但相速度（phase velocity）与一阶空间相干性（first order spatial coherence）仍能可靠判定不稳定性阈值。

Conclusion: 光子BEC阵列的同步-失同步转变可由动力学不稳定性刻画，其临界势差具有明确的热化速率依赖与N^{-3}标度；量子涨落虽展宽转变，但相速度与一阶空间相干性可作为稳健的不稳定性判据。

Abstract: We investigate the synchronization of coupled photonic Bose-Einstein condensates in dye-filled microcavity arrays, subject to a potential gradient. We show that in the absence of noise the desynchronisation threshold is the result of a dynamical instability. For a two-site system, we analytically derive the maximal potential difference that allows for a synchronized solution as a function of the system parameters and find that it crucially depends on the thermalization rate. For systems with $N$ sites, we find a $N^{-3}$ power law scaling for the critical potential difference. When quantum fluctuations are included, stochastic phase slips broaden the sharp transitions, yet the phase velocity and first order spatial coherence still reliably identify the instability threshold.

</details>


### [37] [Vortices in a two-dimensional fermionic superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05017)
*Artak Mkrtchyan,Hans Leonard Michel,Moritz von Usslar,René Henke,Cesar Raymundo Cabrera,Henning Moritz*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在均匀二维（2D）BEC–BCS 交叉区费米气体中实现了量子化涡旋（quantized vortices）的可控产生，观测到涡旋绝对电荷的量子化台阶与以 2π 为单位的相位绕转，并发现仅二维气体中涡旋数目随温度增长，反映 BKT 涡旋对的热激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化涡旋是超流性的标志，并已成为从超流氦到第二类超导体等系统的重要探针；但在相位涨落增强、超流转变由 Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless（BKT）机制主导的二维超冷费米气体中，涡旋观测始终未能实现。

Method: 在均匀二维 BEC–BCS 交叉区气体中，通过连续调节两个移动障碍物（moving obstacles）的速度实现涡旋的可控产生；采用物质波干涉测量（matter-wave interferometry）提取相位绕转，并与三维气体测量进行对比。

Result: 随障碍物速度连续调节，涡旋绝对电荷呈现量子化台阶；干涉测量显示特征性的相位绕转，且以 2π 为量子化单位。二维与三维气体表现显著差异：仅二维中涡旋数目随温度增加，体现 BKT 涡旋对的热激活（thermal activation）。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了二维费米子超流性的证据，并为研究 BKT 物理、涡旋控制的输运与电阻（transport and resistance），以及探测自旋不平衡二维气体中的非常规超流性提供了手段。

Abstract: Quantized vortices are a hallmark of superfluidity. They have proven to be an indispensable probe for systems ranging from superfluid helium to type-II superconductors. In ultracold two-dimensional (2D) Fermi gases, where phase fluctuations are enhanced and the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) mechanism governs the superfluid transition, their observation has remained elusive. Here, we report on the controlled creation of quantized vortices in a homogeneous 2D gas in the BEC--BCS crossover. By continuously tuning the velocity of two moving obstacles, we observe quantized steps in the absolute vortex charge. Matter-wave interferometry reveals the characteristic phase winding, quantized in units of $2π$. Measurements on 2D and 3D gases show a striking difference: only in 2D does the vortex number grow with temperature, reflecting thermal activation of BKT vortex pairs. Our approach provides evidence of fermionic superfluidity in 2D and offers a means to study BKT physics, vortex-controlled transport and resistance, as well as to detect unconventional superfluidity in spin-imbalanced 2D gases.

### [38] [Observation of Scaling of Higher-Order Flow in a Unitary Fermi Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05028)
*Shuxian Yu,Zhijie Zhang,Yunyi Zhao,Shujin Deng,Meng-Zi Huang,Haibin Wu,Yu-Gang Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在酉费米气体（unitary Fermi gas）中通过释放各向异性近均匀势阱，测量至七阶的集体流谐波，发现早期归一化演化具有普适性，而晚期普适性破缺并呈现与粘性声学标度一致的二次衰减规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体流（collective flow）是强相互作用量子物质的基本表现，但不同空间谐波（spatial harmonics）间集体流如何涌现、耗散如何改变其演化仍缺乏实验认识。

Method: 将酉费米气初始囚禁于带有工程化角向形变的近均匀势阱中，突然撤去束缚以触发非平衡膨胀（nonequilibrium expansion），并解析至七阶的各向异性流谐波，同时开展温度依赖测量。

Result: 经归一化并按特征膨胀时间尺度重标度后，不同谐波与系统尺寸在早期呈现普适演化；较长时间尺度下普适性破缺，谐波衰减对阶数近似呈二次依赖，符合粘性声学标度（viscous acoustic scaling）；温度依赖结果显示剪切粘度（shear viscosity）在高阶流耗散演化中的作用。

Conclusion: 结果提供了探究普适流体力学膨胀（universal hydrodynamic expansion）与耗散输运（dissipative transport）相互作用的途径，并将超冷量子气体的集体动力学与强相互作用核物质中的各向异性流相联系。

Abstract: Collective flow emerging from spatially anisotropic many-body systems is a fundamental manifestation of strongly interacting quantum matter, from ultracold quantum gases to the quark-gluon plasma created in relativistic heavy-ion collisions. Yet how collective flow emerges across different spatial harmonics and how dissipation modifies its evolution remain largely unexplored. Here we investigate the nonequilibrium expansion of a unitary Fermi gas initially confined in nearly homogeneous potentials with engineered angular deformations. By suddenly releasing the confinement, we resolve anisotropic flow harmonics up to the seventh order. Remarkably, after normalization and rescaling by the characteristic expansion timescale, the different harmonics and system sizes exhibit a universal early-time evolution. At longer times, this universality breaks down and we observe an approximately quadratic dependence of the harmonic attenuation on harmonic order, consistent with viscous acoustic scaling. Furthermore, temperature-dependent measurements reveal the role of shear viscosity in the dissipative evolution of higher-order flow. Our results open a new route to exploring the interplay between universal hydrodynamic expansion and dissipative transport, connecting collective dynamics in ultracold quantum gases with anisotropic flow in strongly interacting nuclear matter.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Accurate prediction of phase equilibria is essential for chemical process design, particularly in systems characterized by complex intermolecular interactions. This work investigates the influence of conformer selection and sigma-profile characteristics on COSMO-RS predictions of liquid-liquid equilibria (LLE), using the tetrahydrofurfuryl alcohol (THFA)-methyl isobutyl ketone (MIBK)-water system as a case study. Calculations were performed at the TZVP and TZVPD-FINE levels of theory. While TZVP failed to capture the experimentally observed phase behavior, TZVPD-FINE provided substantially improved predictions. The differences are attributed to the relative importance assigned to less-polar conformers and more-polar conformers capable of hydrogen bonding. Although the TZVPD-FINE calculations reproduced the main experimental trends, the predicted organic-phase composition showed a mean percentage deviation of 5.27%, slightly exceeding the adopted 5% agreement threshold. Investigations using individual conformers demonstrated that predictions can be strongly influenced by conformer selection and that representative conformer ensembles are necessary for reliable results. Further analysis showed that diversity in sigma profiles is more important for predictive accuracy than the total number of conformers. These findings emphasize the importance of carefully selecting conformers when applying COSMO-RS to liquid-liquid equilibria involving phases of contrasting polarity.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以反对称性与变分原理为起点推导能量泛函、Fock 算子与轨道方程；讨论限制型、非限制型与广义表述（restricted/unrestricted/generalized formulations），并涵盖正交化、自洽场迭代（SCF iteration）、收敛加速、基于重叠的占据选择及轨道分析；基组部分经高斯乘积定理（Gaussian product theorem）推导单、双电子积分、递推关系与积分筛选，并联系二阶 Møller–Plesset 微扰理论（MP2）的相关能表达式。

Result: 阐明变分空间的选择如何影响自旋对称性与对称性破缺，区分轨道驻定性（stationarity）与稳定性（stability），并明确基组不完备性、收缩与叠加误差（superposition error）以及积分变换成本等问题。

Conclusion: 通过例题与习题解答将推导与实用计算相连接，并借助历史视角与记法约定，为分子电子结构理论的近似与算法基础提供系统的教学性梳理。

Abstract: This chapter accompanies the lectures on Hartree--Fock theory and on basis sets and molecular integrals at the \href{https://www.esqc.org}{European Summer School of Quantum Chemistry (ESQC) 2026}. Intended for doctoral students, it develops the single-determinant description of electronic structure from antisymmetry and the variational principle to the orbital equations and their finite-basis matrix representation. Detailed derivations connect the energy functional, the Fock operator, excited determinants, and the interpretation of orbital energies. Restricted, unrestricted, and generalized formulations show how the choice of variational space affects spin symmetry, symmetry breaking, and the limitations of the Hartree--Fock approximation.
  The computational development covers orthogonalization, self-consistent-field iteration, convergence acceleration, overlap-based occupation selection, and orbital analysis, distinguishing orbital stationarity from stability. A connection to second-order Møller--Plesset perturbation theory explains the correlation-energy expression and the cost of transforming electron-repulsion integrals from atomic to molecular orbitals.
  The basis-set and integral development examines radial and angular flexibility, contraction, basis-set incompleteness, and superposition error. The Gaussian product theorem leads to one- and two-electron integrals, recurrence relations, integral screening, and low-rank representations. Worked examples and exercises with detailed solutions connect these derivations to practical calculations. Historical perspectives and explicit notation conventions provide context for the approximations and algorithms that underpin molecular electronic-structure theory.

</details>


### [41] [samQEq: universal charge equilibration for molecules and conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03687)
*Nicholas Solan,David Sun,Tod A. Pascal*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: samQEq 是一个面向 LAMMPS 的统一电荷平衡（charge equilibration, QEq）软件包，将分子中性约束与导体类体系的自能正则化整合进同一能量泛函与无矩阵求解框架，可同时处理分子、导体及其界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局约束下的电荷平衡允许电荷在分子间无限流动，而涨落电荷（fluctuating-charge）模型以每分子一个中性约束加以限制；金属则需要相反设定——全局约束配合趋近于零的硬度（hardness），但此时电荷算符会变为不定，除非纳入每个电荷自身的自能。

Method: 以正交投影算符（orthogonal projector）施加逐分子中性约束，并针对指定导体类型引入宽度窄于导体自身高斯分布的有限宽度自能项，使导体部分在任何硬度下保持正定；倒空间静电（reciprocal-space electrostatics）在矩阵-向量积内部求值，三种屏蔽核（shielding kernels）作为运行时设置。

Result: 在覆盖水膜的金板（gold slab）上，算符在模型导体极限下仍保持正定，金属在 4 Å 以上对点电荷呈现恒定屏蔽量，水膜电荷在动力学中保持有界；无需重新拟合即复现已发表的涨落电荷水模型，并以约 $10^{-9}\,e$ 精度复现原生 ReaxFF 电荷平衡步骤；单个四卡 V100 节点的吞吐量约为 128 核 CPU 节点的两倍。

Conclusion: samQEq 为分子、导体及其界面提供了统一的电荷平衡层，并具备 GPU 加速与较高精度一致性。

Abstract: Charge equilibration assigns atomic charges by equalizing an atomic chemical potential built from electronegativity, hardness and the Coulomb interaction. Under one global constraint it lets charge flow without limit between molecules, which fluctuating-charge models prevent with one neutrality constraint per molecule. A metal needs the opposite: a global constraint and a hardness near zero, and there the charge operator becomes indefinite unless it includes each charge's own self-energy. Here, we present samQEq (solids-and-molecules QEq), a unified charge-equilibration package for LAMMPS that combines the two constructions in one energy functional and one matrix-free solve. An orthogonal projector imposes one neutrality constraint per molecule, and the finite-width self-energy of a Gaussian charge on designated conductor types, narrower than the conductor's own Gaussian, keeps the conductor's part of the operator positive definite at any hardness. On a gold slab carrying a water film, the operator stays definite down to the conductor limit of the model, the metal screens a point charge by a constant amount at every height beyond 4 Å, and the film's charges stay bounded under dynamics. Reciprocal-space electrostatics is evaluated inside the matrix-vector product, and three distinct shielding kernels are run-time settings. We verify the implementation by reproducing a published fluctuating-charge water model without refitting, and the native ReaxFF charge-equilibration step to approximately $10^{-9}\,e$. On a single four-V100 node the largest tested systems run at approximately twice the throughput of a 128-core CPU node. samQEq thus provides one charge-equilibration layer for molecules, conductors and the interfaces between them.

</details>

TL;DR: 该工作为开源水平集（level-set）仿真器 ViennaPS 提出一种表面模型，其速率定律、雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）与表面速度均由反应文件自动推导，并结合光线追踪（ray-traced）通量求解覆盖率。同一编译模型即可覆盖八种化学工艺，并对已有的手写硅刻蚀模型实现高精度复现。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水平集刻蚀/沉积仿真中，吸附物覆盖率（adsorbate coverage）的平衡方程需针对每种化学体系手工推导与编码，而基于体素的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）剖面仿真器则以数据形式读取反应机理，两者在可扩展性上存在差距。该研究旨在让水平集仿真器同样以反应文件为输入，减少手工建模负担。

Result: 在单点计算中，该模型以七位有效数字复现手写硅刻蚀模型；在带掩模沟槽（masked trench）情形下，结果处于蒙特卡洛噪声范围内。在高深宽比（high-aspect-ratio）空腔中，氧化铝（alumina）的共形性（conformality）由三甲基铝（trimethylaluminium）的反应概率决定，且该概率落在实测范围内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，数据驱动、由反应文件生成的表面模型可在水平集框架中替代逐化学体系的手工编码，并在多类工艺与几何结构下保持与既有模型及实验范围一致的可信度。

Abstract: Level-set simulators of etching and deposition move the surface with a velocity that depends on adsorbate coverages. These coverages are usually computed from balances solved and coded by hand for each chemistry, whereas voxel-based Monte Carlo profile simulators read the reaction mechanism as data. For the open-source level-set simulator ViennaPS, this work presents a surface model whose rate laws, Jacobian and velocity are derived from a reaction file and evaluated with ray-traced fluxes. Steady-state coverages are obtained with Newton's method and transient coverages with a conservative integrator. One compiled model is applied to eight chemistries for chemical vapour deposition, selective epitaxy, plasma etching, sputtering and atomic layer deposition. It reproduces a hand-written silicon etch model to seven significant digits at a single point and within the Monte Carlo noise in a masked trench. In a high-aspect-ratio cavity, alumina conformality is determined by a trimethylaluminium reaction probability within the measured range.

</details>


### [43] [Bicarbonate/carbonic acid equilibria in aqueous solutions: Conformational Dynamics, Ion-Pair Speciation, and Free Energy Landscapes from Ab Initio Molecular Dynamics and Metadynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04121)
*Prabhat Prakash,Karnamohit Ranka,Frances A. Houle,William A. Goddard*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 结合无偏PBE-D3分子动力学与元动力学，解析碳酸（H2CO3）在水中的酸解离与脱水反应，指出只有以溶剂共享离子对（SSIP）为反应态才能将pKa1估计修正至2.4，并给出脱水势垒与自由能面路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳酸的酸解离与脱水两个基元反应均与氢键网络中的质子转移（proton transfer）耦合，导致二者难以分辨，需在显式溶剂环境下加以解析。

Result: 无偏轨迹显示经长寿命碳酸氢根（bicarbonate）频繁发生cis-cis ⇌ cis-trans互变，并存在短寿命三水环[H2O…H3O+…H2O]构成的溶剂共享离子对（SSIP），仅16–23%的去质子化事件达到溶剂分离离子对（SShIP）；以SSIP为反应态将pKa1由1.1修正为2.4（实验值3.45–3.59）。脱水势垒为17.8（1D）与15.3 kcal/mol（2D），产物CO2+H2O比H2CO3低2.8 kcal/mol；C–O断裂经CO2+OH-中间态，再下降9.9 kcal/mol至产物。

Conclusion: 离子对（ion pair）结构的正确界定显著影响pKa1估计；脱水路径经由CO2+OH-中间体完成，二维自由能面揭示了酸解离与脱水的耦合机制。

Abstract: Carbonic acid (H2CO3) is connected with the dissolved CO2 and bicarbonate (HCO3-) equilibria in aqueous solutions. Its two elementary reactions in water, acid dissociation and dehydration to CO2, are difficult to resolve because both are coupled to proton transfer through hydrogen-bonded networks. In this work, we combine 0.5 ns of unbiased PBE-D3 molecular dynamics of 1 molar solution of H2CO3 with metadynamics on the O - H coordination (H2CO3 = HCO3- + H+) and the C - O distance of the leaving hydroxyl (H2CO3 <=> CO2 + H2O). These unbiased trajectories show frequent cis-cis <=> cis-trans interconversion through long-lived bicarbonate (well separated from solvated proton, H3O+, and also mediated by a short-lived three-water [H2O...H3O+...H2O] ring that acts as a solvent-shared ion pair (SSIP); only 16-23% of deprotonation events reach a solvent-separated ion pair (SShIP). Resolving ion-pairs as SSIP for proper reaction leads to an improved estimate of pKa1 from 1.1 to 2.4, toward the experimental 3.45-3.59 (measured at supposedly infinite dilution). Further, metadynamics simulations provide a dehydration barrier of 17.8 (1D) and 15.3 kcal/mol (2D), suggesting that the final product CO2 + H2O is 2.8 kcal/mol below H2CO3. Our two-dimensional free energy surface also shows that C--O cleavage proceeds through the CO2 + OH- which further goes down by 9.9 kcal/mol to product CO2 + H2O.

</details>

*Øystein Gullbrekken,Sondre Kvalvåg Schnell*

TL;DR: 采用含质子转移的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）分子动力学模拟，计算不同浓度 KOH 水溶液的输运性质，发现质子转移机制对电导率的贡献在低浓度下显著、在极高浓度下减弱。

Abstract: Proton transfer is responsible for the anomalous high conductivity in acidic or basic aqueous solutions. Yet, the contribution of the proton transfer mechanism to the conductivity of concentrated aqueous solutions is unknown. In this work, we compute the transport properties of KOH(aq) systems with different concentrations, using Molecular Dynamics simulations with a Machine Learning Interatomic Potential (MLIP), which includes proton transfer. We quantify the contribution of the proton transfer mechanism to the diffusivities of hydroxide ions and water molecules, and the ionic conductivity and transport numbers of the electrolytes. The contribution is substantial at lower concentrations, but diminishes at very high concentrations.

</details>


### [45] [Efficient simulations of spectra and transport for multiorbital quantum impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05185)
*Hao-En Li,Zhen Huang,Lin Lin*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出耦合簇耦合Lindblad方法（C$^3$L），将耦合-Lindblad紧凑浴表示（coupled-Lindblad compact bath）与耦合簇理论（coupled-cluster theory）结合，确定性求解量子杂质（quantum impurity）非平衡稳态及格林函数，计算成本较QMC降低4–5个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多关联轨道显式处理时的量子杂质计算困难，尤其非平衡稳态情形；现有实时量子蒙特卡罗（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）求解器存在符号问题（sign problem）与高昂计算成本，限制了可及问题范围。

Method: C$^3$L为确定性方法，融合近期发展的耦合-Lindblad紧凑浴表示与耦合簇理论，可直接在虚频与实频下计算稳态与格林函数（Green's functions）；具有多项式计算复杂度（polynomial computational complexity），并规避QMC的符号问题，且可在GPU上加速。

Result: 实时多轨道基准中与最先进QMC结果高度一致，同时计算成本降低4–5个数量级，GPU上再获10–100倍加速；实现此前实时QMC难以触及的四轨道非平衡杂质（含一般相互作用与偏置库）计算，并获得与分子结扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）实验相关的共振分辨输运特征。

Conclusion: C$^3$L为多轨道非平衡量子杂质提供确定性、多项式复杂度且无符号问题的求解途径，显著拓展实时杂质计算的可及范围，并具备与实验输运谱对比的能力。

Abstract: Quantum impurity calculations become challenging when multiple correlated orbitals are treated explicitly, particularly in nonequilibrium steady states. We introduce Coupled-Cluster Coupled Lindblad (C$^3$L), a deterministic approach that combines the recently developed coupled-Lindblad compact bath representation with coupled-cluster theory to compute steady states and Green's functions directly in imaginary and real frequency. C$^3$L has favorable polynomial computational complexity and avoids the sign problem of quantum Monte Carlo (QMC). In a real-time multiorbital benchmark, it agrees closely with state-of-the-art QMC while reducing computational cost by four to five orders of magnitude, with a further 10--100-fold speedup on a GPU. Beyond the practical reach of established real-time QMC solvers, four-orbital nonequilibrium impurities with general interactions and biased reservoirs become accessible with C$^3$L. We obtain resonance-resolved transport signatures relevant to scanning tunneling microscopy experiments on molecular junctions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仪器化压痕（instrumented indentation）可用于评估异质聚合物体系的局部力学性能，但试样制备与压痕协议会显著影响测量响应的定量解释，因此需要建立可重复、参数可控的实验方法学。

Method: 以非增强材料体系作为参考材料，系统考察压痕间距（indentation spacing）、表面制备、最大压痕载荷（500、1000、1750 mN）、时间加载参数与卸载评价的影响；表面抛光用于减小形貌扰动并识别残余压痕轮廓与堆积（pile-up）。

Conclusion: 短纤维增强同时提高平均局部刚度并引入显著的空间异质性，体现在刚度相关与时间相关的细观尺度压痕响应上；所建立的实验方法学可为异质聚合物复合材料的空间分辨表征提供参数依据。

Abstract: Instrumented indentation enables assessment of local mechanical properties in heterogeneous polymer systems, but specimen preparation and the selected indentation protocol significantly influence the quantitative interpretation of the measured response. This study develops and evaluates an experimental methodology for spatially resolved mesoscale characterization of both unreinforced and short fiber-reinforced composites. An unreinforced material system is used as a reference material to systematically examine the effects of indentation spacing, surface preparation, maximum indentation load, temporal loading parameters, and unloading evaluation. Indentation loads of 500, 1000, and 1750 mN are investigated in complementary experimental campaigns. The apparent indentation modulus exhibits moderate sensitivity to indentation load and loading time within the studied ranges. In contrast, indentation depth and the creep-related depth increment display greater sensitivity to the applied load history. Surface polishing minimizes topographical disturbances and enables reliable identification of residual indentation profiles and pile-up. Based on these methodological findings, a final load-hold-unload protocol with an indentation load of 1750 mN, a loading time of 6 s, a holding period of 300 s, and an unloading time of 4 s is applied to both unreinforced material and short fiber-reinforced composites. The mean apparent indentation modulus increases from 2.91 GPa for unreinforced material to 6.65 GPa for short fiber-reinforced composites, while the mean depth of indentation creep decreases from 2.36 to 1.91 microns. The coefficient of variation also increases for both quantities, indicating that short-fiber reinforcement enhances mean local stiffness and introduces pronounced spatial heterogeneity in both stiffness-related and time-dependent mesoscale indentation responses.

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究分析随机耦合循环神经网络在次线性奇激活函数与时间相关噪声下的动力学，通过大N方法区分局部线性稳定性丧失与混沌起始，揭示二者之间存在一个中间区制，并给出去相关时间的临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常将局部稳定性丧失与混沌起始视为同一事件，但噪声时间相关性与激活函数渐近增长可能使二者分离，因此有必要厘清这两个阈值及噪声对内在相关时间发散的影响。

Result: 局部不稳定性与渐近稳定性可在一个共同噪声历史下共存，形成中间区制，其分离程度取决于噪声相关时间与激活函数渐近增长；有限相关时间的内部噪声会在混沌边缘截断内在相关时间发散，给出 tau_{corr,max} ∝ 1/√D_0 的临界标度，而静态随机输入下两阈值重合且相关时间发散；零频参与维度与网络相关时间四次方成反比。

Conclusion: 噪声的时间相关性是分离局部失稳与混沌起始的关键因素，并决定相关时间的截断标度；数值模拟支持大网络预测，同时显示明显的有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of recurrent neural networks with random couplings, sublinear odd activation functions, and temporally correlated noise. Using large N methods, we determine stationary correlations and distinguish the loss of local linear stability from the onset of chaos, characterized by a positive largest Lyapunov exponent. These thresholds delimit an intermediate regime in which local instability coexists with asymptotic stability under a common noise history. Their separation depends on both the noise correlation time and the asymptotic growth of the activation function. We show that internal noise with any finite correlation time cuts off the divergence of the network's intrinsic correlation time tau_corr at the edge of chaos; it exhibits a critical scaling tau_{corr,max} propto 1/sqrt{D_0}, where D_0 is the integrated noise strength. Static random input instead produces coincident thresholds and a divergent correlation time. We also calculate the frequency-dependent participation dimension from a four-point correlation function. Its zero-frequency value is inversely proportional to the fourth power of the network correlation time, predicting a smooth minimum at the weak-noise chaos threshold. Numerical simulations support the large-network predictions and reveal appreciable finite-size effects.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We present the first next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) predictions for inclusive color-singlet toponium hadroproduction within NRQCD in the zero-width limit for the top quark. The central new result is the NNLO QCD correction to the short-distance coefficient for pseudoscalar toponium production. The predictions include electroweak and relativistic corrections, additional production channels, and perturbatively evaluated bound-state matrix elements. At the Large Hadron Collider proton-proton center-of-mass energy of $13\,\mathrm{TeV}$, we obtain a cross section of $4.87^{+0.63}_{-0.65}\,\mathrm{pb}$, with scale and parametric uncertainties combined. The NNLO corrections reduce the renormalization- and factorization-scale uncertainties by approximately a factor of four relative to NLO, to the few-percent level. The dominant remaining uncertainty arises from the Coulomb-scale dependence of the bound-state matrix elements.

Conclusion: 基于简化模型给出的标夸克质量极限偏保守，需修正对 LHC 结果及 HL-LHC、FCC-hh 未来探测潜力的解读。

Abstract: We argue that the squark simplified model commonly used in collider analyses fails to represent realistic low-energy supersymmetric models and significantly underestimates squark production rates at hadron colliders. In particular, we find that the current LHC squark mass limit is realistically in the range of 2.6-3.0 TeV, rather than the 1.7-1.8 TeV range that is typically quoted based on the simplified model.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方案假设希格斯二重态（Higgs doublet）的色三重态（color-triplet）伴侣必须超重，以避免媒介质子衰变（proton decay）；而文献[1]提出分裂可发生在汤川耦合（Yukawa couplings）而非质量部分，使色三重态以 TeV 量级的长寿命 T-粒子（T-particle）形式存在，从而为 GUT 物理提供低能窗口。本文旨在以显式模型实现并检验该机制。

Result: 超对称破缺诱导的重场移动可生成 T-粒子与标准模型场之间的微小耦合，其大小依赖引力微子质量（gravitino mass）的量级：T-粒子寿命或约为 1 秒，或超过宇宙年龄。分析还表明，没有任何 GUT 真空具有正能量密度，而 Minkowski 真空对隧穿（tunneling）是安全的，从而验证了此前由 S-矩阵施加的约束。

Conclusion: 该显式 SO(10) 模型可实现汤川耦合分裂机制并给出可检验的低能唯象学预言；同时强调了 species scale 截断在低能可观测量对 GUT 物理敏感性中的关键作用。

Abstract: In traditional approach to the doublet-triplet splitting problem in grand unified theories (GUTs) it is assumed that color-triplet partner of the Higgs doublet must be superheavy in order not to mediate proton decay. However, it has been proposed [1] that the splitting, instead of the mass sector, can occur among the Yukawa couplings. In this scenario the color-triplet emerges as a long-lived $T$-particle with the mass around the TeV scale. This framework provides a direct low-energy window into GUT physics. In the present paper, we study an explicit supersymmetric $SO(10)$ GUT which realizes this mechanism, including effects of gravity-mediated supersymmetry breaking. We show how the shift of the heavy fields, triggered by supersymmetry-breaking, generates a tiny coupling between the $T$-particle and the Standard Model fields and explore the resulting phenomenology. Depending on the order of gravitino mass in which the coupling emerges, the lifetime of the $T$-particle is either around a second or exceeds the age of the Universe. In our analysis, we validate some earlier constraints on (super)gravity vacua imposed by the $S$-matrix: based on the Goldstino Argument, we show that none of the GUT vacua have positive energy densities, whereas the Minkowski vacua are safe against tunneling. We also highlight the role of the species scale cutoff in sensitivity of the low energy observables towards the GUT physics.

Abstract: Recent studies show that in addition to the flat spacetime, Integration-by-parts (IBP) relations and differential equations can also provide a systematic approach to Feynman integrals in de Sitter spacetime. dSIBP is a Mathematica package for the automated construction of IBP systems and differential equations. For dS integral families with massive and massless propagators, it generates a complete set of IBP seeds, assembles linear systems for external reduction, and constructs differential equations for the reduced master integrals. Method: 采用保持对称性的接触相互作用（contact interaction）描述u、d、s夸克，耦合参数在真空中定标，矢量排斥取标量耦合的0<η<3/2，并对比有效耦合是否含味依赖修饰（flavor dressing）。杂化星部分使用拟合GM1饱和性质（GM1 saturation properties）的相对论平均场（relativistic mean-field）强子相，分别采用Maxwell与Gibbs构造，并引入退禁闭袋位移（deconfinement bag shift）。

Result: 纯夸克物质在该模型中并非自束缚（self-bound），任何η值都无法在满足GW170817潮汐约束的同时给出达到两倍太阳质量的星体，最重构型质量依赖组分夸克包层（envelope）。将退禁闭与手征恢复（chiral restoration）挂钩并要求压强连续，可给出袋位移的上界；在该界内，夸克物质仅当η<1/2时出现于稳定星中，且从不低于约4n0，只存在于2.2–2.35倍太阳质量的星体，稳定质量窗口至多0.02倍太阳质量。

Conclusion: 纯夸克星所需的大矢量强度反而在杂化星中抑制退禁闭；因此中等质量星体的半径与潮汐形变率（tidal deformability）完全由强子状态方程（EoS）决定。采用较软的SLy4 EoS时，相变位置移至最大质量构型本身。

Abstract: We study cold, electrically neutral, beta-equilibrated quark matter with u, d, and s quarks described by a symmetry-preserving contact interaction whose parameters are fixed in vacuum. The vector repulsion is varied as a fraction 0 < eta < 3/2 of the scalar coupling, and results are given with and without the flavor dressing of the effective coupling. Pure quark matter is not self-bound in this model, and no value of eta produces a star that reaches two solar masses while satisfying the tidal constraint from GW170817; the heaviest configurations owe their mass to an envelope of constituent quarks. We then construct hybrid stars with a relativistic mean-field hadronic phase fitted to GM1 saturation properties, using both Maxwell and Gibbs constructions and a deconfinement bag shift. Tying deconfinement to chiral restoration and requiring a continuous pressure yields an upper bound on this shift. Within that bound, quark matter appears inside stable stars only for eta < 1/2, never below about 4 n0, and only in stars between 2.2 and 2.35Msun, over a stable mass window of at most 0.02Msun. The vector strength that pure quark stars need to become massive prevents deconfinement in hybrid stars. Radii and tidal deformabilities of intermediate-mass stars are therefore set by the hadronic EoS alone; with the softer SLy4 EoS the transition moves to the maximum-mass configuration itself.

</details>

Using these inferred neutron form factors, we jointly analyze the COHERENT CsI, Ge, and Ar data and obtain $\sin^2θ_W=0.231^{+0.016}_{-0.015}$, consistent with the Standard Model prediction. These results provide the first electroweak-experimentally constrained benchmarks for nuclear-structure studies, neutrino-sector electroweak tests, and CE$ν$NS signal predictions in dark-matter detectors.

After validating the model against data on $^{20}$Ne and $^{22}$Ne fragmentation on various targets, the production of spectator nuclear fragments, neutrons, and protons in $^{20}$Ne-$^{20}$Ne collisions at the LHC was modeled at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$ TeV. Comparing results obtained with these profiles and with or without MST clustering shows that the calculated yields of spectator nucleons and fragments, their momentum distributions are sensitive to the details of nuclear structure of $^{20,22}$Ne and fragmentation modeling. These models are used as key input in determining the level shifts and widths of the lowest states. We establish the capture orbit in $^3$He to be states of angular momentum $L=2$. Several N-N optical potentials are used to compute the $R_{n/p}$ values for several heavy nuclei and the results are found to be consistent with the data.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 结果表明标量场φ同时驱动宇宙加速膨胀，且模型能够再现观测到的哈勃-勒梅特参数随红移的演化。然而在线性微扰分析中，增长因子方程显示模型预测宇宙结构形成起始红移偏晚，约为z≲1。

Conclusion: 结论是该无暗物质的Horndeski–折射引力模型在背景层面具有可行性，但在大尺度结构形成方面存在明显缺陷，因结构形成启动过晚而无法正确描述LSS，需进一步修正或扩展。

Abstract: We study the formation and evolution of the cosmic large-scale structure (LSS) in a scalar-tensor Horndeski theory of gravity without dark matter. We impose that this theory reduces to Refracted Gravity (RG) in the weak-field limit. RG is a non-relativistic theory where the gravitational permittivity $ε(ρ)$, with $ρ$ the baryonic matter density, provides the gravitational boost required to describe both the dynamics of galaxies and galaxy clusters without dark matter. We show that $ε(ρ)$ equals the function $G_4(\varphi)$ of the scalar field $\varphi$ of our Horndeski model. The background evolution of a flat, matter-dominated, homogeneous and isotropic universe shows that $\varphi$ is also responsible for the accelerated expansion of the universe and that the model is capable of reproducing the observed redshift evolution of the Hubble-Lemaître parameter. We finally develop the linear perturbation theory and study the growth factor equation. The model fails to correctly describe the LSS by predicting a late onset of the formation of cosmic structure at redshift $z\lesssim 1$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究轴子—磁单极相互作用暗能量（axion-monopole interacting dark energy）的宇宙学动力学，通过自治系统（autonomous system）分析其临界点、稳定性与数值演化。结果表明，有限正哈勃参数下的稳定 de Sitter 吸引子需显式宇宙学常数，但无宇宙学常数时仍可出现瞬态加速；相互作用还导致物质期的跟踪（tracking）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在检验微观动机下（microscopically motivated）的暗部门（dark sector）相互作用能否支撑稳定的晚期宇宙加速，并探讨磁单极作为暗物质、超轻轴子作为暗能量的相互作用模型是否具有可行宇宙学行为。

Result: 有限正哈勃参数下的稳定 de Sitter 吸引子需要正宇宙学常数，且对应轴子势的极小值，而势极大值处不稳定；无显式宇宙学常数时仍可能存在瞬态轴子驱动加速。相互作用引发物质期跟踪（吸引性缓慢漂移而非精确临界点）。相互作用有效暗能量状态方程可跨越幻影分界（phantom divide），但物理状态方程保持非幻影。推广至完整 BPS 双子质量后主要动力学特征不变，场演化和相互作用强度依赖微观双子性质。

Conclusion: 结果表明，微观动机下的暗部门相互作用能够产生独特的宇宙学演化和有效暗能量行为，为该类相互作用暗能量模型提供了动力学系统层面的系统刻画。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics of axion-monopole interacting dark energy, examining whether a microscopically motivated dark-sector interaction can sustain stable late-time acceleration. In particular, magnetic monopoles constitute dark matter and an ultralight axion contributes to dark energy, with their interaction arising from the Witten effect through field-dependent dyon masses. We formulate the autonomous system, classify its critical points and stability, and investigate the evolution analytically and numerically. We find that stable de Sitter attractors at finite positive Hubble parameter require a positive cosmological constant and correspond to axion-potential minima, whereas those at maxima are unstable. Nevertheless, transient axion-driven acceleration remains possible without an explicit cosmological constant. Furthermore, the interaction induces matter-era tracking, namely an attracting slow-drift regime rather than an exact critical point. The interaction-effective dark-energy equation of state can cross the phantom divide, although the physical equation of state remains non-phantom. Extending the analysis to the full Bogomol'nyi-Prasad-Sommerfield dyon mass, we find that the main dynamical features persist, while the field evolution and interaction strength depend on the microscopic dyon properties. Our results demonstrate how a microscopically motivated dark-sector interaction can give rise to distinctive cosmological evolution and effective dark-energy behavior.

</details>


### [63] [Bright Siren Cosmology Beyond Fisher: DALI Forecasts for Third-Generation Gravitational Wave Observatories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05338)
*Mayara H. Pacheco,Josiel M. S. de Souza,Odylio D. Aguiar,Márcio E. S. Alves*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作评估了基于似然导数近似（Derivative Approximation for Likelihoods, DALI）的非高斯（non-Gaussian）扩展在明亮警报源（bright siren）宇宙学预报中的表现；相比传统Fisher近似，一阶DALI修正（Doublet）在参数不确定度上更接近精确似然（Exact），且显著降低计算成本。
