  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Fisher矩阵近似依赖高斯似然假设，在明亮警报源宇宙学预报中可能低估或扭曲参数约束；因此需要检验非高斯近似能否在保持计算效率的同时提高预报精度。

Method: 模拟第三代引力波天文台（Cosmic Explorer 与三角形 Einstein Telescope 网络）的明亮警报源目录，采用频域旋近波形（frequency-domain inspiral waveforms）与选择相关的先验；分别用Fisher、Doublet（一阶DALI修正）重建近似似然，并将距离信息传播至 w0waCDM 模型的宇宙学推断。

Result: Doublet近似给出的宇宙学约束比Fisher更紧，不确定度约缩小一倍，与Exact结果一致；在该网络配置下，z=1以内300个明亮警报源探测使H0精度达约2.7%（Doublet），Exact为约2.5%。相对Exact宇宙学似然，Doublet的参数不确定度平均仅差约10%，而Fisher约差195%。非高斯引力波先验还使推断光度距离相对基准值产生系统性偏移，该偏移在Exact重建中同样存在并传播至宇宙学参数。计算成本方面，Doublet使引力波分析约加速55倍、宇宙学似然评估约加速12倍，联合分析总成本估计降低约55倍。

Conclusion: 基于DALI的近似可为明亮警报源宇宙学预报提供兼顾精度与计算效率的工具，明显优于传统Fisher近似，具备进一步应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work we assess non-Gaussian extensions of Fisher forecasting based on the Derivative Approximation for Likelihoods (DALI). We simulate ensembles of bright siren catalogs for third-generation observatories, adopting a Cosmic Explorer (CE) and a triangular Einstein Telescope (ET) network, frequency-domain inspiral waveforms, and selection-motivated priors for counterpart detection. We reconstruct approximate likelihoods using Fisher and the first DALI correction (Doublet), and propagate the inferred distance information to cosmological inference in a w0waCDM model. We find that the Doublet approximation provides tighter cosmological constraints than the Fisher approximation, reducing the inferred uncertainties by approximately a factor of two in agreement with non-approximate likelihood (called Exact) results. For our GW network onfiguration, 300 bright-siren detections up to z=1 yield an H0 precision of ~2.7% with the Doublet approximation, compared with ~2.5% for the exact likelihood. The adopted non-Gaussian GW priors induce a systematic displacement of the inferred luminosity distances from the fiducial values, which is also present in the exact GW reconstruction and propagates into the cosmological parameters. Compared with the exact cosmological likelihood, the Doublet results differ by only ~10% on average in the parameter uncertainties, compared with ~195% for the traditional Fisher approximation. The Doublet approximation also substantially reduces the computational cost, by a factor of ~55 for the GW analysis and by ~12 for cosmological likelihood evaluations, yielding an estimated reduction of ~55 in the total computational cost of the combined analysis. Our results show the potential of DALI-based approximations to provide accurate and computationally efficient tools for forecasting in bright siren cosmology.

</details>

### [64] [Bayesian BiLO: Bilevel Local Operator Learning for Efficient Uncertainty Quantification of Bayesian PDE Inverse Problems with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17019)
*Ray Zirui Zhang,Christopher E. Miles,Xiaohui Xie,John S. Lowengrub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向偏微分方程（PDE）贝叶斯反演的B-BiLO双层局部算子学习框架，以哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）在参数空间采样、以低秩适配（LoRA）下层微调神经网络逼近解算子，实现无需合成数据与伴随方程的高效不确定性量化（UQ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE反问题中的不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）在诸多应用（如新兴成像技术与临床洞察）中至关重要；科学机器学习（scientific machine learning）虽能在保持物理结构的前提下实现数据驱动学习，但需要在后验采样与高维权重空间之间取得可扩展且自适应的权衡。

Method: 构建双层局部算子学习（bilevel local operator learning）框架：上层通过哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）从后验分布采样参数；下层利用低秩适配（low-rank adaptation, LoRA）微调神经网络以局部逼近解算子。权重通过确定性优化获得，从而避免在高维权重空间中采样，亦无需合成数据或伴随方程（adjoint equations）。同时对下层近似优化误差及其对后验精度的影响进行理论分析。

Result: 误差分析刻画了下层近似优化误差（approximate lower-level optimization）对后验精度（posterior accuracy）的影响；在包括肿瘤生长（tumor growth）在内的多个PDE模型数值实验中，B-BiLO 实现了准确且高效的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: B-BiLO 将参数空间采样与确定性权重优化相结合，为PDE贝叶斯反演提供了一种兼具物理结构保持性与计算可扩展性的不确定性量化路径。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification in PDE inverse problems is essential in many applications. Scientific machine learning and AI enable data-driven learning of model components while preserving physical structure, and provide the scalability and adaptability needed for emerging imaging technologies and clinical insights. We develop a Bilevel Local Operator Learning framework for Bayesian inference in PDEs (B-BiLO). At the upper level, we sample parameters from the posterior via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, while at the lower level we fine-tune a neural network via low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to approximate the solution operator locally. B-BiLO enables efficient gradient-based sampling without synthetic data or adjoint equations and avoids sampling in high-dimensional weight space, as in Bayesian neural networks, by optimizing weights deterministically. We analyze errors from approximate lower-level optimization and establish their impact on posterior accuracy. Numerical experiments across PDE models, including tumor growth, demonstrate that B-BiLO achieves accurate and efficient uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [65] [Quantifying out-of-distribution data severity with Wasserstein distances: benchmarking large language models for alloy property prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03767)
*YuJie Liu,Dezhen Xue,Jun Sun,Xiangdong Ding,Zhibin Gao,Turab Lookman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较通用大语言模型（LLM）与领域专用材料模型的性质预测能力，发现通用LLM（如Gemini、DeepSeek）在Ti、钢、高熵合金（HEA）和Al体系上优于SteelBERT、MatSciBERT与SciBERT；并提出以先验模型数值估计作为提示引导LLM推理校正的混合方法，配合高质量小样本数据策略，可低成本地超越先验模型与无引导模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Gemini、DeepSeek等通用大语言模型（general-purpose LLM）推理能力增强，其性质预测能力是否可与领域专用预训练材料模型（domain-specific pretrained materials models）相比尚未明确；同时，领域专用预训练成本高昂，亟需评估更经济的替代或协同方案。

Method: 在Ti、钢（steels）、高熵合金（HEA）与Al四类材料上评测通用LLM与SteelBERT、MatSciBERT、SciBERT的预测表现；提出混合方法（hybrid approach），将先验模型（CatBoost或SteelBERT）的数值先验作为提示（prompts）输入Deepseek V3.1与Gemini-2.5-Flash，使LLM充当推理校正器（reasoning corrector）；并以基于相似度的度量（similarity-based metric）考察数据策略的影响。

Result: 通用LLM的预测表现优于三个领域专用BERT类模型；混合方法在精度上同时超过先验模型与无引导模式（unguided mode）；仅使用训练集中5%的高相似度参考数据即可获得与使用100%训练集相近的表现，表明数据质量优于数量；LLM还表现出良好的不确定性校准（uncertainty calibration），自报“高”置信度与偏离真值最小强相关。

Conclusion: 将通用LLM与先验模型数值基线及高质量数据筛选相协同，可构成一种成本有效且准确的材料发现范式（paradigm），减少对昂贵领域专用预训练的依赖。

Abstract: As general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs), such as Gemini and DeepSeek, exhibit increasingly advanced reasoning capabilities, a critical question arises: how do their property prediction abilities compare to those of domain-specific pretrained materials models? In this study, we evaluate predictions for Ti, steels, high-entropy alloy (HEA) and Al, demonstrating that general-purpose LLMs surprisingly outperform SteelBERT, MatSciBERT, and SciBERT. To further enhance performance, we introduce a hybrid approach that utilizes numerical priors from a prior model, such as CatBoost or SteelBERT, to guide the general LLMs (Deepseek V3.1, Gemini-2.5-Flash). By inputting these initial estimates from a prior model as prompts, the LLM acts as a reasoning corrector, achieving accuracy superior to the prior model and "unguided mode". Furthermore, we investigate the impact of data strategy using a similarity-based metric, revealing that data quality dominates quantity; using merely 5% of high-similarity reference data of training set as input yields performance similar to the results of using 100% of training set. Additionally, the LLMs demonstrate excellent uncertainty calibration, where self-reported "high" confidence strongly correlates with minimal deviation from ground truth. Our findings suggest that synergizing general-purpose LLMs with prior model numerical baselines and high-quality data curation creates a cost-effective and accurate paradigm for materials discovery, reducing the need for expensive domain-specific pretraining.

</details>


### [66] [Surrogate Models for Stochastic Al-Cu Phase-Field Solidification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04695)
*Simon Savukoski,Tatu Pinomaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出保方差去噪扩散桥模型（variance-preserving denoising diffusion bridge model, VP-DDBM），以当前相场、浓度场和温度场为条件自回归预测Al-Cu合金二维快速凝固演化，长期误差优于确定性基线且比相场求解器快约73倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型（phase-field model）能解析快速合金凝固中的微观组织演化，但计算代价高，限制了长轨迹模拟、随机系综（stochastic ensemble）研究及跨工艺条件探索。

Method: 构建保方差去噪扩散桥模型（VP-DDBM），以当前相场、浓度场与给定温度场为条件预测下一时刻状态，并以自回归（autoregressive）方式滚动推演二维Al-Cu凝固过程；与确定性U-Net及SI-ConvNeXt基线对比。

Result: 在两个留出热梯度（held-out thermal gradient）条件下，VP-DDBM在域平均相、域平均Cu浓度与前沿尖端位置（leading-tip position）上的长期预测误差低于确定性基线；长程推演中保留精细枝晶结构，而U-Net逐渐平滑形貌；速度约为相场求解器的73倍。

Conclusion: VP-DDBM可作为相场模拟的快速代理，具备工艺条件筛选与基于系综的微观组织研究潜力，但摘要未给出更广泛的泛化性验证。

Abstract: Phase-field models resolve microstructural evolution during rapid alloy solidification, but their computational cost limits long trajectories, stochastic ensembles, and studies across process conditions. We present a variance-preserving denoising diffusion bridge model (VP-DDBM) for state-conditioned prediction of two-dimensional Al-Cu solidification. Given the current phase, concentration, and prescribed temperature fields, the model predicts the subsequent state and is applied autoregressively. Across two held-out thermal gradients, VP-DDBM produces lower long-horizon errors than deterministic U-Net and SI-ConvNeXt baselines in domain-mean phase, domain-mean Cu concentration, and leading-tip position. It also retains fine dendritic structure over long rollouts, whereas the deterministic U-Net progressively smooths the morphology. VP-DDBM predicts the phase and concentration evolution approximately $73\times$ faster than the phase-field solver, showing potential for faster process-condition screening and ensemble-based microstructure studies.

</details>

### [67] [When do machine-learning potentials need a configuration-dependent kernel? Learning long-range interactions with Fourier neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03678)
*Wei Chen,Gian-Marco Rignanese*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用傅里叶神经算子（Fourier neural operator, FNO）学习局域主干（MACE）描述符所构建潜源场之间的长程耦合，检验“预设/配置无关核函数”是否足够。结果表明：非均匀屏蔽下耦合依赖远端介质的构型，只有非线性FNO能表征，并在界面与掺杂体系上更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）中的长程相互作用通常以预设静电核或学习到但与构型无关的核来耦合潜源，但这类核是否充分取决于实际屏蔽相互作用，本文即针对此问题展开检验。

Method: 以局域主干（此处为MACE）的描述符构建潜源场，用傅里叶神经算子（FNO）学习其在模拟盒内的相互作用；在可调屏蔽的合成离子体系中对比预设库仑核、线性FNO（等价于配置无关核）与非线性FNO。

Result: 强屏蔽下预设库仑核低估长波电荷涨落43%，FNO误差在3%以内并恢复局域主干残差方差的95–99%；线性FNO在均匀情形同样有效。非均匀屏蔽下，仅非线性FNO能响应远端介质调制，力误差比配置无关模型低27–41%；在液态水、LLZO与Au2/MgO(001)基准上表现相当或更优，并复现Al掺杂引起的Au2润湿偏好反转，偏差小于5 meV/cell。

Conclusion: 一旦屏蔽介质发生变化，配置相关的核函数（如FNO所学）即成为必要，尽管在固定介质下能量对电荷仍保持二次形式。

Abstract: Long-range interactions in machine-learning interatomic potentials are commonly treated by coupling learned sources through either a prescribed electrostatic kernel or a learned but configuration-independent one. Whether such a kernel suffices depends on the underlying screened interaction. We address this question by using Fourier neural operators (FNOs) to learn how latent source fields, built from the descriptors of a local backbone (here MACE), interact across the simulation cell. For a synthetic ionic system with tunable screening, a prescribed Coulomb kernel underestimates the long-wavelength charge fluctuations by 43% under strong screening, whereas the FNO reproduces them within 3% and recovers 95-99% of the variance of the residual left by the local backbone. A linear FNO, which learns a configuration-independent kernel, performs equally well in the homogeneous case. Under inhomogeneous screening, rearranging the remote medium modulates the coupling between distant ions, a dependence that no configuration-independent kernel can represent. Among the models tested, only the nonlinear FNO responds to this modulation, and its force errors are 27-41% lower than those of configuration-independent models. In first-principles benchmarks of liquid water, lithium lanthanum zirconate, and Au2/MgO(001), MACE-FNO is comparable to a prescribed-kernel model for the bulk systems and more accurate for the interface. It further captures the reversal of the Au2 wetting preference induced by remote Al dopants, with deviations below 5 meV per cell from density functional theory. These results indicate that a configuration-dependent kernel, as learned by FNOs, becomes necessary once the screening medium varies, even though the energy remains quadratic in the charges for a fixed medium.

</details>


### [68] [Current-Confined Formation of a Buried Ge/Si Free Surface for Defect Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03762)
*Youcef A. Bioud,Meriem Bouchilaoun,Waldemar Schreiber,Redouane Amrar,Gilles Patriarche,Ali Soltani,Stefan Janz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出电流限制电化学（current-confined electrochemistry）方法，将Ge/Si异质界面转化为横向连续的纳米级自由表面，同时保留Ge外延层，实现埋入界面的解耦、缺陷过滤与应变调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格失配异质外延（lattice-mismatched heteroepitaxy）受限于埋入界面处的失配位错（misfit dislocations）、穿透缺陷（threading defects）和残余应变，亟需可后外延处理的控制手段。

Method: 在导电p型Si衬底上使用低掺杂Ge层，使阳极氧化电流被重定向至富失配位错的界面；结合包含电荷转移动力学（charge-transfer kinetics）的数值模拟与电化学标定确定工艺窗口，并用STEM、EDX、几何相位分析、平面TEM、拉曼光谱和扩展电阻 profiling 进行表征。

Result: STEM显示界面间约15 Å的间隔，EDX显示界面处局部氧富集；软退火后应变弛豫路径改变、穿透位错生成被强烈抑制，残余拉伸应变由约0.10%降至0.05%，且Ge载流子分布得以保持。

Conclusion: 电流限制电化学为晶格失配半导体异质结构提供了一条可控的后外延途径，用于埋入界面解耦、缺陷过滤与应变控制。

Abstract: Lattice-mismatched heteroepitaxy is limited by misfit dislocations, threading defects, and residual strain generated at buried interfaces. Here, we show that current-confined electrochemistry transforms the Ge/Si heterointerface into a laterally continuous nanometric free surface while preserving the Ge epilayer. A low-doped Ge layer on a conductive p-type Si substrate redirects anodization current toward the misfit-dislocation-rich interface. Numerical modelling including charge-transfer kinetics shows that this confinement arises from hole-limited charge transfer and, together with electrochemical calibration, defines a process window that combines interfacial current confinement with controlled Si porosification. STEM reveals an approximately 15 Ang separation, while EDX shows localized interfacial oxygen enrichment. After soft annealing, geometric phase analysis and plan-view TEM show a modified strain-relaxation pathway and strongly suppressed threading-dislocation emergence. Raman spectroscopy indicates a reduction in residual tensile strain from approximately 0.10 % to 0.05 %, whereas spreading resistance profiling confirms preservation of the Ge carrier profile. Current-confined electrochemistry therefore provides a controllable post-epitaxial route for buried-interface decoupling, defect filtering, and strain control in lattice-mismatched semiconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [69] [Agentic inverse design of spatially programmed materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03795)
*Denisse Córdova Carrizales,Abhijatmedhi Chotrattanapituk,L. Dagny Davis,Jeremy Ramsey,Ryotaro Okabe,Mouyang Cheng,Mingda Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（large language model, LLM）智能体（agent）以基于物理的离子—物质相互作用模拟器为工具，可从任意目标深度剖面（depth profile）反演出可执行的离子注入（ion implantation）制备方案，实现物理引导的智能体式逆向设计（agentic inverse design）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间编程功能材料（spatially programmed functional materials）依赖空间结构调控物理性质，但工艺条件与材料结构之间关系复杂，将目标空间结构转化为实际制备配方（fabrication recipe）仍然困难。

Method: 提出由LLM智能体进行逆向设计：给定任意注入离子或空位（vacancies）的深度剖面，智能体选择离子种类、能量、入射角与靶材层，自行编写、运行并解读模拟，迭代优化配方直至模拟剖面匹配目标，且所需人工干预极少。

Result: 在最少人工输入下，智能体能够迭代精化配方，使模拟得到的深度剖面与任意给定目标剖面相匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了物理引导的智能体式逆向设计（physics-guided agentic inverse design）是使材料空间结构成为可编程变量（programmable variable）的可行途径，并为探索涌现物理现象与物质量子功能相提供新机遇。

Abstract: Spatially programmed functional materials, from functionally graded alloys and metamaterials to quantum superlattices, use their spatial structure to tailor physical properties. However, translating a desired spatial structure into a practical fabrication recipe remains challenging due to the complex relationship between processing conditions and resulting material structures. Here we show that a large language model (LLM) agent can inverse-design such fabrication recipes by using a physics-based simulator of ion-matter interactions as a tool. Given an arbitrary depth profile of implanted ions or vacancies, the agent selects the ion species, energies, incident angles, and target material layers; writes, runs, and interprets simulations; and iteratively refines the recipe until the simulated profile matches the target, with minimal human input. Our work demonstrates physics-guided agentic inverse design as a practical approach to making the spatial structure of materials a programmable variable, opening new opportunities to explore emergent physical phenomena and functional phases of matter.

</details>


### [70] [Possibility of Potts Spin-Ice in Rare Earth Twisted-Kagome Layers: an Example of DyAgGe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03842)
*Gunnar F. Schwertfeger,Po-Hao Chang,Predrag Nikolic,Igor I. Mazin,Hari Bhandari,Nirmal J. Ghimire*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究指出稀土扭曲Kagome磁性化合物DyAgGe实为一种Potts自旋冰（color ice），每个格点允许四个自旋方向而非两个，并预测该类体系可能存在8态自旋冰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维自旋冰研究已较充分，而二维（2D）类比体系稀少：迄今仅发现Ising自旋冰，二维材料中仅HoAgGe被确认为自旋冰，亟需拓展二维自旋冰的候选材料与态类型。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculation）研究DyAgGe的磁相互作用，并将模型预测与已有实验数据进行比对验证。

Result: 计算表明DyAgGe具有4态Potts结构，即其Potts自旋冰（或color ice）特征得到确认；模型预测与现有实验数据相符。研究还提出该类体系中可能存在8态自旋冰。

Conclusion: DyAgGe是除HoAgGe之外的又一二维Kagome自旋冰材料，且突破了Ising（两态）限制，属于四态Potts自旋冰；这为在该类体系中寻找更高态数（如8态）自旋冰提供了依据。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) spin-ice compounds, predominantly based on the pyrochlore or spinel structure, have been attracting considerable interest in the last two decades. A two-dimensional (2D) analogue is the spin-ice consisting of kagome planes. So far, only Ising spin ice has been discovered in any material, and only one 2D material, the kagome compound HoAgGe, has been identified as a spin ice. In this Letter, we show that another rare-earth twisted Kagome magnetic compound, DyAgGe, is in fact a Potts spin ice (or color ice), where at each site not two but four spin directions are allowed. We argue that an 8-state spin ice may also exist in this class of systems. We present a first-principles calculation of magnetic interactions in DyAgGe, and confirm the 4-state Potts structure of this material by matching our model predictions with the available experimental data.

</details>


### [71] [Early-age formation of MgO-SiO$_2$ binders and the effect of hydromagnesite and dehydrated hydromagnesite: An in situ synchrotron X-ray diffraction and pair distribution function study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03929)
*Jordan Mravca-Bailey,Claire E. White*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用原位同步辐射X射线衍射与对分布函数分析，追踪含与不含水菱镁矿（hydromagnesite）或脱水水菱镁矿的MgO-SiO₂胶凝材料在最初24 h（并辅以42天离线测量）的物相演化，揭示M-S-H在约45 min即已生成，且水合状态调控HCB析出时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MgO-SiO₂胶凝材料是可永久封存CO₂的低CO₂波特兰水泥替代材料，添加镁碳酸盐可将初始水化产物由结晶态氢氧镁石转变为无序的含碳酸盐水合氢氧镁石（HCB），但其最初数小时的演化机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用原位同步辐射X射线衍射（XRD）与对分布函数（PDF）分析，对纯MgO-SiO₂浆体及掺水菱镁矿（HY）或脱水水菱镁矿（DHY，325 °C加热除水）的浆体进行前24 h连续跟踪，并辅以延长至42天的离线（ex situ）测量，通过实空间物相定量分析物相演变。

Result: 实空间定量显示所有浆体在首次扫描（约45 min）即检测到硅酸镁水合物（M-S-H），早于任何氢氧镁石或HCB的形成；纯浆体中42天时M-S-H的一半在24 h内已生成，但因该相本征无序，XRD在任何龄期均未检出。两种碳酸盐源均提高MgO溶解程度（24 h即无未反应MgO，纯浆体则需>7天），但DHY在约6 h前减缓MgO溶解初期，此期间DHY再水化显著而无HCB生成。

Conclusion: MgO-SiO₂-(D)HY浆体中HCB析出的起始受DHY相水合状态影响：当DHY处于再水化过程中时HCB析出被延迟。该结果强调PDF等实空间方法对表征无序相演化的重要性。

Abstract: MgO-SiO$_2$ binders are promising low-CO$_2$ alternatives to Portland cement that can permanently store CO$_2$ by incorporating magnesium carbonate phases. Adding a magnesium carbonate such as nesquehonite or hydromagnesite is known to change the initial hydration product from crystalline brucite (Mg(OH)$_2$) to disordered hydrous carbonate-containing brucite (HCB), yet how these binders evolve during the initial hours of reaction remains largely unknown. Here, in situ synchrotron X-ray diffraction (XRD) and pair distribution function (PDF) analysis follows an MgO-SiO$_2$ paste with and without hydromagnesite (HY, Mg$_5$(CO$_3$)$_4$(OH)$_2$$\cdot$4H$_2$O) or dehydrated hydromagnesite (DHY, HY heated at 325 °C to remove water) over the first 24 h, with ex situ measurements to 42 days. Real space phase quantification reveals that magnesium-silicate-hydrate (M-S-H) is detected in the first scan (~45 min) of every paste, before formation of any brucite or HCB. The PDF results also show that in the neat MgO-SiO$_2$ paste, half of the M-S-H present at 42 days has formed by 24 h, while no M-S-H is detected by XRD at any age due to the intrinsic disorder present in this phase. Both carbonate sources (HY and DHY) are found to increase the extent of MgO dissolution, where no unreacted MgO is detected at 24 h in contrast with >7 days for the neat paste. However, DHY is seen to slow the initial stages of MgO dissolution (prior to ~6 h) compared with the neat paste, a period during which significant DHY rehydration is seen to occur without any HCB formation. Hence, this investigation shows that the onset of HCB precipitation in MgO-SiO$_2$-(D)HY paste is influenced by the hydration state of the DHY phase, where precipitation is delayed while DHY is rehydrating.

</details>


### [72] [Triboelectric nanogenerators across length scales: linking materials, multiscale modelling and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04008)
*MD Tanzib Ehsan Sanglap,Ans Al Rashid,Abdullah Sayam,Injamamul Arief,Andrei G. Shvarts,Lukasz Kaczmarczyk,Muammer Koc,Mokarram Hossain*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述摩擦纳米发电机（triboelectric nanogenerators, TENGs）中材料、界面、接触力学与电路之间的跨尺度耦合关系，评估现有建模方法的能力与局限，并提出分层验证策略以支撑可预测的器件设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TENG性能受材料属性、表面形貌、接触力学、电荷产生与外电路等多因素交织影响，而现有材料与界面描述能否支撑预测性器件设计尚缺乏系统评估，尤其实际载荷下预测受限的问题需要梳理。

Method: 综述性分析：讨论聚合物、无机、复合与天然材料在电荷产生与保持、力学顺应性、耐久性等方面的表现；梳理分子动力学（molecular dynamics）与第一性原理计算、粗糙表面接触力学、器件尺度静电学与等效电路（equivalent circuits）等建模方法，并按所需输入、预测量与验证实验证据评估其能力。应用按宏观、毫米、微米尺度器件尺寸分类讨论材料、架构与集成需求。

Result: 指出预设表面电荷密度（prescribed surface charge density）、理想化几何与名义接触面积假设在真实载荷条件下对预测构成约束；识别演化中的真实接触面积（real contact area）以及接触依赖的电荷产生与衰减为界面行为与电输出之间的关键环节。

Conclusion: 提出针对接触力学、静电学与电路响应的分层验证策略（staged validation strategy），强调在标定条件之外进行独立测试，以指导多尺度模型（multiscale models）发展与可靠TENG系统的构建。

Abstract: Triboelectric nanogenerators (TENGs) convert low-frequency mechanical energy into electricity and enable self-powered sensing. Their performance depends on interactions among material properties, surface morphology, contact mechanics, charge generation and external circuits. This review examines these interactions across length scales to assess how material and interface descriptions support predictive device design. Polymeric, inorganic, composite and natural materials are discussed in relation to charge generation and retention, mechanical compliance, durability and application requirements. Modelling approaches span molecular dynamics and first-principles calculations, rough-surface contact mechanics, device-scale electrostatics and equivalent circuits. Their capabilities are assessed through the inputs they require, the quantities they predict and the experimental evidence used for validation. Particular attention is given to prescribed surface charge density, idealised geometries and nominal contact-area assumptions, which constrain predictions under realistic loading conditions. Applications are organised by macroscale, milliscale and microscale device dimensions to examine requirements for materials, architecture and integration. The review identifies evolving real contact area and contact-dependent charge generation and decay as critical links between interfacial behaviour and electrical output. A staged validation strategy is proposed for contact mechanics, electrostatics and circuit response, with independent testing beyond calibration conditions. These priorities guide the development of multiscale models and reliable TENG systems.

</details>


### [73] [Dynamical Phonon Anharmonicity Drives Charge-Density-Wave Softening and Electronic Quasiparticle Decoherence in Monolayer 1H-NbSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04038)
*Yi Xia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以单层1H-NbSe₂为对象，揭示频率依赖的三声子与四声子相互作用导致的动态非谐软化（dynamical anharmonic softening）如何重塑声子谱函数并影响电子的准粒子谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）不稳定性由强非谐软声子（anharmonic soft phonons）主导，但其动力学行为在静态重整化振动图像之外仍缺乏理解。

Method: 采用频率依赖的三声子与四声子相互作用计算单层1H-NbSe₂的声子谱函数，并将动态重整化声子频率与电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）引入Fan-Migdal电子自能（electron self-energy）计算。

Result: 三声子相互作用引起CDW模式显著动态软化，使估计的相变温度高于静态自洽声子理论（self-consistent phonon theory）结果；四声子相互作用进一步促进软化并增强谱展宽，产生挑战准粒子图像的非洛伦兹线型（non-Lorentzian lineshapes）；动态重整化后电子谱出现增强的kink、卫星特征及动量依赖的退相干。

Conclusion: 动态非谐性（dynamical anharmonicity）是连接软模晶格涨落与电子谱学特征的关键环节，为二维量子材料中晶格-电子耦合动力学提供新认识。

Abstract: Charge-density-wave (CDW) instabilities in two-dimensional materials are governed by strongly anharmonic soft phonons, whose dynamical behavior remains poorly understood beyond the statically renormalized vibrations. Here, using monolayer 1H-NbSe2, we investigate how frequency-dependent three- and four-phonon interactions reshape phonon spectral functions and influence electronic excitations. We find that three-phonon interactions induce substantial dynamical softening of CDW modes, increasing the estimated transition temperature relative to static self-consistent phonon theory. Four-phonon interactions further promote such dynamical softening and enhance spectral broadening, producing non-Lorentzian lineshapes that challenge the quasiparticle picture. Incorporating dynamically renormalized phonon frequencies and electron-phonon coupling into Fan-Migdal electron self-energy calculations reveals enhanced electronic kinks, satellite features, and momentum-dependent quasiparticle decoherence, driven by the strong momentum dependence of the electron-phonon coupling matrix elements. These findings establish dynamical anharmonicity as a critical link between soft-mode lattice fluctuations and electronic spectral signatures, providing insights into coupled lattice-electron dynamics in two-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [74] [Multiplicity of elastic wave propagation in random polycrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04058)
*Ming Huang,Stanislav I. Rokhlin,Michael J. S. Lowe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合理论、晶粒尺度数值模拟与晶粒速度估计，阐明无织构多晶中短波长下每种极化存在两个相干弹性波模的物理起源与振幅分配规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无织构多晶中的相干弹性波是超声与地震表征的基础，近期观测到短波长下每种极化存在两个模式，但其物理起源与振幅分配尚未解决。

Method: 采用一阶平滑近似（first-order smoothing approximation）并结合远场近似（far-field approximation）的理论框架，配合晶粒尺度数值模拟，以及无需拟合的新残差法（residue method）和基于单晶常数的晶粒速度估计。

Result: 理论预测三种波：经典有效波与纵、横散射波各一种。长波长下仅有效波弱衰减；短波长下第二种波以相当衰减加入，第三种衰减远高。残差法给出相同的振幅分配模式，模拟在铁的横波与强各向异性SrMgSn的纵波中恰好呈现两个模式而从未出现第三种。

Conclusion: 机制为入射波在一个晶粒关联长度内被消光、相干场经前向多重散射重建；晶粒速度估计将两模式溯源为单个晶粒的平面波并复现其速度。多波的可观测性由衰减与振幅分配共同决定，随机介质短波长测量须容许不止一个相干模式。

Abstract: The coherent elastic wave in untextured polycrystals, central to ultrasonic and seismic characterization, was recently observed to have two modes per polarization at short wavelengths. However, their physical origin and amplitude partition have remained open. We address both by theory, grain-scale numerical simulations and a grain velocity estimate. The first-order smoothing approximation, simplified by the far-field approximation, predicts three waves, which are the classical effective wave and one for each type of scattered wave (longitudinal and transverse). At long wavelengths, only the effective wave is weakly attenuated. At short wavelengths, one of the other two waves joins it with comparable attenuation, whereas the third stays far more attenuated. A new residue method, which needs no fitting, gives the same pattern for the amplitude partition, with the two waves sharing the coherent field and the third carrying little. The simulations reveal exactly these two modes, for transverse waves in iron and longitudinal waves in strongly anisotropic SrMgSn, and never the third. The wavefields reveal the mechanism, in which the incident wave is extinguished within one grain correlation length and the coherent field is rebuilt by forward multiple scattering. Strikingly, a grain velocity estimate from single-crystal constants provides independent evidence, tracing the two modes to plane waves of the individual grains and closely reproducing their velocities. Observability of multiple waves is thus set by attenuation and amplitude partition together, and short-wavelength measurements in random media must allow for more than one coherent mode.

</details>


### [75] [Minimal model of a Plateau-Rayleigh instability of a solid binary alloy nanowire: impacts of surface segregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05283)
*Mikhail Khenner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文建立了混合固体二元合金纳米线 Plateau-Rayleigh（PR）失稳的最小模型，并用线性稳定性分析（LSA）考察表面偏析与表面成分偏离如何使长波失稳截止波长 λ_c 偏离经典值 2πr_0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 PR 失稳理论（单组分、表面扩散主导）给出 λ_c 与最快增长波长 λ_max 均大于纳米线周长 2πr_0，但二元合金中表面偏析与表面成分变化可能改变这一图像，需定量评估其偏离程度。

Method: 构建最小模型：仅考虑合金组元的表面扩散、无非各向异性表面能，并纳入经典热力学表面偏析；采用线性稳定性分析，将 λ_c 表示为表面成分偏离平衡值、纳米线半径及少数组元体浓度的函数，并对 AuNi、PtNi、CoNi 纳米线进行计算。

Result: 对每种纳米线均存在可观的参数区域使 λ_c<2πr_0，且 λ_max 亦小于周长，与单组分情形形成鲜明对比；CoNi 的表面偏析曲线与 AuNi、PtNi 定性不同；强表面偏析是 λ_c 与 λ_max 异常偏小的主要原因。

Conclusion: 结果表明，二元合金纳米线在退火解体过程中可能生成大量尺寸小、间距紧密的纳米颗粒，为合金纳米线的可控破碎提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Minimal model and its linear stability analysis (LSA) for Plateau-Rayleigh (PR) instability of an intermixed, solid binary alloy nanowire is presented. Model accounts only for the surface diffusions of the alloy components without anisotropies of a surface energies, and for a classical thermodynamic surface segregation. Model allows to assess a deviation of a longwave instability cutoff wavelength $λ_c$ from the classical value $2πr_0$ (a wire circumference) as a function of a surface composition deviation from its equilibrium value, a wire radius, and a bulk concentration of a minority component. Results of LSA are presented for AuNi, PtNi, and CoNi nanowires, where an important distinction is that a surface segregation curve for CoNi is qualitatively different from the one for AuNi and PtNi. For each nanowire there is a sizable region of a parameter space where $λ_c<2πr_0$ and correspondingly, a fastest growing perturbation wavelength $λ_{max}$ is also less than a wire circumference. This is in stark contrast to PR instability of a single-component nanowire transporting mass by surface diffusion - in this case $λ_c$ and $λ_{max}$ are larger than a wire circumference. Strong surface segregation is primarily responsible for unusually small $λ_c$ and $λ_{max}$. Results suggest that a large number of small and closely spaced nanoparticles may emerge upon disintegration via annealing of a binary alloy nanowire.

</details>


### [76] [greenWTE: Frequency-domain solver for the phonon Wigner Transport Equation with arbitrary heating using Green's functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04156)
*Laurenz Kremeyer,Bradley J. Siwick,Samuel Huberman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文介绍了一个求解维格纳输运方程（Wigner Transport Equation, WTE）的 Python 软件包，可在空间与时间的傅里叶空间中处理任意源项，从而在微观尺度上研究固体热输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体热输运通常借助傅里叶定律（Fourier's law）做唯象描述，但在低热导率材料的小时间与空间尺度上需要微观描述；传统声子玻尔兹曼输运方程（phonon Boltzmann Transport Equation, BTE）对高导热材料有效，却难以涵盖低导热材料中的相干（coherences）即声子隧穿（phonon tunneling）过程。

Method: 基于 BTE 的推广形式——WTE，开发 Python 软件包，在空间与时间的傅里叶空间（Fourier space）中求解该方程，并支持任意源项（arbitrary source terms）输入。

Result: 该工具可计算从体材料到纳米尺度（bulk to nanoscale）的静态与动态热导率（static and dynamic thermal conductivities），并可用于研究声子系统对任意热源的响应。

Conclusion: 该软件包为小空间与时间尺度下的热输运研究提供了微观计算手段，扩展了 WTE 在低热导率材料及任意热源场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Heat transport in solids is usually described in a phenomenological way via Fourier's law. However, at small time and length scales in low thermal conductivity materials, a microscopic description is needed. This is traditionally done by invoking the phonon Boltzmann Transport Equation (BTE), which has been successful in describing transport in high thermal conductivity materials. Recently, a generalization of the BTE, the Wigner Transport Equation, was developed to be able to microscopically describe transport in low thermal conductivity materials by taking into account not only phonon scattering processes but also phonon tunneling processes, so-called coherences. We present a Python package that solves the Wigner Transport Equation (WTE) in spatial and temporal Fourier space for arbitrary source terms, thereby enabling the study of heat transport at small space and time scales. This allows one to compute both static and dynamic thermal conductivities from bulk to nanoscale. Beyond that, it can be used to study the response of phonon systems in materials to arbitrary heat sources.

</details>


### [77] [Orbital Hall effect from the Wannier function approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04188)
*Tapesh Gautam,S. Satpathy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文基于Wannier函数构建了轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum, OAM）算符与轨道霍尔电导率（orbital Hall conductivity, OHC）的规范协变（gauge-covariant）形式化框架，并在Haldane模型的有限晶格计算中精确验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch态中位置算符（position operator）存在固有模糊性，导致轨道输运理论缺乏清晰而严格的基础，需要一种能消除该模糊性的形式化方案。

Method: 采用Wannier函数方法构建规范协变的OAM算符与OHC表述；并在Haldane模型的有限晶格计算中，由实空间定义精确求值OAM算符以验证该形式化。

Result: Wannier函数表述解决了Bloch态的位置算符模糊性问题，给出了透明且严格的轨道输运理论，并在Haldane模型计算中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架亦可推广至轨道Edelstein效应（orbital Edelstein effect）、轨道力矩产生（orbital torque generation）等以OAM为核心的其他固体轨道现象。

Abstract: Using the Wannier function approach, we develop a gauge-covariant formalism for the orbital angular momentum (OAM) operator and the orbital Hall conductivity (OHC). The Wannier function formulation resolves the position-operator ambiguity inherent to Bloch states, providing a transparent and rigorous theory of orbital transport. Our formalism is validated through a finite-lattice calculation of the Haldane model, where the OAM operator is evaluated exactly from its real-space definition. Our results are also relevant to other orbital phenomena in solids such as the orbital Edelstein effect, orbital torque generation, and the like, where the OAM plays a crucial role.

</details>


### [78] [Thermally enhanced magnon-phonon resonance in van der Waals antiferromagnet FePS3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04229)
*Kai-Xuan Zhang,Beom Hyun Kim,Seungbok Lee,Hao Li,Eugene Park,Heejun Yang,Feng Jin,Qingming Zhang,Je-Geun Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用高场拉曼光谱（high-field Raman spectroscopy）研究FePS3中磁振子-声子耦合（magnon-phonon coupling）的温度演化，发现耦合强度随升温保持稳健，且零场失谐量（detuning）随升温减小，使体系在奈尔温度附近趋于共振，为解释太赫兹（terahertz）驱动的长寿命亚稳态提供了关键依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体激发（collective excitations）间的强耦合是调控量子物态（quantum states of matter）的途径，但其在热涨落（thermal fluctuations）下的演化尚不清楚。FePS3中太赫兹实验观测到反铁磁转变处出现寿命约5毫秒的长寿命亚稳态，挑战了“转变温度附近磁涨落与热涨落不利于亚稳态相干调控”的传统认识，因而需要独立验证。

Method: 采用高场拉曼光谱（high-field Raman spectroscopy），系统测量FePS3中磁振子-声子耦合随温度的演化，考察磁振子与声子模式间的回避交叉（avoided crossing）及零场失谐（zero-field detuning）的变化。

Result: 在较宽温度范围内观察到磁振子与声子模式间显著的回避交叉，且耦合强度随温度升高保持稳健；零场磁振子-声子失谐随升温逐步减小，驱动体系趋向共振，从而在转变温度附近增强二者的杂化（hybridization）。

Conclusion: 该反常的热演化行为为理解FePS3中太赫兹驱动的异常物态提供了关键解释；范德华磁体（van der Waals magnets）可作为集体激发强耦合及通过光-物质相互作用（light-matter interactions）调控磁态的有前景平台。

Abstract: Strong coupling between collective excitations provides a route to controlling quantum states of matter, yet its evolution under thermal fluctuations remains poorly understood. In the van der Waals antiferromagnet FePS3, strong magnon-phonon coupling is established at low temperatures. At the same time, recent terahertz experiments revealed the counterintuitive stabilization of a long-lived metastable magnetic state with an exceedingly long lifetime of 5 msec at the antiferromagnetic transition, not at the base temperature. This was attributed to a strongly coupled spin-lattice scenario, challenging the conventional wisdom that increased magnetic and thermal fluctuations near TN should be detrimental to the coherent control of a metastable phase. Thus it requires an independent examination for correct understanding. Using high-field Raman spectroscopy, we systematically investigate the temperature evolution of magnon-phonon coupling in FePS3. We observe a pronounced avoided crossing between magnon and phonon modes across a wide range of temperatures and find that the coupling strength remains remarkably robust with increasing temperatures. The zero-field magnon-phonon detuning decreases progressively with warming, driving the system toward resonance and thereby enhancing their hybridization near the transition temperature. This anomalous thermal evolution provides the crucial key to understanding the unusual terahertz-driven states recently reported in FePS3. More broadly, our results highlight van der Waals magnets as a promising platform for strong coupling among collective excitations and for controlling magnetic states through light-matter interactions.

</details>


### [79] [Emergence of Berry curvature dipole and nonlinear Hall effect in a novel hexagonal borophosphene allotrope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04335)
*Ranjan Kumar Tung,Dimple,Venkatesan Subramanian,Vijay Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理与紧束缚分析，研究As取代硼磷烯衍生单层As@MBP与As@AMBP的电子结构、局域轨道耦合与应变可调非线性霍尔响应，二者在无自旋轨道耦合下即具本征Berry曲率偶极与显著非线性霍尔电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索不依赖强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的非线性霍尔响应机制，并考察轻元素（light-element）体系作为机械可切换非线性输运平台的可能性。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与紧束缚分析（tight-binding analysis），计算As@MBP与As@AMBP的能带、Berry曲率偶极（Berry-curvature dipole, BCD）及应变下的非线性霍尔电导。

Result: 无自旋轨道耦合时两体系均呈现有限BCD与可观非线性霍尔电导；As取代打开120 meV（As@MBP）与90 meV（As@AMBP）带隙，远高于室温热涨落（约25.9 meV）；紧束缚分析显示As引入破坏B-P跃迁等价性并在As@AMBP中引起关键最近邻跃迁符号反转。应变下BCD重分布，As@MBP最大BCD达22 Å，约为未应变结构的10倍，并出现BCD符号反转。

Conclusion: As取代硼磷烯衍生物可作为轻元素平台，在无需强自旋轨道耦合的条件下实现机械可切换的非线性输运（nonlinear transport）与量子材料应用。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic structure, local orbital coupling, and strain-tunable nonlinear Hall response of two arsenic-substituted borophosphene-derived monolayers, As@MBP and As@AMBP, using first-principles calculations and tight-binding analysis. In the absence of spin--orbit coupling, both systems exhibit a finite Berry-curvature dipole (BCD) and a substantial nonlinear Hall conductivity, demonstrating that the nonlinear response is intrinsically driven by orbital physics and inversion-symmetry breaking. $As$ substitution opens sizable band gaps of $120~\mathrm{meV}$ in As@MBP and $90~\mathrm{meV}$ in As@AMBP, well exceeding room-temperature thermal fluctuations ($k_{\mathrm{B}}T \approx 25.9~\mathrm{meV}$). Tight-binding analysis reveals that $As$ incorporation breaks the equivalence of $B-P$ hoppings in As@MBP and induces sign reversals in key nearest-neighbor hoppings in As@AMBP, reflecting a pronounced electronic reconstruction beyond simple geometric effects. Under mechanical strain, the redistribution of Berry curvature yields a maximum BCD of $22~\mathring{\text{A}}$ in As@MBP, approximately $10$ times larger than that of unstrained structure, and induces a reversal of the BCD sign. These results establish $As$-substituted borophosphene derivatives as promising light-element platforms for mechanically switchable nonlinear transport and quantum-material applications without relying on strong spin-orbit coupling.

</details>


### [80] [TT-SCHA for Thermodynamics of Binary Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04402)
*Boian S. Alexandrov,Dimiter Petsev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出 TT-SCHA 方法，用张量列（tensor-train, TT）积分结合自洽谐近似（self-consistent harmonic approximation, SCHA）并在温度上作三阶修正，无需随机采样即可计算有序合金晶体自由能，精度与大规模分子动力学相当而计算成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体自由能是对全部原子位置在 3N 维空间上的积分，传统做法依赖随机采样（如分子动力学、蒙特卡罗），既存在采样误差又计算代价高，因此需要一种无采样误差且高效的自由能评估途径。

Method: 针对 B2 NiAl 与 γ-TiAl 两种有序合金，采用嵌入原子势（embedded-atom potentials），构建由单原子及其近邻组成的张量列积分（tensor-train integrals），在其上计算热平均，实现自洽谐近似（SCHA）并作温度三阶修正，即 TT-SCHA。

Result: 结果对应无采样误差的无限晶体，数值误差由插值初始枢轴（starting pivots）决定（压力误差至多 9 MPa，三次运行平均后降低）。与 65,536 原子分子动力学（600 K 下 Frenkel-Ladd 自由能并积分能量）相比，600–1000 K 区间每原子自由能偏差 0.13 meV，单次运行核心机时（core-seconds）少 420–500 倍。

Conclusion: 零压下结果与采用相同嵌入原子势的已发表计算一致：γ-TiAl 弹性常数吻合至 1 GPa（静态点阵计算），热膨胀与蒙特卡罗采样结果相差 0.012 个百分点，表明 TT-SCHA 在精度与效率上均可替代采样型方法。

Abstract: The free energy of a crystal is an integral over the positions of all its atoms, in 3N dimensions. We evaluate it without random sampling for the ordered alloys B2 NiAl and gamma-TiAl with embedded-atom potentials, using a self-consistent harmonic approximation (SCHA) corrected to third order in temperature, calculated via thermal averages of tensor-train (TT) integrals built from one atom and its neighbors, which we call TT-SCHA. The result is for an infinite crystal without sampling error; its numerical error is set by the starting pivots of the interpolation (up to 9 MPa in the pressure, reduced by averaging three runs; SM Table S26). Against the molecular dynamics of 65,536 atoms (Frenkel-Ladd free energy at 600 K, integrated energy above), TT-SCHA free energy agrees to 0.13 meV per atom at 600-1000 K, using 420-500 times fewer core-seconds for one run. At zero pressure, results agree with published calculations that use the same embedded-atom potential: the published elastic constants of gamma-TiAl (to 1 GPa, by a static lattice calculation) and its thermal expansion from Monte Carlo sampling (to 0.012 percentage points).

</details>


### [81] [Annealing-Induced Structural and Optical Evolution of Ion-Beam-Sputtered Ta2O5 Films for High-Power Laser Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04429)
*Chang Chen,Yuan Fang,Yanzhi Wang,Jijun Feng,Yesheng Lu,Yu Chen,Ge Zhang,Jianda Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 系统研究离子束溅射（IBS）沉积的Ta2O5单层膜在400–800 °C退火过程中的结构与光学演变，确定400 °C为兼顾低粗糙度、低弱吸收并保持非晶相的最佳退火窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ta2O5薄膜是高功率激光系统的关键元件，沉积后退火常用于优化膜质，但其结构与光学性能随退火温度的演变机制及最佳退火条件尚需系统厘清。

Method: 对IBS沉积的Ta2O5单层膜在400–800 °C范围内进行退火，表征其结构（非晶-多晶转变、表面粗糙度、氩气泡尺寸）与光学性能（透射光谱红移、弱吸收）。

Result: 600–700 °C间发生非晶向多晶转变并伴随粗糙度显著上升；弱吸收从沉积态约6 ppm降至400–600 °C的约2 ppm，700 °C因晶界缺陷回升，800 °C晶界弛豫后再降；氩气泡尺寸与粗糙度的相关性在800 °C解耦，揭示由基体弛豫主导转向表面扩散主导。

Conclusion: 确定400 °C为IBS沉积Ta2O5单层膜的最佳退火窗口，此时表面粗糙度最低，弱吸收有效降低且非晶相得以保持。

Abstract: Tantalum pentoxide (Ta2O5) thin films deposited by ion beam sputtering (IBS) are essential components in high-power laser systems, where post-deposition annealing is routinely used to optimize film quality. In this work, we systematically investigate the structural and optical evolution of IBS-deposited Ta2O5 single layers subjected to post-deposition annealing at temperatures from 400 to 800 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$. The films undergo an amorphous-to-polycrystalline transition between 600 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ and 700 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$, accompanied by a marked increase in surface roughness. Argon bubbles inherently incorporated during IBS serve as structural markers: their size correlates positively with the surface roughness from the as-deposited to 700 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ but decouples at 800 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$, revealing a transition from matrix-relaxation-dominated to surface-diffusion-dominated behavior. Transmittance spectra show a red shift at 400 and 600 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ which can be explained by physical thickness increase. Weak absorption decreases from ~6 ppm in as-deposited films to ~2 ppm at 400-600 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ due to annihilation of oxygen vacancies, rises markedly at 700 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ due to grain-boundary defects and then drops again at 800 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ as grain boundaries relax. We identify 400 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ as the optimal annealing window for IBS-deposited Ta2O5 single layers, with the lowest surface roughness and effectively reducing weak absorption while preserving the amorphous phase.

</details>


### [82] [Strain-Glass Dynamics in the Superionic Conductor Na$_3$PS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04547)
*Erik S. Lamb,Nicolò D'Anna,Phillip Ridley,Nelson Hua,Ivan Zaluzhnyy,James Burrow,Emilio Escauriza,Robin Glefke,Boyan K. Stoychev,Mingze Wan,Zhe Ren,Fabian Westermeier,Michael Sprung,Alex Frañó,Ying Shirley Meng,Oleg G. Shpyrko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用布拉格X射线光子相关光谱（Bragg X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy）测量单个球磨Na₃PS₄颗粒的纳米尺度结构动力学，发现立方相表现为“应变玻璃（strain glass）”，其畴壁运动由应力驱动并在退火后不可逆地转变为热激活动力学，重排遵循无标度雪崩统计。四方向相则无这些特征且弛豫慢五十倍。这些动力学的时间尺度与估测的颗粒内离子扩散相当，表明宿主结构在输运相关时间尺度上不能被视作静态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上固态电解质（solid-state electrolytes）中的离子传导被描述为可移动阳离子在静态宿主晶格中迁移，但最快的导体往往最为无序，而无序如何在介观尺度（mesoscale）增强输运尚未明确，且超出系综平均（ensemble-averaged）探针的观测能力。

Method: 采用布拉格X射线光子相关光谱，对单个球磨（ball-milled）Na₃PS₄颗粒的纳米尺度结构动力学进行测量，比较立方相与四方向（tetragonal）多晶型的行为。

Result: 立方相呈应变玻璃行为，即三种简并四方向变体（degenerate tetragonal variants）具短程有序（short-range order）的受挫构型；其畴壁运动由应力驱动，随残余球磨应力耗散在退火后不可逆地转为热激活（thermally activated）动力学，重排遵循无标度雪崩（scale-free avalanche）统计，类似铁弹马氏体（ferroelastic martensites）但无外场驱动。四方向相不具备这些特征，弛豫慢约五十倍。

Conclusion: 结构动力学时间尺度与颗粒内离子扩散的估计值相当，说明在输运相关时间尺度上宿主结构不能假设为静态，提示需重新审视介观无序动力学对离子传导的作用。

Abstract: Ionic conduction in solid-state electrolytes is conventionally described by mobile cations migrating through a static host lattice. Yet the fastest conductors are often the most disordered, and how disorder enhances transport remains unresolved at the mesoscale, inaccessible to ensemble-averaged probes. We use Bragg X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy to measure nanoscale structural dynamics of a single ball-milled Na$_3$PS$_4$ particle. We find that the cubic phase behaves as a strain glass: a frustrated configuration of three degenerate tetragonal variants with short-range order. Its domain-wall motion is stress-driven, crossing over irreversibly to thermally activated dynamics on annealing as residual milling stress is depleted. Rearrangements follow scale-free avalanche statistics, matching ferroelastic martensites but without external driving. The tetragonal polymorph shows none of these features and relaxes fifty times more slowly. These dynamics occur on timescales comparable to estimated ionic diffusion across the particle, indicating the host structure cannot be assumed static on transport-relevant timescales.

</details>


### [83] [A general sharp-interface simulation framework for microstructure evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04582)
*Albert Linda,Dierk Raabe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出高斯投影动力学（Gaussian-projected kinetics, GPK），一种锐界面（sharp-interface）框架：每个网格单元存储整数晶粒/相标签，仅在边界附近临时做高斯投影以获取界面法向与曲率后即丢弃。该方法统一驱动毛细、化学、储能、传输与弹性效应，在二维与三维中模拟晶粒长大、再结晶、多相演化与弹性耦合凝固，精度匹配相场法且快 8.5–96 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟微观组织演化（microstructure evolution）需跟踪大量运动的晶界与相界，并与热力学场和力学场耦合，现有方法（如扩散界面/相场）在网格加密时毛细尺度一致性等问题上存在困难。

Method: 采用锐界面框架，单元存储整数晶粒或相标签；在边界附近对标签图做临时高斯投影（Gaussian projection）以获得界面法向与曲率后丢弃；毛细、化学、储能、传输与弹性在一次保拓扑（topology-preserving）更新中共同推动界面。因高斯测量长度以物理单位固定，网格细化可保持一致的毛细尺度而无需扩散界面。

Result: GPK 可模拟二维与三维的晶粒长大（grain growth）、再结晶（recrystallisation）、多相演化与弹性耦合凝固；在解析基准（analytical benchmarks）上得到验证，并与相场模拟逐晶粒对比，在精度匹配下速度快 8.5 至 96 倍。结合 CALPHAD 热力学数据的 Al-Cu 应用与实测再结晶和析出数据相符。

Conclusion: GPK 以锐界面加临时高斯投影的方式统一处理多物理场驱动的界面迁移，兼顾网格细化下的毛细尺度一致性与计算效率，并具备与实测数据对接的实用能力。

Abstract: Simulating microstructure evolution requires tracking many moving grain and phase boundaries coupled to thermodynamic and mechanical fields. We present Gaussian-projected kinetics (GPK), a sharp-interface framework in which every cell stores an integer grain or phase label. Near a boundary, a temporary Gaussian projection of the label map provides the interface normal and curvature and is then discarded. Capillarity, chemistry, stored energy, transport and elasticity move the boundaries through one topology-preserving update. Because the Gaussian measurement length is fixed in physical units, mesh refinement keeps a consistent capillary scale without a diffuse interface. GPK models grain growth, recrystallisation, multiphase evolution and elastic-coupled solidification in two and three dimensions. It is verified on analytical benchmarks and compared grain by grain with phase-field simulations, running 8.5 to 96 times faster at matched accuracy in our tests. An Al-Cu application with CALPHAD thermodynamic data connects it to measured recrystallisation and precipitation data.

</details>


### [84] [Interfacial Hole Accumulation Enables Selective Free-Surface Formation in Ge-on-Si](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04589)
*Youcef A Bioud,Meriem Bouchilaoun,Waldemar Schreiber,Redouane Amrar,Gilles Patriarche,Ali Soltani,Stefan Janz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过有限体积电流分布建模与一维泊松分析，解释了电化学形成二维自由表面（2DFS）在Ge/Si界面处的选择性来源：空穴受限的电荷转移与价带偏移共同将阳极电流高度局域于界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电化学形成的二维自由表面可使Ge外延层与Ge/Si界面的失配位错网络（misfit-dislocation network）解耦，但其选择性起源此前尚不明确，本文旨在阐明该机理。

Method: 结合含电荷转移动力学（charge-transfer kinetics）的有限体积电流分布建模，以及对p-Ge/p+-Si异质结的一维泊松分析（one-dimensional Poisson analysis）；对比欧姆描述与空穴受限电荷转移两种情形，并考察电解质电导率的影响。

Result: 在真实电导率下，欧姆描述会使77%的阳极电流流经Ge，而空穴受限的电荷转移将该比例降至0.07%，且与电解质电导率无关；价带偏移（valence-band offset）在Ge侧自限制的纳米层内累积空穴至约10^19 cm^-3，使溶解局域于界面。

Conclusion: 模型给出了掺杂对比度（NSi/NGe ≈ 60）与台面宽度（约3.6λ，λ≈1.7 μm）的设计规则，为2DFS的选择性形成提供定量指导。

Abstract: Electrochemically formed two-dimensional free surfaces (2DFS) decouple Ge epilayers from the misfit-dislocation network at the Ge/Si interface, but the origin of their selectivity has remained unclear. We combine finite-volume current-distribution modelling, including charge-transfer kinetics, with a one-dimensional Poisson analysis of the p-Ge/p+-Si heterojunction. With realistic conductivities, an ohmic description would drive 77% of the anodic current through Ge, whereas hole-limited charge transfer reduces this fraction to 0.07%, independently of the electrolyte conductivity. The valence-band offset accumulates holes on the Ge side up to ~1019 cm--3 within a self-limited nanometric layer, which localizes dissolution at the interface. The model yields design rules for the doping contrast (NSi/NGe ___ 60) and the mesa width (___ 3.6$λ$, with $λ$ $\approx$ 1.7 $μ$m).

</details>


### [85] [Spin-Density-Driven Hybrid Spin Splitting in Collinear Compensated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04617)
*Peibo Xu,Yixuan Che,Yuxuan Li,Haifeng Lv,Xiaojun Wu,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一类“混合补偿磁体（hybrid compensated magnets）”，在同一种共线补偿磁态中同时实现交替型（alternating）与塞曼型（Zeeman-type）自旋劈裂，并以不等宽Janus石墨烯纳米带交叉双层结构给出第一性原理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共线补偿磁体（collinear compensated magnets）中，非相对论自旋劈裂（nonrelativistic spin splitting）受自旋群对称性（spin-group symmetry）约束，交替型与塞曼型劈裂通常分别对应交变磁体（altermagnets）与完全补偿亚铁磁体（fully compensated ferrimagnets）；作者希望打破这种“二分”对应，探索两类劈裂共存的可能性。

Method: 从自旋密度（spin-density）视角出发构造最小双扇区模型（minimal two-sector model），分析方向性亚晶格色散（directional sublattice dispersion）与源自同一结构不等价性的扇区依赖能量偏置（sector-dependent energy biases）之间的竞争；并用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）在不等宽Janus石墨烯纳米带（Janus graphene nanoribbons）的交叉双层中做概念验证。

Result: 最小模型表明两类劈裂的共存源于上述竞争机制；第一性原理计算证实，结构不等价的纳米带仍保持近似对称相关的自旋密度分布，其中价带扇区支持方向选择性自旋输运（direction-selective spin transport），导带扇区支持能量选择性自旋极化态（energy-selective spin-polarized states）。

Conclusion: 研究将自旋密度的空间组织与对结构不等价性的电子响应联系起来，为补偿磁体中的混合自旋劈裂（hybrid spin splitting）提供了一条设计原则。

Abstract: Nonrelativistic spin splitting in collinear compensated magnets is constrained by spin-group symmetry, with distinct alternating and Zeeman-type splitting typically associated with altermagnets and fully compensated ferrimagnets, respectively. From a spin-density perspective, we propose a class of hybrid compensated magnets in which alternating-like and Zeeman-type spin splitting coexist within a single collinear compensated state. Using a minimal two-sector model, we demonstrate that this coexistence arises from competition between directional sublattice dispersion and the sector-dependent energy biases arising from the same structural inequivalence. First-principles calculations provide a proof of concept in a crossed bilayer of unequal-width Janus graphene nanoribbons, where the structurally inequivalent ribbons retain approximately symmetry-related spin-density distributions. The valence and conduction sectors support direction-selective spin transport and energy-selective spin-polarized states, respectively. These results connect the spatial organization of spin density with the electronic response to structural inequivalence, providing a design principle for hybrid spin splitting in compensated magnets.

</details>


### [86] [Hydrogen-induced modification of the local charge density in MnSb$_2$Te$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04649)
*Kyriaki Samioti,Weiyan Qi,Stefano Ponzoni,Antonio Salame,Entela Buzi,Afrin Nahar Tamanna,Ayesha Lakra,Steven Francis Udaundo,Ding Pei,Pavel Dudin,Marta Zonno,François Bertran,Amina Taleb-Ibrahimi,Lia Krusin-Elbaum,Luca Perfetti,Evangelos Papalazarou,Marino Marsi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、时间分辨ARPES及共振与芯能级光电子能谱，揭示氢离子（H⁺）在范德华磁性材料MnSb₂Te₄中优先与晶格本征缺陷相互作用，充当选择性掺杂剂（selective dopant），从而定点重排电荷密度并重塑价带谱权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究离子氢（H⁺）引入对范德华磁性材料MnSb₂Te₄占据态与未占据态电子结构的影响，并评估氢作为调控层状二维材料电子与磁性质工具的可行性。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与时间分辨ARPES，结合共振光电子能谱和芯能级光电子能谱，对氢化前后MnSb₂Te₄的占据态与未占据态进行谱学表征。

Result: 氢在特定晶格位点附近成键，局域重排电荷密度并重塑价带谱权重；带隙与皮秒级载流子动力学（carrier dynamics）在氢化后变化较小，而导带（conduction band）表现出显著的化学计量比依赖响应。

Conclusion: 氢与晶格本征缺陷强相互作用，在MnSb₂Te₄中表现为选择性掺杂剂；该工作为利用氢调控层状二维材料的电子与磁性质提供了谱学依据。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of ionic hydrogen (H$^+$) intake on the occupied and unoccupied states of MnSb$_2$Te$_4$, a van der Waals magnetic material. Using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and time-resolved ARPES, combined with resonant and core level photoemission, we reveal that hydrogen binds near specific lattice sites, locally redistributing charge density and reshaping the valence band spectral weight. While the band gap and picosecond carrier dynamics show only minor changes upon hydrogenation, the conduction band exhibits a striking stoichiometry-dependent response. These findings indicate that hydrogen interacts strongly with the intrinsic defects in the lattice, acting as a selective dopant in MnSb$_2$Te$_4$. Our work provides a spectroscopic basis for using hydrogen as a tool for the control of the electronic and magnetic properties in layered 2D materials.

Abstract: Intrinsic electron localization in amorphous silica (a-SiO2) contributes to charge trapping and defect generation in silica-based electronic materials - a phenomenon that degrades logic-device performance but can be exploited for memory applications. However, the structural distribution of one- and two-electron traps remains incompletely characterized, largely because the disordered covalent network of a-SiO2 spans a vast configurational space. To address this, we perform statistical sampling to select a representative 200-model subset of defect-free bulk a-SiO2 structures from a structurally converged parent set of 2512 models, generated via classical melt-quench molecular dynamics simulations. We then characterize the intrinsic one- and two-electron traps in this subset using HSE06 density functional theory calculations. Injection of a second electron drives stronger lattice relaxation, most commonly Si-O bond elongation leading to rupture, thereby forming threefold-coordinated-Si (Si3) bipolarons and, in some cases, Si-Si-bonded bipolaron configurations. The calculated bipolaron energy ranges are consistent with optical absorption features at 3.7, 4.7, and 6.4 eV associated with intrinsic electron localization.

*I. A. Kokurin,N. S. Averkiev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出(Ga,Mn)As中磁有序的理论：基于两竞争性对间接交换机制，导出含Mn含量、补偿度、配位与交换参数的分子场型方程，并预测顺磁、铁磁与类自旋玻璃三种磁相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III-V族稀磁半导体（diluted magnetic semiconductors）如Ga_{1-x}Mn_xAs中铁磁性的真实机制仍有争议，实验结果指向在杂质带（impurity band）填充接近一半时出现明显铁磁性，且该填充可用补偿度（compensation degree）表述。

Method: 基于近期关于存在两种竞争性对间接交换（pair indirect exchange，铁磁与反铁磁排列Mn d壳层）的结果，构建(Ga,Mn)As磁有序理论；推导分子场型（molecular-field-like）方程，其有效交换常数整合材料信息，包括Mn含量、补偿度、晶体配位（crystal coordination）及两种竞争对交换相互作用参数。

Result: 居里温度（Curie temperature）强烈依赖补偿度；依Mn含量与补偿度预测三种磁相：顺磁（paramagnetic）、铁磁（ferromagnetic）与类自旋玻璃（spin-glass-like）。由于As反位（As antisites）引入不可避免的额外补偿，(Ga,Mn)As中类自旋玻璃相表现上并不实现；铁磁相变可由Mn含量变化或补偿度改变引发。

Conclusion: 提出并讨论光铁磁效应（photoferromagnetic effect）新机制以澄清磁有序机制；提出提高居里温度的主要障碍是最近邻Mn间反铁磁超交换（antiferromagnetic superexchange）贡献增强，而非此前认为的Mn间隙（Mn interstitials）的补偿（电或磁）效应；并将预测与现有实验数据比较。

Abstract: The real mechanism of ferromagnetism in III-V diluted magnetic semiconductors such as Ga$_{1-x}$Mn$_x$As is still debated. The experimental results point out the realization of appreciable ferromagnetism at filling of the impurity band close to one-half that can be expressed in terms of compensation degree. Based on our recent results on the existence of two competing mechanisms of pair indirect exchange (with ferro- and antiferro alignment of Mn $d$ shells), a theory of magnetic ordering in (Ga,Mn)As is presented. The molecular-field-like equation is derived in which the effective exchange constant contains all necessary information about material, including the Mn content, the compensation degree, the crystal coordination, and the parameters of two competing pair exchange interactions. The Curie temperature depends crucially on the compensation degree. Depending on Mn content and compensation degree, three possible magnetic phases (paramagnetic, ferromagnetic, and spin-glass-like) are predicted. However, the spin-glass-like phase in (Ga,Mn)As is apparently not realized due to the additional inevitable compensation caused by As antisites. The phase transition into the ferromagnetic state occurs not only with Mn content variation but through the change of the compensation degree as well. The new mechanism of photoferromagnetic effect is predicted and discussed in the context of possible clarification of the magnetic ordering mechanism. It is assumed that the main obstacle to a possible increase in the Curie temperature is the increasing contribution of the antiferromagnetic superexchange between the nearest-neighbor Mn's, rather than compensatory (electric or magnetic) effects from Mn interstitials, as was previously stated. The comparison of our predictions with available experimental data is presented.

</details>


### [89] [Controlling nitrogen incorporation in (Eu,Ba,Ca)TiO$_3$-derived perovskite-type oxynitrides through Fermi Level Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04745)
*Margarida Barroso,Marie Neumann,Rachele Sciotto,Shama Perween,Magdalena O. Cichocka,Jefferson Bettini,Adrián López Vergara,Patrick Zeller,Gaurav Anand,Verena Streibel,Barbara A. J. Lechner,Melanie Appelmann,Oliver Clemens,Ute Kolb,Jan P. Hofmann,Karsten Albe,Andreas Klein,Marc Widenmeyer,Anke Weidenkaff*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合理论建模与实验表征研究钛酸钡氧氮化物（oxynitride），以费米能级工程（Fermi Level Engineering）概念解释并调控氮化过程中的自限制行为，并借助A位阳离子取代与Eu$^{2+/3+}$氧化还原对将氮掺入量显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷态不仅影响功能性质，还影响氨解（ammonolysis）过程中的氧氮化物形成，并与结构稳定性和电荷平衡共同导致氮掺入不均匀，因此需要一种理性化氧氮化物合成的方法。

Method: 采用理论建模与实验表征相结合的方式，以电子化学势（即费米能级）为唯一描述符，预测并关联缺陷形成焓与电荷转变能级（charge transition levels）；并通过A位阳离子取代（Eu$_{1-x}$Ba$_x$TiO$_3$与Eu$_{1-x}$Ca$_x$TiO$_3$体系）进行实验调控。

Result: 逐渐的氮掺入使费米能级下移，导致氧位进一步氮取代在能量上不利，掺入上限约为每化学式单元0.03个N；引入Eu$^{2+/3+}$氧化还原对可将费米能级钉扎在比价带顶（VBM）高约2.9 eV的铕电荷转变能级处，实现高达每化学式单元0.65个N的掺入量（由热气体提取法测得）。

Conclusion: 进一步提升氮掺入受Ti(O,N)$_6$八面体内键角方差引起的畸变所限制；结果表明费米能级工程为预测并克服氧氮化物合成中的本征合成与热力学限制提供了路径。

Abstract: Defect states directly influence not only functional properties, but also oxynitride formation during ammonolysis. Along with structural stability and charge balance, these defects can cause non-uniform nitrogen incorporation. To address this, the recently proposed Fermi Level Engineering concept provides a powerful alternative of rationalising oxynitride synthesis. By using the electron chemical potential, i.e., the Fermi level, as a unique descriptor, this approach can predict, understand, and manipulate defect formation enthalpies and charge transition levels, subsequently allowing to incorporate defect-property correlations to the concept. This work combines theoretical modelling and experimental characterisation of barium titanium oxynitrides to explain an intrinsic, self-limiting nitridation process. Progressive nitrogen incorporation causes a Fermi level downward shift, leaving further nitrogen substitution on oxygen sites energetically unfavourable, up to 0.03 N per unit formula. We demonstrate that A-site cation substitution in Eu$_{1-x}$Ba$_x$TiO$_3$- and Eu$_{1-x}$Ca$_x$TiO$_3$-derived oxynitrides can be applied to control compensation of the additional negative charge from N$^{3-}$ and keep the Fermi level high. The Eu$^{2+/3+}$ redox couple enables substitution of oxygen by nitrogen through pinning of the Fermi level at the europium charge transition level, at $\sim$ 2.9 eV above the VBM. This allows for higher nitrogen uptake of up to 0.65 per formula unit (taken from hot gas extraction) through a more controlled process. Further incorporation is then limited by an intra-Ti(O,N)$_6$ octahedra distortion due to internal bond-angle variance. These findings demonstrate that Fermi Level Engineering provides a pathway to predict and overcome intrinsic synthetic and thermodynamic limitations in oxynitride synthesis.

</details>


### [90] [Effect of molybdenum content on phase transformations during continuous heating of metastable β Ti-Mo alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04858)
*Veronika Kočiščáková,Anna Veverková,Josef Stráský,Jana Šmilauerová,Kristína Bartha,Petr Harcuba,Dalibor Preisler,Jozef Veselý,Miloš Janeček*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了三种不同钼含量的亚稳β钛合金（Ti-12Mo、Ti-15Mo、Ti-18Mo，wt.%），通过连续加热条件下的原位实验揭示β相稳定性对相变序列的影响，并结合多种互补手段一致描述了加热过程中的相演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定β相稳定性（β-phase stability）对亚稳β钛合金（metastable β titanium alloys）在连续加热过程中相变（phase transformations）的影响，钼含量被用作调控β相稳定性的变量。

Method: 在5 °C/min线性加热至750 °C过程中开展电阻测量（electrical resistance measurement）、膨胀测量（dilatometry）与差示扫描量热法（differential scanning calorimetry, DSC）；并以加热至特定温度后水淬样品的电子显微观察（electron microscopy）作为原位实验的补充。

Result: 所有合金均呈现相同的相变序列：非热ω相（athermal ω phase）的部分溶解、等温ω相（isothermal ω phase）的稳定与长大、随后ω相溶解及α相（α phase）析出。钼含量升高逐步抑制ω相演化及后续α相析出，并使与ω相关转变对应的温度区间变窄。

Conclusion: 电阻测量、DSC、膨胀测量与电子显微观察之间高度吻合，可一致地描述加热过程中的相演化；结果说明组合互补实验技术（complementary experimental techniques）对于研究亚稳β钛合金中复杂相变路径具有优势。

Abstract: Three metastable $β$ titanium alloys with different molybdenum contents, Ti-12Mo, Ti-15Mo and Ti-18Mo (wt.%), were investigated to determine the influence of $β$-phase stability on phase transformations during continuous heating. Electrical resistance measurement, dilatometry and differential scanning calorimetry were performed during linear heating at 5 °C/min up to 750 °C. The in-situ experiments were complemented by electron microscopy observations of specimens heated to selected temperatures and subsequently water-quenched. All alloys exhibited a sequence involving partial dissolution of athermal $ω$ phase, stabilization and growth of isothermal $ω$ phase, subsequent $ω$ dissolution and precipitation of $α$ phase. Increasing molybdenum content progressively suppressed $ω$ phase evolution and subsequent $α$ precipitation and narrowed the temperature interval associated with $ω$-related transformations. The excellent agreement between electrical resistance measurements, DSC, dilatometry and electron microscopy enabled a consistent description of phase evolution during heating and demonstrated the benefits of combining complementary experimental techniques for investigation of complex transformation pathways in metastable $β$ titanium alloys.

</details>


### [91] [Aging-Improved NH$_3$ Sensing in Polypyrrole Nanotube Chemiresistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04917)
*Arya Mohan,Olivia Kamdom,Antoine Enel,Benoît Hackens*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究对比了纳米管（nanotube, NT）与平面薄膜（planar film, PF）两种形貌的聚吡咯（polypyrrole）NH₃化学电阻器（chemiresistor），并在60天环境储存与反复NH₃暴露过程中追踪其性能演变，结果表明纳米管形貌在响应动力学与长期稳定性上均优于平面薄膜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对NH₃传感应用，需明确器件形貌如何影响响应幅度、检测限（limit of detection, LOD）、灵敏度与响应/恢复动力学，并在长期使用与储存条件下评估其可制造性与鲁棒性。

Method: 在相同材料体系下制备纳米管与平面薄膜两种形貌的聚吡咯化学电阻器，比较新鲜器件的响应与动力学参数，并将同一批传感器在环境储存60天及反复NH₃暴露中进行跟踪测量，采用快、慢两个时间常数（τ₁、τ₂）刻画动力学行为。

Result: 新鲜器件中，NT在各浓度下快过程时间常数τ₁均短于PF，平均快38%；慢过程τ₂在两种形貌间重叠且不随浓度变化。老化过程中，NT的τ₁与τ₂随60天单调显著下降，PF时间常数无一致趋势；NT的LOD、响应与灵敏度改善或至少保持稳定，而PF各项参数均退化。

Conclusion: 纳米管形貌具备更快的响应动力学与更优的老化稳定性，被认定为长期NH₃传感应用中更鲁棒、更利于规模化制造的形貌选择。

Abstract: Polypyrrole NH$_3$ chemiresistors in nanotube (NT) and planar film (PF) morphologies are compared on response magnitude, limit of detection (LOD), sensitivity, and response/recovery kinetics, including the performance of the same sensors tracked over 60 days of ambient storage and repeated NH$_{3}$ exposure. In the case of fresh sensors, the time constants for the fast process ($τ_1$) is shorter in the NT than in the PF at every concentration. NT is 38 \% faster on average than PF. The slow process ($τ_2$) overlaps between two morphologies and shows no concentration dependence. The two time constants differentiate the two morphologies through aging: NT $τ_1$ and $τ_2$ decrease substantially and monotonically over 60 days, while the time constants of PF show no consistent trend. All measured sensing parameters, LOD, sensor response and sensitivity improve or at least remain stable with aging in the case of NT, while all parameters degrade in the PF case. These improved sensor performance and aging identify the nanotube morphology as the more robust and manufacturable choice for long-term NH$_3$ sensing applications.

</details>


### [92] [Band structure of two-dimensional graphene and of one-dimensional nanoribbons via variational quantum algorithms: from energies to wavefunction quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04975)
*Raúl Medrano Millán,Rodrigo Gil-Merino y Rubio,Juan Borge,Unai Aseguinolaza Aguirreche*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以单层石墨烯及锯齿型/扶手椅型纳米带为对象，在紧束缚近似下构建从VQE基带到VQD激发子带的统一变分工作流，并在IBM量子处理器上验证，揭示实际电路深度受噪声而非表达力限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法模拟量子多体系统存在指数级瓶颈，而NISQ时代硬件噪声制约精度；作者希望以石墨烯能带计算作为通向强关联材料研究的过渡性基准，并强调无相互作用基态可为子空间及热启动变分方法提供初始参考态。

Method: 在$p_z$紧束缚近似下，将布洛赫哈密顿量（Bloch Hamiltonian）映射为泡利算符（Pauli operators），用变分量子本征求解器（Variational Quantum Eigensolver, VQE）提取基带、变分量子亏损法（Variational Quantum Deflation, VQD）提取激发子带，并从理想模拟推进到IBM量子硬件执行；同时引入波函数层面的子空间包含度（subspace containment）诊断。

Result: 理想模拟下工作流高精度复现精确对角化（exact diagonalization）结果，硬件噪声引入约百分之几跳跃参数（hopping parameter）量级的能量偏差；比特数随纳米带宽度对数增长，但固定深度拟设（ansatz）精度随希尔伯特空间维度下降；加深拟设使理想精度提升逾三个数量级，却在噪声下恶化能量估计。

Conclusion: 实际电路深度受硬件噪声而非拟设表达力约束；所提出的子空间包含度诊断可捕捉简并流形（degenerate manifolds）并揭示仅凭能量一致性所掩盖的优化失效模式，为在真实材料中研究拓扑与强关联相提供受控基准。

Abstract: Simulating quantum many-body systems classically encounters exponential bottlenecks, which quantum devices circumvent by natively encoding wavefunctions. In the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, however, hardware noise constrains simulation accuracy. As a stepping stone toward strongly correlated materials, we compute the band structure of 2D monolayer graphene and 1D nanoribbons (zigzag and armchair) using a single variational workflow in the $p_z$ tight-binding approximation. Non-interacting ground states also provide essential initial reference states for subspace and warm-started variational methods to solve interacting spectra. By mapping the Bloch Hamiltonian to Pauli operators, we extract the ground band via the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and excited subbands via Variational Quantum Deflation (VQD), validating our approach from ideal simulations to execution on physical IBM Quantum processors. In ideal simulations, the workflow reproduces exact diagonalization results with high precision, whereas hardware noise introduces energy deviations of a few hundredths of the hopping parameter. While qubit counts scale logarithmically with ribbon width, fixed-depth ansatz accuracy degrades with Hilbert space dimension. Crucially, deepening the ansatz improves ideal simulation accuracy by over three orders of magnitude but worsens energy estimates under noise, proving that practical circuit depth is bounded by hardware noise rather than expressivity. Finally, we complement energy evaluations with a wavefunction-level diagnostic measuring subspace containment. This diagnostic captures degenerate manifolds and exposes optimization failure modes disguised by energy agreement alone. Operating directly on NISQ hardware, this validated workflow establishes a controlled baseline for studying topological and strongly correlated phases in realistic materials.

</details>


### [93] [Ferron polariton Hall effect: Deflecting optically rectified polarization with magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05025)
*Omer Yaniv,Dominik M. Juraschek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该理论工作表明，铁电体中的铁电振子极化激元（ferron polariton）在外加磁场下表现出霍尔效应（Hall effect）：入射太赫兹脉冲激发的极化激元可产生垂直于传播方向的极化输运。以SnTe为例，基于第一性原理计算与声子极化激元电流的微观理论验证了该效应，且输运方向与大小可由太赫兹脉冲偏振和磁场调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电振子（ferrons）作为铁电序的基本激发可传播并输运极化，但如何在光激发下实现对电极化输运的方向性控制尚不明确。该研究旨在探索磁场与光场联合调控铁电体中极化定向输运的可能性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）结合声子极化激元电流（phonon-polariton current）的微观理论，以铁电材料SnTe为范例，理论分析铁电振子极化激元在磁场下的横向极化输运行为。

Result: 铁电振子极化激元呈现霍尔效应：施加磁场时，太赫兹脉冲激发的极化激元产生垂直于传播方向的极化输运；其横向输运的方向与大小可通过太赫兹脉冲的偏振和应用磁场加以控制。

Conclusion: 研究确立了在铁电体中实现光控电极化定向输运（light-controlled directional transport of electric polarization）的途径，为磁光联合调控铁电极化输运提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Ferrons are elementary excitations of ferroelectric order that can propagate and transport polarization when excited with light. Here, we show theoretically that ferron polaritons exhibit a Hall effect: an applied magnetic field generates polarization transport perpendicular to the propagation direction of a ferron polariton excited by an incident terahertz pulse. We demonstrate this effect for the example of ferroelectric SnTe using first-principles calculations and a microscopic theory of the phonon-polariton current. The direction and magnitude of the transverse polarization transport can be controlled by the polarization of the terahertz pulse and the applied magnetic field. Our results establish a route toward light-controlled directional transport of electric polarization in ferroelectrics.

</details>


### [94] [Bond-valence theory is a general electrostatic valence rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05339)
*M. L. Whittaker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在第一性原理电子密度中定义键价（bond valence），证明鲍林静电价规则（Pauling's electrostatic valence rule）与键价理论（bond-valence theory）可通过原子盆（atomic basin）表面上的电通量统一：求和规则在此为高斯定理的推论，键价定律在3680个化合物中位偏差约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲍林静电价规则将阳离子价均匀分配给各配位键，键价理论则用指数形式 s=exp[(R₀−d)/B] 与求和规则替代等分假设，但二者缺乏共同的物理基础，且键价参数的可迁移性（transferability）存疑。作者试图以电子密度为出发点，为这些经验规则提供可推导的物理图像。

Method: 以第一性原理（first-principles）电子密度构造原子盆，每个键临界点（bond critical point）对应一个盆面；用高斯定理（Gauss's law）计算通过该面的电通量作为键电荷。在3680个氧化物、卤化物、硫族化物与磷族化物上，逐位点检验通量是否服从键价定律，并考察软度（softness）B 与 R₀ 的变化及参数可迁移性。

Result: 通量逐位点符合键价定律，中位偏差为10%；软度 B 从刚性硫化物到较软的砷化物变化近一个数量级，且在各情形下均由盆面电通量决定，R₀ 由键长及其差值推出。多数氧化物、氟化物、硫化物与氮化物位点上，阴离子价与成键阳离子价之差在5%以内；而 (R₀, B) 的可迁移性在106个族中有96个失效。

Conclusion: 通过求和规则消去 B，得到 s=(z/n)^{(R₀−d)/(R₀−d_B)}，即鲍林键强度以软度加权平均键长 d_B 为基准的距离指数次幂。因此键价理论可视为带距离指数的鲍林规则，而电子密度提供了二者等价成立的界面；软度是受盆电荷定义的局域位点性质，而非给定阳离子–阴离子对的可迁移常数。

Abstract: Pauling's electrostatic valence rule assigns to each bond of a cation of charge $z$ with $n$ anion neighbors the strength $z/n$. Bond-valence theory replaces the equal split of valence among bonds by $s=\exp[(R_0-d)/B]$ in the bond length $d$ and takes the sum rule, that the valences at each atom add to its formal charge, as its first axiom. Here the bond valence is defined in the electron density. Atomic basins of a first-principles density are bounded by faces, one per bond critical point, and the electric flux of that density through a face is a charge associated with the bond. The fluxes leaving a cation sum to its basin charge by Gauss's law, so on this surface the sum rule is a theorem. Across 3680 oxides, halides, chalcogenides and pnictides the flux follows the bond-valence law site by site, with a median deviation of ten percent. The softness $B$ varies nearly an order of magnitude from stiff sulfides to softer arsenides. In all cases it is set by the electrostatic flux at atomic basin faces. $R_0$ then follows from the bond lengths and their differences. Anion valences equal those of bonded cations to within 5\% for most of the oxide, fluoride, sulfide and nitride sites. Transferability of $(R_0,B)$ between compounds fails in 96 of 106 families, suggesting that the softness is a local site property defined by the basin charge rather than a transferable constant of a given cation--anion pair. Eliminating $B$ by the sum rule gives $s=(z/n)^{(R_0-d)/(R_0-d_B)}$, Pauling's strength raised to a distance exponent at the softness-weighted mean bond length $d_B$. Thus, bond-valence theory is Pauling's rule with a distance exponent, and the electron density supplies the surface on which the two are equivalent.

</details>
