### [95] [The First Deep Learning all-sky List of Fermi-LAT Gamma-ray Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37287)
*Saptashwa Bhattacharyya,Giacomo Principe,Dmitry Malyshev,Roberto Ruiz de Austri,Gabrijela Zaharijas,Sascha Caron,Fiorenzo Stoppa,Guðlaugur Johannesson*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作将深度学习流水线（U-Net 分割＋多个专用深度神经网络）扩展至 γ 射线数据，构建了首个基于深度学习的 Fermi-LAT 全天源表（1FDL），含 3800 余个源，并在星际辐射模型（IEM）变动下表现出与或优于似然方法的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一套能自动构建 γ 射线源表并定量评估背景不确定性对源探测与表征影响的流程；此前 AutoSource-ID 算法仅用于光学观测，本文将其扩展并改进以适配 γ 射线数据。

Method: 源探测采用 U-Net 架构进行图像分割（image segmentation），源定位、通量估计以及真源/虚假源分类分别由专用深度神经网络（deep neural network, DNN）完成；训练数据由基于 10 年 Fermi-LAT 观测的蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）生成。

Result: 给出首个深度学习全天源表 1FDL，包含 3800 多个源及其位置、通量与表征真实性的概率分数；并检验了流水线对星际辐射模型（interstellar emission model, IEM）变化的稳健性，且与 4FGL-DR2 星表进行了对比。

Conclusion: 在 IEM 变动测试中，该深度学习方法的性能与标准 Fermi-LAT 星表流程所用似然方法（likelihood-based method）相当或更优；1FDL 与同 10 年观测期的 4FGL-DR2 总体一致，并包含若干在近期 16 年星表（FL16Y）中确认的新源，显示 DNN 方法在高能天体物理分析中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Aims: We develop a deep-learning-based pipeline to construct a list of γ-ray sources and to quantitatively assess the impact of background uncertainties on source detection and characterization. This work extends and enhances the existing AutoSource-ID algorithm, previously adopted on optical observations, for applications on γ-ray data.
  Methods: Source detection was performed through image segmentation using a U-Net architecture, while dedicated deep neural networks (DNNs) were employed for source localization, flux estimation, and the classification of detected sources as either genuine or spurious. The training data were generated from Monte Carlo simulations based on 10 years of Fermi-LAT observations.
  Results: We present the first Fermi-LAT γ-ray all-sky source list derived using a deep-learning-based method (1FDL), containing more than 3800 sources with estimated positions, fluxes, and probability scores that quantify the likelihood of each detection being genuine or spurious. We further evaluated the robustness of the pipeline against variations in the interstellar emission model (IEM) and compared the resulting source list with the 4FGL-DR2 catalog.
  Conclusion: We find that the our deep-learning-based approach achieves performance comparable to, or better than, the likelihood-based methods employed in the standard Fermi-LAT catalog pipeline when tested against changes in the IEM. The 1FDL source list is broadly consistent with the Fermi-LAT 4FGL-DR2 catalog obtained over the same 10-year observational period, including a few new sources confirmed in the recent Fermi-LAT 16-year source list (FL16Y). These results demonstrate the potential of DNN methods for the detection and characterization of γ-ray sources and provide a foundation for their application in future high-energy astrophysical analyses.

</details>


### [96] [Multimuon Events from Cosmic Rays with the ALICE Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04677)
*Mario Sitta,for the ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: ALICE 利用 LHC Run 2 期间 62.5 天的宇宙线数据，分析多缪子（multimuon）事件的缪子多重数分布（MMD）与高缪子多重数（HMM）事件率，并与三种强子相互作用模型在纯质子与纯铁两种极端原初成分假设下比较。结果显示仅 QGSJET-II-04 在重成分假设下能较好复现数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）实验的宇宙线取数，检验描述大气簇射（air shower）发展的强子相互作用模型及其对原初宇宙线（primary cosmic rays）成分的敏感性，弥补传统宇宙线实验在模型区分能力上的不足。

Method: 基于 ALICE 在 2015–2018 年（LHC Run 2）共 62.5 天实际观测时间的宇宙线数据，选取探测到四个以上缪子的多缪子事件；在低中等多重数区间（4 < N_μ < 50）研究缪子多重数分布，在 N_μ > 100 区间测量高缪子多重数事件率，并与 QGSJET-II-04、EPOS-LHC、SIBYLL 2.3d 三种模型的蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟对比，原初成分取纯质子与纯铁两种极端假设。

Result: 三种模型在描述原初质子与铁核通量随原初宇宙线能量变化的趋势方面均存在困难；在所研究的整个能量范围内，仅 QGSJET-II-04 在重成分假设下能较为合理地复现缪子多重数分布与高缪子多重数事件率。N_μ > 100 区间由数据的大统计涨落主导。

Conclusion: 多缪子观测量对强子相互作用模型与原初成分具有区分能力，QGSJET-II-04 配合重成分假设与 ALICE 宇宙线数据最为吻合，但模型在能量依赖趋势上的整体不足仍提示需进一步改进强子相互作用建模。

Abstract: ALICE experiment at the CERN's Large Hadron Collider, carried out a cosmic data-taking campaign in the period 2015-2018 (LHC Run 2), for a total of 62.5 days of real observation time. In this contribution, the analysis of these data is limited to multimuon events, i.e. events with more than four detected muons. In particular, the muon multiplicity distribution (MMD) is studied in the low-intermediate multiplicity range ($4 < N_μ < 50$), while for multiplicities $N_μ > 100$, dominated by large statistical fluctuations of the data, the rate of the high muon multiplicity events (HMM) is measured. The results are compared with Monte Carlo simulations using three of the main hadronic interaction models describing the air shower development in the atmosphere: QGSJET-II-04, EPOS-LHC, and SIBYLL 2.3d, and assuming two extreme compositions of primary cosmic rays, pure proton and pure iron, representing respectively the lightest and the heaviest composition. Although the models have difficulty precisely describing the trend of primary p and Fe as a function of cosmic ray primary energy, QGSJET-II-04 is the only model that reproduces reasonably well the MMD and the rate of HMM events assuming a heavy composition for the entire energy range studied.

</details>


### [97] [Quasi-normal Modes and Instabilities of Rotating Relativistic Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03986)
*Yong Gao,Masaru Shibata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文基于完整广义相对论（general relativity）的二阶作用量推导耦合流体—时空本征值问题方程，并以外行波边界条件精确求解快速旋转恒星的准正模（quasi-normal modes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一套可直接用于快速旋转恒星准正模计算的相对论性流体—时空耦合本征值框架，并厘清不同稳定性判据（Chandrasekhar不稳定性、长期与动力学不稳定性阈值）之间的关系。

Method: 从完整广义相对论二阶作用量（second-order action）出发导出耦合流体—时空本征值方程，施加外行波（outgoing-wave）边界条件进行数值求解；对MIT袋模型奇异星（MIT-bag strange stars）与中子星（neutron stars）分别计算模式谱，并用静态稳定性分析与基频F模频率趋零追踪两种独立途径定位坍缩阈值。

Result: 奇异星谱呈现Chandrasekhar不稳定性图像，具有可区分的Chandrasekhar–Friedman–Schutz中性条件与类Jacobi中性条件，并过渡到动力学棒模（bar-mode）增长；中子星给出f模、r模及其他惯性模（inertial modes）谱及辐射阻尼/增长率，f模与惯性模相互作用表现为避免交叉（avoided crossings）及阻尼/增长率的交换；指出均匀旋转下动力学不稳定性 onset 的中心密度始终大于长期不稳定性（secular instability）对应值。

Conclusion: 所提出的形式体系与数值实现可自洽处理上述模式问题，并能分辨高度阻尼的w模（w-modes），为旋转恒星的相对论性振荡与稳定性研究提供统一工具。

Abstract: We derive the basic equations suitable for the coupled fluid--spacetime eigenvalue problem from the second-order action in full general relativity and accurately determine quasi-normal modes of a variety of rapidly rotating stars with outgoing-wave boundary conditions. For MIT-bag strange stars, the spectrum realizes Chandrasekhar's instability picture, with distinct Chandrasekhar--Friedman--Schutz and Jacobi-like neutral conditions and the transition to dynamical bar-mode growth. For neutron stars, we determine the $f$-, $r$-, and other inertial-mode spectra and their radiative damping or growth times. Interactions between $f$- and inertial modes reveal avoided crossings and exchanges for radiative damping or growth rates. We locate quasiradial collapse thresholds through a static stability analysis and independently verify them by tracking the fundamental $F$-mode frequency to zero, clarifying that the central density at the onset of the dynamical instability is always larger than that of the secular instability for uniform rotation. Our formalism and numerical implementation can also resolve highly damped $w$-modes.

</details>


### [98] [How Fast do Black Holes Spin?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04361)
*Chen Yuan,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过500组广义相对论磁流体力学模拟，系统刻画了吸积黑洞自旋演化，给出平衡自旋范围约0.88–0.98，并指出净磁通量通过喷流力矩调控平衡自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统研究吸积黑洞的自旋演化规律，厘清磁场配置与吸积几何如何影响黑洞平衡自旋，并为解释X射线双星与活动星系核中观测到的高自旋提供理论依据。

Method: 采用BHAC代码开展广义相对论磁流体力学（general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic, GRMHD）模拟，对自旋参数χ=0至0.99的Kerr黑洞周围磁化吸积盘进行500组高分辨率模拟。基线采用Fishbone–Moncrief环面（torus）设置，并考察多重极向磁环、环面初始位置、磁场强度与径向环调制的影响；由视界处质量通量与角动量通量计算准稳态后的时间平均自旋演化率。

Result: 平衡自旋从基准模型的χ_eq≈0.88到弱磁通模型的χ_eq≈0.98。应用于人马座A*（Sgr A*）时，即便采用乐观的快速自旋演化估计，时标仍超过10^13年，提示其自旋源于更早期的增长或并合历史。

Conclusion: 结果与“穿越视界的净磁通量越大，喷流力矩（jet-torques）越强、平衡自旋越低；弱净磁通则导致近极大自旋”的图景一致。该工作为解释X射线双星与活动星系核（active galactic nuclei, AGN）的高自旋提供了途径，并增强了依赖近极值黑洞（near-extremal black holes）的近视界高能过程的物理可行性。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the spin evolution of accreting black holes. Using the BHAC code for general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations, we conduct a total of 500 high resolution simulations of magnetized accretion disks around Kerr BHs with spin parameters ranging from $χ=0$ to $χ= 0.99$. The baseline configuration follows the default Fishbone--Moncrief torus setup in BHAC. We further explore the effects of: I) introducing multiple poloidal magnetic loops, II) the initial position of the torus, III) magnetic field strength and IV) radial loop modulation. From each simulation we extract the mass flux and angular momentum flux through the horizon to compute the time-averaged spin evolution rate after the system becomes quasi-stationary. The equilibrium spin ranges from $χ_{\rm eq}\simeq0.88$ in the fiducial model to $χ_{\rm eq}\simeq0.98$ for weak-flux model. Applied to Sgr A$^*$, even an optimistic estimate for rapid spin evolution gives a timescale longer than $10^{13}$ yr, indicating an earlier growth or merger history. These trends are consistent with the picture that larger net magnetic flux threading the horizon enhances jet-torques and reduces the equilibrium spins, whereas weak net flux yields near-maximal spins. Our results provide a pathway to interpret the high spins reported for X-ray binaries and active galactic nuclei and they sharpen the astrophysical viability of near-horizon high-energy processes that rely on near-extremal black holes.

</details>

### [99] [Schrodinger group along the horocycles and Egorov's theorem on compact hyperbolic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04131)
*Xiaolong Han*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文在紧双曲曲面（compact hyperbolic surfaces）上研究 Zelditch 量子化中拉普拉斯特征函数的 Egorov 定理，明确了正向时间（forward in time）可延展至所有对数时间，并给出逆向时间（backward in time）与量子唯一遍历性（QUE）猜想的等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Egorov 定理在半经典（semiclassical）框架下的有效时间尺度通常止于 Ehrenfest 时间 T_E=|log h|/2，作者希望精确刻画特征函数情形下余项（remainder）的结构，并厘清其与量子唯一遍历性（quantum unique ergodicity, QUE）猜想之间的定量联系。

Method: 在 Zelditch 量子化下将 Egorov 定理的余项显式表示为符号沿测地流（geodesic flow）弱稳定叶状结构（weak stable foliation）的二阶导数；正向时间直接估计余项，逆向时间则引入沿稳定 horocycle 的 Schrödinger 群（Schrödinger group）以吸收指数增长部分。

Result: 正向时间上 Egorov 定理对所有对数时间成立，远超 Ehrenfest 时间；逆向时间上可成立至 -T_E+T_h（T_h→∞ 且 0≤T_h≤T_E），且 -T_E 处余项的极限恰为半经典测度（semiclassical measure）与 Liouville 测度之差，用 Schrödinger 群传播的符号检验。

Conclusion: 序列在 -T_E（乃至任意 -T_E-T）处满足 Egorov 性质当且仅当其趋于相空间等分布（equidistributed），故 QUE 猜想等价于每个特征基（eigenbasis）均具此 Egorov 性质；QUE 具多项式速率（polynomial rate）当且仅当 Egorov 定理在 -T_E 处具多项式余项，且 QUE 速率越强，Egorov 定理成立的时间越长。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Egorov's theorem for Laplacian eigenfunctions on compact hyperbolic surfaces in Zelditch's quantization. In this quantization, the remainder in Egorov's theorem for eigenfunctions is given explicitly by the second derivatives of the symbols along the weak stable foliation of the geodesic flow. Forward in time, we show that Egorov's theorem for eigenfunctions is valid to all logarithmic times, far beyond the Ehrenfest time T_E=|log h|/2, where h going to 0 is the semiclassical parameter.
  Backward in time, we introduce the Schrodinger group along the stable horocycles, which absorbs the exponentially growing part of the remainder. We show that Egorov's theorem for eigenfunctions is valid up to -T_E+T_h for any Th going to infinity with 0<=T_h<=T_E. Moreover, the limit of the remainder in Egorov's theorem at the Ehrenfest time -T_E is exactly the difference between the semiclassical measure and the Liouville measure, tested against the symbols propagated by the Schrodinger group. Consequently, Egorov's theorem for a sequence of eigenfunctions holds at -T_E (and in fact, at -T_E-T for any fixed T in R if and only if the sequence tends to be equidistributed in the phase space. Thus, the quantum unique ergodicity (QUE) conjecture is equivalent to this Egorov property holding for every eigenbasis. Furthermore, QUE holds with a polynomial rate if and only if Egorov's theorem holds at -T_E with a polynomial remainder. In this case, Egorov's theorem holds beyond -T_E with a polynomial remainder, and the stronger the rate is in QUE, the longer the time is in Egorov's theorem.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Weak uniqueness and autonomous approximation for the SLE-reversible Weil-Petersson response diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04417)
*Chunhao Cai*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转标记的SLE焊接概率（welding probability）之Weil–Petersson响应微分所对应的自洽共同噪声方程（self-consistent common-noise equation）。在0<κ≤4范围内，证明了联合弱唯一性、马尔可夫性，并通过条件高斯提升（conditional Gaussian lift）与容量局部化（capacity localization）完成正则化与收敛分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注SLE焊接概率与Weil–Petersson响应微分所诱导的自洽共同噪声方程的解的唯一性与正则化问题，旨在为相应随机动力系统的良定义性及不变测度性质提供理论依据。

Method: 在0<κ≤4条件下，于连续适应弱解（continuous adapted weak solutions）类中证明联合弱唯一性（joint weak uniqueness），要求每个确定性时刻的分布为焊接概率；借助条件高斯提升（conditional Gaussian lift）与容量局部化（capacity localization）获得所需的L¹生成元-值域稠密性（generator-range density）；并构造具有路径唯一全局强解的自主Fejér正则化与Poisson正则化。

Result: 证明了联合弱唯一性与原滤子下的马尔可夫性（Markov property）；与典范律（canonical law）的等同给出平稳性（stationarity）与可逆性（reversibility）；所构造的Fejér与Poisson正则化具有路径唯一全局强解，且对任意关于焊接概率绝对连续的初始分布，其联合状态-噪声律收敛到典范律。

Conclusion: 该方程在0<κ≤4下具有良好适定性与马尔可夫结构，典范律为平稳可逆分布，且Fejér与Poisson正则化在广泛初值下收敛至典范律，为SLE焊接概率相关随机系统的分析提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study the self-consistent common-noise equation associated with the Weil--Petersson response differential of the rotationally marked SLE welding probability. For $0<κ\leq4$, we prove joint weak uniqueness among continuous adapted weak solutions whose law at each deterministic time is the welding probability, and obtain the Markov property in the original filtration. A conditional Gaussian lift and capacity localization give the required $L^1$ generator-range density. Identification with the canonical law gives stationarity and reversibility. We also construct autonomous Fejér and Poisson regularizations with pathwise-unique global strong solutions. Their joint state-noise laws converge to the canonical law for every initial probability absolutely continuous with respect to the welding probability.

</details>


### [101] [Exponential decay for the two-dimensional Heisenberg model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04908)
*Jian Ding,Fenglin Huang,Peng Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明自旋 O(N) 模型（spin O(N) model）在 N≥3 且任意逆温 β>0 时自旋关联呈指数衰减（exponential decay），从而证实 Polyakov 1975 年关于海森堡模型（Heisenberg model，即 N=3）的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是验证 Polyakov 在 1975 年对海森堡模型（N=3）提出的指数衰减预言，并将结果推广至一般 N≥3 的 O(N) 模型及所有逆温 β>0。

Method: 在度量图（metric graph）上采样强度为 N/2 的圈汤（loop soup），以每个格点局部时（local time）为 β 为条件，再将各圈独立分配到 ⌈N⌉ 个副本中；借助 Lupu 同构定理（Lupu's isomorphism theorem）为每个副本中各圈汤簇独立赋随机符号，构造出与整数 N 时 O(N) 模型一致的自旋场，并把模型延拓到实数 N。

Result: 对每个实值 N>2 建立指数衰减，因而覆盖 N≥3 的整数情形与任意 β>0；结果揭示了指数衰减的内在机制在 N>2（即超出 XY 模型范围）时即已出现。

Conclusion: 指数衰减并不必然依赖球面 S² 上的旋转对称性（rotation symmetry），而是只要 N>2 便出现的更普遍机制；这证实并推广了 Polyakov 的预言。

Abstract: In this work, we prove exponential decay for the spin correlation of the spin $O(\mathsf{N})$ model for $\mathsf{N}\geq3$ at every inverse temperature $ β>0 $. In particular, our work confirms Polyakov's prediction in 1975 on the Heisenberg model, i.e., the case of $\mathsf{N}=3$.
  To this end, we investigate a generalized $O(\mathsf{N})$ model where $ \mathsf{N} $ is a positive \emph{real number}, as follows: we sample a loop soup on the metric graph (say of a box) with intensity $ \tfrac{\mathsf{N}}{2} $ subject to the conditioning that the local time at each lattice vertex is $β$, and then for each loop we independently assign it to copies $1\leq \mathsf{i}\leq \lceil\mathsf{N}\rceil$ such that it is assigned to copy $\mathsf{i}$ with probability $ \tfrac{1}{\mathsf{N}} $ for $ 1\leq \mathsf{i}\leq \lfloor\mathsf{N}\rfloor $. Then, by Lupu's isomorphism theorem, we can construct a spin field by assigning a random sign to each loop soup cluster in each copy independently, such that the field coincides with the spin $O(\mathsf{N})$ model when $\mathsf{N}$ is an integer. We then establish exponential decay for every real-valued $\mathsf{N}>2$. Our result reveals an underlying mechanism for exponential decay, which emerges as soon as $\mathsf{N}>2$ (i.e., as soon as beyond the range of the XY model) and does not necessarily rely on the rotation symmetry in the sphere $ \mathbb{S}^{2} $.

</details>


### [102] [Monochromatic arm exponents for critical planar percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05101)
*Gefei Cai*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文通过一个显式正积分算子族的精确谱阈值，确定了临界平面渗流（critical planar percolation）的单色三臂指数（monochromatic three-arm exponent）ξ₃=0.76928375⋯，并给出所有 n≥4 的单色 n 臂指数的显式谱刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单色两臂（骨干，backbone）事件不同，三臂事件可被两个不同的白色簇（white clusters）阻断，因此马尔可夫探索（Markovian exploration）必须保留过去的连接信息，需要新的可观测手段来处理这一非马尔可夫结构。

Method: 引入一个记录这些连接的两点边界可观测（two-point boundary observable）；借助共形环圈集合（CLE）与刘维尔量子引力（LQG）之间的耦合，该可观测满足以量子边界长度（quantum boundary lengths）表述的更新方程（renewal equation），从而导出谱信息决定 ξ₃ 的显式算子。

Result: 得到单色三臂指数 ξ₃=0.76928375⋯，来自所构造正积分算子族的精确谱阈值（spectral threshold），其核为初等核（elementary kernels）。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅确定 ξ₃，还给出所有 n≥4 的单色 n 臂指数的显式谱刻画（spectral characterization），表明谱方法可系统处理该类臂事件指数。

Abstract: We determine the monochromatic three-arm exponent for critical planar percolation through the exact spectral threshold of an explicit positive integral operator family with elementary kernels, obtaining $ξ_3=0.76928375\cdots$. Unlike the monochromatic two-arm (backbone) event, the three-arm event can be obstructed by two distinct white clusters, so a Markovian exploration must retain the past connections. We introduce a two-point boundary observable that records these connections. Using the coupling between conformal loop ensemble (CLE) and Liouville quantum gravity (LQG), this observable satisfies a renewal equation in terms of quantum boundary lengths, and yields the explicit operator whose spectral information determines $ξ_3$. Our approach also gives an explicit spectral characterization of monochromatic $n$-arm exponents for all $n\ge4$.

</details>


### [103] [On the one-arm probability for the metric graph Gaussian free field in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05357)
*Pierre-François Rodriguez,Jialu Shi,Wen Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 在体积增长指数 α、格林函数衰减指数 ν 满足 2α<3ν（在 Z^d 上即 d>6）的暂态加权图的缆索系统上，高斯自由场符号簇（sign cluster）连通到距离 R 的概率为 R^{-β} 阶，β=α-ν 为游走维数；临界情形 2α=3ν（d=6）上界相差对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高斯自由场（Gaussian free field）符号簇的连通概率随距离的衰减规律，将 d>6 与 d=6 的经典结论推广到不具备传递性（transitivity）的一般加权图框架，并刻画衰减指数与图的体积增长、格林函数衰减之间的关系。

Method: 上界：结合比较临界环路汤（critical loop soup）与有质量环路汤下簇分布的熵型界（entropic bound），以及由马尔可夫性（Markov property）导出的有质量场的 Simon-Lieb 型不等式；下界：基于簇的容量（capacity）与体积之间的竞争。上下界的关键量均为随机游走从球中离开的期望逃逸时间（expected exit time）。

Result: 当 2α<3ν 时，顶点簇到达距离 R 的概率为 R^{-β} 阶（β=α-ν）；当 2α=3ν 时，上界在相差对数因子的意义下成立。在 Z^d 上对应 d>6 与 d=6 两种情形。

Conclusion: 符号簇连通概率的标度指数由游走维数 β=α-ν 决定，且论证具有稳健性、无需传递性假设，表明该结果适用于更广泛的一类暂态加权图。

Abstract: We consider the sign clusters of the Gaussian free field on the cable system of a transient weighted graph with volume growth exponent $α$ and Green function decay exponent $ν$. We prove that the probability that the cluster of a vertex reaches distance $R$ is of order $R^{-β}$ when $2α<3ν$, where $β=α-ν$ is the walk dimension, and that the same holds up to a logarithmic factor in the upper bound when $2α=3ν$. On $\mathbb Z^d$ these conditions read $d>6$ and $d=6$. The upper bound combines a general entropic bound, which compares the law of the cluster under the critical loop soup and under a massive one, with a Simon-Lieb-type inequality for the massive field derived from its Markov property. The arguments are robust and require no transitivity. The lower bound follows from a competition between the capacity and the volume of the cluster. In both bounds, the key quantity is the expected exit time of the random walk from balls, which links the volume of the cluster to its radius.

</details>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Almost primes in short intervals and averages of long Dirichlet polynomial approximations to $(ζ'/ζ)^{k}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05000)
*Siegfred Baluyot,John Brian Conrey,Ayesha Irfan,Nina C. Snaith*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本论文在临界线上研究 $(\zeta'/\zeta)^k$ 的 Dirichlet 级数长截断均方（mean square）的渐近行为，并将其与卷积 von Mangoldt 函数 $\Lambda^{*k}$ 在短区间及算术级数中的短区间方差（short-interval variance）相联系；在平移矩猜想（shifted moments conjecture）与黎曼假设（Riemann Hypothesis, RH）下，证明了短区间方差的首项系数等于均方首项系数的导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示 $(\zeta'/\zeta)^k$ 的 Dirichlet 级数截断均方与 $\Lambda^{*k}$ 的短区间方差之间的内在联系，并借助函数域上的迹和（trace-sum）平均所给出的系数 $\delta_k(c)$，为整数情形下短区间和方差的首项系数提供对应解释。

Method: 方法上先在平移矩猜想与 RH 假设下处理临界线上均方与短区间方差的联系；再在 $\mathbb{F}_q[T]$ 上的首一多项式（monic polynomials）中，用酉迹和平均（unitary trace-sum average）刻画 $\Lambda^{*k}$ 求和方差，其首项系数记为 $\delta_k(c)$，此处总迹次数与矩阵规模以固定比例 $c$ 增长；最后在较弱的平移矩猜想与 RH 下将 $\delta_k(c)$ 还原为整数短区间和方差的首项系数。

Result: 主要结果为：在平移矩猜想与 RH 下，短区间方差的首项系数是均方首项系数的导数；并且 $\delta_k(c)$ 可作为整数上 $\Lambda^{*k}$ 短区间和方差的首项系数被重新得到。$\delta_k(c)$ 被视为与因子和（divisor sums）方差所得的分段多项式 $\gamma_k(c)$ 相对应的对象，区别在于 $\zeta(s)^k$ 的 Dirichlet 系数被 $(-\zeta'/\zeta)^k$ 的系数替换。

Conclusion: 结论表明函数域上的酉迹和平均与整数上的短区间方差之间存在对应关系，$\delta_k(c)$ 构成 $\gamma_k(c)$ 的自然对偶量，从而在假设条件下统一刻画了 $(-\zeta'/\zeta)^k$ 相关均方与方差的渐近首项行为。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behaviour of the mean square of long truncations of the Dirichlet series for $\bigl(ζ'/ζ\bigr)^{k}$ on the critical line; we establish the connection between this problem and the short-interval variance of the convoluted von Mangoldt function $Λ^{*k}$ in short intervals and in arithmetic progressions. Under a shifted moments conjecture and the Riemann Hypothesis (RH), we prove that the short-interval variance leading coefficient is the derivative of the mean-square leading coefficient. A unitary trace-sum average governs the variance of sums of $Λ^{*k}$ over monic polynomials in $\mathbb F_q[T]$; we denote its leading coefficient by $δ_k(c)$, where the total trace degree and matrix size grow with a fixed ratio $c$. Under a weaker shifted moments conjecture and RH, we recover this $δ_k(c)$ as the leading coefficient of the variance of short-interval sums of $Λ^{*k}$ over the integers. It is the counterpart of $γ_k(c)$, the piecewise polynomial from the variance of divisor sums, with the Dirichlet coefficients of $ζ(s)^k$ replaced by those of $\bigl(-ζ'/ζ\bigr)^k$.

</details>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Asymptotic electromagnetic transition conditions for thin layers: the arithmetic-mean field and susceptibility hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04324)
*Asbjorn Hansen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文用单尺度渐近展开严格推导了有限厚度薄层的过渡条件（transition conditions），证明"算术平均场"与"以零阶矩表征极化率"并非人为假设，而分别是领头阶结果与重求和后的自然选择，并揭示非对称剖面会引起面内-面外响应杂化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料与超表面（metasurface）的电磁建模中，常将薄极化层替换为零厚度薄片（sheet），但标准过渡条件中对极化场的算术平均取值及仅用极化率零阶矩（$0^\mathrm{th}$ moment）表征层的做法均是直接施加而非推导所得，缺乏严格依据。

Method: 针对有限厚度、面内均匀且面内各向同性、具有一般非局域极化率（nonlocal susceptibility）的薄层，采用以 $\eta=l\max(k_0,q)$ 为小参数的单尺度渐近展开（single-scale asymptotic expansion），并在对层外面退极化场（out-of-plane depolarizing field）重求和（resumming）之后再取薄片极限。

Result: 算术平均作为领头阶极化场（leading-order polarizing field）对一般极化率剖面均成立；层剖面不对称只修正有效极化率而不改变算术平均场的规定。若剖面在两个面外坐标上均非偶对称，则面内与面外响应发生杂化（hybridization），对角体极化率变为含非对角分量、依赖 $q$ 的有效张量表面极化率，且仅凭镜像对称（mirror symmetry）不能消除该杂化。与局域层及可分离层的精确解对比表明，所得条件在传播与倏逝激发（evanescent excitations）下均保持 $O(\eta)$ 精度，而标准薄片条件在杂化显著时失效。

Conclusion: 该工作为薄层过渡条件提供了系统性推导基础，明确了薄片极限须在退极化场重求和后取得以保证表面极化率与厚度无关，并指出标准薄片条件在剖面非对称导致响应杂化时不再适用，需采用含非对角项的有效张量表面极化率。

Abstract: Effective transition conditions that replace a thin polarizable layer by a zero-thickness sheet are commonly applied in electromagnetic models of two-dimensional (2D) materials and metasurfaces. These conditions usually take the arithmetic mean of the fields on either side as the polarizing field at the boundary, and characterize the layer by the $0^\mathrm{th}$ moment of its susceptibility, but both choices are typically imposed rather than derived. We derive transition conditions for a finite-thickness, in-plane homogeneous, in-plane isotropic layer with a general nonlocal susceptibility, using a single-scale asymptotic expansion in $η= l\max(k_0,q)$, where $l$ is the layer thickness, $k_0$ the free-space wavenumber and $q$ the in-plane wavenumber. The sheet limit must be taken after resumming the out-of-plane depolarizing field, as this ensures that the resulting sheet susceptibility is independent of the material thickness. The arithmetic mean then emerges as the leading-order polarizing field for general susceptibility profiles. Asymmetries in the layer profile modify the effective susceptibility appearing in the transition conditions, rather than altering the arithmetic-mean field prescription. If the spatial profile is not even in both out-of-plane coordinates, in-plane and out-of-plane response hybridize, turning the diagonal volumetric susceptibility into a $q$-dependent effective tensor surface susceptibility with off-diagonal components. Mirror symmetry alone does not remove this hybridization. Comparison with exact solutions for local and separable layers shows that the derived conditions remain accurate to $O(η)$ across propagating and evanescent excitations, whereas the standard sheet conditions fail once hybridization becomes appreciable.

</details>

### [106] [Nonlinear stability of Schwarzschild black holes for the Einstein-massless Dirac system in spherical symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03708)
*Lamberto Tresoldi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文证明了球对称爱因斯坦-无质量狄拉克系统（spherically symmetric Einstein-massless Dirac system）中施瓦西外部时空（Schwarzschild exterior）的非线性稳定性，包括轨道稳定性与渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究施瓦西解在狄拉克场耦合下的非线性稳定性，以弥补标量波情形之外的空缺；主要难点在于狄拉克算子与几何的细致耦合，以及狄拉克应力-能量张量不满足正能条件。

Method: 采用几何与狄拉克场耦合的自举论证（bootstrap argument），在适配渐近零边界的“目的论双零规范”（teleological double-null gauge）下建立加权能量估计，综合积分局部能量衰减、视界附近红移估计（red-shift estimates）与趋向零无穷的 r^p 估计；核心是对旋量分量（spinor components）的重新加权。

Result: 对足够小的施瓦西特征初值（characteristic initial data）扰动，相应极大发展在外部区域保持全局受控，并在适当规范下收敛到具有最终质量的施瓦西时空；极大未来发展包含完备的未来零无穷（future null infinity）与规则事件视界（event horizon），内部存在非空黑洞区。

Conclusion: 球对称爱因斯坦-无质量狄拉克系统的施瓦西外部具有非线性稳定性，旋量分量的重新加权是闭合加权能量估计的关键技术手段。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the nonlinear stability of the Schwarzschild exterior for the spherically symmetric Einstein-massless Dirac system. More precisely, we consider sufficiently small perturbations of Schwarzschild characteristic initial data and prove that the corresponding maximal development remains globally controlled in the exterior region (orbital stability), and converges, in a suitable teleological double-null gauge, to a Schwarzschild spacetime with a final mass (asymptotic stability). In particular, the maximal future development contains a complete future null infinity and a regular event horizon, with a nonempty black hole region in its interior. The main difficulties in the proof arise from the delicate coupling between the Dirac operator and the geometry, compared with the scalar wave counterpart, as well as from the failure of the Dirac stress-energy tensor to satisfy a positive energy condition. To overcome these difficulties, we rely on a bootstrap argument coupling the geometry and the Dirac field. Working in suitable teleological double-null gauges adapted to the asymptotic null boundaries, we establish weighted energy estimates combining integrated local energy decay, red-shift estimates near the event horizon, and an rp-estimates towards null infinity. A core ingredient is the use of suitable reweightings of the spinor components, which allow us to close these estimates despite the nonlinear coupling to the geometry.

</details>


### [107] [Gauge-invariant non-universal scalar couplings in teleparallel gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03740)
*Giovanni Otalora,Rudranil Roy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在标架形式的远平行引力（teleparallel gravity）框架下构建规范不变的模型，允许标量场（scalar field）的引力耦合不具普适性，并研究其宇宙学后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是放宽引力耦合的普适性（universality of gravitational coupling）这一通常假设，同时保持引力部分仍为广义相对论（General Relativity）的远平行等价形式，并维持局域平移规范不变性（local translational gauge invariance）。

Method: 引入引力荷（gravitational charge）依赖于场的标量扇区四元标架（scalar-sector tetrad），使其与普适平移规范势（translational gauge potential）的耦合由场依赖的引力荷控制；通过 Stückelberg 完备化（Stückelberg completion）恢复局域平移规范不变性，推导引力、标量场与 Stückelberg 方程及反对称约束（antisymmetric constraint）和一致性关系。

Result: 在均匀各向同性宇宙学中，Stückelberg 约束给出控制非普适耦合演化的首次积分（first integral）。动能主导（kination-dominated）区域渐近恢复标准刚性流体（stiff-fluid）行为；势主导区域中，加速膨胀（accelerated expansion）需要超出势主导的额外条件。存在非普适性被动力学压低的相容膨胀解，包括趋近幂律暴胀（power-law inflation）或渐近 de Sitter 膨胀的示例；加入普适耦合完美流体（perfect fluid）与常标量势后，得到晚期解，在背景 Friedmann 方程中呈亚主导的类物质贡献并趋于渐近 de Sitter。

Conclusion: 结果为非普适标量耦合提供了规范理论实现，并显示其对早期与晚期宇宙学具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant teleparallel framework in which the universality of the gravitational coupling is relaxed for a scalar field, while the gravitational sector retains the form of the teleparallel equivalent of General Relativity. The non-universal interaction is encoded in a scalar-sector tetrad whose coupling to the universal translational gauge potential is controlled by a field-dependent gravitational charge. A Stückelberg completion preserves local translational gauge invariance, and we derive the gravitational, scalar-field, and Stückelberg equations, together with the antisymmetric constraint and their consistency relations. As a first application, we investigate homogeneous and isotropic cosmology, where the Stückelberg constraint provides a first integral governing the evolution of the non-universal coupling. In the kination-dominated regime, the standard stiff-fluid behavior is recovered asymptotically, whereas in the potential-dominated regime, accelerated expansion requires additional conditions beyond potential domination. We identify compatible expanding solutions in which departures from universality are dynamically suppressed, including examples approaching power-law inflation or asymptotically de Sitter expansion. Including a universally coupled perfect fluid and a constant scalar potential, we obtain a late-time solution exhibiting a subdominant matter-like contribution to the background Friedmann equation and an asymptotic de Sitter limit. These results provide a gauge-theoretic realization of non-universal scalar couplings and highlight their potential implications for early- and late-time cosmology.

</details>


### [108] [Reference-renormalized Jacobi primitives for finite-distance massive-particle deflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03746)
*Reggie C. Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出“参考重整化（reference-renormalized）Jacobi-原函数 Gauss-Bonnet 形式”，用同一守恒能量下的参考 Jacobi 几何替代圆轨道锚定，从而在无圆轨道或圆轨道不在 Jacobi 域内时仍可定义有限距离弱偏折角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有做法以圆型类时轨道（circular timelike orbit）处原函数（primitive）取零来固定径向曲率原函数的加性常数，该锚定在无可用圆轨道、轨道超出 Riemann 型 Jacobi 域或无透镜比较几何非欧时失效，因此需要一种不依赖圆轨道的原函数表示。

Method: 采用与类时测地线对应的固定能量 Jacobi 度规（Jacobi metric），将径向曲率原函数视为仅定义到加性常数；改为减去同一守恒粒子能量下的参考 Jacobi 几何，并对曲率密度差施加端点归一化（endpoint normalization），同时通过局部源与接收方向保持偏折角的操作性有限距离定义。

Result: 证明在两者均适用时，参考归一化与圆轨道归一化方案给出相同结果，因为其原函数仅相差一个在精确曲率条带积分（curvature strip integral）中被抵消的常数；并在 Schwarzschild-like bumblebee、Kottler 与 Janis-Newman-Winicour（JNW）三类时空中演示了该形式。

Conclusion: 圆轨道锚定应被视为一种辅助性的原函数规范选择（primitive gauge choice），而非有限距离 Jacobi Gauss-Bonnet 偏折分析的前提条件。

Abstract: We develop a reference-renormalized Jacobi-primitive Gauss-Bonnet formalism for finite-distance weak deflection of massive particles in static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. The construction uses the fixed-energy Jacobi metric associated with timelike geodesics and treats the radial curvature primitive as a quantity defined only up to an additive constant. Instead of fixing this constant by requiring the primitive to vanish at a circular timelike orbit, we subtract a reference Jacobi geometry at the same conserved particle energy and impose endpoint normalization on the curvature-density discrepancy. This gives a circular-orbit-free primitive representation of the Gauss-Bonnet curvature contribution while preserving the operational finite-distance definition of the bending angle through local source and receiver directions. We prove that the reference-normalized and circular-orbit-normalized prescriptions agree whenever both are admissible, since their primitives differ only by a constant that cancels from the exact curvature strip integral. The method remains applicable when no admissible circular timelike orbit exists, when the orbit lies outside the Riemannian Jacobi domain, or when the natural no-lens comparison geometry is non-Euclidean. We demonstrate the formalism in three representative settings: Schwarzschild-like bumblebee, Kottler, and Janis-Newman-Winicour (JNW) spacetimes. The formalism therefore identifies circular-orbit anchoring as an auxiliary primitive gauge choice rather than a prerequisite for finite-distance Jacobi Gauss-Bonnet deflection.

</details>


### [109] [Effective Optical Geometry and Holographic Phase Structure of a Magnetic AdS Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03749)
*Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,Mohammad Reza Alipour,Saeed Noori Gashti,Jafar Sadeghi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维磁荷 AdS 黑洞在非线性电动力学下的圆型零轨道、热力学拓扑及全息热力学描述，采用 Duan phi-mapping 对轨道结构分类，并剖析局部稳定性、相共存与不同系综下的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景动机在于将黑洞的轨道结构与热力学拓扑、全息热力学性质联系起来，并检验非线性电动力学及有效光学度规对轨道条件的修正；同时厘清局部稳定性指标与平衡共存之间的区别。

Method: 采用 Duan 的 phi-mapping 构造（phi-mapping construction）对背景轨道结构进行分类，并引入含额外径向因子的有效光学度规（effective optical metric）以修正轨道条件；随后在正则、巨正则与混合系综（mixed ensemble）中分析自由能与局部稳定性指数。关键英文术语：热力学拓扑（thermodynamic topology）、全息（holographic）、非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）。

Result: 四个代表性构型中，五个孤立轨道的局部指数（local indices）不变，但半径最多偏移 5.187%；使用总质量时背景质量阈值在 sigma = 1/4 处平滑变化，而固定 m0 参数化在此处奇异，中心正则性要求 sigma > 1 且 m0 = 0。稳定 Gibbs 自由能分支仅在依赖模型的参数区间内相交；边界正则系综呈平均场指数（mean-field exponents）临界行为，巨正则系综存在 Hawking-Page 交叉，混合系综自由能分支有有限端点不连续，但所取竞争态不满足完整局部稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 结论是有效光学度规主要改变轨道半径而非局部拓扑指数，背景质量阈值的行为依赖于质量参数化方式，且热力学局部稳定性与平衡共存须区分对待；混合系综中采样竞争态未满足完整局部稳定性要求。

Abstract: We study circular null orbits, thermodynamic topology, and the holographic thermodynamic description of a four-dimensional magnetically charged AdS black hole in nonlinear electrodynamics. The background orbit structure is classified using Duan s phi-mapping construction and compared with the extraordinary electromagnetic characteristics. The latter defines an effective optical metric with an additional radial factor that modifies the orbit condition and introduces explicit dependence on the AdS radius. For four representative configurations, the local indices of the five isolated orbits are unchanged while their radii shift by up to 5.187 %. The background mass thresholds vary smoothly across sigma = 1/4 when the total mass is used, whereas the fixed-m0 parametrization is singular there. Regularity at the center requires sigma > 1 and m0 = 0. The thermodynamic analysis separates local stability indices from equilibrium coexistence: stable Gibbs-free-energy branches cross only in model-dependent parameter intervals. The boundary canonical ensemble exhibits critical behavior consistent with mean-field exponents, and the examined grand-canonical ensemble has a Hawking-Page crossing. In the mixed ensemble, the free-energy branches have a finite endpoint discontinuity, but the sampled competing states do not satisfy the full local-stability criterion

</details>


### [110] [Nonexistence of Multiple Black Holes in Stationary Analytic Vacuum spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03766)
*Yishen Huang,Junbin Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在稳态、渐近平直、$I^+$-正则的解析真空时空中，不存在非旋转且退化的视界分量，从而补全了稳态解析黑洞时空分类中的最后一个情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态解析黑洞时空的分类尚存最后一个未决情形，即非旋转且退化（non-rotating and degenerate）的视界分量是否可能存在。该问题一旦解决，即可完整确立黑洞唯一性猜想（black hole uniqueness conjecture）。

Method: 采用解析（analytic）方法，在稳态（stationary）、渐近平直（asymptotically flat）、$I^+$-正则（$I^+$-regular）真空时空的假设下，对视界分量的非存在性进行严格证明。

Result: 证明了非旋转且退化的视界分量在该时空类中不存在，由此排除分类中的最后一个未决情形。

Conclusion: 稳态、渐近平直、$I^+$-正则、解析真空黑洞的唯一性猜想（uniqueness conjecture）由此得到完全肯定的解决。

Abstract: We prove the nonexistence of non-rotating and degenerate horizon components in the stationary, asymptotically flat, $I^+$-regular, analytic vacuum spacetime. This result rules out the final case in the classification of stationary analytic black-hole spacetimes. Consequently, the stationary, asymptotically flat, $I^+$-regular, analytic vacuum black hole uniqueness conjecture is settled affirmatively in full.

</details>


### [111] [Bouncing and QNM convergence in Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03791)
*Paolo Arnaudo,Benjamin Withers*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证 Kerr 时空（Kerr spacetime）推迟格林函数（retarded Green's function）的准正规模（quasimnormal modes, QNM）表示，其收敛区域由复时间反弹奇点（complex-time bouncing singularities）决定，并给出解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续作者此前对 Schwarzschild 情形的研究，检验复时间反弹奇点控制收敛区域这一机制在旋转时空（Kerr）与带电时空（Reissner-Nordström）中是否成立。

Method: 以复时间反弹奇点为出发点，分析 QNM 表示的收敛性质；针对 Kerr 情形区分 prolate 与 oblate 两个 QNM 族，在 Boyer-Lindquist 坐标（Boyer-Lindquist coordinates）下推导反弹半径与相应收敛区域的显式解析表达式，并对 Reissner-Nordström 时空做收敛性分析。

Result: Kerr 时空的收敛区域由复时间反弹奇点设定，与 Schwarzschild 情形一致；主要差异在于 Kerr 存在 prolate 与 oblate 两个 QNM 族，其收敛区域不同，并给出二者的解析公式；同时得到 Reissner-Nordström 时空的收敛性分析结果。

Conclusion: 复时间反弹奇点决定 QNM 表示收敛区域这一图像可推广至 Kerr 与 Reissner-Nordström 时空，且时空参数（自旋、电荷）通过多族 QNM 结构影响收敛区域的具体形式。

Abstract: We show that the convergence region of the QNM representation of the retarded Green's function of the Kerr spacetime is set by complex-time bouncing singularities, as in our previous work for the Schwarzschild case. The main difference between Kerr and Schwarzschild is that there are two distinct QNM families, prolate and oblate, which have different convergence regions. We provide explicit analytical formulae for the bounce radii and corresponding convergence regions in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates. We also provide an analysis of convergence in the Reissner-Nordström spacetime.

</details>


### [112] [Electrically charged black holes with electroweak hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03800)
*Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦引力耦合电弱理论玻色子扇区中构造带电梯形黑洞，视界正则性给出与带电超辐射（charged superradiance）阈值对应的共振条件，并揭示两类看似不连通的玻色子星族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电弱理论（electroweak theory）中带电玻色场能否在黑洞视界外形成正则、指数局域化的云，从而产生"毛发"（hairy）黑洞解。

Method: 采用微扰分析与数值求解，限定静态、球对称、渐近平直解，弱混合角（weak mixing angle）取标准模型值；通过视界正则性导出共振条件，并考察非极端 Reissner-Nordström 背景上的微扰行为。

Result: 视界正则性要求带电矢量频率与视界处电磁势满足共振条件，即带电超辐射阈值；微扰分析表明非极端 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞不支持满足该条件的正则、指数局域、球对称电弱云；数值研究显示存在两个看似不连通的玻色子星（bosonic stars）族。

Conclusion: 毛发解不能由该电真空（electrovacuum）解族分岔产生；在所探索参数空间内，带毛黑洞与强引力一侧的玻色子星族相连。

Abstract: We construct electrically charged black holes in Einstein's gravity coupled to the bosonic sector of the electroweak theory, focusing on static, spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat solutions, with the weak mixing angle fixed to its Standard Model value. Horizon regularity imposes a resonance condition relating the charged-vector frequency to the electromagnetic potential at the horizon, corresponding to the threshold of charged superradiance. A perturbative analysis further shows that nonextremal Reissner-Nordström black holes do not support regular, exponentially localized, spherically symmetric electroweak clouds satisfying such condition. Thus, the hairy solutions cannot emerge via a bifurcation from this electrovacuum family. Moreover, our numerical investigation reveals two apparently disconnected families of bosonic stars. Within the explored parameter space, the hairy black holes are connected to the strongly gravitating one.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-domain spectral method for Kerr spacetimes: a hyperboloidal Teukolsky $m$-mode solver for self-force computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03870)
*Brad Cownden,Rodrigo Panosso Macedo,Christiana Pantelidou,Samuel D. Upton,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作求解Kerr黑洞背景下点质量圆轨道源的频域m模式Teukolsky方程，采用双曲切片与m模式穿刺/有效源方案、四区域伪谱法，并用Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit验证能量通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为求解自旋权重（spin-weight）s=-2的Weyl标量（Weyl scalar）ψ₄在Kerr黑洞点质量源下的辐射问题，需在无需人为施加辐射条件的前提下自然选取物理推迟解（retarded solution），并处理赤道面对称性被破坏的二维椭圆问题。

Method: 将频域m模式Teukolsky方程置于可达事件视界（event horizon）与未来零无穷远（future null infinity）的双曲切片（hyperboloidal slices）上，由正则性（regularity）选取推迟解；粒子用m模式穿刺/有效源方案（puncture/effective-source scheme）处理，二维椭圆问题用四区域伪谱法（pseudo-spectral method）求解，并通过场的偶/奇分解（even/odd decomposition）处理赤道对称性破缺。

Result: 对轨道半径r₀=10M、自旋（black hole spin）至|a|/M=0.9（含顺行与逆行轨道）进行测试：主模（dominant modes）的无穷远能量通量与Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit独立结果吻合至~10⁻⁷，m≤20的全部模式在中等分辨率下优于2×10⁻⁵；静态m=0模式无需特殊处理。

Conclusion: 所提频域方法在无需施加辐射条件的情况下正确选取物理推迟解，数值精度与独立工具高度一致，且自然涵盖静态m=0模式，适用于大自旋及顺/逆行轨道情形。

Abstract: We solve the frequency-domain, $m$-mode Teukolsky equation for the spin-weight $s=-2$ Weyl scalar $ψ_4$ sourced by a point mass on a circular, equatorial orbit around a Kerr black hole. The equation is posed on hyperboloidal slices that reach the event horizon and future null infinity, so that the physical (retarded) solution is selected by regularity alone, without imposing radiation conditions. The particle is treated with the $m$-mode puncture/effective-source scheme, and the resulting two-dimensional elliptic problem is solved with a four-domain pseudo-spectral method in which the equatorial symmetry is broken and handled by an even/odd decomposition of the field. We test the method for orbital radius $r_0=10M$ and spins up to $|a|/M=0.9$, covering prograde and retrograde orbits. The energy fluxes to infinity agree with independent Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit results to $\sim10^{-7}$ for the dominant modes and to better than $2\times10^{-5}$ for all modes up to $m=20$ at moderate resolutions. The static $m=0$ mode requires no special treatment.

</details>


### [114] [Kerr metric perturbations for second-order self-force theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03888)
*Benjamin Leather,Samuel D. Upton,Patrick Bourg,Kevin O. Cunningham,Sam R. Dolan,Conor Dyson,Zachary Nasipak,Nami Nishimura,Andrew Spiers,Maarten van de Meent,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文给出Kerr时空中点质量沿圆轨道运动所产生的线性（一阶）度规扰动的高精度计算，采用Teukolsky型可分方程与超曲面切片谱方法求解，并通过新颖的穿刺（puncture）技术解决近粒子处收敛缓慢问题，精度约10^{-10}/m-mode，可直接服务于二阶自力（second-order self-force）理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶自力理论对一阶度规扰动提出了严格要求：需高精度、包含黑洞质量与角动量扰动的全部贡献、在世界线附近为Lorenz规范而在大半径处为（近）Bondi–Sachs规范，并能在整个外部时空高效求值。现有计算难以同时满足这些条件。

Method: 从可分的Teukolsky型方程重构度规，按自旋加权球谐（spin-weighted spheroidal harmonic）模式分解，并在超曲面切片（hyperboloidal slices）上进行谱求解；针对点质量附近模式求和的缓慢收敛，采用穿刺（puncture）方法提取精确的m-mode数据。

Result: 所得(m-mode数据精度约10^{-10}，适用于二阶计算；度规扰动结果覆盖Kerr自旋-0.9 ≤ a/M ≤ 0.9及至50M的轨道半径，且在各区域分别满足Lorenz规范与（近）Bondi–Sachs规范要求。

Conclusion: 该形式体系可高效给出全外部时空的高精度一阶度规扰动，直接用于二阶自力计算，亦可作为环境效应（environmental effects）与准正规模（quasinormal modes）等其他Kerr度规扰动研究的输入。

Abstract: We present a calculation of the first-order metric perturbation arising from a point mass moving on a circular, equatorial orbit in Kerr spacetime. The metric perturbation is specifically aimed at meeting the requirements of second-order self-force theory: it is highly accurate; incorporates all contributions, including those associated with perturbations to the black hole's mass and angular momentum; is in Lorenz gauge near the worldline and in (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge at large radius; and can be efficiently evaluated across the entire exterior spacetime. The Lorenz gauge gives a well-understood singular structure at the particle, while the (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge ensures the asymptotic fall-off needed for an asymptotically flat second-order source. Our formalism reconstructs the metric from separable Teukolsky-type equations, decomposed into spin-weighted spheroidal harmonic modes and solved spectrally on hyperboloidal slices. The resulting $(\ell,m)$-mode sums converge rapidly across most of the domain, but slowly near the point mass; we overcome this with a novel use of a puncture to extract accurate $m$-mode data, yielding output accurate to $\sim 10^{-10}$ per $m$-mode and suitable for use at second order. Our metric-perturbation results cover a range of Kerr spins $-0.9 \le a/M \le 0.9$ and orbital radii out to $50M$. In addition to its immediate application in second-order self-force calculations, our results can also be used as input to other calculations involving Kerr metric perturbations, including studies of environmental effects and quasinormal modes.

</details>


### [115] [Quasi-Normal Modes of Covariant Loop Quantum Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03902)
*Chao Zhang,Sushant G. Ghosh,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究在静态球对称协变圈量子黑洞（covariant loop quantum black hole, cLQBH）背景上，用高斯局部化 holonomy 剖面构造标量有效势的局部形变，系统分析准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）谱与时间域响应，并提出“局部波形偏离时间”以界定可观测窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈量子引力（loop quantum gravity）的 holonomy 修正如何在黑洞扰动层面留下可检验印记尚不清楚，需要将局部化 holonomy 修正与 QNM 谱敏感性和波形演化建立联系，为引力波（gravitational-wave）可探测性评估提供基础。

Method: 采用唯象的高斯局部化 holonomy 剖面，在同一背景下一致地确定标量有效势的局部形变（含正、负两部分）与 tortoise 坐标映射，实现 bump effect 的几何实现；以频率域打靶法（shooting）与时间域演化为主，辅以矩阵铅笔法（matrix-pencil）模式提取与波形重构，并用位置、幅度、宽度三个参数刻画形变。

Result: QNM 谱随 bump 位置变化呈螺旋轨迹（spiral trajectories），高阶泛音（overtones）敏感性增强，并出现模式超越（mode overtaking）——由第一 Schwarzschild 泛音延续的分支比延续的基频分支更长寿。尽管谱结构显著改变，早期响应仍可接近 Schwarzschild 对应结果，差异在后期更明显；局部波形偏离时间对 bump 位置呈非单调依赖。

Conclusion: 引入局部波形偏离时间并与相对振幅暗淡时间比较，可定义操作性观测窗口（operational observation window）；适当参数下偏离可在暗淡阈值到达前显现。结果将局部 holonomy 修正与谱敏感性、波形演化相联系，为后续引力扰动研究与引力波可探测性评估奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) and time-domain response of a massless, minimally coupled test scalar field on static, spherically symmetric covariant loop quantum black hole (cLQBH) backgrounds. Focusing on a phenomenological, Gaussian-localized holonomy profile, we obtain a localized deformation of the scalar effective potential containing both positive and negative parts. This provides a geometric realization of the bump effect, with the potential deformation and tortoise-coordinate mapping determined consistently by the same background. We characterize the deformation by its location, amplitude, and width, and study its effects using frequency-domain shooting and time-domain evolution, supplemented by matrix-pencil mode extraction and waveform reconstruction. The QNM spectrum exhibits spiral trajectories as the bump location varies, enhanced sensitivity of the overtones, and mode overtaking, whereby the branch continued from the first Schwarzschild overtone becomes longer-lived than the continued fundamental branch. Despite substantial spectral changes, the early response can remain close to its Schwarzschild counterpart, with more pronounced differences emerging later. To quantify this behavior, we introduce a local waveform-deviation time and compare it with a relative-amplitude faintness time, thereby defining an operational observation window. The deviation time depends nonmonotonically on the bump location, and suitable parameter choices allow appreciable departures to emerge before the adopted faintness threshold is reached. These results connect localized holonomy corrections with spectral sensitivity and waveform evolution, providing a basis for future gravitational-perturbation studies and assessments of gravitational-wave detectability.

</details>


### [116] [Noisy pipelines II: Non-parametric Noise Inference for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03911)
*Charlie Hoy,Joseph Bayley,Ollie Burke,Laura K. Nuttall*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向 LISA 的非参数噪声推断方法，无需对天文台先验知识，即可从 7 天纯噪声模拟数据中估计功率谱与交叉谱密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 数据高度相关，噪声协方差矩阵在频率与数据流维度上不能假设为对角，导致传统噪声建模困难；噪声建模不佳会造成信号丢失、参数估计偏差与天体物理解释错误。

Method: 采用样条（splines）与逆洛伦兹函数（inverse Lorentzians）的组合对完整噪声协方差矩阵（noise covariance matrix）进行拟合，实现非参数（non-parametric）噪声推断。

Result: 基于 7 天纯噪声模拟数据，模型能够估计 LISA 的功率谱密度（power spectral densities）与交叉谱密度（cross spectral densities）；其中位数估计可将数据白化（whiten）至符合预期的单位正态分布。

Conclusion: 该非参数模型可纳入全局拟合（global fit），并与参数化方法结合，用于识别、量化与约束未知噪声伪迹（noise artifacts）的频率依赖。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to observe gravitational-wave signals from massive black hole binaries, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and more. Despite the significant scientific potential, accurately extracting these signals from the data will only be possible if LISA's instrumental noise is understood: poor noise modelling will result in lost signals, biased parameter estimates, and incorrect astrophysical insights. Modelling LISA's instrumental noise is more challenging than its ground-based counterpart since the data is highly correlated, meaning that the noise covariance matrix can no longer be assumed diagonal in frequency and in the different data streams. In this work, we present an initial approach to perform non-parametric noise inference for LISA that requires no prior knowledge of the LISA observatory. By fitting the full noise covariance matrix with a combination of splines and inverse Lorentzians, we show that our model can estimate the power and cross spectral densities for LISA from 7 days of simulated noise-only data. We find that the median estimate from our non-parametric model whitens the data according to the expected unit normal distribution. We envision that our non-parametric model can be used as part of the global fit, as well as in combination with modelled approaches to identify, quantify, and constrain the frequency dependence of unknown noise artifacts.

</details>


### [117] [Binary black hole mergers in massive Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03913)
*Harry L. H. Shum,Llibert Aresté Saló,Miguel Bezares,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作首次在带质量的 Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet（EsGB）引力中完成完全非线性的双黑洞并合模拟，并提出抑制由初始数据缺失导致的“垃圾辐射”的方案，从而揭示标量四极模在铃宕阶段由自旋-2污染频率向标量质量频率过渡的丰富结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EsGB 引力尚缺乏成熟的初始数据求解器（initial data solver），针对无质量标量场的传统变通方案在带质量标量场情形下会使垃圾辐射（junk radiation）被束缚并污染晚期动力学，因此需要新的处理手段以开展有质量情形下的并合研究。

Method: 对具有线性 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合函数的带质量 EsGB 引力进行完全非线性二体黑洞并合数值模拟；通过在模拟早期阶段适当修改运动方程（equations of motion）来压制垃圾辐射，并据此对标量与张量模式的铃宕（ringdown）频谱作初步分析。

Result: 传统无质量标量场变通方案产生的垃圾辐射会在有质量理论中残留于束缚区域并污染晚期演化；所提出的方程修正方案可有效抑制该辐射，使黑洞并合过程中的标量场动力学得以研究。四极标量模（quadrupole scalar mode）的铃宕呈丰富结构：早期由自旋-2“污染”频率主导，晚期形态则主要由标量质量频率（scalar mass frequency）决定。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在有质量 EsGB 理论中，垃圾辐射处理是开展可靠并合模拟的关键环节；标量四极模晚期由标量质量频率主导这一特征，为在铃宕信号中辨识带质量标量场的印记提供了可能途径。

Abstract: We study fully non-linear binary black hole mergers in massive Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (EsGB) gravity with a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling function. In the absence of a well-developed initial data solver for EsGB gravity, we demonstrate that the conventional workaround solution for massless scalar fields results in confined junk radiation that contaminates late-time dynamics in the massive theory. We propose a solution to this problem by suitably modifying the equations of motion at the early stages of the simulation to tame such junk radiation, thereby allowing us to study the dynamics of the scalar field during the process of a black hole merger. This is followed by a preliminary analysis of the frequency content in the ringdown of the scalar and tensor modes. In particular, the quadrupole scalar mode exhibits a rich ringdown structure: while it is dominated by the spin-2 ``contamination'' frequency in the early stages, its late time morphology becomes primarily determined by the scalar mass frequency.

</details>


### [118] [Multimessenger astrophysics drives the case for an Australian Gravitational-Wave observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04150)
*Fiona H. Panther,Paul D. Lasky,Nikhil Sarin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究评估澳大利亚引力波天文台（gravitational-wave observatory）在全球网络中对多信使天文学（multimessenger astronomy）的科学价值，指出其可使每年探测多达八个引力波-千新星（kilonova）多信使事件，且定位精度优于1–10平方度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代国际引力波观测网络（gravitational-wave observatories）正进入高级规划阶段，澳大利亚凭借其在引力波领域的长期贡献、平方公里阵列（Square Kilometer Array）等大型科学项目经验以及独特的地理位置与条件，具备承办观测台的理想条件；已有研究证明澳大利亚探测器能显著降低引力波事件的天区定位（sky localization）不确定性，因此有必要系统论证其科学价值。

Method: 研究聚焦一个具体科学案例，即澳大利亚天文台对多信使天文学（multimessenger astronomy）的潜在贡献，基于第二代与第三代全球探测网络（2G/3G networks）以及XG观测台网络的情景，定量评估多信使引力波加千新星事件的年探测数量与天区定位精度。

Result: 结果表明，澳大利亚天文台可使每年探测到多达八个多信使引力波加千新星事件，在第二代与第三代网络中定位精度均优于1–10平方度；在XG观测台组成的全球网络中，即使澳大利亚天文台仅具第二代灵敏度（2G sensitivity），仍能推动多信使科学取得显著进展。

Conclusion: 作者强调须在更广阔的背景中全面考量澳大利亚天文台的价值，但本工作表明其可为多信使天体物理（multimessenger astrophysics）作出实质性贡献。

Abstract: The next generation of an international network of gravitational-wave observatories is now in the advanced planning stages. With Australia's substantial history of contributions in this field, its history of scientific mega-projects like the Square Kilometer Array, and its unique geographic location and conditions, it is ideally placed to host an observatory in the global network. It has already been demonstrated that a gravitational wave detector in Australia plays an important role in minimizing the sky localization uncertainty of gravitational wave events. We study a particular science case for an Australian observatory, focusing on its potential contributions to multimessenger astronomy. We demonstrate that an observatory in Australia can enable the detection of up to eight multimessenger gravitational-wave plus kilonova events per year, with localisations better than $1-10°^2$ in both second- and third-generation networks. We show that, in a global network of XG observatories, an Australian observatory with 2G sensitivity would still enable significant advances in multimessenger science. The full case for an Australian observatory must be considered in a much broader context; however, this work indicates it could make substantial contributions to multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [119] [Imaginary Time as a Complex Representation of Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04372)
*William McConville*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文提出基于虚时间坐标 X^0 = ict 的洛伦兹相对论复表示框架，将这一替换视为复向量空间实子空间上的可逆表示变换，并系统证明几何、动力学与波动方程在该表示下保持常规内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上 X^0 = ict 的替换常被视为通往欧几里得时空的 Wick 旋转（Wick rotation），其几何地位模糊。论文旨在厘清虚时间坐标的几何角色，并揭示相对论几何、作用量、波传播与解析延拓之间的结构联系。

Method: 将物理时空视为复向量空间（complex vector space）的实子空间，把 X^0 = ict 表述为可逆的表示变换；引入复双线性型（complex-bilinear form）与厄米型（Hermitian form）两种度规结构，并将表示推广至固有时、四维动量、Hamilton-Jacobi 表述及弯曲时空中的度规、联络、曲率张量与爱因斯坦方程。

Result: 两种度规结构限制到物理时空时均还原为原始洛伦兹区间；在弯曲时空中，度规、联络、曲率张量、爱因斯坦方程与守恒律保持常规内容；对 Kerr 与 FLRW 几何的显式应用验证了旋转与含时时空下的一致性；相对论波方程及其非相对论极限（薛定谔方程与欧几里得时间演化）可在同一复时间框架内导出。

Conclusion: 该表述不引入新的物理预言，但澄清了虚时间坐标的几何作用，并揭示相对论几何、作用量、波传播与解析延拓（analytic continuation）之间的结构性关联。

Abstract: We develop a systematic complex representation of Lorentzian relativity based on the imaginary temporal coordinate $X^0=ict$. Rather than treating this substitution as a Wick rotation to Euclidean spacetime, we formulate it as an invertible change of representation in which physical spacetime is identified with a real subspace of a complex vector space. This construction introduces two related metric structures: a complex-bilinear form and a Hermitian form, both of which reproduce the original Lorentzian interval when restricted to physical spacetime. We extend the representation to relativistic dynamics, including proper time, four-momentum, and the Hamilton-Jacobi formulation, and show that the metric, connection, curvature tensors, Einstein equations, and conservation laws retain their conventional content in curved spacetime. Explicit applications to the Kerr and Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker geometries demonstrate consistency for rotating and time-dependent spacetimes. We further examine relativistic wave equations and their nonrelativistic limit, showing how the Schrödinger equation and its relation to Euclidean-time evolution emerge within the same complex temporal framework. The resulting formulation introduces no new physical predictions, but clarifies the geometric role of imaginary temporal coordinates and reveals structural connections among relativistic geometry, action, wave propagation, and analytic continuation.

</details>


### [120] [Single-horizon regular black holes and the fate of the inner-horizon instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04385)
*Chen Lan,Hao Yang,Maxim Khlopov,Maxim Krasnov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文证明正则黑洞（regular black holes, RBHs）的双视界结构并非正则性的必然结果，提出"视界宇称定理"（horizon-parity theorem），并构造两类具有有限面积核心的单视界正则黑洞，分析其物质内容、能量条件、热力学性质，并证明其模稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有正则黑洞常被构造为具有正则点状中心与两个视界（其中内视界为柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）），易受质量膨胀（mass inflation）影响；论文旨在检验双视界是否为正则性的必要条件，并寻求可规避内视界不稳定性的替代构型。

Method: 针对具有渐近平稳外部的静态球对称几何，建立视界宇称定理：正则点状中心要求 f 的变号零点数为偶数。据此构造两类有限面积核心的单视界正则黑洞（视界位于核心之外或与之重合），并分析其物质内容与能量条件，检验有效势非负性以证明标量扰动下的模稳定性（mode stability）。

Result: 双视界结构并非正则性的必然结果；所构造的两类单视界正则黑洞不含内柯西视界，从而在单视界区域内消除经典的质量膨胀机制；其有效势非负，具备对球对称标量扰动的模稳定性。

Conclusion: 中心几何与渐近结构共同约束视界重数；单视界构型可避免经典的内视界不稳定性，为构建无质量膨胀问题的正则黑洞提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) are often constructed with a regular point-like center and two horizons, including an inner Cauchy horizon susceptible to mass inflation. We show that a two-horizon structure is not a necessary consequence of regularity. For static, spherically symmetric geometries with an asymptotically static exterior, we establish a horizon-parity theorem: a regular point-like center requires an even number of sign-changing zeros of $f$. We then construct two classes of single-horizon RBHs with a finite-area core, distinguished by whether the horizon lies outside the core or coincides with it, and analyze their matter content, energy conditions, and thermodynamic properties. The absence of an inner Cauchy horizon eliminates the classical mass-inflation mechanism on the single-horizon patch. We further establish mode stability against scalar perturbations for these finite-area-core configurations by showing that the effective potential is non-negative. Our results establish how the central geometry and asymptotic structure constrain horizon multiplicity and identify single-horizon configurations that avoid the classical inner-horizon instability.

</details>


### [121] [Late-time echoes from planar wave-packet scattering by an ultracompact star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04525)
*Shanshan Deng,Jian-hua He*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作用时域多极模态求和法模拟超紧凑无事件视界恒星球对称时空中标量平面波的传播与散射，发现早期响应类似黑洞铃宕，晚期出现由内部稳定光子球俘获及外部势垒泄漏产生的延迟回波，其相干叠加形成复杂但具时间组织的波形，并证明第三代引力波探测器可分辨此类弱信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超紧凑无事件视界天体（horizonless ultracompact star）与黑洞的动力学响应是否可区分，是检验黑洞视界存在性与替代引力模型的关键问题；需考察其散射波形尤其是晚期信号特征。

Method: 采用时域多极模态求和法（time-domain multipole mode-sum method），模拟入射平面标量波（plane scalar wave）在恒定密度超紧凑星时空中的传播与散射，并重建相干叠加的时域波形（coherently superposed time-domain waveforms），与史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）响应对比；另以假定的应变归一化（strain normalization）做信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）分析。

Result: 早期响应因外部不稳定光子球（unstable photon sphere）附近散射而高度类似黑洞铃宕（ringdown）；晚期信号呈现源自内部稳定光球（stable light ring）俘获并经外部势垒（potential barrier）泄漏的序列延迟回波（delayed echoes）。单个多极呈规则回波，但相干叠加引致干涉，形成不规则复杂波形，仍保留交替峰间距的时间组织性。

Conclusion: 无事件视界恒星的晚期延迟回波相干叠加可产生复杂但有规律的波形结构，作为概念验证（proof of concept），在假设应变归一化下这些弱晚期信号可被第三代引力波探测器（third-generation gravitational-wave detectors）分辨，为区分黑洞与超紧凑天体提供潜在观测途径。

Abstract: We simulate the propagation and scattering of an incident plane scalar wave in the spacetime of an ultracompact, horizonless constant-density star using a time-domain multipole mode-sum method. By reconstructing the coherently superposed time-domain waveforms, we compare the star's dynamical response with that of a Schwarzschild black hole. We find that the early-time response closely mimics a black-hole ringdown due to wave scattering near the exterior unstable photon sphere. Conversely, the late-time signal develops a sequence of delayed echoes originating from wave trapping near the interior stable light ring and subsequent leakage through the exterior potential barrier. While individual multipoles exhibit regular echoes, their coherent superposition induces interference, producing complex waveforms with irregular profiles that nonetheless retain an underlying temporal organization with alternating peak separations. Finally, as a proof of concept, we perform a signal-to-noise ratio analysis using an assumed strain normalization, demonstrating that these weak, late-time signatures can be resolved by third-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [122] [On mass inflation and late-time divergences in limiting-curvature regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04618)
*Johanna Borissova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文分析非极端正则黑洞中质量暴涨的瞬态特征与晚期非指数发散，指出内视界处物理量发散需非物理质量参数越出正实域或需动力延拓，并以分数幂律标度筛选出广义Fan-Wang黑洞族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当Misner-Sharp质量对渐近质量m呈非线性依赖时，质量暴涨（mass inflation）可能仅为瞬态现象，随后出现模型依赖的非指数晚期发散，需厘清其机制与可行模型。

Method: 基于广义Ori模型（generalised Ori model），针对一类具de Sitter核心、且Misner-Sharp质量密度与Kretschmann标量存在与m无关上界的静态球对称渐近平坦正则黑洞，剖析内视界处物理量发散的几何机制，并施加分数幂律（fractional power-law）晚期发散形式。

Result: 内视界处物理量发散要求壳内质量参数演化出正实域（非物理），或需静态度规的另一类动力延拓；分数幂律晚期发散可在所考虑近似内调至任意温和，从而有效抑制质量暴涨。

Conclusion: 满足上述约束可唯一筛出一族广义Fan-Wang正则黑洞，表明温和的分数幂律晚期发散是非极端正则黑洞质量暴涨的可控归宿。

Abstract: Mass inflation in non-extremal regular black holes with a non-linear dependence of the Misner-Sharp mass $M$ on the asymptotic mass $m>0$ may be a transient phenomenon followed by a model-dependent non-exponential late-time divergence. We analyse these regimes based on the generalised Ori model for a class of static spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat regular black holes with a de Sitter core and an $m$-independent upper bound on the Misner-Sharp mass density and Kretschmann scalar inspired by quasi-topological gravity. We discuss the geometric mechanism behind divergences of physical quantities at the inner horizon of the perturbed spacetime, and show that these require the unphysical mass parameter inside the shell to evolve outside the positive-real domain, or in some cases force a distinct type of dynamical continuation of the static metric. Imposing an asymptotic late-time divergence in form of a fractional power-law scaling, tunable to be arbitrarily mild within the considered approximation and effectively taming mass inflation, allows us to single out a family of generalised Fan-Wang regular black holes.

</details>


### [123] [On Isaacson's Procedure in $f(R)$-Gravity with Second Order Metric Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04639)
*Petr V. Tretyakov,Alexander N. Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文考察在 f(R) 引力（f(R) gravity）中，度量张量（metric tensor）展开的二阶项对高频引力波（high frequency gravitational waves）平均能量-动量反作用（back-reaction）的贡献，发现在 de Sitter 背景下该贡献被强烈抑制，故先前结果不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作 arXiv:2504.02253 将经典 Isaacson 程序（Isaacson's procedure）推广至 de Sitter 背景下的 f(R) 引力，但仅采用度量张量的一阶分解，可能遗漏二阶项对反作用过程的贡献，需予以检验。

Method: 在 f(R) 引力、de Sitter 背景框架下，将度量张量分解扩展至包含二阶项，并计算其对高频引力波平均能量-动量反作用（即二阶效应）的可能贡献。

Result: 在 de Sitter 背景上，度量张量二阶项的所有可能贡献均受到强烈抑制（strongly suppressed）。

Conclusion: arXiv:2504.02253 中获得的结论在纳入二阶项后依然成立，先前结果无需修改。

Abstract: In the recent paper arXiv:2504.02253 we generalized classical Isaacson's procedure for describing back-reaction of the averaged energy-momentum for high frequency gravitational waves to $f(R)$ gravity case with a choice of the de Sitter background. To describe this second order effect we used standard first order decomposition of metric tensor. In this paper, we take into account possible contribution to this process from second order term in metric tensor decomposition. We find that on the de Sitter background all possible contributions from this term are strongly suppressed and therefore results obtained in the paper arXiv:2504.02253 are left unchanged.

</details>


### [124] [Final state of superradiant instability in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04646)
*Afsaneh Rahmani,Samaneh Oghbaiee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子（Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton）理论中，带电黑洞在特定频率区间会因无质量带电标量场（massless charged scalar field）产生超辐射不稳定性（superradiant instability），并在低于临界温度时形成带标量毛的黑洞相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在考察带电黑洞的不稳定性（instability），并说明在更一般的引力理论中标量毛（scalar hair）与标量化黑洞（scalarized black holes）如何产生。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子理论与无质量带电标量场，对耦合场方程（coupled field equations）进行数值积分（numerical integration），分析纯带电黑洞与带毛黑洞之间的相变。

Result: 当温度低于临界温度 T_C 时出现带毛相，高于 T_C 时保持纯带电状态；临界温度随黑洞尺度增大而升高，表明大视界下相变更难发生；系统在临界温度处进入稳定平衡，即超辐射不稳定性的最终态。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了非线性超辐射事件（nonlinear superradiant events）的演化方式，并为更完备引力理论中标量化黑洞的起源提供了有用信息。

Abstract: This article examines the instability of charged black holes, using Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory and a massless charged scalar field. Our study demonstrates that these black holes become unstable in a certain range of frequencies, which causes scalar "hair" to form outside the event horizon. By using numerical integration on the coupled field equations, we show that there is a phase transition between a purely charged black hole and a hairy black hole configuration. The results show that the hairy phase appears when the temperature is less than $T_C$, the critical temperature, whereas for $T>T_C$, the system stays purely charged. Also, our findings show that the critical temperature increases with the size of the black hole, implying that the onset of the phase transition becomes progressively more difficult for larger horizons. Lastly, we see that the system enters a stable equilibrium at the critical temperature, which is the final state of the superradiant instability. These results provide us with useful information on how nonlinear superradiant events change and how scalarized black holes arise in more comprehensive theories of gravity.

</details>


### [125] [A Unified AI Framework for Rapid and Accurate Waveform Generation of Compact Binary Coalescences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04713)
*Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Yuanhong Zhong,Jie Wu,Borui Wang,Xianghe Ma,Minghui Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个将物理建模与深度学习（deep learning）结合的波形生成统一框架，可统一产生双黑洞、双中子星及中子星-黑洞并合的引力波波形，失配度（mismatch）介于约5.89×10⁻⁴至5.34×10⁻²，批量生成最高加速约1800倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来引力波探测器（gravitational wave detectors）的发现能力取决于准确且快速的波形生成；第三代探测器预计将观测到大量长时程致密双星并合（compact binary coalescence）信号，可用于研究双星形成演化及极端条件下的物态性质。

Method: 构建统一框架，将物理建模与深度学习结合，采用解析残差谱表示（analytic residual spectral representation）、共享学习（shared learning）以及针对各双星类型的解码器（decoder）。

Result: 单一生成器覆盖双黑洞（binary black holes）、双中子星（binary neutron stars）与中子星-黑洞（neutron star black hole）双星系统；波形包含各参考模型描述的物理效应，如自旋进动（spin precession）、高阶模式（higher order modes）、物质效应（matter effects）与并合后辐射（postmerger radiation）；波形长度达数百至数千个周期，相对参考波形的平均失配度为5.89×10⁻⁴至5.34×10⁻²。

Conclusion: 批量生成相较参考波形生成实现最高约1800倍加速，表明该统一框架在保持物理完备性的同时显著提升波形生成效率，具备支撑第三代探测器观测需求的潜力。

Abstract: The astrophysical discovery potential of future gravitational wave detectors will hinge on accurate and rapid waveform generation. Third generation detectors are expected to observe many long duration compact binary coalescence signals that can probe binary formation and evolution as well as the nature of matter under extreme conditions. We develop a unified framework that combines physical modeling with deep learning. The framework uses an analytic residual spectral representation, shared learning, and decoders for each binary type. It generates waveforms for binary black holes, binary neutron stars, and neutron star black hole binaries, providing a single unified generator for all compact binary coalescences. The generated waveforms include the physical effects described by each reference model, such as spin precession, higher order modes, matter effects, and postmerger radiation for binary neutron stars. The waveforms span hundreds to thousands of cycles, with mean mismatches ranging from $5.89\times10^{-4}$ to $5.34\times10^{-2}$ relative to the reference waveforms. Batch generation achieves a maximum speedup of about 1800 over reference waveform generation.

</details>


### [126] [Particle dynamics in rotating charged Bumblebee black holes: An analytical correspondence with Kerr-Newman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04750)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了缓慢旋转带电爱因斯坦—大黄蜂（Einstein--Bumblebee）黑洞时空中无自旋与自旋试验粒子赤道动力学与有效Kerr--Newman时空之间的解析对应关系，并指出该对应在圆轨道半径与角速度层面成立，但在径向动力学与轨道不稳定性上带有洛伦兹破坏（Lorentz violation）依赖的标度修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清洛伦兹破坏背景下黑洞时空能否借助有效Kerr--Newman（Kerr--Newman）描述来刻画粒子轨道动力学，即明确该有效对应成立的范围与失效边界。

Method: 方法上先构造缓慢旋转带电Einstein--Bumblebee黑洞与有效Kerr--Newman时空之间的参数匹配；对自旋粒子在Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon框架下作对应分析，并比较两者的圆轨道半径、坐标角速度、径向动力学及局部不稳定性增长率。

Result: 结果表明，匹配参数下对应圆轨道具有相同半径与坐标角速度；而径向动力学与局部不稳定性速率呈现依赖洛伦兹破坏的标度关系。同时，轨道轨迹与每个方位角周期内的径向扰动增长一般不同，说明二者并非完全动力学等价。

Conclusion: 结论界定了有效Kerr--Newman对应的适用范围与局限：参数匹配可保留部分动力学量（圆轨道半径、坐标角速度），但由洛伦兹破坏引起的径向形变会修改轨道演化与扰动增长，因而不能视为完全等价。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an analytical correspondence between the equatorial dynamics of spinless and spinning test particles in a slowly rotating charged Einstein--Bumblebee black hole spacetime and those in an effective Kerr--Newman spacetime. For spinning particles, the correspondence is established within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon framework under appropriate parameter matching. We show that corresponding circular orbits have identical radii and coordinate angular velocities, while their radial dynamics and local instability rates exhibit a Lorentz-violation-dependent scaling. Nevertheless, the correspondence does not imply complete dynamical equivalence, as orbital trajectories and radial perturbation growth over an azimuthal period generally differ. These results delineate the scope and limitations of the effective Kerr--Newman correspondence by distinguishing the dynamical properties preserved under parameter matching from those modified by the Lorentz-violating radial deformation.

</details>


### [127] [On the Motion of a Point Electric Charge in the Field of Wu-Yang Monopoles in Bertotti-Robinson Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04816)
*Tekin Dereli,Yorgo Senikoglu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究带非最小 $RF^2$ 型曲率耦合的 $SU(2)$ Einstein-Yang-Mills 系统，证明 Wu-Yang 磁极在共形平坦的 Bertotti-Robinson 几何 $AdS_2\times S^2$ 上是任意耦合下的精确解，并将 Wu-Yang 场投影为均匀 Dirac 型磁场后给出点电荷运动的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察 Yang-Mills 场与曲率的非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）如何影响已知的规范场精确解及其几何背景，并探讨此类解在探测电荷动力学上的可观测含义。

Method: 采用外微分形式（exterior differential forms）表述，并对 Levi-Civita 联络引入拉格朗日乘子约束（Lagrange-multiplier constraint）；在此基础上求解 $SU(2)$ Einstein-Yang-Mills 方程，并利用规范群流形的 Hopf 纤维化（Hopf fibration）$S^3/S^2\cong S^1$ 将 Wu-Yang 场投影为均匀 Dirac 型磁场，进而解析求解点电荷运动。

Result: 共形平坦（conformally flat）的 Bertotti-Robinson 几何 $AdS_2\times S^2$ 以 Wu-Yang 磁极为精确解，且对任意非最小耦合均成立，耦合仅重整化（renormalise）$S^2$ 的半径；文中同时回顾了该系统的非共形平坦解分支。在投影得到的均匀 Dirac 型磁场中，点电荷沿锥面运动，并在视界（horizon）与最大半径之间振荡，解以闭式给出。

Conclusion: 非最小 $RF^2$ 型耦合不破坏 Wu-Yang 磁极解的存在性，仅改变球面半径这一几何参数；该解通过 Hopf 纤维化联系到均匀磁场中的可积电荷运动，为研究强引力/规范场背景下的精确解与粒子动力学提供了可解析处理的范例。

Abstract: We study the $SU(2)$ Einstein-Yang-Mills system with non-minimal $RF^2$-type couplings of Yang-Mills fields to curvature, formulated with exterior differential forms and a Lagrange-multiplier constraint for the Levi-Civita connection. We show that the conformally flat Bertotti-Robinson geometry $AdS_2\times S^2$ carries the Wu-Yang magnetic pole as an exact solution for arbitrary non-minimal couplings; the couplings only renormalise the radius of the $S^2$. The non-conformally-flat branch of solutions known for this system is recalled. The Hopf fibration $S^3/S^2\cong S^1$ of the gauge group manifold projects the Wu-Yang field on a uniform Dirac-type magnetic field, and the motion of a point electric charge in it is solved in closed form: the charge moves on a cone and oscillates between the horizon and a maximal radius.

</details>


### [128] [Mixed-Order Parity-Violating Corrections to Rotating Black Holes in Combined Scalar Gauss-Bonnet and Dynamical Chern-Simons Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04945)
*Abhishek Rout,Brett Altschul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在标量高斯-博内（scalar Gauss-Bonnet）与动力学陈-西蒙斯（dynamical Chern-Simons）联合有效场论中，构造旋转黑洞（rotating black holes）的领头阶混合形变（mixed-order deformation），并用伪谱法（pseudospectral）求解耦合的标量与度规方程，给出视界与赤道测地线可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多分别处理宇称偶（parity-even）与宇称奇（parity-odd）修正，缺乏二者同时作用时（混合扇区）旋转黑洞解及其可观测量的系统刻画，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 在固定质量与角动量、自旋范围 0.1≤a≤0.9 内，采用伪谱法数值求解标量与度规的耦合方程，计算视界角速度、表面引力、面积与 Wald 熵，以及最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）、光子环半径与光子环程函（eikonal）估计的铃宕（ringdown）量，并核验 Kerr/Schwarzschild 极限与纯扇区基准。

Result: 混合解满足场方程全部分量（包括构造中未施加的那一个），Wald 熵以约 10^-5 精度满足第一定律；混合系数随自旋的依赖性与各单独扇区定性不同，四个视界量及若干轨道与程函量随自旋发生符号变化。

Conclusion: 混合宇称扇区在旋转黑洞中引入区别于纯扇区的自旋依赖效应，可作为该有效场论截断阶下的可检验预言；但铃宕结果仅为领头光子环程函估计，完整耦合有限 ℓ 微扰计算留待未来工作。

Abstract: We construct the leading mixed-order $\mathcal{O}(ζ_{\rm GB}ζ_{\rm CS})$ deformation of rotating black holes in a combined scalar Gauss-Bonnet and dynamical Chern-Simons effective theory, the sector in which the parity-even and parity-odd corrections act simultaneously. Using a pseudospectral solution of the coupled mixed scalar and metric equations, we compute horizon and equatorial-geodesic observables--the horizon angular velocity, surface gravity, area, and Wald entropy; the innermost stable circular orbit and photon-ring radii; and photon-ring eikonal estimates of the ringdown--at fixed mass and angular momentum over the spin range $0.1\le a\le0.9$, where the solution is numerically convergent and the perturbative expansion is controlled for sufficiently small dimensionless couplings. We verify the exact Kerr and Schwarzschild limits, reproduce available pure-sector benchmarks, and quantify spectral, residual, and extraction uncertainties. The mixed solution satisfies every component of the field equations, including the one that is not imposed in its construction, and its Wald entropy satisfies the first law to about one part in $10^{5}$. The mixed coefficients exhibit spin dependence qualitatively distinct from the individual sectors within this EFT truncation. All four horizon quantities, and several orbital and eikonal ones, change sign as functions of spin. The ringdown results are leading photon-ring eikonal estimates; a full coupled finite-$\ell$ perturbation calculation is left to future work.

</details>


### [129] [Scale-Dependent Weak-Field Dynamics in Horndeski Theory: Galaxy Rotation Curves and Turnaround Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05089)
*Hatice Özer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Horndeski引力中与引力波兼容子类的弱场动力学，在星系与星系际尺度上分析标量导数非线性相互作用对引力的尺度相关修正，得到旋转速度修正、动力学质量与透镜质量间的引力滑移，以及由有效吸引与大尺度曲率平衡导出的转折尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Horndeski引力中与引力波兼容的子类，需明确其在星系及星系际尺度上的弱场（weak-field）行为，特别是非线性标量导数相互作用（nonlinear scalar derivative interactions）所导致的尺度相关引力修正。

Method: 在弱场近似下处理静止且近似球对称的位形（stationary, approximately spherically symmetric configurations），保留引起尺度相关修正的非线性标量导数相互作用；星系尺度上通过有效势（effective potential）、圆轨道稳定性、星系旋转曲线（galaxy rotation curves）及动力学质量与透镜质量关系进行分析，星系际尺度上分析渐近弱场动力学。

Result: 标量场修正轨道动力学与旋转速度的径向依赖，并产生非零的引力滑移（gravitational slip），使动力学质量与透镜质量估计出现差异；星系际尺度上导出转折尺度（turnaround scale），其源于有效引力吸引与大尺度曲率贡献的平衡，远距离处渐近引力场由背景曲率贡献主导。

Conclusion: 该Horndeski子类在保持引力波兼容性的同时，可在星系尺度产生可观测的动力学与透镜效应差异，并在大尺度上以背景曲率主导渐近引力场，提示其弱场现象学具有尺度依赖性。

Abstract: In this work, the weak-field dynamics of a gravitational-wave compatible subclass of Horndeski gravity is investigated on galactic and intergalactic scales. The analysis is performed within the weak-field approximation for stationary and approximately spherically symmetric configurations, retaining the nonlinear scalar derivative interactions responsible for scale-dependent modifications of the gravitational field. On galactic scales, the transition regime is investigated through the effective potential, the stability of circular orbits, galaxy rotation curves, and the relation between dynamical and lensing masses. The analysis shows that the scalar field modifies the orbital dynamics and the radial dependence of the rotational velocity, while generating a nonvanishing gravitational slip between the dynamical and lensing mass estimates. On intergalactic scales, the asymptotic weak-field dynamics is investigated and a turnaround scale is derived from the balance between the effective gravitational attraction and the large-scale curvature contribution. At large distances, the asymptotic gravitational field is governed by the background curvature contribution.

</details>


### [130] [Gravitational-wave lensing beyond the diffraction integral: proper-time methods and the bound lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05152)
*Ginevra Braga,Raul Jimenez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文用固有时（worldline）表示系统梳理引力波（GW）透镜的波光学（wave optics）基础，说明衍射积分（diffraction integral）的成立条件，并将其推广到束缚透镜（bound lens）情形。结果表明，点质量透镜的放大因子源于库仑势（Coulomb potential）的精确传播子（propagator），而非各项近似；束缚透镜的时域信号需为每条世界线赋予各自的推迟时间（retarded time）与多普勒因子（Doppler factor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波波长可与透镜的史瓦西半径（Schwarzschild radius）相当，且探测器记录相位，因此透镜效应须用波光学处理；而标准工具衍射积分依赖多个近似，需要说明这些近似何时失效、能否被更精确的表述取代。

Method: 采用固有时表示，引入辅助固有时使波动方程精确变为一阶；借助 Duru-Kleinert（DK）规范与 Kustaanheimo-Stiefel 映射得到点质量的精确传播子（propagator），再由精确积分的驻相性导出几何光学与近轴薄透镜下的衍射积分；随后把该方法用于束缚透镜，即绕透镜运行的引力波源。

Result: 远场观察者下 DK 时间与纤维角对应透镜平面的半径与方位角，所得结果即点透镜放大因子（amplification factor），其偏移由超出路径决定；点质量情形的吻合反映的是库仑势而非近似，精确程函条件（eikonal condition）在近透镜和大角度处失效，单向步骤属于非相对论极限。束缚透镜的精确时域信号中无单一放大因子可描述；在衍射区，仅用 Shapiro 相位会遗漏其后的全部透镜效应，几何光学在 w=0.3 合（conjunction）附近遗漏 46%–100%，近轴几何在远侧失效，沿轨道的静态放大因子误差约为 (1–2)wM/R，其余位置则为回波振幅量级。

Conclusion: 固有时表示为衍射积分提供了自洽的推导基础，并揭示其有效范围的来源；对束缚透镜，静态放大因子不足以描述信号，须采用包含逐条世界线推迟时间与多普勒因子的精确时域处理。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) can have wavelengths comparable to the Schwarzschild radius of their lenses, and detectors record their phase, so lensing requires wave optics. Its standard tool, the diffraction integral, follows from the particle path integral of a scalar wave on a static weak field, under the eikonal (one-way) and paraxial approximations, plus the thin-lens limit. We give a self-contained account of how the proper-time (worldline) representation overcomes these steps. An auxiliary proper time makes the wave equation exactly first order, with frequency as $1/\hbar$, so geometric optics and, for paraxial thin-lens paths, the diffraction integral follow from stationarity of an exact integral. For a point mass, the Duru-Kleinert (DK) gauge and Kustaanheimo-Stiefel map give the exact propagator; for a distant observer the DK time and fibre angle become the radius and azimuth of a lens plane, the result is the point-lens amplification factor with the offset set by the excess path, and geometric optics requires $w\gg1$ and $wy\gg1$. This agreement reflects the Coulomb potential, not the approximations: the exact eikonal condition, $\varepsilon_E\simeq\barλ/d+GM/c^2d+\mathcal O(\sin^2θ/2)$, fails near the lens and at large angles, and the one-way step is a non-relativistic limit. We then treat the bound lens, a GW source orbiting its lens, which emits a backscattered echo and moves during the lensing delays. Its exact time-domain signal gives each worldline its own retarded time and Doppler factor, so no single amplification factor describes it.
