In the diffraction regime the Shapiro phase alone misses all lensing beyond it, geometric optics misses 46-100% of it near conjunction at $w=0.3$, the paraxial geometry fails on the far side, and the static amplification factor along the orbit errs by $\approx(1-2)\,wM/R$ near conjunction and by the echo amplitude elsewhere.

</details>


### [131] [Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05201)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Tieguang Zi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用精确分离的引力电磁方程（gravitoelectromagnetic equations）研究 Kerr–Newman 黑洞准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）的激发因子（excitation factors）与本征点（exceptional points, EPs），共识别出 42 个模对合并的本征点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清自旋（spin）与电荷（charge）如何塑造不同模式的相对激发强度，以及非厄米谱（non-Hermitian spectrum）中模式合并的图样。

Method: 基于精确分离的引力电磁方程，在固定电荷条件下计算 Kerr–Newman 准正规模的激发因子，并搜寻引力主导（gravitational-led）与电磁主导（electromagnetic-led）两个扇区中模对合并的本征点。

Result: 引力主导模式的激发因子幅度随自旋增大而减小，电磁主导模式则呈单调与非单调两类行为；第一泛音（first overtone）的激发因子幅度可与基模（fundamental mode）相当甚至更大。共确认 42 个本征点，部分构成涉及相邻泛音对的序列，高阶泛音对在更接近极端极限（extremal limit）处合并；序列之外的其余本征点与序列成员具有相同的模对标签，但出现在不同的黑洞自旋与电荷值处。

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明了旋转与电荷对 Kerr–Newman 黑洞各模式相对激发强度及模式合并图样的作用。

Abstract: We study excitation factors and investigate exceptional points (EPs) of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes using the exact separated gravitoelectromagnetic equations. At fixed charge, the magnitudes of the excitation factors for the gravitational-led modes studied here decrease with increasing spin, whereas those for electromagnetic-led modes exhibit both monotonic and nonmonotonic behavior. The magnitude of the excitation factor for the first overtone can be comparable to or larger than that for the fundamental mode. We identify 42 EPs at which pairs of modes coalesce in the gravitational-led and electromagnetic-led sectors. Some EPs form sequences involving successive overtone pairs, with higher-overtone pairs coalescing progressively closer to the extremal limit. The additional EPs identified outside these sequences all share the same mode-pair labels as sequence members, but occur at different values of the black hole spin and charge. These findings clarify how rotation and charge shape the relative excitation strengths of different modes and the pattern of mode coalescence in the non-Hermitian spectrum of Kerr--Newman black holes.

</details>


### [132] [Full-Stokes response to a stochastic gravitational-wave background in pulsar timing arrays: Fully analytic overlap reduction functions at finite distance and coherence of close pulsar pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05219)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文给出了广义相对论下脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）对anisotropic tensor引力波背景full-Stokes响应的完全解析overlap reduction function，保留脉冲星距离与完整传播相位，并系统分析nulls、相干性、偏振协方差与误差预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTA overlap reduction function多基于短波长近似或忽略脉冲星项与距离信息，难以刻画有限距离、各向异性张量背景下的full-Stokes响应，故需构建含完整传播相位的解析框架。

Method: 在各固定天球多极上解析推导响应函数：强度与圆偏振响应化为指数积分与对数组成的有限组合，电/磁线偏振仅需初等函数，计算至ℓ=8；并用均匀近似连接长波与短波极限、Gaussian differential averaging与独立天球积分基准检验。

Result: 对称性区分精确null与可被传播抬升的Earth-term null；非偏振背景亦可产生imaginary cross-spectrum；当2πfd/c≲1时脉冲星项可保持相干。共距误差从对齐对相位中消去，相对深度误差直接影响相干性；固定低多极范数与全多极平方和随响应向高多极转移而以不同速率衰减。

Conclusion: 解析表达式经matched-accuracy与天空积分基准验证；偏振协方差界与相对深度噪声预算给出差分抑制背景的条件，为PTA距离精度、带宽与timing fits的作用提供定量刻画。

Abstract: We derive fully analytic overlap reduction functions for the full-Stokes response of a pulsar timing array to an anisotropic tensor gravitational-wave background in general relativity. The response retains both pulsar distances and their complete propagation phases. At each fixed finite sky multipole, the intensity and circular polarization responses reduce to finite combinations involving exponential integrals and logarithms, while the electric and magnetic linear polarization responses require only elementary functions. We evaluate all components through $\ell=8$. Symmetry separates exact nulls from additional Earth-term nulls that propagation can lift. Even an unpolarized background can produce an imaginary cross-spectrum. For a pair with three-dimensional separation $d$, the pulsar terms can remain coherent when $2πfd/c\lesssim1$, even if both pulsars are many wavelengths from Earth. A uniform approximation retaining all four Earth and pulsar terms connects this regime to the short-wavelength limit, and a rotationally invariant relation fixes the leading transverse magnetic response. Common-distance errors cancel from the aligned pair phase, while relative-depth errors directly affect coherence. Under Gaussian differential averaging, fixed low-multipole norms and the squared norm summed over all multipoles decay at different rates as the response shifts to higher multipoles. Published pulsar configurations illustrate the roles of distance accuracy, bandwidth, and timing fits. At fixed pointwise intensity, a polarization covariance bound and a relative-depth noise budget quantify when differencing suppresses the background. Independent sky integration and matched-accuracy benchmarks test the analytic expressions over the parameter ranges studied.

</details>


### [133] [Dirac stars in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05240)
*Salvatore Capozziello,Allah Ditta,M. Yousaf,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究带电经典旋量场与引力及四阶截断Euler-Heisenberg（EH）非线性电动力学耦合的自引力解，将场方程化为无量纲本征值问题并数值求解，发现EH四阶项在固定费米子数下使星体更致密、更紧束缚并降低最大质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作熟知广义相对论中的Dirac星（Dirac stars），但带电旋量场与非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）耦合的自引力构型尚缺乏系统研究，尤其EH四阶项对星体质量与稳定性的影响不明确。

Method: 考虑U(1)规范化的带电旋量场（可含四阶自相互作用），耦合引力与四阶截断的Euler-Heisenberg非线性电动力学；对静态球对称构型推导场方程，约化为关于旋量频率的无量纲本征值问题（eigenvalue problem）并数值求解。

Result: 无电荷极限下解退化为已知Dirac星；电荷使频率、质量与尺度增大，最大质量由0.709增至1.027（单位M_Pl^2/μ，电荷耦合0→0.75）；四阶项削弱星内电场。固定费米子数时四阶项对质量改变小于0.2%，但使星体更致密、更紧束缚并移动转折点，在电荷耦合0.6时使最大质量由0.924降至0.840（下降9%，EH耦合0→200）。

Conclusion: 弱场QED极限下相对质量修正为−1.5×10^-5(E_max/E_c)^2，故较大耦合下四阶模型应视为有效非线性电动力学而非受控QED修正；所有解满足零、弱、强与主能量条件（energy conditions），具正压强各向异性且无事件视界，稳定性通过束缚能与质量曲线转折点讨论。

Abstract: We study self-gravitating configurations of a charged classical spinor field coupled to gravity and to Euler-Heisenberg (EH) nonlinear electrodynamics, truncated at quartic order. The spinor is gauged under a U(1) field and may carry a quartic self-interaction. For static and spherically symmetric configurations we derive the field equations, reduce them to a dimensionless eigenvalue problem for the spinor frequency and solve it numerically. In the uncharged limit our solutions coincide with the known Dirac stars of general relativity. The electric charge increases the frequency, the mass and the size of the stars, and the maximal mass grows from $0.709$ to $1.027$ (in units of $M_{\rm Pl}^2/μ$) when the charge coupling rises from zero to $0.75$. The quartic term changes the relation between the electric field and the enclosed charge, so that the field inside the star is weaker than in the Maxwell case. Our central result concerns stars with the same number of fermions, and therefore the same total charge. For them the quartic term changes the mass by less than $0.2\%$, but it makes the stars more compact and more strongly bound and shifts the turning point of the family to a smaller particle number. This shift is what lowers the maximal mass, by $9\%$ (from $0.924$ to $0.840$) at charge coupling $0.6$ as the dimensionless EH coupling goes from zero to $200$. In the weak-field regime of quantum electrodynamics (QED) the relative mass correction is $-1.5\times10^{-5}(E_{\max}/E_c)^2$, so the larger couplings describe the quartic model as an effective nonlinear electrodynamics rather than a controlled QED correction. All solutions satisfy the null, weak, strong and dominant energy conditions, show a positive pressure anisotropy, and remain free of horizons. Their stability is discussed through the binding energy and the turning point of the mass curve.

</details>


### [134] [Kerr metric perturbations in Lorenz gauge via numerical relativity methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05259)
*Nami Nishimura,Nils L. Vu,Adrià Gispert-Fernández,Cillian Kelly,Lawrence E. Kidder,Benjamin Leather,Thomas Osburn,Jonathan E. Thompson,Samuel D. Upton,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于数值相对论（numerical relativity）方法的计算框架，在频域中按方位角 m 模求解 Lorenz 规范的克尔（Kerr）度规微扰方程，首次实现自旋至 Thorne 极限（|a|/M=0.998）的圆赤道轨道一阶引力自力（gravitational self-force）计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述小质量天体在旋转（Kerr）黑洞时空中的运动需要引力自力计算，这对预测非对称质量致密双星的引力波信号至关重要；LISA 任务要求至少达到质量比的二阶微扰，而受爱因斯坦方程非线性复杂性限制，此前二阶计算仅限无自旋（Schwarzschild）黑洞。

Method: 将微扰 Lorenz 规范爱因斯坦方程以频域中的方位角 m 模表述，得到椭圆型偏微分方程（elliptic partial differential equations），并采用谱间断伽辽金离散（spectral discontinuous Galerkin discretization）、自适应网格加密（adaptive mesh refinement）与预条件迭代求解器（preconditioned iterative solvers）求解。

Result: 对 Kerr 时空中圆赤道轨道（circular equatorial orbits），实现指数收敛（exponentially convergent）的一阶引力自力计算，自旋覆盖至 |a|/M=0.998 的 Thorne 极限，并将此前标量自力（scalar self-force）结果推广至完整广义相对论。

Conclusion: 该框架可为非线性源项构造提供精确且行为良好的（well-behaved）一阶数据，并可作为二阶度规微扰本身的求解器，从而设计上可延拓至二阶计算。

Abstract: Gravitational self-force calculations are needed to accurately describe the motion of a small body in a spinning (Kerr) black hole spacetime, which is vital to predict gravitational wave signals from asymmetric-mass compact binaries. Accurate calculations for the upcoming LISA mission require a minimum of second perturbative order in the mass ratio, which has thus far only been achieved for nonspinning (Schwarzschild) black holes due to nonlinear complexities of the Einstein equations. In this work, we calculate Kerr metric perturbations with a new computational approach based on numerical relativity methods, extending recent scalar self-force results to full general relativity. We formulate the perturbative Lorenz-gauge Einstein equations in terms of azimuthal $m$-modes in the frequency domain and solve the resulting elliptic partial differential equations using a spectral discontinuous Galerkin discretization, adaptive mesh refinement, and preconditioned iterative solvers. With our new method, we achieve exponentially convergent first-order gravitational self-force calculations for circular equatorial orbits in Kerr spacetime up to spin $|a|/M=0.998$ (the Thorne limit). Our framework is designed to carry over to second order by delivering accurate and well-behaved first-order data for nonlinear source construction, and as a solver for the second-order metric perturbations themselves.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Restricted triangular ice beyond the solvable manifolds: chiral ice and the critical region of dominant chiral vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03760)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究三角晶格（triangular lattice）上 Baxter 对称二十顶点模型（symmetric twenty-vertex model）的两种限制——手性冰（chiral ice）与仅排除两个交替顶点的模型——并借助转移矩阵（transfer matrix）与共形不变性（conformal invariance）修正，确认二者均处于临界态、长程行为由无质量自由场（free massless field）描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baxter 二十顶点模型虽有精确解，但其权重空间的子区域（尤其被认为存在有序相的区域）尚缺乏系统刻画；作者希望厘清两种自然限制是否属于已知可积族，并检验所提出的有序相边界是否真实存在。

Method: 先对两种限制作可解性归类，并将手性冰刻画为每个顶点恰有一条贯通线（through line）的冰模型，由此导出每条闭合晶格线上的宇称约束（parity constraint）；随后在周长至多十二个顶点的圆柱上计算转移矩阵，用适配三角几何、并在两个精确可解点验证过的共形不变性有限尺寸修正（finite-size correction）外推残余熵（residual entropy）；再利用向上箭头数目的守恒性及其改变所需代价判定临界性。

Result: 两种模型均不属于二十顶点模型任何已知精确可解族；残余熵由有限尺寸外推得到。穿越圆柱的向上箭头数守恒，改变它的代价表明两模型均为临界，长程行为对应无质量自由场。手性冰是曾被建议存在有序的区域相图的极端点，而穿过该区域的两条路径上各点均呈同一临界行为，在所提出的区域边界处没有特征信号；沿 Baxter 精确可解曲线给出了相应指数（exponent）的闭式表达。

Conclusion: 结果表明两种限制模型都是临界的，且所推测的有序相区域及其边界未获得数值支持；手性冰作为该区域极端点同样呈临界行为，提示原相图图像需要修正。

Abstract: We study two restrictions of Baxter's symmetric twenty-vertex model on the triangular lattice, in which the three classes of vertices allowed by the ice rule carry the weights $a$, $b$ and $c$: chiral ice, in which only the twelve chiral vertices are allowed, and the model that excludes only the two alternating vertices. We show that neither model belongs to any of the known exactly solvable families of the twenty-vertex model, and we characterise chiral ice as the ice model in which every vertex has exactly one through line, a property that imposes a parity constraint along every closed lattice line. We compute the residual entropies of both models with transfer matrices on cylinders of up to twelve vertices in circumference, extrapolated with the finite-size correction predicted by conformal invariance, which we adapt to the triangular geometry and verify at two exactly solved points. The number of upward arrows crossing the cylinder is conserved, and the cost of changing it shows that both models are critical, with the long-distance behaviour of a free massless field. Chiral ice is the extreme point of a region of the phase diagram in which ordering had been suggested; along two paths through that region we find the same critical behaviour at every point, with no feature at the proposed boundary of the region. Along Baxter's exactly solved curve we obtain the corresponding exponents in closed form.

</details>


### [136] [The Inverse Mean First-Passage Time Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03831)
*Tommer D. Keidar,Shlomi Reuveni,Igor M. Sokolov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维过阻尼粒子平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time）的逆问题：给定 τ(x)，反求能实现该时间的势能景观，并给出其可实现性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教科书通常由给定势能求解首次通过时间，本文转向其逆问题（inverse problem），即从观测或设计的 τ(x) 反推所需势能，以明确哪些时间依赖形式在物理上可被一维势能实现。

Method: 基于一维过阻尼（overdamped）动力学下的平均首次通过时间 τ(x) 表达式，推导其能被某势能实现的充要条件，并分析幂律依赖 τ(x)∼x^α 与单调势（monotonic potential）及势阱（potential well）位置之间的关系。

Result: 结果显示，α≤2 的幂律依赖可由单调势实现；任何超二次增长（superquadratic growth）的可实现 τ(x) 都需要势阱位于距目标任意远处；α=2 区分了滑动（sliding）与俘获主导（trapping-dominated）两类运动机制。

Conclusion: 平均首次通过时间作为起始距离的函数并非任意可实现，其可实现性受势能结构严格约束，α=2 构成滑动与俘获主导运动机制的分界。

Abstract: We consider the mean first-passage time $τ(x)$ for an overdamped particle in a one-dimensional potential landscape, and address the problem inverse to the textbook one: Given $τ(x)$ as a function of starting distance, we look for a potential that realizes it. We derive the conditions on $τ(x)$ under which such a potential exists. We show that while the power-law dependences $τ(x)\sim x^α$ with $α\leq 2$ may be realized by monotonic potentials, any realizable $τ(x)$ with superquadratic growth requires potential wells arbitrarily far from the target; $α= 2$ separates sliding from trapping-dominated motion regimes.

</details>


### [137] [Lattice spin systems coupled to a compact U(1) gauge field with negative gauge coupling favoring frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03837)
*Claudio Bonati,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作通过蒙特卡洛模拟与有限尺寸标度分析，研究负规范耦合下三维紧致格点阿贝尔希格斯模型的相图与相变性质，发现比非负耦合时显著更丰富的相结构，包括一阶相变线及属于Z2与XY规范普适类的连续（拓扑）相变线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负规范耦合（negative gauge coupling）倾向于产生阻挫（frustrated）的元格（plaquette）构型，与物质场铁磁型相互作用抑制阻挫的趋势相竞争，而该竞争导致的相结构与相变性质此前缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 针对单位电荷的单分量与两分量复标量场耦合紧致U(1)规范场的三维格点模型，采用蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）与有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling analysis），并结合能量累积量标度（energy-cumulant scaling）确定临界指数与相变性质。

Result: 两分量情形下，与规范不变双线性序（gauge-invariant bilinear ordering）相关的O(3)相变线延伸至负耦合区，并随K减小转为向完全阻挫K→−∞极限过渡的一阶相变线；单分量模型亦出现类似一阶相变线。此外，两类模型在大物质耦合下各有两条连续相变线，其能量累积量标度与三维Ising及XY临界指数一致，暗示分别属于Z2与XY规范普适类（gauge universality classes）的拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 结果表明物质有序（matter ordering）与动力学规范阻挫（dynamical gauge frustration）之间的竞争会生成额外的相，这些相的物理刻画仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram and nature of the phase transitions of three-dimensional compact lattice Abelian Higgs models with negative gauge coupling. These models describe multicomponent complex scalar fields coupled to a compact U(1) gauge field. Negative gauge coupling favors frustrated plaquette configurations, competing with the tendency of the matter ferromagnetic-like interaction to suppress frustration. Monte Carlo simulations and finite-size scaling analyses of models with one- and two-component scalar fields of unit charge reveal a substantially richer phase structure than at nonnegative gauge coupling. For two components, the O(3) transition line associated with gauge-invariant bilinear ordering extends into the negative-coupling region, and turns into a first-order transition line with decreasing $K$, toward the fully-frustrated $K\to -\infty$ limit. An analogous first-order transition line is also found for the one-component scalar matter with negative gauge coupling. Both one- and two-component models exhibit two additional continuous transition lines at sufficiently large matter coupling. Their energy-cumulant scaling is consistent with three-dimensional Ising and XY critical exponents, suggesting topological transitions in the $\mathbb Z_2$ and XY gauge universality classes, respectively. These results uncover additional phases generated by the competition between matter ordering and dynamical gauge frustration, whose physical characterization remains an open question.

</details>


### [138] [Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz for the Toda chain from inverse scattering transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03857)
*Alberto Brollo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文直接从Toda链的逆散射变换（inverse scattering transform）出发，推导其热力学Bethe拟设（Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz, TBA）方程，从而在经典场论中首次一致地纳入孤子（solitons）、呼吸子（breathers）、辐射（dispersive modes）及其相互相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang-Yang发展的TBA给出可变的自由能泛函并奠定广义流体力学（Generalized Hydrodynamics）框架，近来已成功应用于可积经典粒子系统，但尚未用于经典场论：此前缺乏一致处理孤子、呼吸子、辐射模式及其相互作用的经典推导。

Method: 作者从Toda链的逆散射变换（inverse scattering transform）直接推导其TBA方程，并借此获得孤子模式与辐射相互作用的描述，扩展了孤子气体（soliton gas）图像。

Result: 所导出的方程显示孤子模式与辐射相互作用，并通过对已有的粒子模式TBA方程进行数值对比验证了其正确性。

Conclusion: 该推导流程预期可推广至KdV及纯辐射模型（如defocusing NLS与Sinh-Gordon），同时论文讨论了在focusing NLS（及Ablowitz-Ladik）与Sine-Gordon等其他模型中当前存在的局限。

Abstract: The Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz (TBA) procedure developed by Yang-Yang provides the free energy functional to be minimized via a variational problem. Their result not only gives the correct thermodynamics, but it also sets the framework for Generalized Hydrodynamics. Originally developed for quantum systems, this procedure has been recently successfully applied to (integrable) classical particle systems. However, this has never been done for classical field theories: no classical derivation has consistently accounted for solitons, breathers, radiation (dispersive modes), and their mutual interactions. We here derive the TBA equations for the Toda chain directly from its inverse scattering transform. This results in solitonic modes interacting with radiation, extending the soliton gas picture. We provide numerical verification of our equations against well established TBA equations in terms of particle modes. We expect the same procedure should apply to KdV, and pure radiative models as defocusing NLS and Sinh-Gordon, while we discuss current limitations in other models such as focusing NLS (and Ablowitz-Ladik) and Sine-Gordon.

</details>


### [139] [Maximum entropy explanation for the ubiquity of power-law mechanical responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03999)
*Sverre Holm*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文指出幂律弛豫（power-law relaxation）满足最大熵准则（maximum entropy criterion），并据此预测其有效频率范围随幂律指数增大而减小，且与分数阶标准线性固体（fractional standard linear solid）及拉伸指数（stretched exponential）行为相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂律弛豫普遍出现于具有大量相互作用指数响应（exponential responses）的复杂力学系统中，其内在组织原理尚不明确，论文旨在为这一普遍现象提供基于信息约束的解释框架。

Method: 将幂律响应表述为在部分知识（partial knowledge）条件下对各个体贡献进行调控的最大熵准则问题，并借助其与分数阶标准线性固体的密切关系进行分析。

Result: 由最大熵准则导出一个预测：幂律响应有效性的频率范围（frequency range of validity）随幂律指数（power-law exponent）的增大而减小；该性质在某些生物材料（biological materials）中得到验证。

Conclusion: 最大熵准则可作为统一描述幂律弛豫的判据，并通过与分数阶标准线性固体的联系，将同样普遍的拉伸指数行为（stretched exponential behavior）纳入同一框架。

Abstract: The ubiquitous power-law relaxation emerges from complex mechanical systems with a vast number of interacting exponential responses. Here we demonstrate that power-law responses satisfy a maximum entropy criterion, regulating the individual contributions under partial knowledge. This leads to a prediction that frequency range of validity decreases with power-law exponent, a property which is confirmed for some biological materials. The close relation with the fractional standard linear solid also leads to an incorporation of the equally ubiquitous stretched exponential behavior.

</details>


### [140] [Capillary Coarsening Drives Re-entrant Statistical Transitions in Early Coffee-Ring Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04388)
*Arundas Chandradasan,Dileep Mampallil*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 蒸发接触线处的颗粒沉积被用作协同一维沉积（cooperative one-dimensional deposition）的实验实现，沉积后毛细吸引（capillary attraction）驱动颗粒迁移、成团与粗化，使团簇宽度分布（cluster-width distribution）出现从指数分布到Gamma分布再回到指数分布的重入转变（re-entrant transition），并伴随涨落标度（fluctuation scaling）指数κ由1变至1/2再回到1的非单调交叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡系统（nonequilibrium systems）在不同统计区间之间如何演化，仍是统计物理（statistical physics）中的基础性难题；需要一个简单、可控的实验模型来揭示局部相互作用如何生成统计行为的转变。

Method: 以蒸发接触线（evaporating contact line）上的颗粒沉积作为协同一维沉积的实验平台，考察沉积后毛细吸引驱动的颗粒迁移、成团与粗化过程，测量团簇宽度分布及其涨落标度关系σ_w ~ ⟨w⟩^κ，并用蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）进行对照。

Result: 随沉积推进，团簇宽度分布呈现显著的重入转变：由初始的指数形式（exponential form）演化为有峰的Gamma分布（Gamma distribution），随后又回到指数形式；涨落标度指数κ依次由1变为1/2、再回到1，表现出非单调交叠（non-monotonic crossover）。蒙特卡洛模拟重现了上述统计转变。

Conclusion: 蒸发接触线沉积可作为相关一维组装（correlated one-dimensional assembly）的简单模型，表明局部相互作用足以在非平衡系统中产生重入式统计行为。

Abstract: Understanding how nonequilibrium systems evolve between distinct statistical regimes remains a fundamental challenge in statistical physics. Here we show that particle deposition at an evaporating contact line provides an experimental realization of cooperative one-dimensional deposition, in which post-deposition capillary attraction drives particles to migrate, cluster, and coarsen. As deposition proceeds, the cluster-width distribution undergoes a striking re-entrant transition, evolving from an initial exponential form to a peaked Gamma distribution and subsequently returning to an exponential form. This dynamic transition is accompanied by a non-monotonic crossover in fluctuation scaling, $σ_w\sim\langle w\rangle^κ$, with $κ$ changing successively from $1$ to $1/2$ and back to $1$. Monte Carlo simulations reproduce these statistical transitions. Our results establish evaporative contact-line deposition as a simple model of correlated one-dimensional assembly and reveal how local interactions can produce re-entrant statistical behaviour in a nonequilibrium system.

</details>


### [141] [Crossover duality for the quantum long-range Ising chain: perturbation theory around the short-range endpoint and a DMRG test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05284)
*Junchen Rong*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出长程横场 Ising 链（long-range transverse-field Ising chain）在短程交叉点 s*=7/4 附近的弱耦合描述，通过 σχ 耦合将 Ising 共形场论与瞬时高斯场结合，导出 z、1/ν、ω 的展开式并用 DMRG 进行数值检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程横场 Ising 链在 s*=7/4 附近存在短程交叉（short-range crossover），该区域缺乏可控的弱耦合理论描述，需要一种能同时刻画静态与动力学临界行为的框架。

Method: 将 Ising 共形场论（conformal field theory）经 σχ 项耦合到瞬时高斯场（instantaneous Gaussian field），把经典长程 Ising 对偶（long-range Ising duality）推广到量子链；等时相互作用产生应力张量形变（stress-tensor deformation），使速度跑动并重整化动力学临界指数 z。采用共形微扰论（conformal perturbation theory）并在最长 128 个格点的周期链上做 DMRG 计算进行一致性检验。

Result: 微扰论给出 z=1-0.720δ+O(δ²)、1/ν=(1+z)/2+O(δ²)、ω=δ+O(δ²)，其中 δ=s*-s；DMRG 以理论给出的 1/ν 与 ω 作为输入，在考虑缓慢有限尺寸修正后，z 的静态与动力学估计一致。

Conclusion: 数值结果支持 s=1.5 处的交叉展开，并支持在 s=1、1.25、1.5 处采用双侧 Padé 插值（two-sided Padé interpolation）。

Abstract: We propose a weakly coupled description of the long-range transverse-field Ising chain near its short-range crossover at $s_*=7/4$, where the couplings decay as $r^{-1-s}$. The Ising conformal field theory is coupled to an instantaneous Gaussian field through $σχ$, extending the classical long-range Ising duality to quantum chains. The equal-time interaction generates a stress-tensor deformation that causes the velocity to run and renormalizes the dynamical critical exponent $z$. Conformal perturbation theory gives $z=1-0.720\,δ+O(δ^2)$, $1/ν=(1+z)/2+O(δ^2)$, and $ω=δ+O(δ^2)$, with $δ=s_*-s$. DMRG calculations on periodic chains of up to $128$ sites provide a consistency test using theoretical inputs for $1/ν$ and $ω$. After accounting for slow finite-size corrections, static and dynamical estimates of $z$ agree, supporting the crossover expansion at $s=1.5$ and a two-sided Padé interpolation at $s=1$, $1.25$, and $1.5$.

</details>


### [142] [Critical amplitudes of the Baxter-Wu model from MCPA and Wolff simulations: free-exponent and fixed-exponent fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04733)
*Vyacheslav Mozolenko,Ian Pile,Lev Shchur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文以二维 Baxter-Wu 模型（Baxter-Wu model）的精确振幅为基准，比较两种模拟算法与两种临界振幅提取方法；结果表明固定指数并显式引入解析修正项的拟合策略可显著消除漂移偏差，并据此给出一致性良好的磁化率振幅与普适振幅比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 Baxter-Wu 模型属于四态 Potts 模型（four-state Potts model）普适类，其能量、比热、磁化与极化振幅均有精确表达式，为检验模拟算法与临界振幅提取方法提供了理想基准；同时标度修正项（corrections to scaling）导致的慢衰减可能会系统性污染振幅估计。

Method: 采用微正则群体退火（microcanonical population annealing, MCPA）与无子格冻结的 Wolff 算法（sublattice-freezing Wolff algorithm）在相近尺寸格点上进行模拟，并对比两种临界振幅提取途径：一是在自动识别的平台区进行自由指数拟合，二是将指数固定为精确值并显式计入解析修正级数（以约化温度立方根展开）。

Result: 两种算法给出的能量与序参量估计高度一致；按精确 Joyce 级数，局域有效指数（local effective exponent）并不稳定为常数，而是单调漂移，源自修正项在约化温度立方根下展开、领头项相对量级为 |t|^{2/3}。自由指数拟合会将该漂移吸收进指数估计，导致振幅出现显著偏差；固定指数并计入修正项的拟合则与已知精确振幅高度吻合，由此得到的磁化率振幅与普适振幅比（universal amplitude ratios）与形状因子（form-factor）计算预言相符，并数值确定了极化的领头修正系数。

Conclusion: 临界振幅的可靠提取依赖于对慢衰减标度修正的显式处理；固定指数并引入解析修正级数优于平台区自由拟合，可为缺乏精确解的模型提供可信的振幅与普适振幅比估计，并补充了极化修正系数等定量结果。

Abstract: The two-dimensional Baxter-Wu model belongs to the universality class of the four-state Potts model. Exact expressions are available for its energy, specific heat, magnetization, and polarization amplitudes. We use these results as a benchmark for evaluating two simulation algorithms and two approaches to extracting critical amplitudes. Microcanonical population annealing (MCPA) and the sublattice-freezing Wolff algorithm yield highly consistent estimates for the energy and order parameters when applied to lattices of comparable size. According to the exact Joyce series, the local effective exponent does not stabilize at a constant value but instead undergoes a monotonic drift. This behaviour stems from corrections to scaling, which can be expressed as a series in the cube root of the reduced temperature. As a result, these corrections decay relatively slowly, with the leading term being of relative order $|t|^{2/3}$. When performing a fit with a free exponent on an automatically identified plateau, this drift gets incorporated into the estimated exponent, introducing a substantial bias in the derived amplitudes. In contrast, a fit that fixes the exponent to its exact value and explicitly accounts for the analytic correction series delivers a close match to the known exact amplitudes. Using this refined fitting approach, we obtain estimates for the susceptibility amplitudes and the universal amplitude ratios. These values are in good agreement with predictions from form-factor calculations. Additionally, we numerically determine the leading correction coefficient for the polarization.

</details>


### [143] [Stochastic synthesis and degradation of arbitrary diffusive processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04843)
*Denis Boyer,Gabriel Mercado-Vásquez*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文推导了恒定速率随机合成与降解（SSD）下非相互作用粒子的密度分布与首次通过性质，并推广至爆发式合成情形，进而分析最优合成率及与重置（resetting）过程的联系与区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标搜索常由随机引入与移除的扩散元素完成（如细胞内蛋白质的随机合成与降解），导致搜索者数目随时间波动，需刻画此类多搜索者过程的时空分布与首次通过特性。

Method: 在给定源位置与单个非降解粒子的任意传播子（propagator）条件下，解析推导随机合成与降解（SSD）粒子的密度分布；由单粒子首次通过时间分布推出多搜索者首次通过性质，并在小速率与大速率极限下分析特征反应时间与平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time）；进一步推广至爆发式合成（bursty synthesis），并应用于一维布朗搜索。

Result: 计入合成成本后存在最优合成率；爆发式合成情形下，低合成率或大降解率区间平均首次通过时间可显著缩短，约为按平均爆发规模（mean burst size）折算的结果；并指出SSD与重置过程在排队协议（queueing protocol）上的联系与差异。

Conclusion: SSD框架可定量刻画波动搜索者群体的密度与首次通过行为，其与重置过程存在联系但对应不同的排队协议，为细胞生物学等场景中的目标搜索提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Target search processes are often carried out by diffusive elements that are introduced and removed at random times, causing fluctuations in the number of searchers in the system over time. For instance, proteins are synthesized and degraded stochastically in the cell, and susceptible to react with specific binding sites during their lifetime. Here we derive the density profile generated by non-interacting particles subject to stochastic synthesis and degradation (SSD) at constant rates, given a source location and an arbitrary propagator for the single, non-degrading particle. We also study the first-passage properties of these many-searcher processes, which can be deduced from the knowledge of the first-passage time distribution of a single particle. We analyse the behaviours of the characteristic reaction time and of the mean first-passage time in the limits of small and large rates. Taking into account the costs associated with synthesis leads to the existence of an optimal synthesis rate. We further generalise these results to bursty synthesis, when production events involve more than one particle at a time, as often observed in cell biology. The mean first-passage time in these situations can be significantly reduced at low synthesis or large degradation rates, as it is divided by the mean burst size in these regimes. The results presented here are applied to one-dimensional Brownian searches. Some general connections between SSD and resetting processes are pointed out as well as important differences, since SSD is associated with a distinct queueing protocol.

</details>


### [144] [Real-Space renormalization for the drawups and drawdowns of stochastic trajectories: universality classes for diffusion processes and random walks via the scale function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04853)
*Cecile Monthus*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究具有任意扩散系数与Ito力的1维扩散过程在尺度Γ下重整化轨迹的统计性质，证明普适类由决定区间逃逸概率的尺度函数s(x)唯一确定，并将框架推广至格点上的离散时间与连续时间随机游走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画扩散轨迹中保留大幅涨落极值点后的重整化统计规律，并厘清哪些微观细节会影响该统计、哪些不影响，从而建立扩散过程的普适类划分。

Method: 采用实空间重整化（real-space RG）方法，即强无序重整化群（Strong-Disorder-RG）思想的推广：在Γ与Γ+dΓ之间消去幅度为Γ的拉升与回撤（drawups/drawdowns），只保留极小与极大交替序列；随后将框架适配到1维格点上的离散时间与连续时间随机游走（任意最近邻跳跃概率或速率）。

Result: 主要结论是普适类由尺度函数（scale function）s(x)决定，该函数满足 D(x)s''(x)+F_I(x)s'(x)=0 并支配区间逃逸概率；凡共享同一s(x)的扩散过程，即使微观轨迹完全不同，重整化轨迹的统计也相同。文中以Cauchy、Student、Bessel、Fisher-Snedecor、几何布朗运动等例子说明了整条直线与半直线上最简单的普适类，涵盖有稳态与无稳态的情形。

Conclusion: 重整化轨迹的统计由尺度函数而非扩散系数与力的具体形式决定，因而不同微观机制的扩散可归入同一普适类；该结论可自然延伸至格点随机游走的离散与连续时间表述。

Abstract: For one-dimensional diffusion processes with arbitrary diffusion coefficient $D(x)$ and Ito force $F_I(x)$, we analyze the statistical properties of the renormalized trajectories at scale $Γ$, where one keeps only the alternating sequence of local minima and local maxima that are separated by drawups and drawdowns of amplitude bigger than $Γ$. The elementary RG-step between $Γ$ and $Γ+d Γ$ thus amounts to eliminate the drawups and drawdowns of amplitude $Γ$, a real-space-RG-procedure that is well-known for Brownian trajectories in the context of the Strong-Disorder-RG approach applied to the disorder landscape of random models. The main result is that the universality classes are determined by the scale function $s(x)$ satisfying $D(x) s''(x)+F_I(x) s'(x)=0$ that governs the exit probabilities from an interval : all the diffusions sharing the same scale function $s(x)$ have the same statistics of renormalized trajectories, even if their initial microscopic trajectories are completely different. The simplest universality classes for diffusions on the whole line $x \in ]-\infty,+\infty[$ and on the half-line $x \in ]0,+\infty[$ are illustrated with several examples of processes, with or without steady states: Cauchy, Student, Bessel, Fisher-Snedecor, Geometric Brownian. This framework concerning diffusion processes is then adapted to discrete-time and to continuous-time random walks on the one-dimensional lattice with arbitrary nearest-neighbor jump probabilities or rates.

</details>


### [145] [Universality in Anomalous Diffusion: Power Laws and Faster-Decaying Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05258)
*Claudio Giberti,Alessandro Vezzani,Raffaella Burioni,Lamberto Rondoni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文用渐近理论刻画强反常扩散（strong anomalous diffusion），给出谱指数 θ、ζ 与交叉矩 q_c 之间的超标度关系（hyper-scaling relations），并论证幂律尾部既是该现象的充分条件也是必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强反常扩散通常由位移矩的分段线性谱描述，其小矩与大矩斜率 θ、ζ 及斜率改变的交叉矩 q_c 各自物理含义尚未被统一关联，中间尺度行为缺乏解析刻画。

Method: 采用渐近理论（asymptotic theory）匹配中间尺度的行为，推导约束条件与超标度关系，并分析有限时间修正、提出基于分布的稳健估计方法；对三个典型模型做数值与解析验证。

Result: 所得约束解释了代数尾部（algebraic tails）如何导致强反常扩散，并将 q_c 与相应幂律指数关联；给出矩的渐近幂律行为前阶修正的显式表达及 θ、ζ、q_c 的估计方法，结论得到三个模型支持。

Conclusion: 幂律尾部是该情形的必要条件：高斯或快速衰减主体加指数稀有弹道偏移（ballistic excursions）只给出单线性谱且第二分支随观测时间漂移；若主体快速衰减而尾部为幂律，强反常扩散仍存在但超标度关系被破坏。

Abstract: Strong anomalous diffusion is often characterized by a piecewise-linear spectrum of the moments of displacement. The spectrum is characterized by the slopes $θ$ and $ζ$ for small and large moments, respectively, and by the crossover moment $q_c$ at which the slope changes. The exponents $θ$ and $ζ$ characterize the asymptotic scaling of the bulk and of the tails of the probability distribution function of displacements, respectively. Here, we adopt asymptotic theory to match the behaviors at intermediate scales. The resulting constraint explains how distributions with algebraic tails imply strong anomalous diffusion, and it relates $q_c$ to the corresponding power law. Our theory provides universal hyper-scaling relations between the exponents characterizing strong anomalous diffusion, and it yields explicit expressions for the leading-order corrections to the asymptotic power-law behavior of the moments of displacement. We also characterize finite-time corrections and propose a robust distribution-based method to estimate $θ$, $ζ$, and $q_c$. The findings are supported by numerical and analytical results for three paradigmatic models exhibiting strong anomalous diffusion. Finally, we show that power-law tails are not only sufficient but also necessary for this scenario: with a Gaussian, or generally fast-decaying, bulk and exponentially rare ballistic excursions, the spectrum of moments is single-linear, and the crossover to the apparent second branch drifts with the observation time. When the bulk is fast decaying and the tail is a power law, strong anomalous diffusion is still present, but the hyper-scaling relations among the exponents are violated.

</details>


### [146] [Exact Rokhsar-Kivelson points hosting first-order phase transitions in $S=1$ compass models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05326)
*Jay Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在正方与kagome晶格上求解由角度θ参数化的S=1罗盘模型（compass model），在区间π/4≤θ≤3π/4内给出精确基态流形，并揭示端点处的RK点、守恒的ℤ₂线荷及简并结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究键依赖的双线性与双二次相互作用（bilinear and biquadratic interactions）在S=1罗盘模型中的基态性质，并确定其精确可解点与相图结构。

Method: 对相容几何，利用端点处格点幺正变换（product of on-site unitaries）将哈密顿量映射为构型图（configuration graph）𝒢的拉普拉斯量，在S=1笛卡尔基（Cartesian basis）下分析；并结合守恒的ℤ₂线荷（ℤ₂ line charges）论证简并度。

Result: 两个端点均为精确的Rokhsar-Kivelson（RK）点，每个动力学连通分量对应一个等幅叠加基态；模型具有m=O(L)个独立且相互对易的守恒ℤ₂线荷，导致基态简并至少为2^m。在内部区间π/4<θ<3π/4，基态流形由两个被哈密顿量湮灭的乘积态（product states）张成，且每个RK端点处存在进入该内部相的一阶相变（first-order transition）。

Conclusion: S=1罗盘模型在RK端点精确可解并具高度简并，内部区域则表现为两个乘积态张成的长程有序相，端点与内部相之间以一阶相变连接。

Abstract: We study $S=1$ compass models on the square and kagome lattices with bond-dependent bilinear and biquadratic interactions parametrized by an angle $θ$. For compatible geometries, we determine the exact ground-state manifolds throughout the interval $π/4\leqθ\leq3π/4$. At each endpoint, a product of on-site unitaries maps the Hamiltonian to the Laplacian of a configuration graph $\mathcal{G}$ in the $S=1$ Cartesian basis. These endpoints are exact Rokhsar-Kivelson (RK) points, with one ground state per dynamically connected component of $\mathcal{G}$. Each such state is an equal-amplitude superposition in the transformed basis and acquires configuration-dependent phases upon transforming back to the original basis. Both models possess $m=O(L)$ independent and mutually commuting conserved $\mathbb{Z}_2$ line charges, where $L$ is the linear system size, forcing a ground-state degeneracy of at least $2^m$ at the RK endpoints. Throughout the interior region, $π/4<θ<3π/4$, the ground-state manifold on each lattice is instead spanned by two product states, both annihilated by the Hamiltonian. On both lattices, each RK endpoint also hosts a first-order transition into this intervening interior phase spanned by the two product states with long-range order.

</details>


### [147] [Higher-order interactions renormalize the thermodynamic signature of neural population codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05336)
*Narendra N. Hegade*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文对同一视网膜神经群体的两体与三阶最大熵模型做了精确热力学计算，发现由留出似然（held-out likelihood）所要求的三阶项会显著移动比热峰（specific-heat peak）的位置，且方向取决于记录的时间分辨率下脉冲计数（spike count）结构而非物种；因此神经编码的热力学临界性特征取决于读取数据所用模型的相互作用阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵模型可视为虚构温度（fictitious temperature）T 下的玻尔兹曼分布，已有研究以比热峰随群体规模增长并趋于 T=1 作为临界性（criticality）证据，但这些论证基于两体模型（pairwise models），而视网膜编码需要高阶交互（higher-order interactions）；带此类交互、逐细胞拟合的模型的热力学尚未被计算。

Method: 对蝾螈（salamander）与狨猴（marmoset）最多 20 个视网膜神经节细胞（retinal ganglion cells）的同一群体，分别精确计算两体与三阶（third-order）模型的热力学量，并对峰的移动进行精确分解，辅以仅约束脉冲计数分布的模型、第二次记录、重新分箱（re-binning）与共同输入替代（common-input surrogate）检验。

Result: 三阶项造成的峰移动超过有限样本偏差（finite-sample bias），且两种视网膜中符号相反：蝾螈峰更低、增长更慢，狨猴峰更高、增长更快，但两者峰位都更接近 T=1。精确分解显示大部分移动源于群体脉冲计数上的能量方差（energy variance）；仅约束脉冲计数分布即可在蝾螈中完全复现、在狨猴中复现过半。

Conclusion: 第二次记录、重新分箱与共同输入替代均表明峰移动的符号由记录在其时间分辨率下的脉冲计数结构决定，而非物种差异；神经编码的热力学特征并非仅由数据决定，还由用于解读数据的模型的相互作用阶数决定。

Abstract: Maximum-entropy models of neural recordings can be treated as Boltzmann distributions at a fictitious temperature $T$, with the fitted model at $T=1$. Their specific-heat peak grows with population size and approaches that temperature, which has been read as a sign of criticality. The case rests on pairwise models, yet retinal codes require higher-order interactions. The thermodynamics of a model with such interactions fitted cell by cell has not been computed. Here I compute it exactly for pairwise and third-order models of the same populations of up to $20$ retinal ganglion cells, in salamander and marmoset. The triplet terms, required by the held-out likelihood, shift the peak by more than the finite-sample bias, with opposite sign in the two retinas. The peak is lower and grows more slowly in salamander, higher and grows faster in marmoset, and lies closer to $T=1$ in both. An exact decomposition traces most of the shift to the energy variance across population spike counts. A constraint on the spike-count distribution alone reproduces all of it in salamander and over half in marmoset. A second recording of each species, re-binning, and a common-input surrogate show that the sign is set by the spike-count structure of a recording at its temporal resolution, not by species. The thermodynamic signature of a neural code is therefore set not by the data alone but by the interaction order of the model used to read it.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum Error Correction in Nearly Critical Toric Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05375)
*Zack Weinstein,Samuel J. Garratt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究横向场环面码（transverse-field toric code, TFTC）在偏离稳定子极限、逼近量子相变时的量子纠错解码；证明对"裸"稳定子的投影测量配合基于Monte Carlo的最优测量后解码器，在比特翻转与相位翻转退相干下均具有有限阈值，且相位翻转阈值在整个拓扑相内与横向场强度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑码通常在其稳定子极限下研究，但实际情形中系统可能被形变并推向进入平庸相的量子相变点；此时横向场引起基态中 m 任意子（anyon）的强烈量子涨落，稳定子测量是否仍能支撑有效解码与有限阈值尚不明确。

Method: 聚焦 TFTC 基态流形中存储的量子信息，考虑独立的比特翻转（bit-flip）与相位翻转（phase-flip）退相干。利用 TFTC 哈密顿量的 stoquastic 性质，将其基态描述为经典格点规范理论（classical lattice gauge theory）；先证明稳定子测量在无退相干基态上近似幺正作用，再通过受约束格点规范理论的 Monte Carlo 模拟高效实现最优测量后解码器（optimal postmeasurement decoder）。

Result: 数值上展示在接近量子临界点处仍存在有限阈值；给出理论证据（非严格证明）表明稳定子测量在该设定下构成最优解码策略；对相位翻转退相干进一步证明最优测量后解码阈值在整个拓扑相内与横向场强度无关。

Conclusion: 尽管横向场导致任意子强涨落，"裸"环面码稳定子的投影测量仍可用于构造高效解码算法并保持有限纠错阈值，即使横向场被调至极靠近量子临界点；stoquastic 性是解码器构造与可计算性的关键。

Abstract: We investigate quantum error correction protocols for topological codes deformed away from their stabilizer limits, particularly as they are pushed towards quantum phase transitions into trivial phases. Our focus is primarily on quantum information stored within the ground-state manifold of the transverse-field toric code (TFTC), subjected to independent bit-flip and phase-flip decoherence. Although the transverse field induces strong quantum fluctuations of $m$ anyons in the ground states, we show that projective measurements of the "bare" toric code stabilizers can nevertheless be used to construct an efficient decoding algorithm which exhibits a finite threshold to both types of decoherence, even when the transverse field is tuned infinitesimally close to the quantum critical point. Our construction of the optimal postmeasurement decoders---that is, the optimal decoders assuming that the stabilizers are first measured---crucially relies on the stoquastic nature of the TFTC Hamiltonian, which affords a description of its ground states in terms of a classical lattice gauge theory. Using stoquasticity, we first prove that stabilizer measurements act unitarily on the undecohered TFTC ground states to excellent approximation, and that the optimal postmeasurement decoder can be efficiently implemented via Monte Carlo simulation of a constrained lattice gauge theory. We then construct the optimal postmeasurement decoder for the TFTC states under bit-flip and phase-flip decoherence, where we numerically demonstrate a finite threshold close to the quantum critical point. We provide theoretical evidence (without a rigorous proof) that stabilizer measurements constitute an optimal decoding strategy in this setting. For phase-flip decoherence, we further prove that the optimal postmeasurement decoding threshold is independent of the transverse field strength throughout the topological phase.

</details>
