### [149] [Feynman Propagators on Exponentially Decaying Perturbations of Stationary Spacetimes with Compact Cauchy Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04133)
*Selim Amar*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 文中在紧致柯西面的稳态时空的指数衰减扰动上构造了费曼传播子，将其定义为波算子在由复围道定义的指数加权索伯列夫空间上的拟基本解，并借此通过连续 Wick 型旋转（Wick-like rotation）联系黎曼情形以计算狄拉克算子指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出研究动机在于：在稳态时空（stationary spacetime）的指数衰减扰动背景下建立费曼传播子理论，并将其用于狄拉克算子（Dirac operator）指标（index）的计算。

Method: 方法上，将费曼传播子（Feynman propagator）构造为波算子（wave operator）的拟基本解（parametrix），其所在空间为借助复围道（complex contour）Γ 定义的指数加权索伯列夫空间（exponentially weighted Sobolev space）；随后采用连续 Wick 型旋转，把问题与黎曼情形（Riemannian case）相联系。

Result: 摘要仅说明该理论被应用于狄拉克算子指标的计算，且通过连续 Wick 型旋转将其与黎曼情形关联；未给出具体指标数值或定量结论。

Conclusion: 结论为：在稳态时空的指数衰减扰动上可系统建立费曼传播子理论，并可借助连续 Wick 型旋转将该框架用于狄拉克算子指标的计算。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct Feynman propagators on exponentially decaying perturbations of stationary spacetime with compact Cauchy surface. The propagators are defined as parametrices of the wave operator on exponentially weighted Sobolev spaces defined by complex contours $Γ$. We then apply this theory to the computation of indexes of Dirac operators using a continuous Wick-like rotation to relate it to the Riemannian case.

</details>


### [150] [The Characteristic Initial Value Problem for the 3D Compressible Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04201)
*Jared Speck,Sifan Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在几何表述框架下证明了3D可压缩Euler方程在特征初值问题（characteristic initial value problem）下的局部存在唯一性、Sobolev正则性传播与延拓准则，初值给在一对横截相交的声学零超曲面上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对3D可压缩Euler方程的特征初值问题（即在相交零超曲面上给定数据）缺乏系统适定性结果，而既有工作如Rendall对波动方程的处理无法直接适用于结构更复杂的Euler方程组。

Method: 采用[Speck19]建立的流动几何表述（geometric formulation），将方程分解为“波动部分（wave-part）”与耦合的“输运-散度-旋度部分（transport-div-curl part）”，并借鉴Rendall[90]的思路，但需引入实质性新思想以处理Euler方程较纯波动方程更复杂的结构；不假设对称性、无旋性或等熵性，状态方程仅要求声速为正。

Result: 证明了解的局部存在性与唯一性、Sobolev正则性传播（propagation of Sobolev regularity）以及延拓准则（continuation criteria）；存在区域为数据零超曲面并集的未来邻域，其大小依赖于数据的各类范数及数据零超曲面的结构。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在一般状态方程与无对称性假设下，3D可压缩Euler方程的特征初值问题是局部适定的；几何分解方法与针对耦合波动-输运结构的分析技术是处理该问题的关键。

Abstract: We prove local existence and uniqueness, propagation of Sobolev regularity, and continuation criteria results for the characteristic initial value problem for the 3D compressible Euler equations. This is the Cauchy problem with initial data given on a pair of transversally intersecting (acoustically) null hypersurfaces. The region of existence in our main theorem is a future-neighborhood of the union of the data null hypersurfaces, where the size of the neighborhood depends on various norms of the data as well as the structure of the data null hypersurfaces. We allow for any equation of state with positive sound speed, and we do not make any symmetry, irrotationality, or isentropicity assumptions. Our analysis relies on the geometric formulation of the flow derived in [Speck19], which decomposes the equations into a "wave-part" coupled to a "transport-div-curl" part. Compared to Rendall's celebrated solution of the characteristic initial value problem for wave equations [Rendall90], substantial new ideas are needed to handle the fact that the compressible Euler equations are more complicated than pure wave equations.

</details>


### [151] [Scaling laws for linear-elastic two-variant martensitic phase transitions in general domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04329)
*Sergio Conti,Oleksandr Misiats*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究几何线性弹性（geometrically linear elasticity）中的双井问题（two-well problem），证明在总表面能（total surface energy）正则化下能量标度分为 ε^{2/3} 与 ε^{4/5} 两种区制，分别为存在两条或仅一条相容孪晶法向（twin-normal）方向的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前相关标度结果多局限于矩形等特殊几何、主要限于二维，且下界由 Fouriers 方法得到；本文旨在对有界 Lipschitz 区域的一般 n≥2 情形给出统一处理与更简洁的证明。

Method: 在有界 Lipschitz 集上考虑经对称秩一连接（symmetrized rank-one connection）相容的双井问题，边界位移取两井平均值，并引入基于总表面能的正则化；通过显式构造（含边界附近振荡的自相似细化）给出上界，并给出匹配下界的简短实空间（real-space）证明。

Result: 能量与表面能系数 ε 的标度为：存在两条不同相容孪晶法向方向时为 ε^{2/3}，仅存在一条时为 ε^{4/5}；该结论与矩形及其他特殊几何中的既有结果以及 Raiţă、Rüland、Tissot、Tribuzio（2024）用 Fourier 方法得到的下界一致。

Conclusion: 双井问题的能量标度由相容孪晶法向方向的数目决定，且该二分现象在一般有界 Lipschitz 区域、维数 n≥2 下成立；显式构造与实空间下界证明为相关标度律提供了简化且更一般的推导路径。

Abstract: We consider a two-well problem within geometrically linear elasticity, on a bounded Lipschitz set in dimension $n\ge 2$. We assume that the two wells are compatible through a symmetrized rank-one connection and that the displacement matches their average on the boundary, and we include a regularization based on the total surface energy. We show that there are two distinct regimes, with the energy scaling differently in terms of the surface-energy coefficient $\eps$: it is proportional to $\eps^{2/3}$ if there are two distinct compatible twin-normal directions, and to $\eps^{4/5}$ if there is only one. This matches previous findings in rectangles and other special geometries, mostly in two dimensions, and the lower bounds obtained by a Fourier method by Raiţă, Rüland, Tissot and Tribuzio (2024). Our results are obtained by explicit constructions, which contain a self-similar refinement of oscillations close to the boundary, and by a short real-space proof of the matching lower bounds.

</details>


### [152] [Uniqueness in an incompressible fluid with objective heat conduction model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04516)
*Kinza Arshad,Martina Nunziata,Vincenzo Tibullo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对Angeles提出的一类更一般的客观型（objective）热传导Maxwell-Cattaneo-Christov方程，在不可压缩流体（incompressible fluid）情形下证明了唯一性结果（uniqueness result）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热传导（heat conduction）背景下，Maxwell-Cattaneo方程是傅里叶定律（Fourier's law）的成功替代；2009年Christov提出了其与参考系无关（frame-independent，即客观的）推广形式，而Angeles进一步提出了更一般的客观型Maxwell-Cattaneo-Christov方程，此类方程的解的唯一性尚待确立。

Method: 对Angeles提出的更一般的客观型热传导Maxwell-Cattaneo-Christov方程类，在不可压缩流体（incompressible fluid）框架下进行数学分析，证明其解的唯一性（uniqueness）。

Result: 所考虑的一般类客观型Maxwell-Cattaneo-Christov方程在不可压缩流体情形下具有唯一性。

Conclusion: 该唯一性结果为Angeles所提出的客观型热传导Maxwell-Cattaneo-Christov方程类的数学适定性提供了理论支持，适用于不可压缩流体情形。

Abstract: In the context of heat conduction, the equations of Maxwell-Cattaneo is a successful alternative to the Fourier's law. In 2009, Christov proposed a frame-independent (or objective) generalization of Maxwell-Cattaneo equation. For a more general class of objective heat conduction Maxwell-Cattaneo-Christov equations proposed by Angeles, a uniqueness result is proved, applied to an incompressible fluid.

</details>


### [153] [Stochastic interpretation of the Water Waves equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04739)
*Antoine Moneyron*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文基于位置不确定性原理（location uncertainty principle, LU）给出水波方程（Water Waves equations）的随机解释，并由此通过尺度极限（scaling limits）推导出若干简化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为水波方程建立随机解释，并借助尺度极限从复杂方程推断更简单的模型，以刻画随机效应下的波浪行为。

Method: 采用位置不确定性原理（location uncertainty principle, LU）对水波方程作随机化表述，再通过尺度极限（scaling limits）导出Saint-Venant、Boussinesq与Serre-Green-Naghdi等简化模型，并将其适配到随机框架后进行数值实现。

Result: 适配随机设定后的简化模型已成功实现数值计算，作者展示了多项实验以评估其性质，包括波浪对称性的破坏（breaking of the wave symmetry）以及时间相关噪声（time correlated noises）的影响。

Conclusion: 位置不确定性原理可为水波方程及其简化模型提供一致的随机化框架，且相应模型具备可行的数值实现，能够揭示对称性破坏与时间相关噪声等随机效应。

Abstract: The aim of this article is to derive a stochastic interpretation of the Water Waves equations, based on the location uncertainty principle (LU). Next, these equations are used to infer simpler models from scaling limits, such as the Saint-Venant, Boussinesq and Serre-Green-Naghdi ones. Once adapted to the stochastic setting, these models have been successfully implemented numerically, and we show several experiments to assess their properties -- for example the breaking of the wave symmetry, and the influence of time correlated noises.

</details>


### [154] [The Lee-Huang-Yang Formula in the Hard-Core Simplified Approach to the Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05293)
*Mohamed El Hedi Bahri,Ian Jauslin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造并分析了稀释玻色气体（dilute Bose gas）基态问题中 Simplified Approach 的硬核（hard-core）变体。作者用单调迭代格式证明其积分微分方程解的存在性与（受限意义下的）唯一性，并证明能量、密度与凝聚体分数（condensate fraction）的渐近展开满足 Lee-Huang-Yang 公式与 Bogolyubov 理论预测，从而将 Carlen、Jauslin、Lieb 的软核结果推广至硬核情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬核势（hard-core potential）近期重新受到关注，且与真实原子玻色气体的物理情形密切相关；此前的严谨结果主要针对软核势，硬核情形尚未被覆盖，因此有必要将 Simplified Approach 的分析推广到硬核相互作用。

Method: 硬核 Simple Equation 为一积分微分方程（integro-differential equation），通过单调迭代格式（monotone iteration scheme）构造其解；证明上采用软核势（soft-core potentials）m·χ_{|x|≤1} 逼近硬核势，令高度 m 递增，在固定能量下建立对应解的 L¹ 收敛性。

Result: 证明了解在受限意义下的存在性与唯一性；解的每粒子能量 e 与密度 ρ 满足 Lee-Huang-Yang 公式 e = 2πρ(1 + 128/(15√π)·√ρ + o(√ρ))，凝聚体分数（condensate fraction）满足 η = 1 − 8/(3√π)·√ρ + o(√ρ)（当 ρ→0），与 Bogolyubov 理论一致。逼近方面，密度以 O(m^{-1/2}) 的速率收敛，且软核方程的能量与凝聚体分数展开具有关于 m 一致的误差。

Conclusion: 硬核 Simple Equation 可独立定义并求解，其解的渐近行为与软核情形一致，因而 Carlen、Jauslin、Lieb 的结果被成功推广到硬核情形；由于 Lee-Huang-Yang 展开的误差关于 m 一致，相关结论在硬核极限下得以保持。

Abstract: We study the hard-core variant of the Simplified Approach to the ground state of the dilute Bose gas. The hard-core potential has been the subject of renewed attention, and is physically relevant to realistic atomic Bose gases. The hard-core Simple Equation is an integro-differential equation, and we construct its solution via a monotone iteration scheme and prove existence and uniqueness (in a restricted sense) of the solution. The energy per particle $e$ and the density $ρ$ of the solution are shown to satisfy the Lee-Huang-Yang formula $e = 2πρ(1 + \frac{128}{15\sqrtπ}\sqrtρ + o(\sqrtρ))$ as $ρ\to 0$, and the condensate fraction $η$ predicted by the hard-core Simple Equation is shown to satisfy $η= 1 - \frac{8}{3\sqrtπ}\sqrtρ + o(\sqrtρ)$ as $ρ\to 0$, in agreement with Bogolyubov's theory. We thus extend results of Carlen, Jauslin, Lieb to the hard-core case.
  The hard-core equation is defined on its own terms, and the proofs proceed by approximating the hard-core potential with the soft-core potentials $m\,χ_{\{|x| \le 1\}}$ of increasing height $m$: at fixed energy, we establish the $L^1$ convergence of the corresponding solutions and the convergence of their densities at an $O(m^{-1/2})$ rate, and we show that the Lee-Huang-Yang expansions of the energy and of the condensate fraction for the soft-core equations hold with errors uniform in $m$, so that they survive the hard-core limit.

</details>


### [155] [Uniform local smoothing for the derivative NLS and the soliton mass threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05330)
*Mohamed Majdoub*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究导数非线性薛定谔方程（DNLS）在质量球上的一致局部光滑性（local smoothing）阈值问题。作者给出以质量与显式频率尺度表述的定量估计、小质量球上的一致局部光滑估计，并分析孤子族的行为，从而将驻波孤子质量 $2\pi$ 识别为尖锐阈值的自然候选，并给出相应猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harrop-Griffiths 等人已证明 DNLS 在 $L^2(\mathbb{R})$ 上整体适定（globally well-posed），其关键工具局部光滑估计仅在 $L^2$-等连续集上成立；由于质量均为 $2\pi$ 的伸缩驻波孤子（stationary soliton）违反该估计，故自然要问：在质量球 $\{M(q_0)\le m\}$ 上一致局部光滑性对哪些质量 $m$ 成立。

Method: 证明三项结果：（1）建立定量局部光滑估计，数据仅通过质量与显式频率尺度 $\kappa(q_0)$ 进入，$2T$ 时长区间上界按 $1+\kappa(q_0)^2T$ 增长，并用驻波孤子说明 $\kappa$ 的幂次不可低于 1；（2）在质量充分小的球上证明一致局部光滑性，并给出尺度不变的 Morrey 型形式与局部化 $L^4_{t,x}$ 界；（3）从两侧分析孤子族：一致常数在 $m\uparrow 2\pi$ 时至少以 $(2\pi-m)^{-1}$ 发散，而质量 $m<2\pi$ 的每个孤子满足常数为 $O((2\pi-m)^{-1})$ 的局部光滑性。

Result: 得到了定量局部光滑估计与 $\kappa$ 幂次下界 1；证明了小质量球的一致局部光滑性及 Morrey 型、局部 $L^4_{t,x}$ 估计；揭示了质量 $2\pi$ 处的双向爆破行为，即一致常数至少以 $(2\pi-m)^{-1}$ 发散，且 $m<2\pi$ 的孤子均可容许，从而将驻波孤子质量 $2\pi$（亦即 Hayashi--Ozawa 强制性阈值）突出为尖锐阈值的自然候选。

Conclusion: 作者将 $2\pi$ 作为尖锐质量阈值表述为猜想，并给出基于 Kaup--Newell 散射数据（scattering data）的谱重构表述，同时讨论了证明所面临的障碍。

Abstract: Harrop-Griffiths, Killip, Ntekoume and Vişan proved that the derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation (DNLS) is globally well-posed in $L^2(\mathbb{R})$. A key ingredient of their proof is a local smoothing estimate. It holds on $L^2$-equicontinuous sets of initial data, but it cannot hold on $L^2$-balls, because rescaled stationary solitons, all of mass $2π$, violate it. This raises a natural question: for which masses $m$ does local smoothing hold uniformly on the ball $\{M(q_0)\le m\}$? We prove three results. First, we give a quantitative version of the local smoothing estimate in which the data enter only through the mass and an explicit frequency scale $κ(q_0)$. On time intervals of length $2T$ the bound grows like $1+κ(q_0)^2T$, and the stationary soliton shows that the power of $κ$ cannot be lowered below one. Second, we prove uniform local smoothing on balls of small mass, together with a scale-invariant Morrey-type form and a localized $L^4_{t,x}$ bound. Third, we analyse the soliton family from both sides. The uniform constant must blow up at least like $(2π-m)^{-1}$ as $m\uparrow 2π$, while every soliton of mass $m<2π$ satisfies local smoothing with a constant $O((2π-m)^{-1})$. These facts single out the stationary-soliton mass $2π$, which is also the Hayashi--Ozawa coercivity threshold, as the natural candidate for the sharp threshold. We formulate this as a conjecture, give a spectral reformulation in terms of the Kaup--Newell scattering data, and discuss the obstacles to a proof.

</details>


<div id='physics.atom-ph'></div>

# physics.atom-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Wavefront engineering with atomic Huygens' surfaces made simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04538)
*K. E. Ballantine,J. Ruostekoski*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种极简的双层方晶格原子阵列实现近乎理想的“原子惠更斯超表面”，无需位点依赖能级位移即可获得近单位无反射透射与完整2π相位调控，并给出精准的双模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子阵列可在量子区域实现光介导的非局域超表面相互作用，且无需纳米加工光学元件或本征材料吸收；但既有方案依赖局域位点依赖的能级位移（level shifts），需要同时激发并精确匹配纯电偶极与磁偶极响应，实现复杂。

Method: 构建含双原子单胞的双层方晶格，晶格间距不受小间距限制；利用非局域辐射激发同时提供偶宇称电偶极响应与奇宇称辐射通道（其主导多极解释为磁偶极与电四极贡献的组合），并推导描述该响应的双模模型（two-mode model），与全耦合偶极模拟（coupled-dipole simulations）对比验证。

Result: 该结构实现近单位无反射透射（near-unity reflectionless transmission）与透射光完整2π相位控制，且无需位点依赖能级位移；双模模型在最优晶格间距附近准确重现全耦合偶极模拟结果。

Conclusion: 由于耦合偶极模型适用性广，该微观分析可推广至纳米结构超表面；该平台还可直接衔接亚辐射保护（subradiance-protected）的单光子存储协议，为在简单原子双层中集成波前工程、存储与读取提供路径。

Abstract: Atomic arrays provide a cooperative platform for light-mediated interactions in nonlocal metasurfaces which can operate in the quantum regime without nanofabricated optical elements or intrinsic material absorption. We present a minimal atomic implementation to harness wavefront engineering and create a near-ideal intrinsic atomic Huygens' surface. A simple bilayer square lattice with a two-atom unit cell, not limited to small lattice spacings, provides near-unity reflectionless transmission and a full $2π$ phase control of transmitted light without the localized site-dependent level shifts of previous proposals. This simplification is achieved through nonlocal radiative excitations that provide the familiar electric-dipole response with even radiation parity as well as an odd-parity radiation channel whose leading multipolar interpretation combines magnetic-dipole and electric-quadrupole contributions in atoms exhibiting only electric dipole transitions. This eliminates the need to simultaneously excite pure electric-dipole and magnetic-dipole responses with exactly matched radiation. We derive a two-mode model for this response and show that it accurately captures full coupled-dipole simulations near the optimal lattice spacings. Because of the wide applicability of coupled-dipole models, the relevance of our microscopic analysis extends beyond atomic physics to nanostructured metasurfaces. Moreover, the same platform also connects directly to established subradiance-protected single-photon storage protocols, suggesting a route toward combining wavefront engineering, storage, and retrieval in a simple atomic bilayer.

</details>


### [157] [Ultracold polar molecules for quantum science and technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05040)
*Philip D. Gregory,Simon L. Cornish*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述超冷极性分子（ultracold polar molecules）平台的核心概念，聚焦其内禀电偶极矩（electric dipole moment）所导致的强微波耦合与可调控长程各向异性偶极-偶极相互作用（dipole-dipole interactions），并以此为基础介绍当前研究的关键物理概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷极性分子的内禀电偶极矩可实现对微波场的强耦合，并工程化出长程、各向异性且可调的偶极-偶极相互作用，从而产生分子间纠缠（entanglement）并探索其他体系难以达到的量子多体（quantum many-body）新现象；光镊（optical tweezers）与光晶格（optical lattices）实验的进展及分子气体达到量子简并（quantum degeneracy）推动了该领域的系统梳理需求。

Method: 采用概念性综述（review）方式，围绕超冷极性分子平台的关键概念展开阐释，而非提出新的实验或理论方法。

Result: 文中指出，现有光镊与光晶格实验已实现相干量子态（coherent quantum states）下分子间的受控相互作用，且近期体相分子气体（bulk gases）已被冷却至量子简并。

Conclusion: 受这些标志性进展驱动，该文旨在阐释当前超冷极性分子研究领域所涉及的多项核心概念，为相关研究提供概念性基础。

Abstract: Ultracold polar molecules present a rich platform for experimental quantum science leveraging their unique properties. Of particular relevance is the intrinsic electric dipole moment of the molecule, which results in strong coupling to microwave fields and can be used to engineer long-range, anisotropic, and tunable dipole-dipole interactions. Such interactions can generate entanglement between molecules and enable the study of novel quantum many-body phenomena in parameter regimes inaccessible in other systems. Modern experiments in optical tweezers and optical lattices have demonstrated controlled interactions between molecules in coherent quantum states, and recently bulk gases of molecules have been cooled to quantum degeneracy. Motivated by these landmark advances, we present this article to explain many of the key concepts relevant to current research in the field.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Liouvillian Kramers Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03881)
*Kohei Kawabata*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）与宇称对称性（parity symmetry）的共同作用可在刘维尔超算符（Liouvillian superoperator）中强制实现Kramers简并，并据此构造了具有该简并的耗散多体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kramers定理通常保证半整数自旋的时间反演不变哈密顿量具有谱简并；但由于希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）的双重结构，此前认为作用在混合态上的刘维尔超算符不存在Kramers简并。本文旨在重新审视并挑战这一认识。

Method: 通过论证时间反演对称性与宇称对称性的共同作用，在刘维尔算符层面强制产生Kramers简并；并显式构造相互作用费米子（fermions）与自旋（spins）的耗散多体模型来实现该简并。

Result: 构造的模型复谱统计（complex spectral statistics）遵循辛对称类（symplectic symmetry classes）非厄米随机矩阵（non-Hermitian random matrices）的统计规律；在非厄米随机矩阵三类基本普适类中，辛类此前从未在对称性分辨的多体刘维尔算符中实现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了辛类在对称性分辨多体刘维尔算符中的显式实现，并提供了辛开放量子系统（symplectic open quantum systems）中不可积性（nonintegrability）的谱特征。

Abstract: The Kramers theorem generally guarantees the spectral degeneracy of time-reversal-invariant Hamiltonian operators with half-integer spin, underlying a variety of physical phenomena. Despite its fundamental significance, the Kramers degeneracy has been argued to be absent in Liouvillian superoperators acting on mixed states because of the double structure of the Hilbert space. Here, we instead demonstrate that the interplay of time-reversal symmetry$^†$ and parity symmetry enforces the Kramers degeneracy in Liouvillians. We explicitly construct dissipative many-body models of interacting fermions and spins exhibiting the Kramers degeneracy and show that their complex spectral statistics follow those of non-Hermitian random matrices in the corresponding symplectic symmetry classes. Of the three fundamental universality classes of non-Hermitian random matrices, the symplectic class has never been realized in symmetry-resolved many-body Liouvillians. Our constructions provide its explicit realizations and spectral signatures of nonintegrability in symplectic open quantum systems.

</details>


### [159] [Energy-Resolved Cross-Correlation of THz Field Emission of Electrons from a Metal Nanotip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04036)
*Laurent N. David,Rodrigo T. Paulino,Angela Moskal,David G. Cooke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用两束独立产生的单周期强场太赫兹（THz）脉冲对钨纳米针尖进行互相关测量，在160 ps延迟范围内探测场致发射电子动力学；结果表明每个THz脉冲只产生单一孤立电子爆发，可由能量过滤提取最短、能量最高的成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强THz脉冲结合金属纳米针尖的避雷针场增强（lightning-rod field enhancement）可驱动keV量级、飞秒时长的电子束场致发射，但其发射动力学此前基本未被探索，缺乏对发射时间结构与共振行为的直接实验判定。

Method: 将两束独立生成的单周期THz脉冲聚焦于钨纳米针尖，对能量分辨的发射信号做互相关（cross-correlation），在160 ps范围内扫描相对延迟；并采用纳入低品质因数（low-Q）、亚皮秒天线响应的局域场增强的一维Fowler-Nordheim模型进行对照。

Result: 总电荷接近10⁶ e⁻时，零延迟附近的延迟-能量发射图显示电子最高被加速至2.1 keV，最高能量处的互相关半高全宽约210 fs。除装置中光反射造成的复制峰外，全延迟范围未见发射回波（emission echoes），排除了沿针杆的Sommerfeld波反射等长寿命天线共振；一维模型可复现峰值电子能量及延迟-能量图的不对称性。

Conclusion: 每个THz脉冲产生单一、孤立的电子爆发，且能量过滤（energy filtering）可选出其最短、能量最高的成分，为超快电子源的时域调控提供依据。

Abstract: Intense THz pulses, combined with the lightning-rod field enhancement at metal nanotips, can drive field emission of keV-energy, femtosecond-duration electron bunches, yet the dynamics governing this emission remain largely unexplored. Here we probe these dynamics by cross-correlating the energy-resolved emission driven by two independently generated single-cycle THz pulses focused onto a tungsten nanotip, scanning their relative delay over 160 ps. At total bunch charges approaching $10^6$ e$^-$, the delay-energy emission map near zero delay reveals electrons accelerated up to 2.1 keV, with a cross-correlation width of ~210 fs full width at half maximum at the highest energies. Apart from replicas caused by optical reflections in the setup, no emission echoes are observed over the full delay range, ruling out long-lived antenna resonances such as Sommerfeld-wave reflections along the tip shaft. A 1D Fowler-Nordheim model incorporating a low-$Q$, sub-picosecond antenna response for the local field enhancement reproduces the peak electron energies and the asymmetry of the delay-energy map. Our results indicate that each THz pulse produces a single, isolated electron burst, and that energy filtering can select its shortest, highest-energy component.

</details>


### [160] [Chirality rules for optical vortices in rhombohedral graphene multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04076)
*Felipe Pérez Riffo,Matías Castro Schnaidt,Juan M. Florez,Eric Suárez Morell*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文揭示菱面体多层石墨烯中"光学零点（optical zeros）"即带间偶极矩消失的动量点可作为带守恒荷的光学响应涡旋，其近K谷的电荷星座由堆垛手性整数N唯一确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学零点处带间偶极矩相位绕其缠绕整数圈，构成带守恒电荷的涡旋；作者希望建立一个整数（堆垛手性N）对这类涡旋星座及其响应的统一刻画规则。

Method: 基于解析论证，给出菱面体石墨烯多层中近K谷光学零点的电荷计数规则，并用完整紧束缚模型（full tight-binding model，含远程跃迁）检验规则稳健性。

Result: 每种线偏振的总电荷为N−1，且在三角翘曲（trigonal warping）与层间偏压下守恒；N≥3时K点为精确光学零点（Bernal双层是唯一例外）；翘曲将剩余电荷置于卫星点，其半径随翘曲线性缩放；N≥4时钉扎于K点的电荷与偏压无关。量子化带间特征（quantized interband character）以整数阶梯方式计数所包围电荷，阶梯位于上述半径处；三层中偏压驱动卫星点穿过受保护的K涡旋；同一整数亦决定响应幅度，带边位移电流（shift current）随N线性增长。

Conclusion: 堆垛手性N作为整数拓扑不变量，统一决定菱面体石墨烯多层近K谷光学零点的星座结构与位移电流响应幅度，且该规则对远程跃迁及能带重整修正均具稳健性。

Abstract: Optical zeros are momenta where the interband dipole vanishes. The dipole phase winds around each of them by an integer, so they are vortices of the resonant optical response with a conserved charge. We show that in rhombohedral graphene multilayers one integer, the stacking chirality $N$, fixes their constellation near the $K$ valley: the total charge per linear polarization is $N-1$ and is conserved under trigonal warping and interlayer bias; for $N\ge3$ the $K$ point stays an exact optical zero, the Bernal bilayer being the only exception; warping places the remaining charge on satellites whose radii scale linearly with the warping; and the charge pinned at $K$ is bias independent for $N\ge4$. A quantized interband character counts the enclosed charge as an integer staircase with steps at these radii, and an interlayer bias drives a satellite through the protected $K$ vortex in the trilayer. The rules survive the remote hoppings of the full tight-binding model and, being integer charges, the corrections that reshape the bands. The same integer sets the magnitude of the response: the band-edge shift current grows linearly with $N$.

</details>


### [161] [Anomalous Hall Response and Real-Space Current Correlations in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04312)
*Zhen Bi,Chandra M. Varma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过实空间计算（real-space calculations）研究菱形堆叠石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）中的反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）与磁化，发现不存在 Haldane 模型式的平衡环流（loop currents），AHE 而源于谷极化（valley polarization）下电流算符闭合 Wilson 环（Wilson loops）的路径干涉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清菱形堆叠石墨烯中 AHE 的物理起源，检验其是否可由量子反常霍尔绝缘体（quantum anomalous Hall insulator）的 Haldane 模型环流机制解释。

Method: 采用实空间计算；分析电流算符构成的取向闭合 Wilson 环之间的干涉；利用额外载流子电荷密度（added-carrier charge density）主要分布于顶层与底层这一事实，构建表面哈密顿量（surface Hamiltonian）的实空间展开。

Result: 未发现 Haldane 型平衡环流；AHE 由谷极化下 Wilson 环干涉产生，并给出若干 Wilson 环示例；表面哈密顿量实空间展开能以较好精度重现全模型的霍尔响应（Hall response）；预测平滑局域势（smooth local potential）可诱导平衡循环电流，其强度取决于费米面（Fermi surface）处态的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与轨道矩（orbital moments）。

Conclusion: 菱形堆叠石墨烯的 AHE 机制不同于 Haldane 模型，需以谷极化下的 Wilson 环干涉描述；简化的表面哈密顿量可有效刻画霍尔响应，且局域势可诱导与贝里曲率和轨道矩相关的平衡电流。

Abstract: We investigate the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) and magnetization observed in rhombohedral graphene through real-space calculations which lead to new insights. Our calculations reveal no equilibrium loop currents of the type associated with the Haldane model of a quantum anomalous Hall insulator. Instead, the AHE arises in this instance from interference between electronic paths forming oriented closed Wilson loops of current operators in the presence of valley polarization. We show some examples of these Wilson loops. Using the fact that the added-carrier charge density resides dominantly in the top and bottom layers, we develop a real-space expansion of the surface Hamiltonian that reproduces the Hall response of the full model with good accuracy. We also predict that a smooth local potential induces equilibrium circulating currents whose strength depends on both Berry curvature and the orbital moments of states at the Fermi surface.

</details>


### [162] [Altermagnetism-induced higher-order topological Dirac semimetals in two-dimensional nonsymmorphic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04347)
*Lizhou Liu,Cheng-Ming Miao,Ying-Tao Zhang,Qing-Feng Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用交错磁序（altermagnetism）在二维非对称三层狄拉克体系中诱导高阶拓扑狄拉克半金属（higher-order topological Dirac semimetal），其相兼具体能隙内狄拉克点、能隙化的边缘谱与角态（corner states）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是寻找一种可行机制，在二维非对称体系（nonsymmorphic system）中实现高阶拓扑狄拉克半金属，并检验交错磁序（altermagnetism）能否充当此类相的有效调控手段。

Method: 以三层非对称狄拉克体系为平台：层间杂化（interlayer hybridization）保留体狄拉克交叉并产生螺旋边缘态（helical edge states）；在外层引入 d 波交错磁序使螺旋边缘态打开能隙。并采用精确的层空间分解（layer-space decomposition）分析该相的构成。

Result: 交错磁序打开边缘能隙后，仅高对称点 M 处受对称性保护的狄拉克交叉仍保持无能隙，体系同时呈现残余体狄拉克点、能隙化边缘谱与角态。层空间分解表明这些特征源于无能隙狄拉克扇区与有能隙 Z2 拓扑扇区的共存。

Conclusion: 结果表明交错磁序（altermagnetism）可作为在二维非对称体系中工程化高阶拓扑狄拉克半金属的有效机制。

Abstract: We propose an altermagnetism-induced route to a two-dimensional higher-order topological Dirac semimetal in a trilayer nonsymmorphic Dirac system. Interlayer hybridization preserves the nonsymmorphic bulk Dirac crossings and generates helical edge states in the trilayer phase. Introducing outer-layer $d$-wave altermagnetic order gaps the helical edge states, while only the symmetry-protected Dirac crossing at high-symmetry point $M$ remains gapless. The resulting phase hosts a surviving bulk Dirac point, gapped edge spectra, and corner states. An exact layer-space decomposition further reveals that these features arise from the coexistence of a gapless Dirac sector and a gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological sector. Our results establish altermagnetism as an effective mechanism for engineering higher-order topological Dirac semimetals in two-dimensional nonsymmorphic systems.

</details>


### [163] [Dissipationless valley diode effects in Kekulé graphene superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04396)
*W. Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文理论提出基于Kekulé石墨烯超晶格（Kekulé graphene superlattice）的隧道结可在无超导条件下产生有限谷超电流（valley supercurrent），实现无耗散谷二极管效应（valley diode effect），并能通过栅压调控达到100%整流效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管（superconducting diode）因无耗散输运（dissipationless transport）在非互易器件（nonreciprocal device）应用中备受关注；本文旨在探索在无超导性的情形下实现类似非互易输运的方案。

Method: 采用理论方法，构建基于Kekulé石墨烯超晶格的隧道结模型，分析各横向通道（transverse channel）中电子获得的额外反射相位（reflection phase），并探讨栅压（gate voltage）对该效应的调控。

Result: 该隧道结可承载有限的谷超电流并表现出显著非互易性（nonreciprocity），即使不存在超导性；该效应支持效率达100%的完美谷二极管整流（rectification），且可由栅压方便地调节。

Conclusion: 该发现为以更灵活方式设计无耗散谷电子学（dissipationless valleytronics）提供了器件平台。

Abstract: Superconducting diodes have attracted considerable interest for nonreciprocal device applications owing to their dissipationless transport. Here, we theoretically propose that a tunnel junction based on the Kekulé graphene superlattice can host a finite valley supercurrent that exhibits pronounced nonreciprocity even in the absence of superconductivity, giving rise to a dissipationless valley diode effect. This effect originates from the additional reflection phase acquired by electrons in each transverse channel and supports perfect valley diode rectification with $100\%$ efficiency, which can be readily tuned via the gate voltage. This finding provides a platform for designing dissipationless valleytronics in a more versatile manner.

</details>


### [164] [Nonlinearity-Induced Suppression of Dissipation in Bath-Coupled Floquet Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04752)
*Bhoomija Chaurasia,Sebabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要表明非线性相互作用可实现激光写入Floquet拓扑绝缘体中边缘态耗散的全光调控：中等非线性增强耗散，最优非线性值则通过形成高度局域的传播边缘孤子显著抑制耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体可承载手性、robustness抗无序的边缘态，但其与外浴耦合时会产生能量耗散；因此需要寻找抑制手性拓扑边缘输运耗散的途径。

Method: 采用激光写入的Floquet拓扑绝缘体（Floquet topological insulator），使其与光子浴（photonic bath）周期性耦合，并引入非线性相互作用（nonlinear interactions）以实现全光调控。

Result: 中等非线性因模式混合（mode mixing）而增强耗散；将非线性强度提升至最优值时，通过形成高度局域的传播边缘孤子（traveling edge solitons），耗散被显著抑制。

Conclusion: 工程化的系统—浴耦合（system-bath coupling）与非线性相互作用相结合，可作为抑制手性拓扑边缘输运耗散的可行途径。

Abstract: Topological insulators can host chiral, disorder-robust edge states that dissipate energy when coupled to an external bath. We show that nonlinear interactions enable all-optical control over edge-state dissipation in a laser-written Floquet topological insulator periodically coupled to a photonic bath. Although moderate nonlinearity enhances dissipation due to mode mixing, increasing the nonlinear strength to an optimal value dramatically suppresses dissipation by forming highly localized traveling edge solitons. These results establish engineered system-bath coupling and nonlinear interactions as a route to suppress dissipation of chiral topological edge transport.

</details>


### [165] [Field-free transverse response in altermagnet and topological insulator heterojunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04937)
*Bijay Kumar Sahoo,Abhiram Soori*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究无外场下拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator, TI）与二维交变磁性（altermagnetic, AM）引线构成的 AM-TI-AM 异质结的输运性质，发现由交变磁体内禀时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal symmetry breaking）所驱动的无场横向微分电导（transverse differential conductivity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统横向输运（如霍尔效应）通常依赖外磁场或磁性有序，本文旨在探索能否利用交变磁体的内禀时间反演对称性破缺，在零外磁场下实现自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）边界态中的横向响应。

Method: 构建 TI 与两个二维交变磁引线耦合的 AM-TI-AM 异质结理论模型，在对称与非对称化学势（chemical potential）条件下，分析奈尔矢量（Néel vector）取向对横向电导的影响，并考察纵向与横向微分电导随 TI 化学势、结参数 χ_t=χ_b 及温度的变化。

Result: 横向电导对奈尔矢量取向高度敏感：奈尔矢量含面外分量时横向电导非零；对称化学势（μ_t=μ_b）下面内取向（θ=π/2）因 k_y 与 -k_y 通道对称性恢复而严格为零，而非对称化学势（μ_t≠μ_b）下则出现有限横向响应。纵向电导呈 Fabry-Pérot 型振荡并可在有限温度下保持，横向电导则呈反对称依赖；横向电流贡献在 TI 区域内随位置指数衰减，二者均随结参数 χ_t=χ_b 周期振荡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示交变磁体与拓扑绝缘体异质结中的无场横向响应机制，为自旋-动量锁定边界态的输运理解提供基础，并表明 AM-TI 异质结在下一代自旋电子学（spintronics）应用中的潜力。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the transport properties of a topological insulator (TI) connected to two-dimensional altermagnetic (AM) leads in the absence of an external magnetic field. Exploiting the intrinsic time-reversal symmetry breaking of altermagnets, we report the emergence of a field-free transverse differential conductivity in the resulting AM-TI-AM heterostructure. Our analysis reveals that this transverse response is highly sensitive to the orientation of the Néel vector: a finite transverse conductivity manifests when the Néel vector possesses an out-of-plane component, whereas it strictly vanishes for in-plane orientations ($θ= π/2$) under symmetric chemical potentials ($μ_t = μ_b$) due to the restoration of symmetry between the $k_y$ and $-k_y$ momentum channels. Conversely, when asymmetric chemical potentials are applied ($μ_t \neq μ_b$), this symmetry is broken, yielding a finite transverse response even for an in-plane Néel vector. Furthermore, we demonstrate that while the longitudinal differential conductivity exhibits Fabry-Pérot-type oscillations as a function of the TI's chemical potential, the transverse conductivity displays a distinct antisymmetric dependence. Notably, the oscillatory behavior of the longitudinal conductivity persists even at finite temperatures. Spatially, the transverse current contribution decays exponentially with position inside the TI region. We also find that both the longitudinal and transverse differential conductivity exhibit periodic oscillations with the junction parameter $χ_t=χ_b$. These findings provide a fundamental understanding of field-free transverse responses in spin-momentum locked boundary states, highlighting the potential of AM-TI heterojunctions for next-generation spintronic applications.

</details>


### [166] [Torsion, valley holonomy, and Berry phases of quasiparticles encircling dislocations and defect complexes in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04938)
*G. Q. Garcia,A. M. de M. Carvalho,C. Furtado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究石墨烯中低能准粒子绕缺陷运动所获得的几何相位（geometric phase），指出其可分解为拓扑谷因子（topological valley factor）与色散因子（dispersive factor），并给出缺陷复合体的完整和乐（holonomy）与三重着色规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从连续场论角度统一理解石墨烯缺陷（位错（dislocation）、向错（disclination）及其复合体）对低能准粒子几何相位的影响，并与已有晶格计算结果建立对应关系。

Method: 采用 Anandan 的 Poincaré 联络（Poincaré connection）和乐框架，其平动部分通过挠率（torsion）将动量耦合为 $P_a\int T^a$；在 Katanaev-Volovich 理论中位错挠率局限于缺陷核心。对石墨烯取相关动量为 $\hbar(τ_3\KK+\mathbf q)$，并通过 Volterra 元（Volterra elements）的复合得到缺陷复合体的和乐。

Result: 孤立位错在伯格斯矢量（Burgers vector）$\bb$ 位于 $\sqrt3\times\sqrt3$ 超晶格之外时给出拓扑谷因子 $e^{\mp2πi/3}$，且对任意非零 $\bb$ 均伴随色散因子 $e^{-i\mathbf q\cdot\bb}$；该拓扑因子以挠率—通量相位形式重现 Mesaros 等的晶格结果，而 4-8 位错仅保留色散贡献。零净 Frank 角时复合体和乐仅依赖净伯格斯矢量；谷通量遵循三重着色规则：五边形与七边形按 Kekulé 位点颜色贡献，四边形与八边形不贡献。原子模型显示单纯环组成不足以确定相位：5-8-5 核心作为双空位或 $|\bb|=\sqrt3a$ 的位错时不可见，但在 $\bb=2\mathbf a_1$ 时携带 $\pm1/3$ 通量。

Conclusion: 挠率的迹（torsion trace）不产生几何相位：其矢量耦合破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），而允许的轴矢耦合仅导致各向异性短程散射。几何相位因此可作为缺陷拓扑的敏感探针。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric phase acquired by low-energy quasiparticles encircling defects in graphene. Following Anandan, this phase is the holonomy of the Poincare connection; its translational part couples momentum to torsion, giving $P_a\int T^a$. In the Katanaev-Volovich theory, dislocation torsion is confined to the defect core. For graphene, the relevant momentum is $\hbar(τ_3\KK+\mathbf q)$. We show that an isolated dislocation yields a topological valley factor $e^{\mp2πi/3}$ when its Burgers vector $\bb$ lies outside the $\sqrt3\times\sqrt3$ superlattice, together with a dispersive factor $e^{-i\mathbf q\cdot\bb}$ for every nonzero $\bb$. The topological factor recovers the lattice result of Mesaros et al. as a torsion-flux phase; 4-8 dislocations retain only the dispersive contribution. Composing Volterra elements gives the holonomy of defect complexes. At zero net Frank angle, it depends only on the net Burgers vector. The valley flux obeys a three-coloring rule: pentagons and heptagons contribute according to their Kekule site color, whereas squares and octagons do not. Atomic models confirm that ring content alone does not fix the phase: a 5-8-5 core is invisible as a divacancy or as a dislocation with $|\bb|=\sqrt3a$, but carries flux $\pm1/3$ for $\bb=2\mathbf a_1$. The torsion trace produces no geometric phase: its vector coupling violates time-reversal symmetry, while the allowed axial coupling causes only anisotropic short-range scattering.

</details>


### [167] [Symmetry-protected isolation of the quantum metric in chiral type-II semi-Dirac semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04996)
*Wen-Hao Bian,Jing Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出第二类半狄拉克半金属（type-II semi-Dirac semimetals）的手征极限（chiral limit）可实现对称性保护的机制，将量子度规（quantum metric, QM）响应与贝里曲率（Berry curvature, BC）响应干净分离。当自旋轨道能隙 m_z=0 时，手征与 PT 对称性严格抑制 BC、其偶极（BCD）与 shift current，使 QM 及其偶极（QMD）成为光学与非线性霍尔响应的唯一来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多数固态平台中，贝里曲率（BC）与量子度规（QM）的信号相互纠缠，实验上难以分离，亟需一种对称性保护的机制来单独隔离 QM 效应。

Method: 利用第二类半狄拉克半金属的手征极限（m_z=0），借助手征对称性与 PT 对称性抑制 BC 相关通道；并通过推导非线性霍尔电导的标度律（scaling law），分析其与弛豫时间（relaxation time）的依赖关系以区分不同贡献。

Result: 纯 QM 区域展现出可辨识的实验特征：纵向光电导（longitudinal optical conductivity）发散，以及随化学势趋近节点时出现显著奇异性（singularity）的 QMD 诱导非线性霍尔电导；开启有限 m_z 后 BC 通道被重新激活，系统由纯 QM 相转变为混合几何相。

Conclusion: 手征第二类半狄拉克半金属是可调谐平台，可用于隔离并探测本征量子几何（quantum geometry）效应；基于弛豫时间依赖性的标度律为实验上分离 Drude、BCD 与 QMD 贡献提供了直接策略。

Abstract: Quantum geometry, comprising the Berry curvature (BC) and the quantum metric (QM), underpins a wide array of topological and transport phenomena. However, in most solid-state platforms, BC and QM signals are inherently entangled, making their experimental separation highly challenging. Here, we demonstrate that the chiral limit of type-II semi-Dirac semimetals provides a symmetry-protected mechanism to cleanly isolate the QM. At the chiral limit where the spin-orbit gap $m_z=0$, chiral and $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetries strictly suppress the BC, its dipole (BCD), and the shift current, leaving the QM and its dipole (QMD) as the sole sources of the optical and nonlinear Hall responses. This pure-QM regime exhibits distinct experimental fingerprints, including a divergent longitudinal optical conductivity and a QMD-induced nonlinear Hall conductivity that exhibits a pronounced singularity as the chemical potential approaches the nodal point. Turning on a finite $m_z$ reactivates the BC channels, transitioning the system from a pure-QM phase into a mixed geometric phase. By deriving a scaling law for the nonlinear Hall conductivity, we establish a direct experimental strategy to separate the mixed Drude, BCD, and QMD contributions based on their distinct relaxation-time dependencies. Our findings identify chiral type-II semi-Dirac semimetals as highly tunable platforms for isolating and probing intrinsic quantum geometric effects.

</details>


### [168] [ConvexAD: differentiable phase retrieval with geometric regularization for high-strain, low-signal Bragg coherent diffraction imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05020)
*Matteo Masto,Ewen Bellec,Etienne Polack,Jiangtao Zhao,Vincent Favre-Nicolin,Steven J. Leake,Tobias Schülli,Marie-Ingrid Richard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: ConvexAD 将物体形状参数化为由半空间交集构成的凸多面体，从构造上保证内部振幅平坦，以几何正则化缓解 Bragg 相干衍射成像（BCDI）中相位恢复（PR）的病态性与幅相混叠问题，并在高应变、低信号、低相干条件下优于传统与深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCDI 依赖相位恢复这一非凸、病态（ill-posed）逆问题，其条件数受噪声、部分相干（partial coherence）及纳米晶体中的应变或晶体缺陷等因素恶化。传统交替投影与深度学习方法采用稠密体素网格（voxel grid）表示物体，易产生幅相混叠（amplitude-phase mixing），在上述因素下病态性加剧，常难以可靠地三维映射颗粒形状与应变。

Method: 提出 ConvexAD，一种梯度下降（gradient descent）式相位恢复模型：将物体形状参数化为由半空间（half-spaces）交集构建的凸多面体（convex polyhedron），并通过构造强制其内部振幅平坦（flat amplitude）。该几何正则化消除幅相混叠并减少可训练参数数量，从而促进相位恢复收敛；模型在 JAX 中实现，以获得 GPU 原生加速与前向模型（forward model）的灵活性。

Result: 在模拟与实验 BCDI 数据上验证，ConvexAD 在高应变、低信号与降低相干性（reduced coherence）条件下优于传统方法及深度学习辅助的相位恢复。

Conclusion: ConvexAD 通过凸多面体几何参数化与振幅平坦约束，扩展了可进行定量 BCDI 重建（quantitative BCDI reconstruction）的条件范围。

Abstract: Bragg Coherent Diffraction Imaging (BCDI) relies on phase retrieval (PR), a non-convex, ill-posed inverse problem whose conditioning is degraded by factors like noise, partial coherence, and strain or crystallographic defects in the imaged nanocrystal. Conventional approaches, including alternating projections and deep learning methods, represent the object on a dense voxel grid, which is prone to amplitude-phase mixing and becomes increasingly ill-posed under these factors, often preventing reliable three-dimensional mapping of the particle's shape and strain. Here we present ConvexAD, a gradient descent PR model that parametrizes the object shape as a convex polyhedron, built by the intersection of half-spaces and enforces flat amplitude within it by construction. This geometric regularization eliminates amplitude-phase mixing and reduces the number of trainable parameters, hence facilitating the convergence of the PR. Implemented in JAX for native GPU acceleration and forward-model flexibility, ConvexAD is validated on simulated and experimental BCDI data, where it outperforms conventional and deep learning assisted PR at high strain, low signal, and reduced coherence, extending the conditions under which quantitative BCDI reconstruction is achievable.

</details>


### [169] [Twisting the pseudo-magnetic field lattice in periodically strained Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05122)
*L-M. Benneka,T. Rhouma,C. B. Winkelmann,M. Zelzmann,G. Trambly de Laissardière,V. T. Renard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文用紧束缚（tight-binding）计算表明，应变晶格（strain lattice）与原子晶格的扭转错位会破坏周期性形变导致的石墨烯（graphene）能带平坦化（band flattening），且平坦化由单一赝磁场磁通量（pseudo-magnetic field flux）参数普适控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性形变可压平石墨烯能带，从而有望像扭角石墨烯双层（twisted graphene bilayers）一样承载强关联电子（strongly correlated electrons），但超导、磁性等强关联效应在多次实验尝试后仍未观测到，其阻碍因素尚待澄清。

Method: 采用紧束缚（tight-binding）计算，考察周期性应变晶格相对原子晶格的扭转角对电子能带结构的影响。

Result: 两套晶格错位会消除能带平坦化；同时发现周期性应变引起的能带平坦化具有普适行为，仅由单一参数即赝磁场磁通量（pseudo-magnetic field flux）所控制。

Conclusion: 控制应变晶格的扭转是释放石墨烯周期性应变潜力的关键条件，也为解释相关强关联效应缺失提供了可能线索。

Abstract: Periodic deformations flatten electronic bands of graphene which could host strongly correlated electrons similar to those observed in twisted graphene bilayers. The most spectacular effects of strong correlations like superconductivity or magnetism have however not yet been observed despite several experimental attempts. Here, we use tight-binding calculations to investigate the effect of twisting the periodic strain lattice with respect to the atomic lattice. We find that misaligning the two lattices kills the band flattening. We also report on the universal behavior of the band flattening by periodic strain which is controlled by a single parameter, a pseudo-magnetic field flux. Our study strongly suggests that controlling the twist of the strain lattice is essential to unlock the potential of periodic strains in graphene.

</details>


### [170] [A Resonant-Tunneling Quantum-Dot Demultiplexer for Scalable Cryogenic Single-Electron Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05194)
*Rajat Bharadwaj,Harshita Bose,Madhu Thalakulam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并理论验证一种栅极定义的单个电子解复用器（single-electron demultiplexer）架构：三个并联量子点共享源极储池、耦合独立漏极通道，基于共振隧穿实现片上电子路由，通道选择性接近1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点自旋量子比特与单电子器件规模化受制于低温器件与室温控制电子学之间的布线开销（wiring overhead）。借鉴经典网络中的解复用器（demultiplexer）思路，用少量共享控制线选择性路由电子，可缓解该瓶颈。

Method: 构建栅极定义的三并联量子点结构（共源储池、独立漏极通道），采用解析静电模型（analytical electrostatic model）与紧束缚量子输运模拟（tight-binding quantum transport simulations），结合闭系统本征态分析与开系统输运计算，并考察势垒栅间距与柱塞栅尺寸工程。

Result: 闭系统分析显示各量子点存在局域轨道态；开系统计算在能级与入射电子能量对齐时出现共振电导峰，与本征能量演化一致。通过调控势垒栅间距与柱塞栅相对尺寸获得分离良好、通道重叠极小的共振；键电流密度（bond-current density）计算表明输运局限于共振通道，邻通道泄漏可忽略，所有共振模式通道选择性接近1。

Conclusion: 该架构兼容 Si/SiGe 与 GaAs/AlGaAs 平台并可扩展至更多通道，为可编程量子路由（programmable quantum routing）与基于共振隧穿的量子电路提供可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling quantum electronic systems such as quantum-dot spin qubits and single-electron devices is increasingly limited by the wiring overhead between cryogenic devices and room-temperature control electronics. Multi-channel quantum-dot architectures that selectively route electrons using a few shared control lines, analogous to demultiplexers in classical networks, offer a promising solution. Here, we propose and theoretically investigate a gate-defined architecture of three parallel quantum dots sharing a common source reservoir and coupled to independent drain channels. Using an analytical electrostatic model and tight-binding quantum transport simulations, we show that the device can act as an on-chip single-electron demultiplexer based on resonant tunneling in the cryogenic regime. Closed-system eigenstate analysis reveals localized orbital states in each dot, and open-system transport calculations show resonant conductance peaks when these levels align with the incident electron energy, consistent with the closed-system eigenenergy evolution. By engineering the barrier-gate spacing and the relative plunger-gate dimensions, we obtain well-separated resonances with minimal overlap between channels. Bond-current density calculations show that transport is confined to the resonant channel with negligible leakage into neighboring outputs, yielding channel selectivity approaching unity for all resonant modes. The architecture is compatible with Si/SiGe and GaAs/AlGaAs platforms and can be extended to more channels, offering a route toward programmable quantum routing and resonant-tunneling-based quantum circuitry.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [On the Geometric Structure of Hyperbolic Clifford Bundles and Associated Spin Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03886)
*Eduardo Notte-Cuello*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究实 $n$ 维向量空间 $V$ 所对应双曲 Clifford 代数（Hyperbolic Clifford algebra）$\mathcal{C}l(H_V)$ 的旋量结构（spinor structure），建立其上的正交群、Clifford–Lipschitz 群及 Pin/Spin 群，并导出标架丛与旋量结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Clifford 代数无法在同一代数结构中同时容纳 multiforms 与 multivectors；双曲 Clifford 代数被称为“母代数（mother algebra）”，可为理论物理中的超场（superfields）研究以及将 Clifford 丛（Clifford bundle）形式推广到引力理论中的双曲结构提供自然框架。

Method: 以实 $n$ 维向量空间 $V$ 上的双曲 Clifford 代数 $\mathcal{C}l(H_V)$ 为对象，给出双曲空间 $H_V$ 对应的正交群（orthogonal groups）与正交变换，进而定义 Clifford–Lipschitz 群及 Pin 群与 Spin 群，并在此基础上导出标架丛（frame bundle）与旋量结构。

Result: 构造了与双曲 Clifford 代数 $\mathcal{C}l(H_V)$ 相关联的旋量结构，并系统给出其正交变换、Clifford–Lipschitz 群、Pin 群与 Spin 群，以及相应的标架丛与旋量结构。

Conclusion: 双曲 Clifford 代数 $\mathcal{C}l(H_V)$ 可作为统一处理 multiforms 与 multivectors 的“母代数”，其群结构与旋量结构为超场研究及引力理论中双曲 Clifford 丛形式的推广提供了代数基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the spinor structure associated with the Hyperbolic Clifford algebra of a real $n$-dimensional vector space $V$, which is denoted by $\mathcal{C}l\left(H_{V}\right)$. Unlike the standard Clifford algebra, the Hyperbolic Clifford algebra $\mathcal{C}l(H_V)$ simultaneously accommodates both multiforms and multivectors in a single algebraic structure, making it the natural framework---known as the ``mother algebra''---for the study of superfields in theoretical physics and for generalizing the Clifford bundle formalism to hyperbolic structures arising in gravitational theories. The orthogonal groups and orthogonal transformations associated to the hyperbolic space $H_{V}$ are presented. The Clifford--Lipschitz group and the Pin and Spin groups associated with $\mathcal{C}l\left(H_{V}\right)$ are defined. Then, the frame bundle and spinor structure associated to Hyperbolic Clifford algebra is derived.

</details>


### [172] [A hybrid quantum-classical lattice kinetic scheme with tensor-train feedback for incompressible flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04500)
*Yongwei Guo,Yuan Yu,Yue Yu,Yuting Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种张量列（tensor-train, TT）接口，用于全松弛格子动力学（lattice kinetic）量子-经典混合求解器：在物理振幅尺度保留可复用的经典核心（core），以提供非线性系数并制备量子输入，避免每步按格点规模读出的开销。分析给出扰动界与资源界，并揭示分布精度不足以保障物理保真度（低秩截断会抹除涡旋（vortex））。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性格子动力学模拟中，每步更新后的分布须提供速度与梯度以决定下一步；在量子-经典求解器里读出完整分布的工作量正比于格点规模，成为瓶颈。因此需要一种能在量子传播与非线性反馈之间传递信息的紧凑接口。

Method: 构建张量列接口：保留物理振幅尺度的可复用经典核心，用于供给非线性系数与制备量子输入；采用能量缩放传播与坐标恢复编码更新分布，并用交替最小二乘（alternating least-squares）重建，通过恢复已知归一化因子把带符号的 Hadamard 检验重叠（Hadamard-test overlaps）转换为输出核心；下一步从被接受的核心直接启动，无需重放此前量子演化。

Result: 在正的单位和参考系数、正的密度下界与有限上界、Lipschitz 平衡敏感度假设下，给出将局部缺陷与反馈误差相联系的扰动界，以及涵盖经典操作、线路执行、采样与验证的资源界。诊断显示：$16\times16$ 网格 80 步反馈中，秩上限 4 在最终分布误差低于 $1\%$ 时几乎消除涡旋，而秩上限 12 的相对速度误差为 $1.03\times10^{-3}$；四步二项读出模拟中，核心采样容差收紧三倍使所需采样数预算从 $5.97\times10^{12}$ 增至 $5.37\times10^{13}$。

Conclusion: 该接口可将量子传播与非线性反馈相连接，并支持分别评估压缩、速度精度与读出精度三方面的需求；结果同时表明仅以分布精度作为评价指标并不充分。

Abstract: Nonlinear lattice kinetic simulation requires each updated distribution to supply the velocity and gradients that determine the next step. In a quantum-classical solver, reading out the full distribution incurs work proportional to the lattice size. We construct a tensor-train (TT) interface that retains reusable classical cores at the physical amplitude scale for a full-relaxation lattice kinetic scheme. The cores supply nonlinear coefficients and prepare each quantum input. Energy-scaled propagation and coordinate recovery encode the updated distribution, and alternating least-squares reconstruction converts signed Hadamard-test overlaps into output cores by restoring a known normalization factor. The next step starts from accepted cores without replaying previous quantum evolution. Under positive, unit-sum reference coefficients, positive lower and finite upper density bounds, and Lipschitz equilibrium sensitivity, perturbation bounds relate local defects to feedback errors. Resource bounds account for classical operations, circuit execution, sampling, and verification. Classical diagnostics expose why distribution accuracy alone is insufficient: in 80-step feedback on a $16\times16$ grid, rank cap 4 nearly eliminates the vortex despite a final distribution error below $1\%$, whereas rank cap 12 gives relative velocity error $1.03\times10^{-3}$. Four-step binomial readout simulations show that tightening core sampling tolerance threefold raises the prescribed sufficient budget from $5.97\times10^{12}$ to $5.37\times10^{13}$ shots as the result approaches the fixed-rank, fixed-sweep exact-overlap trajectory. The interface therefore provides a way to connect quantum propagation to nonlinear feedback and to assess the separate demands of compression, velocity accuracy, and readout precision.

</details>


### [173] [Eigenstates on $O^\ast$-algebras with an application to ladder operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04550)
*Fabio Bagarello,Hiroshi Inoue,Salvatore Triolo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文在 $O^\ast$-代数（$O^\ast$-algebra）上定义了一类称为“某元素的本征态（eigenstates of an element）”的状态，推导其性质，并将其应用于满足特定交换关系（commutation relations）的阶梯元素（ladder elements）族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于近期相关结果，作者希望刻画 $O^\ast$-代数上一类特殊状态（本征态）并系统研究其性质，同时考察具有物理动机代数结构的阶梯元素上的表现。

Method: 对 $O^\ast$-代数 $\M$ 中给定元素 $A$ 定义其本征态，推导该类状态的一般性质；随后将定义应用于满足特定代数（交换）关系且具有物理动机的阶梯元素族，讨论相关结果。

Result: 摘要仅说明推导出了本征态的若干性质，并针对阶梯元素族给出了多项相关结果，未给出具体定量结论。

Conclusion: 该定义可为 $O^\ast$-代数上具有物理背景的阶梯元素提供状态分析框架，具体结论需参见正文。

Abstract: Following some recent results, we consider here a particular class of states on a $O^\ast$-algebra $\M$ which we call {\em eigenstates of an element} $A\in\M$, and we deduce some of their properties. Then, we apply our definition to a family of ladder elements, i.e. elements of $\M$ obeying certain algebraic (commutation) relations which are physically motivated, and we discuss several results for these states

</details>


### [174] [An Excursion into Grassmannian Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04613)
*Dmitri Bykov,Viacheslav Krivorol*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究复格拉斯曼流形（complex Grassmannians）上 Laplace-Beltrami 与 Laplace-Bochner 算子的经典谱问题，通过一维 sigma 模型量子化发展两种互补方法，给出更显式的谱几何图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法依赖非交换调和分析（non-commutative harmonic analysis），但对格拉斯曼流形的谱几何缺乏显式刻画；本文旨在借助一维 sigma 模型量子化提供更具体的表述，并厘清调和函数、Young 图、磁丛与 Laplacian 谱之间的关系。

Method: 发展了两条源自一维 sigma 模型（含磁单极场与不含磁单极场两种情形）量子化的互补路径：其一基于 SU(n)-等变的一阶 GLSM 表述，用投影算子（projectors）与 Plücker 坐标给出显式本征函数；其二利用流形上自由粒子的自旋链（spin-chain）实现，以长矩形 SU(n) 表示的极限张量积描述平方可积函数空间（磁性情形下为截面空间）。

Result: 两种方法共同给出了格拉斯曼流形谱几何的更显式图像，并明确了调和函数（harmonics）、Young 图（Young diagrams）、磁丛（magnetic bundles）与 Laplacian 谱之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 一维 sigma 模型的量子化方法可有效补充传统非交换调和分析，为复格拉斯曼流形上的谱问题提供显式本征函数与表示论刻画。

Abstract: We revisit the classical spectral problem for the Laplace-Beltrami and Laplace-Bochner operators on complex Grassmannians. After reviewing the standard approach based on non-commutative harmonic analysis, we develop two complementary methods originating from the quantization of the corresponding one-dimensional sigma model, both with and without a magnetic monopole field. The first is based on $\mathbf{SU}(n)$-equivariant first-order GLSM formulation and gives explicit eigenfunctions in terms of projectors and Plücker coordinates. The second uses a spin-chain realization of free particles on manifolds and describes the space of square-integrable functions, or sections in the magnetic case, through limiting tensor products of long rectangular $\mathbf{SU}(n)$ representations. Together, these approaches provide a more explicit picture of the spectral geometry of Grassmannians and clarify the relation between harmonics, Young diagrams, magnetic bundles, and Laplacian spectra.

</details>


### [175] [Lorentz-Invariant Measure for Path Integrals in the Future Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04669)
*Vladimir V. Belokurov,Vsevolod V. Chistiakov,Klavdiia A. Lursmanashvili,Evgeniy T. Shavgulidze*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在二维空间未来光锥（future cone）内的连续路径上构造了一种测度，其关于正时洛伦兹群（orthochronous Lorentz group）不变、关于微分同胚群（diffeomorphism group）拟不变，并据此得到热方程（heat equation）的基本解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确交代研究背景与动机，仅表明目标是构造兼具洛伦兹不变性与微分同胚拟不变性的路径测度，并检验其可用性。

Method: 在二维空间未来光锥的连续路径空间上构造测度，使其在正时洛伦兹群作用下不变、在微分同胚群作用下拟不变；随后以该测度为主要工具求解热方程的基本解（fundamental solution）。

Result: 由该测度得到的热方程基本解与其他方法所得表达式一致，构成对该测度构造的一种独立验证。

Conclusion: 所构造的路径测度同时具备洛伦兹不变性与微分同胚拟不变性，并可用于导出热方程基本解，其结果与既有方法相符。

Abstract: We construct a measure on continuous paths in the future cone of two-dimensional space that is invariant under the orthochronous Lorentz group and quasi-invariant under the action of the diffeomorphism group.
  We use the proposed measure to obtain the fundamental solution of the heat equation. The result agrees with the expression obtained by other methods.

</details>


### [176] [An exact unstable mode of self-similar Yang--Mills solutionsin 5+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04861)
*Piotr Bizoń*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对五维杨–米尔斯方程（Yang–Mills equations）自相似解（self-similar solutions）谱性质中数值观察到的现象——所有激发解共享最小不稳定特征值 λ=5——给出一个初等解释，通过对相应特征函数（eigenfunction）的构造性刻画来说明这一谱特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自相似解在杨–米尔斯方程奇点形成（formation of singularities）中具有重要作用，五维情形下存在无穷多个不稳定模数递增的自相似解；数值上观察到所有激发剖面（profiles）共享最小不稳定特征值 λ=5，该论文旨在解释这一显著的谱性质。

Method: 采用基于剖面（profile）的初等构造方法，显式构造对应的特征函数（eigenfunction），以解释共享特征值的谱现象。

Result: 通过该构造说明所有激发剖面共享最小不稳定特征值 λ=5 这一数值观察到的谱性质。

Conclusion: 五维杨–米尔斯方程自相似解共享最小不稳定特征值 λ=5 的现象可由基于剖面的特征函数初等构造加以解释，从而为该谱性质提供解析依据。

Abstract: Self-similar solutions play an important role in the formation of singularities in the Yang--Mills equations. In five space dimensions, these equations admit infinitely many self-similar solutions with an increasing number of unstable modes. It was observed numerically that all excited profiles share the least unstable eigenvalue $λ=5$. We explain this remarkable spectral property by an elementary construction of the corresponding eigenfunction in terms of the profile.

</details>


### [177] [Multiplicity and parity in Hirota's $N$-soliton conditions: the bilinear families $L\,(D_x^{3}+Λ)^{2n+1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04953)
*Shi Zhenhua*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文沿两个方向检验双线性方程中"三孤子条件蕴含全 N-孤子解"这一通行蕴含。其一，对 $L\,(D_x^3+\Lambda)^{2n+1}f\cdot f=0$ 族给出四孤子残余的显式公式，证明 Gürses–Pekcan 猜想；其二，对若干特定色散簇的单一非超平面分支证明了该蕴含并给出完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hirota 三孤子条件是可积性判据，但"三孤子条件 ⇒ 所有 $N$ 的 $N$-孤子解"这一公认蕴含缺乏系统论证，而 Hietarinta 的分类正依赖于此。作者因此从具体方程族与一般色散簇两个方向考察该蕴含的适用范围与失效边界。

Method: 在色散簇（dispersion variety）的主分支上，将 $N$-孤子条件化为两个双交替式（bialternant）的符号和，并通过计数奇次指数分析该类方程；同时推导四孤子残余的显式公式，并对多重性为一的单一非超平面分支（non-hyperplane component）重构该蕴含，针对图型、偶二次曲面、抛物型等多种色散给出分类证明。

Result: 计数显示 $n=0$ 时该类为 Hirota 可积（包含 KdV、Ito、BLMP 方程），而 $n\ge1$ 时三孤子条件对任意 $L$ 均成立；四孤子残余公式证明 Gürses–Pekcan 猜想，即 $n\ge1$ 时四孤子条件全部失效，并给出 Gürses–Pekcan 与 Ito 方程的平方和（sum-of-squares）证书。该公式还分类了实成员并确定最大非退化孤子数：$n=1$ 时为四或五（取决于 $L$ 是否落在 $D_x,\Lambda$ 张成空间内），$n=2$ 且 $L=D_x$ 时为五，且对任意 $n\ge1$ 与任意 $L$ 均为有限。

Conclusion: "三孤子条件 ⇒ $N$-孤子解"的蕴含并非普遍成立：在 $L\,(D_x^3+\Lambda)^{2n+1}$ 族中 $n\ge1$ 时四孤子条件即已失败，孤子数存在上限。另一方面，对多重性为一的单一非超平面分支，该蕴含可在图型、偶二次曲面、抛物型、含 KP 曲面及立方图等一大类色散上被证明并完整分类。

Abstract: Hirota's three-soliton condition is the standard test for the integrability of a bilinear equation $P(D)f\cdot f=0$, and the folklore implication ``three-soliton condition $\Rightarrow$ $N$-soliton solutions for all $N$'' underlies the classifications of Hietarinta. We study this implication in two directions. The first is the family $L\,(D_x^3+Λ)^{2n+1}f\cdot f=0$ with $L$, $Λ$ linear forms in the Hirota operators, which for $n=0$ contains the Korteweg--de Vries, Ito and Boiti--Leon--Manna--Pempinelli equations and for $L=D_x$ is the family of Gürses and Pekcan. On the principal component of the dispersion variety the $N$-soliton condition reduces to a sign sum of two bialternants; counting odd exponents shows that for $n=0$ the class is Hirota integrable and that for $n\ge1$ the three-soliton condition holds for every $L$, and an explicit formula for the four-soliton residual proves the Gürses--Pekcan conjecture that the four-soliton condition fails for every $n\ge1$. The formula yields sum-of-squares certificates excluding four-soliton solutions at every real configuration for the Gürses--Pekcan and Ito equations, classifies the real members of the class, and determines maximal numbers of nondegenerate solitons: exactly four or five for $n=1$ according as $L$ lies in the span of $D_x,Λ$ or not, five for $n=2$ and $L=D_x$, and finite for every $n\ge1$ and every $L$. The second direction is a reformulation of the implication for a single non-hyperplane component of multiplicity one, which we prove, with complete classifications, for all graph-type dispersions $L(q(D)+D_t)$, for every irreducible even quadric component in any dimension and with an arbitrary cofactor, for parabolic components, for the dispersions $F(\ell)+Q$ containing the Kadomtsev--Petviashvili surface, and, with a prescribed one-parameter phase family, for the cubic graph.

</details>


### [178] [High-order compact ALE gas-kinetic scheme on three-dimensional unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05095)
*Yibo Wang,Xing Ji,Liang Pan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于三维非结构网格上可压缩任意拉格朗日-欧拉（ALE）计算的紧凑高阶气体动理学格式（HGKS），结合降内存重构策略、GENO重构与多GPU并行加速，并验证其精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALE计算中网格随时间移动，几何信息需每步重算，加之高阶空间重构需大量矩阵求逆，导致计算效率下降，因此需要兼顾高阶精度与效率的紧凑格式。

Method: 采用降内存重构（memory-reduction reconstruction）策略在非结构网格上构造二次多项式：通过微分候选多项式，利用单元自身及邻元平均梯度以最小二乘（least-squares）法获得二次项与线性项；采用广义基本无振荡（GENO）重构和梯度压缩因子抑制间断处振荡；基于CUDA与MPI实现多GPU加速，框架采用单元级并行。

Result: 三维非结构网格上仅需存储3×3系数矩阵并进行四次最小二乘过程即可构造二次多项式；数值算例涵盖强间断流动、俯仰翼（pitching wings）与直升机旋翼（helicopter rotors），验证了格式的精度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数值结果确认该紧凑ALE格式在非结构网格上具有优异性能，适用于含强间断的复杂可压缩流动问题。

Abstract: In this paper, a compact high-order gas-kinetic scheme (HGKS) is developed for compressible arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) computations on three-dimensional unstructured meshes. To achieve high-order spatial accuracy, a large number of matrix inversions are required for spatial reconstruction. In ALE computations, the mesh moves and the geometric information must be recalculated at each time step, which reduces computational efficiency. To improve efficiency, a memory-reduction reconstruction strategy is employed to construct a quadratic polynomial on unstructured meshes. By differentiating the candidate polynomial, the quadratic terms are obtained by the least-squares method using the average gradient values of the cell itself and its neighboring cells. The linear terms of the polynomial are also determined by the least-squares method. On three-dimensional unstructured meshes, a $3\times3$ coefficient matrix must be stored, and four least-squares procedures are required to construct a quadratic polynomial. To suppress numerical oscillations in flows with discontinuities, generalized essentially non-oscillatory (GENO) reconstruction and a gradient compression factor are adopted. To accelerate the computation, a multi-GPU accelerated code is implemented using Compute Unified Device Architecture (CUDA) and Message Passing Interface (MPI). The current scheme is fully explicit, and the acceleration framework can be developed based on cell-level parallelism. Numerical examples are presented to evaluate the accuracy and robustness of the scheme, including flows with strong discontinuities, pitching wings, and helicopter rotors. Numerical results confirm the excellent performance of the compact ALE scheme on unstructured meshes.

</details>


### [179] [Rasterization-based discontinuous integration on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05186)
*Manuel Saberi,Muqtasid Tashfin,Andreas Vogel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于计算机图形学光栅化（rasterization）的 GPU 方法，用于不连续函数（discontinuous functions）的高精度、高效积分，通过改进图形管线将不连续界面编码为几何图元并在光栅化阶段处理，配合多重采样抗锯齿（MSAA）提升界面处精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不连续函数的精确高效积分在数值计算中具有挑战性，而 GPU 的大规模并行架构（massively parallel architecture）为加速此类计算提供了潜力，因此需要一种能充分利用 GPU 图形处理能力的积分方案。

Method: 利用修改后的图形管线（graphics pipeline），将不连续性通过几何图元（geometric primitives）编码并在光栅化阶段处理，采用多重采样抗锯齿（multisample anti-aliasing, MSAA）改善子域界面（subdomain interfaces）上的积分精度；实现硬件与软件两类管线（含单精度与双精度版本），并与 CPU 上的自适应求积（adaptive quadrature）进行精度与计算量对比。

Result: 在约可比的中位相对误差（median relative error）下，硬件管线比自适应求积快约 100 倍，单精度与双精度软件管线分别实现最高约 840 倍与 1200 倍的加速；以有限胞元法（finite cell method）为模型问题、cut-cell 表示物理域与虚拟域界面处的不连续被积函数（integrands），端到端求解运行时间在单精度管线中减少 8.3–9.5 倍、双精度管线中减少 62–72 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于光栅化的积分方法（rasterization-based integration）是 GPU 上对高要求应用中不连续函数进行精确高效积分的一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method based on rasterization in computer graphics for the integration of discontinuous functions accurately and efficiently on the GPU. The proposed method takes advantage of the massively parallel architecture of GPUs via a modified graphics pipeline, where discontinuities are encoded through geometric primitives and processed in the rasterization stage, and multisample anti-aliasing (MSAA) is used to improve integration accuracy across subdomain interfaces. We present both hardware- and software-based pipelines and compare their performance in terms of accuracy as well as computational effort on modern GPU hardware with adaptive quadrature on the CPU. For the tested hardware, at approximately comparable median relative error, the hardware pipeline is up to around $100\text{x}$ faster than adaptive quadrature, while the single- and double-precision software pipelines achieve up to $840\text{x}$ and $1200\text{x}$ speedups compared to their adaptive quadrature counterparts. We use the finite cell method as model problem, where cutcells represent discontinuous integrands at the interface of the physical and fictitious domains and demonstrate the performance of the proposed method using numerical benchmarks. The end-to-end finite cell solution runtime is reduced by a factor of $8.3$ to $9.5$ in the single-precision pipeline and by a factor of $62$ to $72$ in the double-precision pipeline at the prescribed integration settings. Therefore, results indicate that the rasterization-based integration method is a promising alternative for the accurate and efficient integration of discontinuous functions in demanding applications on the GPU.

</details>


### [180] [Non-local approximations for the Schrödinger operator with a general point interaction via the non-relativistic limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05210)
*Lukáš Heriban*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究一维 Dirac 算子（Dirac operator）带一般非自伴点相互作用（point interaction）及其非局部近似，证明近似收敛关于光速 c 一致并给出显式收敛速率，进而说明非局部极限与非相对论极限可交换、双重极限存在，并由此得到 Schrödinger 算子 H_A 的一族秩二非局部近似及其耦合常数重整化（renormalization）公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文关注带一般（不必自伴）点相互作用的一维 Dirac 算子 D_{A,c} 及其非局部近似 D_{A,c}^ε 的收敛性，以及它与非相对论极限（non-relativistic limit）之间的关系，特别是两个极限（ε→0+ 与 c→+∞）能否交换。

Method: 以复 2×2 矩阵 A 描述原点处点相互作用强度并用光速 c 作尺度重整，将 Dirac δ 分布替换为对标度函数 v_ε 的秩一投影得到 D_{A,c}^ε；在范数预解收敛（norm resolvent sense）框架下证明关于 c 的一致性并给出显式收敛速率，同时显式计算固定 ε 时 D_{A,c}^ε 的非相对论极限。

Result: 证明 D_{A,c}^ε→D_{A,c} 在 c→+∞ 邻域内关于 c 一致，并给出显式收敛速率；结合固定 ε 下的非相对论极限计算，表明两个极限可交换且双重极限存在；作为副产品得到 H_A 的一族秩二非局部近似 H_A^ε，其在范数预解意义下收敛于 H_A。

Conclusion: 非局部近似与非相对论极限可交换，相应双重极限存在；所得 H_A^ε 与相对论情形不同，其耦合常数需重整化，文中给出了该重整化的显式公式。

Abstract: We consider the one-dimensional Dirac operator $D_{A,c}$ with a general, not necessarily self-adjoint, point interaction at the origin, whose strength is described by an arbitrary complex $2\times 2$ matrix $A$ and suitably rescaled by the speed of light $c$, together with its non-local approximations $D_{A,c}^ε$ obtained by replacing the Dirac distribution by a rank-one projection onto a scaled function $v_ε$. It is known that $D_{A,c}^ε\to D_{A,c}$ as $ε\to 0+$ and that $D_{A,c}-c^2/2$ converges, as $c\to+\infty$, to the Schrödinger operator $H_A$ with the general point interaction, both in the norm resolvent sense. We prove that the first convergence is uniform with respect to $c$ in a neighbourhood of $+\infty$, and we provide explicit rates of convergence. Combining this with an explicit computation of the non-relativistic limit of $D_{A,c}^ε$ for fixed $ε$, we show that the two limits can be interchanged and that the corresponding double limit exists. As a by-product we obtain a family of non-local, rank-two approximations $H_A^ε$ of $H_A$ that converge to $H_A$ in the norm resolvent sense. Contrary to the relativistic setting, the coupling constants of $H_A^ε$ are renormalized; we give an explicit formula for this renormalization.

</details>


### [181] [Dynamical Properties of the Multiple Elastic Pendulum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05312)
*Luis Rozas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究由任意数量质点经弹性弹簧链式连接构成的多重弹性摆（multiple elastic pendulum）的动力学特性，基于拉格朗日（Lagrangian）形式导出兼顾大角度位移与弹性形变的运动方程，并分析小振动下的本征值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双重弹性摆（double elastic pendulum）等低维系统已有成熟研究，但缺乏对任意质点数的链式弹性摆的一般化描述，本文旨在建立统一框架并将已知低维系统作为特例纳入其中。

Method: 采用拉格朗日形式（Lagrangian formulation）推导系统运动方程，同时计入大角度位移（large-angle displacements）与弹性形变（elastic deformations）；在稳定平衡点附近做小振动线性化，求解本征值问题（eigenvalue problem）。

Result: 一般运动方程在施加适当特定条件后，可直接系统地退化得到经典双重弹性摆等成熟低维系统；小振动情形下线性化方程可解，并显示角位移与弹簧伸长（spring elongations）之间呈解耦（uncoupled）行为。

Conclusion: 所建立的一般化拉格朗日模型为任意规模的链式弹性摆提供了统一动力学描述，兼具向下兼容低维特例与揭示小振动解耦结构的性质。

Abstract: This article studies the dynamical properties of the multiple elastic pendulum formed by an arbitrary number of point masses connected in a chain by elastic springs. First, using the Lagrangian formulation, the equations of motion governing the system's behavior, accounting for both large-angle displacements and elastic deformations, are derived. It is shown that well-established, low-dimensional systems, such as the classical double elastic pendulum, can be systematically recovered as particular cases directly from the general equations of motion after applying the appropriates particular conditions. The behavior of the system under small oscillations near its stable equilibrium is also investigated. For this case, the linearized equations allow the solution of the eigenvalue problem, demonstrating that in this case uncoupled behavior between the angular displacements and the spring elongations.

</details>

### [182] [Two examples in amplituhedron tilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05216)
*Evgeniya Akhmedova,Thomas Lam,Calvin Yost-Wolff*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了 \(m=4\) 放大体（amplituhedron）的一个剖分（tiling），其胞腔数（cell number）少于“神奇数猜想”（magic number conjecture）所预测的数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅可推断其针对“神奇数猜想”所给出的胞腔数下界提出反例，具体背景信息摘要中未涉及。

Method: 采用构造性方法，给出 \(m=4\) 放大体的一个具体剖分，其中使用了一个单射的二次胞腔（injective degree two cell）。

Result: 所构造剖分的胞腔数严格少于神奇数猜想所预期的数量，构成对该猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 神奇数猜想在 \(m=4\) 情形下不成立；引入二次单射胞腔可使剖分更为经济。

Abstract: We give an example of a tiling of the $m=4$ amplituhedron that has fewer cells than that of the magic number conjecture. The tiling uses an injective degree two cell.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Fixed-grid schemes for fractional moving-boundary problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04026)
*Conrard Giresse Tetsassi Feugmo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对分数阶时间导数的记忆通量Stefan问题，提出固定网格上的尖锐界面有限差分格式，以各节点熔化时刻h(x)=s^-1(x)存储记忆极限，并在自相似、源主导与Mittag-Leffler行波解上验证了精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landau变换xi=x/s(t)因分数阶时间导数（fractional time derivative）缺乏链式法则而失效，变换后算子会引入非局部积分且仅对自相似前沿有闭式表达，因此需要适用于固定网格的移动边界（moving boundary）数值方法。

Method: 构建守恒一致的记忆通量分数阶Stefan问题（memory-flux fractional Stefan problem）的尖锐界面（sharp-interface）有限差分格式：在固定网格上存储各节点熔化时间h(x)=s^-1(x)，以前沿积分能量平衡推进界面，并将奇异源定时到子步跨越时刻；对alpha<1的神经网络归一化坐标训练问题亦作讨论。

Result: 与已知自相似解对照，前沿常数误差为0.02–0.11%，而竞争格式在alpha=0.6时偏差达17–27%；残余正性违背源于dt/t0 ~ s0^(1-2/alpha)，在前沿锁定时间网格上消失。导出的源主导解与Mittag-Leffler行波解（traveling wave）均通过验证；经典极限下12 ms即优于耗时177 s的移动边界网络。

Conclusion: 固定网格、以熔化时刻为记忆变量的格式在分数阶移动边界问题中兼具高精度与高效率；归一化坐标下的神经网络求解器在alpha<1时会悄然改变问题本身，而该偏差在alpha=1处消失，恰是常规校验所在之处。

Abstract: The Landau transformation xi = x/s(t) is the usual way to hold a moving boundary in place. It fails for fractional time derivatives because they have no chain rule: the transformed operator gains a nonlocal integral with a closed form only for self-similar fronts. We develop a sharp-interface finite-difference scheme for the conservation-consistent memory-flux fractional Stefan problem on a fixed grid, storing the memory limit h(x) = s^-1(x) as the time each node melts. The front is advanced using an integrated energy balance, with the singular source timed to the sub-step crossing instant. Against a known self-similar solution, the front constant is accurate to 0.02 to 0.11%, whereas competing formulations differ by 17 to 27% at alpha = 0.6. A remaining positivity violation follows from dt/t0 ~ s0^(1 - 2/alpha) and disappears on a front-locked time grid. We also derive two exact solutions with non-power-law fronts: a source-dominated solution and a Mittag-Leffler traveling wave, and verify the scheme against both. Neural-network solvers for moving boundaries are often trained in normalized coordinates; for alpha < 1, this silently changes the problem, while the change vanishes at alpha = 1, where checks are commonly performed. In the classical limit, our scheme is more accurate in 12 ms than a reimplemented moving-boundary network in 177 s.

</details>
