### [184] [Constraints on a Long-Lived X17 Vector Boson with FASER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03904)
*FASER Collaboration,Roshan Mammen Abraham,Xiaocong Ai,Saul Alonso Monsalve,John Anders,Emma Kate Anderson,Akitaka Ariga,Tomoko Ariga,Jeremy Atkinson,Florian U. Bernlochner,Jianming Bian,Tobias Boeckh,Eliot Bornand,Jamie Boyd,Lydia Brenner,Angela Burger,Franck Cadoux,Haichuan Cao,Roberto Cardella,David W. Casper,Charlotte Cavanagh,Shiyang Chen,Xin Chen,Xing Cheng,Dhruv Chouhan,Emily Chown,Andrea Coccaro,Fabio Cufino,Stephane Débieux,Ansh Desai,Sergey Dmitrievsky,Radu Dobre,Monica D'Onofrio,Sinead Eley,Yannick Favre,Jonathan L. Feng,Carlo Alberto Fenoglio,Didier Ferrere,Max Fieg,Wissal Filali,Elena Firu,Haruhi Fujimori,Edward Galantay,Ali Garabaglu,Stephen Gibson,Sergio Gonzalez-Sevilla,Yuri Gornushkin,Yotam Granov,Jinjing Gu,Carl Gwilliam,Elie Hammou,Daiki Hayakawa,Michael Holzbock,Shih-Chieh Hsu,Zhen Hu,Giuseppe Iacobucci,Tomohiro Inada,Luca Iodice,Sune Jakobsen,Cesar Jesus-Valls,Arash Jofrehei,Hans Joos,Enrique Kajomovitz,Hiroaki Kawahara,Alex Keyken,Felix Kling,Daniela Köck,Pantelis Kontaxakis,Jelle Koorn,Umut Kose,Peter Krack,Susanne Kuehn,Thanushan Kugathasan,Sebastian Laudage,Lorne Levinson,Botao Li,Jiaxi Liu,Yi Liu,Joern Mahlstedt,Toni Mäkelä,Yasuhiro Maruya,Anna Mascellani,Lawson McCoy,Josh McFayden,Andrea Pizarro Medina,Hiroaki Menjo,Théo Moretti,Toshiyuki Nakano,Laurie Nevay,Ken Ohashi,Hidetoshi Otono,Lorenzo Paolozzi,Annabelle Parry,Pawan Pawan,Brian Petersen,Titi Preda,Markus Prim,Junkai Qin,Michaela Queitsch-Maitland,Juan Rojo,Hiroki Rokujo,André Rubbia,Jorge Sabater-Iglesias,Osamu Sato,Paola Scampoli,Kristof Schmieden,Matthias Schott,Cristiano Sebastiani,Anna Sfyrla,Davide Sgalaberna,Mansoora Shamim,Yosuke Takubo,Kakeru Tanaka,Simon Thor,Eric Torrence,Serhan Tufanli,Oscar Ivan Valdes Martinez,Svetlana Vasina,Emanuele Villa,Benedikt Vormwald,Chi Wang,Yuxiao Wang,Eli Welch,Aaron White,Monika Wielers,Benjamin James Wilson,Zhongyi Wu,Yue Xu,Heng Yang,Lekai Yao,Daichi Yoshikawa,Stefano Zambito,Shunliang Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Xingyu Zhao,Zijian Zhao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 结合FASER暗光子与轴子类粒子（ALPs）搜索数据，对17 MeV的X17候选粒子（protophobic vector boson）进行联合解释；未观测到超出本底的信号，排除了此前未受约束的电子耦合“低耦合窗口”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATOMKI反常（ATOMKI anomalies）暗示存在质量约17 MeV、与核子和电子弱耦合的新粒子X17；该粒子可能在LHC产生并沿远前向（far-forward）飞行480 m后在FASER衰变为e+e-，因而值得用已有FASER数据加以检验。

Method: 将FASER此前暗光子（dark photon）搜索与轴子类粒子（ALPs）搜索的数据合并，重新解释为对X17候选粒子（即protophobic vector boson）的搜索；所用数据为LHC Run 3中√s = 13.6 TeV的pp碰撞数据，暗光子搜索使用2022年数据，ALP搜索使用2022–2023年数据。

Result: 未发现超出预期本底的多余事件（excess events）；联合的零结果（null results）排除了电子耦合为6×10^-5 < ε_e < 1.2×10^-4的“低耦合窗口”（low-coupling window），该区间此前未被约束；“高耦合窗口”（high-coupling window）仍然存活。

Conclusion: FASER数据可对X17参数空间提供有效约束，已关闭低耦合窗口，但高耦合窗口仍未被排除，X17假设尚未被完全检验。

Abstract: The ATOMKI anomalies motivate the possibility of a new particle, the X17, with a mass of 17 MeV and weak couplings to nuclei and electrons. Such a particle may be produced at the LHC, travel 480 m in the far-forward direction, and decay to $e^+e^-$ in FASER. In this paper, previous FASER searches for dark photons and axion-like particles (ALPs) are combined and interpreted as searches for a candidate X17 particle, the protophobic vector boson. The searches use data collected from $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV in LHC Run 3 from 2022 for the dark photon search and from 2022-2023 for the ALP search. No excess events are found above the expected background, and the combined null results exclude a previously-unconstrained "low-coupling window" with electron coupling $6 \times 10^{-5} < ε_e < 1.2 \times 10^{-4}$. A "high-coupling window" remains.

</details>


### [185] [A New Bound on Light-by-Light Scattering in the Low-Energy Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04531)
*Aimé Matheron,Michal Šmíd,Felix Karbstein,Sebastian Göde,Willi Hippler,Robert Lötzsch,Naser Ahmadiniaz,Michal Andrzejewski,Sergey Antipov,Carsten Baehtz,Christina Bömer,Victorien Bouffetier,Erik Brambrink,Jakub Bulička,Samuele Di Dio Cafiso,Rafael Celestre,Jaromír Chalupský,Thomas E. Cowan,James P. Edwards,Sebastián Franchino-Viñas,Holger Gies,Jörg Grenzer,Travis Griffin,Věra Hájková,Jens Hauser,Thomas Heinzl,Uwe Hernandez Acosta,Peter Hilz,Hauke Höppner,Alžběta Horynová,Lingen Huang,Oliver Humphries,Šimon Jelínek,Libor Juha,Pooyan Khademi,Ben King,Christian Kohlfürst,Dietrich Krebs,Alejandro Laso-García,Alexander J. Macleod,Berit Marx-Glowna,Masruri Masruri,Nikita Medvedev,Anthony Mercuri-Baron,Elena A. Mosman,Motoaki Nakatsutsumi,Gerhard G. Paulus,Jennifer Popp,Thomas R. Preston,Isabella Probst,Sripati V. Rahul,Lisa Randolph,Ralf Röhlsberger,Thomas Roth,Sakshath Sadashivaiah,Felipe C. Salgado,Roland Sauerbrey,Alexander Sävert,Hans-Peter Schlenvoigt,Sebastian M. Schmidt,Ulrich Schramm,Ralf Schützhold,Jan Patrick Schwinkendorf,Frank Seiboth,Daniel Seipt,Georgiy Shoulga,Thomas Stöhlker,Minxue Tang,Abha Thazhathe Peedika,Monika Toncian,Toma Toncian,Maksim Valialshchikov,Vojtěch Vozda,Edgar Weckert,Colin Wessel,Jan Wild,Andreas Wipf,Ulf Zastrau,Matt Zepf*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作报告了X射线自由电子激光与高强度光学激光对撞实验，结合暗场探测（dark-field detection）与偏振测量（polarimetry）抑制本底，在低能区给出迄今最严格的光-光散射（light-by-light scattering）限制，较此前实验极限提升十个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学中的叠加原理（superposition principle）认为真空中电磁场不相互作用，但该原理并非普遍成立：电磁场可通过量子真空涨落（quantum vacuum fluctuations）发生弱耦合，其显著表现即光-光散射与真空双折射（vacuum birefringence）。论文旨在实验上探测并约束这一微弱效应。

Method: 采用X射线自由电子激光（X-ray free-electron laser）与高强度光学激光对撞的实验方案，并引入暗场探测与偏振测量两种手段进行本底抑制（background suppression）。

Result: 在低能区获得了迄今最严格的光-光散射实验限制，相对以往实验极限改善了十个数量级，并显示了该方案用于精密测量（precision measurements）的可行性。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了所提对撞与探测方案的灵敏度，为量子真空效应相关精密测量奠定基础，并有望进一步检验真空双折射等预言。

Abstract: The superposition principle at the heart of optics states that electromagnetic fields do not interact in vacuum. This principle is not universal, instead electromagnetic fields couple weakly via quantum vacuum fluctuations. Striking consequences are light-by-light scattering and vacuum birefringence. Experimental results from colliding X-ray free-electron and optical high-intensity lasers combined with dark-field detection and polarimetry for background suppression are reported. This approach yields the most stringent bound for light-by-light scattering in the low-energy regime achieved to date -- improving upon previous experimental limits by ten orders of magnitude -- and paves the way for precision measurements.

</details>


### [186] [A New Precise Measurement of the $e^+e^-\toπ^+π^-(γ)$ Cross Section with the Initial-State Radiation Method at BaBar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04596)
*BaBar Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用460 fb$^{-1}$ BaBar 初态辐射（initial-state radiation, ISR）数据，测量 $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ 从阈值到1.4 GeV的截面，并给出双π道对μ子磁异常（muon magnetic anomaly）的领头阶强子真空极化（hadronic vacuum polarization, HVP）贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双π道是强子真空极化贡献的主要来源，其精确测量对解释μ子磁异常至关重要；相较2009年BaBar分析，需降低系统误差并给出更稳健的截面结果。

Method: 采用 $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ 与 $e^+e^-\to\mu^+\mu^-$ 的比值方法以抵消共同系统效应，并首次通过角度分布（angular distributions）在统计上将π介子与μ子贡献分离。

Result: 在与2009年结果的相关性（统计与系统）纳入考虑后量化了两者兼容性；0.3–1.4 GeV能区领头阶HVP贡献为 $a_\mu^{\pi\pi}=525.9\pm3.5_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.8_\mathrm{syst}$（2026新测量）与 $517.8\pm2.4_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.3_\mathrm{syst}$（2009与2026合并），单位为 $10^{-10}$。

Conclusion: 新测量以更小的系统不确定性提供了双π道截面及HVP贡献的独立结果，并与其他双π测量在能量依赖与 $a_\mu$ 贡献层面进行了比较。

Abstract: We measure the $e^+e^-\toπ^+π^-(γ)$ cross section from threshold to 1.4 GeV using 460 fb$^{-1}$ of BaBar initial-state radiation data. The result is obtained from the $π^+π^-/μ^+μ^-$ ratio, reducing systematic uncertainties that are in common. Unlike the previous 2009 BaBar analysis, the pion and muon contributions are separated statistically through their angular distributions. The compatibility with the previous BaBar result is quantified including statistical and systematic correlations. The new measurement is also compared with other two-pion results as a function of energy and in terms of its hadronic vacuum polarization (HVP) contribution to the muon magnetic anomaly. The leading-order HVP contribution of the two-pion channel in the energy range of 0.3$-$1.4 GeV is $a_μ^{ππ}=525.9\pm 3.5_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.8_\mathrm{syst}$ and $517.8\pm 2.4_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.3_\mathrm{syst}$ in units of $10^{-10}$ for the new 2026 and combined 2009 and 2026 BaBar measurements, respectively.

</details>


### [187] [A New Precise Measurement of the $e^+e^-\toπ^+π^-(γ)$ Cross Section with the Initial-State Radiation Method at BaBar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04621)
*BaBar Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BaBar全部460 fb$^{-1}$的on/off-$\Upsilon(4S)$数据，利用$\pi^+\pi^-$与$\mu^+\mu^-$初态辐射（ISR）事件比，在阈值至1.4 GeV区间精确测量$e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$截面，并给出缪子反常磁矩（$g-2$）强子真空极化领头阶贡献的新数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$截面是缪子$g-2$预测中强子真空极化（hadronic vacuum polarization）贡献的关键输入，需要更精确且系统误差可控的测量。

Method: 采用460 fb$^{-1}$的on-与off-$\Upsilon(4S)$全数据，通过$\pi^+\pi^-$与$\mu^+\mu^-$两体末态中对应ISR事件的比值提取截面，使多项系统误差相消；粒子鉴别不再依赖2009年分析所用的粒子识别（particle identification），而利用两粒子质心系（center-of-mass system）中两类事件已知的角度分布差异进行$\pi/\mu$区分。

Result: 将2026年新测量与2009年结果的兼容性及相关性进行了分析，并与其他双π结果进行比较；用2026年单独数据及2009+2026合并数据在0.3–1.4 GeV区间计算的领头阶强子贡献分别为$525.9\pm3.5_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.8_\mathrm{syst}$与$517.8\pm2.4_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.3_\mathrm{syst}$（单位$10^{-10}$）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了系统误差受控的高精度双π截面测量，并给出两个版本的领头阶强子真空极化贡献值，可用于更新缪子$g-2$理论预测并评估其与早期BaBar结果的一致性。

Abstract: We report precise results for the $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-(γ)$ cross section in the energy range between threshold and 1.4 GeV, relevant for the hadronic vacuum polarization contribution to the muon $g-2$ prediction. Using the full 460 fb$^{-1}$ of on- and off-$Υ(4S)$ data, the cross section is derived from the ratio of $π^+π^-$ to $μ^+μ^-$ initial-state radiation (ISR) events, in which many systematic uncertainties cancel. The pion/muon separation does not rely on particle identification as in the 2009 BaBar analysis, but rather makes use of the well-known, different angular distributions in the two-particle center-of-mass system. The compatibility and correlation between the 2026 and 2009 measurements are discussed. The new measurement is also compared with other two-pion results. The leading-order hadronic contribution to the muon magnetic anomaly calculated using the new 2026 and combined 2009 and 2026 BaBar measurements in the energy range of 0.3$-$1.4 GeV are $525.9\pm 3.5_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.8_\mathrm{syst}$ and $517.8\pm 2.4_\mathrm{stat}\pm3.3_\mathrm{syst}$ in units of $10^{-10}$, respectively.

</details>


### [188] [Search for heavy neutral leptons in top quark decays with same-sign, same-flavor dilepton final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04904)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS 在 LHC 13 TeV 数据中搜寻顶夸克衰变产生的马约拉纳型重中性轻子（Majorana heavy neutral lepton, HNL），未观测到信号，并对相关衰变分支比给出 95% 置信水平的排除上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 I 型跷跷板模型（type-I seesaw model）中，马约拉纳 HNL 可介导破坏轻子数（lepton number）的顶夸克衰变，因此顶夸克衰变成为搜寻此类新粒子的敏感探针。

Method: 利用 CMS 探测器 2016–2018 年采集的 138 fb$^{-1}$、$\sqrt{s}$=13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据，针对顶夸克对产生事例，要求其中一个顶夸克衰变为底夸克与 W 玻色子，W 再衰变为带电轻子（电子或 μ 子）与 HNL，HNL 随后衰变为 W 玻色子及同号同味带电轻子；实验特征为同号同味轻子对（same-sign, same-flavor lepton pair）加喷注，探测 HNL 质量区间为 20–100 GeV。

Result: 数据与标准模型（standard model）本底预期相符；对顶夸克衰变为 HNL、带电轻子与底夸克的分支比（branching fraction）给出 95% 置信水平上限：双电子道（dielectron channel）观测（预期）值介于 [1.1 (0.71)]×10$^{-5}$ 与 [4.8 (3.0)]×10$^{-5}$，双 μ 子道（dimuon channel）介于 [3.7 (3.4)]×10$^{-6}$ 与 [2.4 (1.3)]×10$^{-5}$。

Conclusion: 在 20–100 GeV 质量范围内未发现马约拉纳 HNL 存在的证据，所设上限对 I 型跷跷板模型参数空间构成约束，具体数值仍依据摘要中的分支比上限。

Abstract: A search for a Majorana-type heavy neutral lepton (HNL) produced in top quark decays is reported. The search is based on proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, collected during 2016$-$2018 with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC. In the type-I seesaw model, a Majorana HNL can mediate lepton-number-violating top quark decays. The search targets top quark pair production events in which one top quark decays into a bottom quark and a W boson, which subsequently decays into a charged lepton (electron or muon) and an HNL. The Majorana HNL then decays into a W boson and a charged lepton with the same sign and flavor as the one produced with the HNL. The experimental signature features a same-sign, same-flavor lepton pair and jets. The HNL masses between 20 and 100 GeV are probed. The data agree with the standard model background prediction. Observed (expected) upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the branching fraction of the top quark decay into an HNL, a charged lepton, and a bottom quark, varying between [1.1 (0.71)] $\times$10$^{-5}$ and [4.8 (3.0)] $\times$10$^{-5}$ in the dielectron channel, and between [3.7 (3.4)] $\times$10$^{-6}$ and [2.4 (1.3)] $\times$10$^{-5}$ in the dimuon channel.

</details>


### [189] [Projected sensitivity of the \anubis detector to photophobic and fermion-coupled axion-like particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05107)
*Riley Aumonier,Oleg Brandt,Theo Reymermier,Francis Sayer,Paul Swallow for the ANUBIS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文给出 ANUBIS 探测器在高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）上对两类轴子样粒子（axion-like particles, ALPs）模型的预期灵敏度：在光恐（photophobic）情形下峰值灵敏度达 $\sigma_{Wa}\cdot Br(a\rightarrow gg)=6.2\times10^{-4}$ pb（$c\tau=1.95$ m，$m_a=10$ GeV），较 ATLAS 现有限制提升约两个数量级，并将 $c\tau$ 覆盖扩展三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子样粒子（ALPs）是寿命前沿（lifetime frontier）实验的常用基准模型，其与标准模型（Standard Model）粒子的受抑耦合可使其在探测器尺度上表现为长寿命粒子，因而需要评估新型探测器的探潜能力。

Method: 针对两个情景开展预期灵敏度研究：一是光恐 ALP 模型；二是对“超越碰撞物理倡议（Physics Beyond Colliders）”中味对角、费米子耦合 ALP 基准模型的修改版本，该版本引入额外的 $h\to Za$ 衰变。分析基于 ANUBIS 探测器在 HL-LHC 条件下的模拟预期。

Result: 光恐情形下，ANUBIS 在 ALP 质量 10 GeV、$c\tau=1.95$ m 处达到峰值灵敏度 $\sigma_{Wa}\cdot Br(a\rightarrow gg)=6.2\times10^{-4}$ pb，相比 ATLAS 现有排除限（exclusion limits）改善约两个数量级，$c\tau$ 覆盖扩展约三个数量级。含量纲七（dimension-seven）希格斯—ALP 相互作用的费米子耦合情形下，峰值灵敏度为 $C_{a^Φ}=4.4\times10^{-8}$，对应 ALP 质量区间 16–23 GeV。

Conclusion: ANUBIS 探测器可为两类模型提供独特的参数空间覆盖，其探测区域既无法被主要 LHC 实验覆盖，也无法被 HL-LHC 的前向探测器（forward detectors）覆盖。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) are a common benchmark for experiments at the lifetime frontier, as their suppressed couplings to Standard Model particles can render them long-lived on detector scales. This article presents the projected sensitivity of the ANUBIS detector at the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) to two scenarios: a photophobic ALP model and a modified version of the flavour-diagonal, fermion-coupled ALP benchmark of the Physics Beyond Colliders initiative that introduces an additional $h\to Za$ decay. For the photophobic case, the ANUBIS detector will reach a peak sensitivity of $σ_{Wa}\cdot Br(a\rightarrow gg)=6.2\times10^{-4}$~pb at $cτ=1.95$ m for an ALP mass of $10$ GeV. This will improve on the current exclusion limits from the ATLAS experiment by two orders of magnitude and extend the $cτ$ coverage by three orders of magnitude. For the fermion-coupled case with a dimension-seven Higgs--ALP interaction, the peak sensitivity to the fermion coupling is $C_{a^Φ}=4.4\times10^{-8}$, reached for ALP masses between $16$ and $23$ GeV. Overall, the ANUBIS detector is shown to provide unique coverage of the parameter space of both models, in regions that can be probed neither by the main LHC experiments nor by forward detectors at the HL-LHC.

</details>

### [190] [Continuum Dirac Theory and Nonlinear Extensions of a Chain of Alternating non-linear springs: an SSH version of the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04049)
*Gerardo G. Naumis,Ilder I. Salgado,Jorge Fujioka*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究由等质量质点与两组交替非线性弹簧构成的机械版 SSH（Su-Schrieffer-Heeger）模型的连续极限，证明该非线性二聚化链可映射为含合成自旋-轨道耦合、非阿贝尔贝里联络及拓扑零模的 Boussinesq 型双场系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 SSH 模型多为线性、单自由度形式，缺乏对非线性机械链中声学与光学自由度耦合、以及拓扑结构如何涌现的系统刻画，本文旨在建立机械体系中拓扑与相对论性孤子的直接实现。

Method: 在连续极限下将等质量、交替非线性弹簧的机械二聚链导出为 Boussinesq 型非线性色散双场双曲方程组，求出对应拉格朗日密度（Lagrangian density），并用变分理论（variational theory）求解孤子解；在布里渊区边界附近化为 Soler 型非线性狄拉克场论（nonlinear Dirac field theory），再通过分离快速空间振荡得到有效 Jackiw-Rebbi 哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）。

Result: 拉格朗日密度显示声学与光学自由度通过类似合成自旋-轨道耦合（synthetic spin-orbit coupling）的结构耦合，空间运动与内部子格自由度锁定，并出现涌现的非阿贝尔贝里联络（non-Abelian Berry connection）项；有效光场服从无阻尼无外力三次-五次 Duffing 方程（cubic-quintic Duffing equation），给出特定孤波解；畴壁处存在拓扑零模（topological zero modes），类比莫尔石墨烯（moire graphene）中的电子平带；在完全二聚化极限下零模表现为软模（floppy modes）。

Conclusion: 该工作为相互作用相对论性孤子与拓扑零模提供了纯机械实现途径，并讨论了系统趋于完全二聚化极限时的机械刚度转变（mechanical rigidity transition）及其对场的影响。

Abstract: A mechanical version of the SSH model, made of equal masses joined by two alternating non-linear springs is studied in the continuum limit. Such a non-linear dimerized mechanical chain generates a nonlinear, dispersive, two-field hyperbolic system of Boussinesq type of differential equations, describing coupled acoustic and gapped optical fields. The corresponding Lagrangian density is found, revealing that the acoustic and optical degrees of freedom are coupled through a structure that is formally analogous to a synthetic spin-orbit coupling, where the spatial motion is locked to the internal sublattice degree of freedom. An emergent non-Abelian Berry connection term also appears. Some particular solitary-wave solutions are found using variational theory, as the effective optical field obeys an undamped and unforced cubic-quintic Duffing equation. Near the Brillouin-zone edge, the system becomes a nonlinear Dirac field theory of the Soler type, providing a direct mechanical realization of interacting relativistic solitons. By factoring out fast spatial oscillations, an effective Jackiw-Rebbi Hamiltonian is obtained, with topological zero modes at domain walls. These modes are akin to the electronic flat bands in moire graphene systems associated with superconductivity and charge fractionalization. Finally, we discuss the mechanical rigidity transition and its effects on the fields as the system evolves toward the fully dimerized limit, in which the zero modes are floppy modes.

</details>


### [191] [Current control of spin-wave transport in YIG via the $\small\mathbf{VO}_{\mathbf{2}}$ insulator-to-metal transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04951)
*Aleksei A. Nikitin,Nikolai Kuznetsov,Lukáš Flajšman,Erkki Lähderanta,Sebastiaan van Dijken*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 通过VO₂的绝缘体-金属相变实现低功耗电控自旋波传输，传输抑制达40 dB，切换时间约10 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电流驱动方案所需的电流密度过高，削弱了自旋波信号处理本应具备的低耗散优势，因此需要低功耗的电控手段。

Method: 构建YIG薄膜与相邻VO₂条带耦合的混合磁子器件，以小幅电流触发VO₂的绝缘体-金属转变（IMT），并结合脉冲电流激励与变温测量及热学建模分离各机制贡献。

Result: VO₂电阻率下降超过三个数量级，局域改变YIG中自旋波色散，使群速度降低并在铁磁共振附近抑制传输达40 dB；脉冲激励实现约10 ms的重构时间尺度；热学建模确认IMT为主导控制机制。

Conclusion: 关联氧化物中的电致相变可作为能效高的动态自旋波操控策略，为可重构磁子器件提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Electrically reconfigurable spin-wave transport is central to realizing energy-efficient magnonic information technologies, yet existing current-driven approaches require current densities that undermine the fundamental dissipation advantage of spin-wave-based signal processing. Here, we demonstrate low-power electrical control of spin-wave propagation in a hybrid magnonic device comprising a low-damping yttrium iron garnet (YIG) film coupled to an adjacent vanadium dioxide ($\small\text{VO}_{2}$) stripe. Passing a small current through $\small\text{VO}_{2}$ triggers its insulator-to-metal transition (IMT), producing a sharp, reversible resistivity reduction exceeding three orders of magnitude. This abrupt phase transition locally modifies the spin-wave dispersion in YIG, reducing the group velocity and suppressing transmission by up to 40 dB near the ferromagnetic resonance frequency. Pulsed current excitation enables reconfiguration of spin-wave transport on a 10 ms timescale. Temperature-dependent measurements, corroborated by thermal modeling, decouple the respective contributions of current-induced heating of YIG and the IMT in $\small\text{VO}_{2}$, establishing the latter as the dominant control mechanism. Our results demonstrate that electrically induced phase transitions in correlated oxides constitute an energy-efficient strategy for dynamic spin-wave manipulation, providing a scalable route toward reconfigurable magnonic devices.

</details>

### [192] [From MHV Gravity Amplitudes and Hodges map to Pentad Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00864)
*Joris Koefler,Emanuele Ventura,Hongmiao Yu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 论文在 $\mathcal N=8$ 超引力（supergravity）散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）语境下，通过向量丛（vector bundle）构造将 Hodges 矩阵（Hodges matrix）实现为从两步旗簇（two-step flag variety）到对称矩阵（symmetric matrices）的典范有理映射（Hodges map），并据此几何化地定义振幅及其消失轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示 $\mathcal N=8$ 超引力散射振幅中出现的 Hodges 矩阵背后的几何结构，为振幅提供一个几何化的刻画方式。

Method: 采用向量丛（vector bundle）构造，证明 Hodges 矩阵给出从两步旗簇（two-step flag variety）到对称矩阵（symmetric matrices）的典范有理映射，即 Hodges 映射（Hodges map）；并受其启发引入两簇的 Hadamard 比（Hadamard ratio）概念。

Result: 振幅的消失轨迹（vanishing locus）是一个除子，称为 MHV 引力除子（MHV gravity divisor）；与引力相关的情形给出两个 Grassmannian 之比，称为五元簇（pentad variety），并与代数统计（algebraic statistics）中的五元约束（pentad constraint）相联系。

Conclusion: 五元簇（pentad variety）与 Martin 仿射斜率簇（Martin's affine slope variety）的组合代数几何（combinatorial algebraic geometry）以及 Below、Krummeck 与 Richter-Gebert 的 phirotopes 密切相关，表明该几何框架串联了振幅、代数统计与组合代数几何多个方向。

Abstract: We study some geometric aspects behind the Hodges matrix appearing in the context of scattering amplitudes for the $\mathcal N=8$ supergravity. Through a vector bundle construction, we show that the Hodges matrix gives rise to a canonical rational map from the two-step flag variety to symmetric matrices, called the $\textit{Hodges map}$. This construction allows to define the amplitude geometrically: its vanishing locus is a divisor - the $\textit{MHV gravity divisor}$. Inspired by the Hodges map, we introduce the notion of Hadamard ratio of two varieties. In the case relevant to gravity, this is the ratio of two Grassmannians, which we call the $\textit{pentad variety}$, connecting it to the pentad constraint from algebraic statistics. Moreover, we show that the pentad variety is closely related to the combinatorial algebraic geometry of Martin's affine slope variety, and the phirotopes of Below, Krummeck, and Richter-Gebert.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Glauber dynamics of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin-glass model: a dynamical cavity method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04078)
*Grzegorz Szamel*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过动态空腔方法构建了一个在 $N\to\infty$ 极限下严格成立的封闭理论，用于描述Sherrington–Kirkpatrick（SK）自旋玻璃模型在Glauber动力学下于 $T_c$ 之上及 $T_c$ 处的平衡自旋关联，理论与计算机模拟高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SK自旋玻璃模型在Glauber动力学下的平衡自旋关联此前缺乏在 $N\to\infty$ 极限下严格且封闭的理论描述，尤其缺乏对转变温度附近及临界点动力学行为的定量预测。

Method: 采用动态空腔方法（dynamic cavity approach）：在被选自旋的演化方程中，其他自旋由涨落场（fluctuating field）代表；该场受被选自旋的影响以线性响应（linear response）水平纳入，表现为被选自旋过去翻转（past flips）的加权求和，权重为未受扰动涨落场的关联；再由自洽条件（self-consistency condition）将两者关联联系起来。

Result: 理论预测临近转变时弛豫时间（relaxation time）呈 $(T-T_c)^{-2}$ 发散，并在 $T_c$ 处自旋关联以 $At^{-1/2}$ 衰减；数值求解给出振幅 $A=0.964\pm 0.007$，且理论预测与Glauber动力学计算机模拟结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该封闭理论在 $N\to\infty$ 极限下严格成立，成功刻画了SK模型在 $T_c$ 之上与 $T_c$ 处的平衡自旋关联动力学，并为临界减速与临界衰减行为提供了可定量检验的预言。

Abstract: We present a closed theory, exact in the $N\to\infty$ limit, of the equilibrium spin correlations of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin-glass model evolving with Glauber dynamics, above and at the transition temperature $T_c$. The derivation uses a dynamic cavity approach: in the evolution equation for the selected spin the other spins are represented by a fluctuating field. The influence of the selected spin on this field is included at the linear response level; it is given as a sum of the spin's past flips weighted by the correlations of the unperturbed fluctuating field. The self-consistency condition relates the time-dependent correlations of the selected spin and those of the fluctuating field. The theory predicts a $(T-T_c)^{-2}$ divergence of the relaxation time upon approaching the transition and an $At^{-1/2}$ decay of spin correlations at $T_c$. A numerical solution of the theory gives the amplitude, $A=0.964\pm 0.007$. The theoretical predictions are in excellent agreement with Glauber dynamics computer simulations.

</details>


### [194] [Optical Ceiling on Polaron Mobility in Disordered Molecular Semiconductors: Lessons from Eumelanin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04986)
*Alvan Hunt*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文提出无序分子半导体（如黑色素 eumelanin）中的极化子跃迁（polaron hopping）问题可由约化极化子吸收带（reduced polaron absorption band）编码；零能处吸收值给出平均跃迁速率，从而给出任意维度迁移率（mobility）上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化子跃迁由能量无序（energetic disorder σ）与重组能（reorganization energy λ）决定，需要建立输运与光学吸收之间的定量联系，以便用光谱手段约束迁移率。

Method: 针对高斯无序（Gaussian disorder）与经典振动，解析推导约化吸收带的一阶矩（centroid）E_op=λ+(1-c)σ²/k_BT、遵循 Hush 关系的方差，以及由零能吸收确定的平均跃迁速率迁移率界；并给出 λ→E_op 时退化为 Marcus 速率（Marcus rate）的界及一维下界。

Result: 零能吸收固定平均跃迁速率，给出任意维度的迁移率上界；质心满足 E_op=λ+(1-c)σ²/k_BT，方差遵循 Hush 关系；界在 λ→E_op 时即 Marcus 速率；一维下界在 k_BT*=(1-c)σ²/λ 处消失，可由光学读取。

Conclusion: 约化极化子吸收谱可作为输运性质的探针：通过光学量（质心、零能吸收、kT*）即可推算迁移率上界及跃迁速率，为无序分子半导体的输运表征提供光谱途径。

Abstract: Polaron hopping in disordered molecular semiconductors such as eumelanin is set by energetic disorder $σ$ and reorganization energy $λ$. We show that the reduced polaron absorption band encodes this transport problem. Its value at zero energy fixes the mean hop rate, which bounds the mobility in any dimension. For Gaussian disorder and classical vibrations its centroid is $E_\mathrm{op}=λ+(1-c)σ^2/k_BT$, its variance obeys the Hush relation, and the bound is the Marcus rate at $λ\to E_\mathrm{op}$; a 1D lower bound vanishes at $k_BT^*=(1-c)σ^2/λ$, read from optics.

</details>

### [195] [The Nonlinear Physics and Statistical Field Theory of Twilight Clouds: Hovering Dynamics and Dynamic Inertia Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04924)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,Ilan Koren*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文构建非平衡统计场论（nonequilibrium statistical field theory），将微弱的暖云（warm cloud）液滴在霾与活化增长间的涨落，与云场组织相联系，统一处理Köhler微物理、过饱和度输送与湍流混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Twilight clouds 光学微弱、液滴在霾与活化增长之间涨落，此前缺乏将微观微物理与云场组织联系起来的理论框架，故需建立统一描述。

Method: 从过饱和度湍流输送耦合液滴增长出发导出液滴—过饱和场系统，纳入Martin-Siggia-Rose框架；借助受极化子理论启发的Gurari型变分约化消去快速浴，得到含记忆项的非线性非马尔可夫伪抛物型随机方程；并与母体双场系统对照检验空间相关的马尔可夫闭合。

Result: 混合导致的过饱和去除引发鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation），改变活化阈值并在湍流涨落下支持间歇性悬浮；湍流"着装"慢液滴场、缩小平衡曲线下方禁区并提高抑制活化所需的混合阈值；每个液滴群产生屏蔽过饱和晕（screened supersaturation halo），其延迟调整带来动态惯性与时间相关噪声。近活化折叠处，保留残余浴涨落记忆可在不重标定漂移、不拟合相关时间的前提下改善回复悬浮频率与斑块范围。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了慢场如何通过其改性的弛豫、扩散环境获得相互作用与惯性；空间相关马尔可夫闭合可保持液滴半径分布与云团组织，为暖云组织提供统计场论基础。

Abstract: Twilight clouds are small, optically faint warm clouds whose droplets fluctuate between haze and activated growth. We develop a nonequilibrium statistical field theory linking this regime to cloud-field organization through Köhler microphysics, supersaturation transport, and turbulent mixing. At parcel level, supersaturation rapidly tracks slow droplet growth with a finite lag. Mixing-induced supersaturation removal drives a saddle-node bifurcation, shifting the activation threshold and supporting intermittent hovering under turbulent fluctuations. Starting from turbulent transport of supersaturation coupled to droplet growth, we derive a droplet-supersaturation field system and formulate it within the Martin-Siggia-Rose framework. A Gurari-type variational reduction inspired by polaron theory eliminates the fast bath. Turbulence dresses the slow droplet field, shrinks the forbidden zone below the equilibrium curve, and raises the mixing threshold required to suppress activation. Each droplet population induces a screened supersaturation halo that couples neighboring cloud elements; its delayed adjustment generates dynamic inertia and temporally correlated noise. The resulting nonlinear, non-Markovian pseudoparabolic stochastic equation has a bounded frozen-background response at short wavelengths. Its long-wave coupling amplifies contrasts where the dressed slow manifold turns downward. Comparisons with the parent two-field system show that a spatially correlated Markovian closure preserves droplet-radius distributions and cloudlet organization. Near the activation fold, restoring residual-bath fluctuation memory improves recurrent-hovering frequency and patch extent without recalibrating the drift or fitting a correlation time. More broadly, the framework identifies how slow fields acquire interactions and inertia through the relaxing, diffusive environments they modify.

</details>


### [196] [Designing turbulence through hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04706)
*Flavio Tuteri,André Freitas*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 摘要提出，仅由几何非线性（geometric nonlinearity）的刚性约束耦合而成的惯性层级（hierarchy of inertia）即可产生湍流的全部核心涌现现象，并以长度与质量几何递减的刚性摆链作为实验/理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流的若干涌现与奇异现象（恒定通量能量级联、间歇性、耗散率在黏性趋于零时仍存活、无黏极限下决定性的自发丧失）的物理来源不明，因为在流体中它们总与不可压缩性、空间结构和涡动力学（vortex dynamics）混杂在一起，难以分离。

Method: 构建平面刚性摆链（planar chain of rigid pendula），其长度与质量按几何比例递减，仅通过最近邻约束耦合；该体系提供惯性层级，非线性完全来自刚性约束的纯几何效应，可在力学或电学上实现。

Result: 系统出现取代热平衡（thermal equipartition）的远离平衡级联（far-from-equilibrium cascade），尽管耦合本质是非局域的，能量传递在统计上仍表现为局域且时间不可逆；涨落呈间歇性（intermittency），但隐藏的标度对称性恢复了自相似性；黏性趋于零时耗散率饱和于有限值，并与涨落-耗散平衡（fluctuation-dissipation balance）一致的热噪声在小尺度到大尺度间于大尺度周期的极小部分内被放大。

Conclusion: 湍流可由可调谐的惯性层级单独实现，且因同一动力学可力学或电学实现，湍流成为可在实验室中设计与测量的性质（property that can be designed and measured）。

Abstract: Turbulence is defined by a set of emergent and singular phenomena: a constant-flux energy cascade, intermittency, a dissipation rate that survives the vanishing of viscosity, and the spontaneous loss of determinism of the inviscid limit. Which physical ingredients produce them remains unclear, because in fluids they always come together with incompressibility, spatial structure and vortex dynamics. Here we show that a hierarchy of inertia, coupled by the purely geometric nonlinearity of rigid constraints, is enough. A planar chain of rigid pendula whose lengths and masses decrease geometrically, coupled only through nearest-neighbor constraints, develops a far-from-equilibrium cascade that replaces thermal equipartition, with statistically local and time-irreversible energy transfer despite intrinsically nonlocal coupling. Its fluctuations are intermittent, yet self-similarity is restored by a hidden scale symmetry. As the viscosity vanishes, the dissipation rate saturates at a finite value, and thermal noise consistent with fluctuation-dissipation balance is amplified from the smallest to the largest scales within a fraction of a large-scale period. Because the hierarchy can be tuned and the same dynamics can be realized mechanically or electrically, turbulence becomes a property that can be designed and measured in the laboratory.

</details>

### [197] [Confining strings in the reconfined phase of trace deformed three dimensional $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03874)
*Claudio Bonati,Michele Caselle,Alessio Negro,Dario Panfalone,Lorenzo Verzichelli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 在三维 $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ Yang-Mills 理论中加入迹形变（trace deformation）后，理论在标准退禁闭温度以上仍保持禁闭（reconfined phase）。格点模拟显示，随形变增强，通量管（flux tube）基态能量偏离 Nambu-Gotō 弦描述，并在深度重禁闭相由 Polchinski-Yang 刚性弦（rigid string）准确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迹形变可使规范理论在高于常规退禁闭温度（deconfinement temperature）的区域维持禁闭，形成所谓重禁闭相（reconfined phase）；该相中禁闭通量管（confining flux tube）的有效弦描述尚不清楚，需要检验其是否仍遵循标准 Nambu-Gotō 模型。

Method: 采用格点模拟（lattice simulations）计算 Polyakov 环关联函数（Polyakov loop correlators），研究三维迹形变 $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ 理论中的禁闭通量管，并考察其基态能量随形变强度的变化。

Result: 随形变增大，基态能量逐渐偏离 Nambu-Gotō 有效弦（effective string）描述；在重禁闭相深处，数据被 Polchinski-Yang 刚性弦（rigid string）准确拟合，该有效弦以外在曲率项（extrinsic-curvature term）为主导。

Conclusion: 迹形变改变了禁闭通量管的有效弦理论性质：从 Nambu-Gotō 型转向由外在曲率主导的刚性弦描述，表明重禁闭相中的禁闭机制具有不同于常规 Yang-Mills 真空的有效自由度特征。

Abstract: Adding a trace deformation to the Yang-Mills action keeps the theory confining above the standard deconfinement temperature, in the so-called reconfined phase. We study the confining flux tube of the trace-deformed $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ theory in three dimensions with lattice simulations of Polyakov loop correlators. As the deformation increases, the ground state energy departs from the usual Nambu-Gotō effective string description, and deep in the reconfined phase the data are accurately described by the Polchinski-Yang rigid string, an effective string dominated by an extrinsic-curvature term.

</details>

### [198] [Linear Response in the $\mathcal{PT}$-Broken Phase: Conductivity of Fermions with Complex Conjugate Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03730)
*V. E. R. Lemes,D. G. Tedesco*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一个BRST不变模型的$\mathcal{PT}$破缺相（$\mathcal{PT}$-broken phase）的线性响应，其费米子质量构成复共轭对$m_\pm=N\pm iav$。模型在一个精确的$\mathbb{Z}_2$下分裂为两个通道，单个欧氏关联函数（Euclidean correlator）即可判定二者性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于判定$\mathcal{PT}$破缺相中哪一通道能实现正范数（positive-norm）的物理希尔伯特空间，并厘清正性（positivity）在有限温度下的适用边界。

Method: 以欧氏关联函数为判据：在对角通道（diagonal channel）中构造负范数检验函数，并逐一排除各类修复方案（包括边界值的选择）；对混合通道（mixed channel）验证关联函数完全单调（completely monotone）。

Result: 对角通道在欧氏时间上振荡，因此不存在正范数实现。混合通道中关联函数的完全单调性证明谱密度为正，并给出闭式真空电导率（vacuum conductivity），阈值实为$\omega=2N$；有限温度下正性在可精确给出起始点的界内成立。该通道纵向密度（longitudinal density）按质量劈裂的平方为负，用反对称导数可将其移除。

Conclusion: 所得场强复合算符（field-strength composite）具有正的单粒子空间与因果对易子（causal commutator），但仅在两点函数层面上成立；原复合算符上的四点反射形式（reflection form）为负。因此该正实现并非费米子模型的某个扇区。

Abstract: We examine the linear response of the $\PT$-broken phase of a BRST invariant model whose fermion masses form the complex conjugate pair $m_{\pm}=N\pm iav$. It splits into two channels, kept apart by an exact $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$, and one Euclidean correlator decides both. In the diagonal channel it oscillates in Euclidean time, so that channel admits no positive-norm realization: we exhibit a test function of negative norm and close every repair, the choice of boundary value among them. In the mixed channel the same correlator is completely monotone, which proves the spectral density positive; there we obtain the vacuum conductivity in closed form, with a real threshold at $ω=2N$, and positivity survives at finite temperature below a bound whose onset we give exactly. The longitudinal density of that channel is negative by the square of the mass splitting; an antisymmetric derivative removes it, and the resulting field-strength composite has a positive one-particle space and a causal commutator --- at the level of the two-point function only, since a four-point reflection form on the original composite comes out negative. The positive realization is therefore not a sector of the fermion model.

</details>


### [199] [Meet biform singletons in your area! Topological actions for edge modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03780)
*Calvin Y. -R. Chen,Euihun Joung,Karapet Mkrtchyan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出以双形式单态场（biform singleton fields）作为边缘模式的拓扑场论（topological field theories）。作者借助辅助体空间 AdS 几何与协变边界约化（covariant boundary reduction）程序，构造出具有显式洛伦兹协变性的新作用量形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双形式单态场在偶数维时空中的对偶性质尚待从拓扑场论角度统一刻画，且需获得显式洛伦兹协变的作用量表述。作者希望几何化单态场（singletons）内禀的隐藏共形对称性，并由此导出新的作用量框架。

Method: 在偶数维时空 D=2p 中，将双形式单态场视为共形代数 so(2,2p) 的不可约表示（对应高度为 p 的双列杨图）。通过辅助体空间 AdS_{2p+1} 几何实现隐藏共形对称性以构造拓扑作用量，再应用已建立的协变边界约化程序得到显式洛伦兹协变的表述。

Result: 双形式单态场的对偶性依赖 p 的奇偶性：奇数 p 时传播手征自由度（chiral degrees of freedom），偶数 p 时呈现电磁对偶（electric–magnetic duality）。新方法给出具有显式洛伦兹协变性的新颖作用量形式；在破坏显式洛伦兹协变的情形下，可回到已知的双形式单态场表述。

Conclusion: 拓扑作用量可由单态场隐藏共形对称性的 AdS 几何化自然构造，协变边界约化提供了统一且显式协变的作用量框架，并能与既有表述相衔接。

Abstract: We present topological field theories propagating biform singleton fields as edge modes. In even spacetime dimensions $D=2p$, these are irreducible representations of the conformal algebra $\mathfrak{so}(2,2p)$ with two-column Young diagrams of height $p$. Their duality properties depend on the parity of $p$: They propagate chiral degrees of freedom for odd $p$, and exhibit electric--magnetic duality for even $p$. We are able to construct the topological actions by realising the hidden conformal symmetry inherent to singletons geometrically in terms of an auxiliary bulk $\text{AdS}_{2p+1}$ geometry. Using these as a starting point, we are able to apply a recently established covariant boundary reduction procedure to yield novel action formulations with manifest Lorentz covariance. We also show how, breaking manifest Lorentz covariance, we recover known formulations of biform singletons.

</details>


### [200] [Worldsheet functional RG and the $α'$-tower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03781)
*Ivano Basile,Francesco Ferrarin,Alessia Platania,Matteo Zatti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, FRG）应用于玻色弦（bosonic string）的世界面理论（worldsheet theory），通过要求外尔反常（Weyl anomaly）消除来推导背景耦合的重整化群流方程及有效时空运动方程，并在一类全阶α'截断下重现Tseytlin有效作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弦论世界面方法难以直接处理强曲率（strong-curvature）区域，作者希望通过泛函重整化群为世界面理论提供一个系统性框架，以获取超越低能有效场论的α'全阶信息，并最终检验弦论中"有效渐近安全（effective asymptotic safety）"的设想。

Method: 在世界面上实施泛函重整化群，将外尔反常消除作为局域重整化群流的约束条件，首先在一般弯曲背景下以两导数（two-derivative）截断推导背景耦合的流方程与有效时空运动方程；随后采用类似f(R)型近似、包含α'全阶但仅捕获部分张量结构的可解析截断。

Result: 两导数截断下得到相应时空运动方程；在α'全阶截断下重现了Tseytlin的时空有效作用量（spacetime effective action），并据此研究了α'全阶解，包括黑洞（black holes）解。

Conclusion: 结果表明泛函重整化群可在弦世界面上实现，并提供进入强曲率区域的框架；长远而言，这或可用于检验弦论中的有效渐近安全情景，即低能有效场论与弦论完备之间存在的近似标度不变区（scale-invariant regime）。

Abstract: We apply the functional renormalization group to the worldsheet theory of the bosonic string. Imposing the Weyl anomaly cancellation as a condition on the local renormalization group flow, we derive flow equations for the background couplings and the corresponding effective spacetime equations of motion, first in a two-derivative truncation, on a general curved background. We then consider a truncation to all orders in $α'$, which captures only a subset of the possible tensor structures but is analytically tractable, in analogy with $f(R)$-like approximations. Within this truncation, we obtain Tseytlin's spacetime effective action, and we use it to study solutions to all orders in $α'$, including black holes. Our results show that the functional renormalization group can be implemented on the string worldsheet and provide a framework to access the strong-curvature regime. As a longer-term goal, this may allow one to test the scenario of effective asymptotic safety in string theory, in which an approximately scale-invariant regime lies between the low-energy effective field theory and a stringy completion.

</details>


### [201] [A New Holographic Duality for Conformal Mass Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03784)
*Raphaël Dulac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在半空间构造 ABJM 理论的空间依赖共形质量形变，证明其真空对偶于一大类形如 AdS₃×S³×S³×Σ 的半 BPS 冒泡解，并首次用场论给出 M2-膜理论中例外超代数参数 γ 的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为 M2-膜场论中例外超代数 D(2,1|γ) 的参数 γ 寻求纯场论的解释，并建立具有空间依赖质量形变的 ABJM 理论与其全息对偶解之间的对应关系。

Method: 在 z>0 半空间上构造空间依赖的共形质量形变，当质量形变按 m(z)=c/z 标度时平面共形对称性得以保持；采用 Polchinski–Strassler 方法，先将 N=4 超杨–米尔斯与 ABJM 的 Janus 界面中的局域质量源与标量匹配条件相联系，再据此推导 γ 与质量形变系数的关系。

Result: 该理论的真空对偶于一类半 BPS 冒泡解，其超代数为 D(2,1|γ)^{⊕2} 且 γ<0；并得到场论关系式 γ=(1−2c)/(1+2c)，即例外超代数参数由 ABJM 反距离质量形变的系数决定。

Conclusion: 研究首次仅从场论侧解释了 M2-膜场论中例外超代数参数 γ 的起源，表明其由 ABJM 反距离质量形变的系数 c 确定，并验证了 Polchinski–Strassler 方法在 Janus 界面与局域质量源分析中的适用性。

Abstract: We construct a space-dependent conformal mass deformation of ABJM on the half-space $z>0$ and show that the vacua of this theory are dual to a large class of half-BPS bubbling solutions of the form $AdS_3\times S^3\times S^3\timesΣ$, with superalgebra $D(2,1|γ)^{\oplus2}$, parametrized by $γ<0$. The conformal symmetry of a plane is preserved provided that the mass deformation scales as $m(z)=\frac{c}{z}$. We give the first field-theory explanation of the exceptional superalgebra parameter $γ$ in M2-brane field theory in terms of the coefficient of the inverse-distance mass deformation of ABJM: $γ=\frac{1-2c}{1+2c}$. We demonstrate this using the methods of Polchinski and Strassler, which we first apply to the Janus interfaces of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and ABJM to relate localized mass sources to scalar matching conditions.

</details>


### [202] [Chaos on the mass shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03787)
*Alexandre Homrich,David Simmons-Duffin,George Yuzhe Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出高能粒子产生在RG流（RG flow）理论中遵循由紫外理论光锥算符（light-ray operators）谱决定的普适能量演化方程，并将Chew-Frautschi图诠释为质量壳（mass shell）上粒子密度的波动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紫外渐近自由或共形理论与红外有质量理论之间RG流下的高能粒子产生，寻求跨理论的普适描述。

Method: 采用探测器匹配（detector matching）框架论证产生粒子分布满足与能量相关的普适演化方程；在2d共形场论（CFT）的可积流中，借助费曼气体（Feynman gas）热力学独立推导这些演化方程。

Result: 将紫外Chew-Frautschi图解释为粒子密度随能量演化的波动方程（CFT情形下该方程与特定温度下双曲空间混沌扩散方程相同）；并讨论对4d规范理论的推论，包括由强子数密度外行波产生的BFKL contrail。

Conclusion: 高能粒子产生分布的演化由紫外理论的光锥算符谱普遍决定，2d可积流检验支持该图景，并给出4d规范理论的可观测推论。

Abstract: We study particle production in high-energy states for theories given by an RG flow between an asymptotically free or conformal theory in the UV and a massive theory in the IR. Using the framework of detector matching, we argue that distributions of produced particles satisfy universal evolution equations with energy, determined by the spectrum of light-ray operators of the UV theory. In particular, we interpret the UV Chew-Frautschi plot as a wave equation for particle densities on the mass shell, evolving with energy. (When the UV theory is a CFT, this wave equation is the same one that controls the spread of chaos in hyperbolic space at a particular temperature.) We test these ideas for integrable flows in 2d CFTs, finding an independent derivation of the evolution equations in terms of the thermodynamics of a Feynman gas. We also discuss consequences for 4d gauge theories, including the presence of a BFKL contrail produced by an outgoing wave of hadron number density.

</details>


### [203] [Inner Product of Closed Universe from Gravitational Path-integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03789)
*Masamichi Miyaji,Sunil Sake,Kotaro Shinmyo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文从引力路径积分出发，为固定拓扑的 AdS/dS Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力构建闭合宇宙的希尔伯特空间，并给出物理态内积的精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化引力涉及 lapse 围道选择、路径积分测度、算符排序、规范群整体结构及初末时刻引力约束与规范对称性实现等多重微妙问题，需要一个统一形式来定义物理态内积并为宇宙学波函数赋予概率。

Method: 发展一套形式体系处理上述问题，在固定拓扑与 lapse 围道下精确计算内积并得到 JT 引力中的恒等分解；将精化代数量子化中的余不变与不变希尔伯特空间解释为初末时刻特定规范固定，并纳入哈密顿 BFV-BRST 形式。

Result: 精确得到物理态内积及相应的恒等分解；阐明固定几何数据的引力波函数作为余不变态（含其积分测度）的精确含义；指出希尔伯特空间的整体结构不同于 BF 理论，且依赖于大爆炸/大挤压附近的处方。

Conclusion: 圆柱几何（cylinder geometry）对所有微扰态（包括 Hartle-Hawking 无边界态）捕捉了引力的内积，为宇宙学波函数的概率赋值提供框架。

Abstract: We construct the Hilbert space of a closed universe of fixed topology in anti-de Sitter (AdS) and de Sitter (dS) Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity from the gravitational path integral. Quantizing gravity involves several subtleties, including the choice of lapse contour, path-integral measure, operator ordering, global structure of the gauge group, the implementation of gravitational constraints and the gauge symmetries at initial and final timeslices. We develop a formalism that addresses these issues and determines the inner product of physical states, providing a framework to assign probabilities to cosmological wavefunctions. For a fixed topology and a lapse contour, we compute the inner product exactly and obtain the corresponding resolution of the identity in JT gravity. We demonstrate that the co-invariant and invariant Hilbert spaces in refined algebraic quantization arise from particular gauge fixing at the initial and final timeslices. In particular, the formalism provides precise meaning for a gravitational wavefunction with fixed geometric data as a co-invariant state, including its integral measure. These constructions can be formulated within the Hamiltonian BFV-BRST formalism. We also discuss the global structure of the Hilbert space, which differs from that of BF theory and depends on the prescription adopted near the big bang/crunch. Our results imply that the cylinder geometry captures the gravitational inner product for all perturbative states, including the Hartle-Hawking no-boundary state.

</details>


### [204] [A Sojourn Through $c=1$ String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03792)
*Sam Christian,Yuchen Wang,Xi Yin,Yutai Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从世界面（worldsheet）视角重新考察 c=1 弦理论的 S-矩阵、热 S-矩阵与热自由能，通过数值积分含 DOZZ 结构常数的 Virasoro 共形块，得到树图阶最高 6 点、单圈阶最高 3 点振幅及亏格二热自由能，结果与 c=1 矩阵模型（matrix model）预言一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于以世界面方法重新审视 c=1 弦理论（c=1 string theory）的 S-矩阵与热力学量，并检验其与 c=1 矩阵模型预言的一致性。

Method: 方法为在世界面框架下数值积分 Virasoro 共形块（Virasoro conformal blocks），并采用 DOZZ 结构常数（DOZZ structure constants）计算散射振幅与热振幅。

Result: 计算覆盖树图阶（tree level）最高 6 点、单圈阶（one loop）最高 3 点的散射与热振幅，以及亏格二（genus two）热自由能；所有结果均与 c=1 矩阵模型的预言相符。

Conclusion: 世界面数值方法可有效复现 c=1 矩阵模型对振幅与热自由能的预言，支持两类描述之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We revisit the $S$-matrix, the thermal $S$-matrix and the thermal free energy of $c=1$ string theory from the worldsheet perspective. By numerically integrating Virasoro conformal blocks with DOZZ structure constants, we compute the scattering amplitudes and thermal amplitudes up to 6-point at tree level and up to 3-point at one loop, in addition to the genus two thermal free energy. All results are in agreement with the predicted answers from the $c=1$ matrix model.

</details>


### [205] [Compact holography via polarized IKKT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03807)
*Arsam Najafian,Mark Van Raamsdonk,Alejandro Vilar López*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文建立了极化 IKKT 矩阵模型（polarized IKKT matrix model）鞍点与超引力（supergravity）鞍点之间的直接对应链条，并证明两者在壳作用量与受保护可观测量上相互匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化 IKKT 矩阵模型被提议对偶于欧氏 IIB 型弦理论（Euclidean type IIB string theory）的 N 个 D-瞬子（D-instanton）扇区，但矩阵模型鞍点与引力鞍点之间的具体联系此前缺乏直接构造。

Method: 给出从矩阵模型 resolvent 出发的显式路径：resolvent → 全纯函数（holomorphic function）→ 二维库仑势（2D Coulomb potential）→ 四维库仑势（4D Coulomb potential）→ 超引力场；其中二维静电图像（2D electrostatics picture）被表述为该矩阵模型局域化形变（localization deformation）的精确有限 N 重构，对应背景场中受约束的偶极子气体（dipole gas）。

Result: 对全部鞍点，超引力与矩阵模型在在壳作用量（on-shell action）与受保护可观测量（protected observables）上匹配；超引力鞍点在弦框架（string frame）下具有紧致非奇异度规（compact and non-singular metric），其爱因斯坦框架（Einstein frame）渐近区域被点状 D-瞬子源替代。另有证据表明，将矩阵模型积分域限制到与相应鞍点关联的吸引域（basin）可得到特定引力鞍点的物理。

Conclusion: 大 N 解耦极限（large N decoupling limits）下部分鞍点族与其他鞍点解耦，给出某些高维场论（higher-dimensional field theories）的引力对偶，从而强化了矩阵模型与超引力之间的对偶图像。

Abstract: The polarized IKKT matrix model is a $U(N)$ supersymmetric matrix integral proposed to be dual to an $N$ D-instanton sector of Euclidean type IIB string theory. We show a direct path from matrix model saddles to supergravity saddles: matrix model resolvent $\to$ holomorphic function $\to$ 2D Coulomb potential $\to$ 4D Coulomb potential $\to$ supergravity fields, where the last step was previously described. Here, the 2D electrostatics picture is an exact finite $N$ reformulation of a localization deformation of the matrix model that gives a constrained gas of dipoles in a background field. We show a matching between supergravity and matrix model on-shell actions and protected observables for all saddles. We show that the supergravity saddles have a compact and non-singular metric in string frame with the Einstein frame asymptotic region replaced by a pointlike D-instanton source. We provide evidence that the physics of particular gravity saddles can be obtained through restricting the domain of the matrix model integral to a basin associated with the corresponding matrix model saddle. We describe certain large $N$ decoupling limits where families of the saddles decouple from the rest to give the gravity duals of certain higher-dimensional field theories.

</details>


### [206] [Resonant electroweak clouds around Schwarzschild black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03809)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在玻色型温伯格-萨拉姆理论中构造了施瓦茨席尔德黑洞周围的非线性电弱云，并给出其共振条件、质量与电荷间隙、存在上限以及磁性与电性、球形与偶极构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索非阿贝尔规范-希格斯理论中黑洞周围非线性、共振型矢量毛（vector hair）的新实现，并区别于线性云情形。

Method: 采用探针近似（probe limit），在电弱耦合偏离测量值条件下，于施瓦茨席尔德背景上构造由谐波时依带电规范场支撑、能量-动量张量保持静态的非线性电弱云，并施加视界正则性条件。

Result: 视界正则性给出场频率与视界静电势之间的共振条件；与线性云不同，解具有有限的质量与电荷间隙，且仅存在于最大视界尺度以内。得到磁性与电性云，含球对称与偶极构型，并在视界消失极限下退化为电弱球。

Conclusion: 还报告了一类新的偶极电弱球，在固定电荷下可比球对称对应物在能量上更有利；结果为非阿贝尔规范-希格斯理论中非线性共振矢量毛提供了新实现。

Abstract: We construct nonlinear electroweak clouds around Schwarzschild black holes in the bosonic Weinberg-Salam theory. These configurations are supported by harmonically time-dependent charged gauge fields, while their energy-momentum tensor remains static. Regularity at the horizon imposes a resonance condition relating the field frequency to the horizon electrostatic potential. Unlike linear clouds, these solutions exhibit a finite mass and electric charge gap, and exist only up to a maximal horizon size. We obtain both magnetic and electric clouds, including spherically symmetric and dipolar configurations, which reduce to electroweak balls in the vanishing-horizon limit. We also report a new family of dipolar electroweak balls, which can be energetically favoured over their spherical counterparts at fixed electric charge. Our results, obtained in the probe limit for electroweak couplings away from their measured values, establish a new realization of nonlinear resonant vector hair in a non-Abelian gauge-Higgs theory.

</details>


### [207] [Reconstruction of the conformal algebra from a critical groundstate on the (fuzzy) sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03817)
*Rolando Ramirez Camasca,Xiang Li,Ting-Chun Lin,John McGreevy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文从正则化共形场论（conformal field theory）基态出发，提出重建完整整体共形代数（global conformal algebra）的方法，并在模糊球正则化（fuzzy sphere regularization）框架下进行检验，优于基于哈密顿量密度（Hamiltonian density）的构造，且可用于识别共形原初态（conformal primary states）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已实现从正则化共形场论基态重建伸缩算子（dilatation operator，即球面哈密顿量），本文旨在将构造推广至完整的整体共形代数，并借此识别共形原初态；同时检验此前提出的纠缠哈密顿量（entanglement Hamiltonian）短程修正模型。

Method: 以同一哈密顿量的基态为起点，构造整体共形代数的方法；与基于哈密顿量密度的共形生成元构造作对比，并借助模糊球正则化进行数值检验，无需额外调节参数。

Result: 所提方法性能优于基于哈密顿量密度的构造，且与模型无关、无需额外调参，并随系统尺寸增大而改善；共形原初态的识别取得一定成功；并获得支持早前纠缠哈密顿量短程修正模型的进一步证据，据此实现系统性改进。

Conclusion: 从基态重建整体共形代数是一种可行且可扩展的方案，可用于识别共形原初态，并为纠缠哈密顿量短程修正模型提供进一步支持。

Abstract: Recently, we found a method to reconstruct the dilatation operator (sphere Hamiltonian) from a regulated conformal field theory groundstate. In this paper, we explore methods to construct the whole global conformal algebra, and test our construction using fuzzy sphere regularization. Starting from a groundstate of the same Hamiltonian, these methods perform better than constructions of the conformal generators based on a Hamiltonian density, are model-independent, require no additional tuning, and improve with system size. One purpose of reconstructing the conformal algebra is to identify conformal primary states, which we do with some success. We also find further evidence supporting the model of the short-distance corrections to the entanglement Hamiltonian proposed earlier and use it to make systematic improvements.

</details>


### [208] [A lattice construction of the Kormos-Runkel-Watts defect flows in minimal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03824)
*Junchen Rong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在格点上数值研究由自旋型缺陷场驱动的 (2,1) Verlinde 缺陷流，确认其保持 (r,1) Verlinde 线不变，并给出 p=4、p=5 的端点界面及其谱、无关维度和格点 β 函数（lattice β-function）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KRW 已证明对角极小模型 M(p,p+1) 的拓扑缺陷（topological defect）在弱相关缺陷场扰动下流向新的共形界面（conformal interface），但具体流行为及端点缺乏格点层面的定量刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用流的保持对称性构造稀释 RSOS 实现，并结合精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）进行数值求解；还用环模型（loop-model）形式延拓至连续 4≤p≤5。

Result: p=4 时流终止于可逆线 η=D_(3,1)；p=5 时终止于非平凡共形界面 C_5，其 g=1.503(2)、透射系数 T=0.665(10)，并给出其谱、主导无关维度（leading irrelevant dimension）及格点 β 函数。对 η 做 gauging 可将 C_5 映射为三态 Potts 模型的界面，该界面破坏 ℤ_3，但在施加 Fibonacci 线 τ_Fib 与电荷共轭（charge conjugation）后稳定。

Conclusion: RSOS/环模型框架下，C_p 族在 4≤p≤5 连续区间光滑地从 C_5 过渡到 η，表明该流族具有统一结构，并可用作识别共形界面性质的定量工具。

Abstract: Kormos, Runkel, and Watts (KRW) showed that a topological defect of a diagonal minimal model $M(p,p+1)$, perturbed by a weakly relevant defect field, flows to a new conformal interface. We study the flow of the $(2,1)$ Verlinde defect $D_{(2,1)}$ driven by the spinful defect field $φ_{(1,3)}\otimes\barφ_{(3,3)}$ on the lattice. The flow preserves the $(r,1)$ Verlinde lines. This preserved symmetry makes a dilute RSOS realisation possible. We study this flow numerically using both exact diagonalization and density matrix renormalization group (DMRG). At $p=4$, the flow ends on the invertible line $η=D_{(3,1)}$. At $p=5$ it ends on a non-trivial conformal interface $C_5$ with $g=1.503(2)$ and transmission coefficient $T=0.665(10)$. We report its spectrum, leading irrelevant dimension, and lattice $β$-function. Gauging $η$ maps $C_5$ to an interface of the three-state Potts model, which breaks $\mathbb{Z}_3$ but is a stable interface once the Fibonacci line $τ_{\mathrm{Fib}}$ and charge conjugation are imposed. A loop-model formulation allows us to follow the family $C_p$ at continuous $4\le p\le5$: it moves smoothly from $C_5$ to $η$ as we lower $p$.

</details>


### [209] [From off-shell strings to entropy flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03827)
*Houwen Wu,Shuxuan Ying*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为比特线（bit threads）的局域容量界及其极大流构造提供弦论基础：由壳外球面（off-shell sphere）导出的靶空间作用量锥形变分确定弦框架熵密度，结合复制构造（replica construction）与加权流-割对偶（weighted flow-cut duality）得到范数界，并构造BTZ背景中具有对数螺线空间形状的稳态F1弦解作为极大流的动力学代表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作虽将投影弦电荷密度（projected string charge density）等同于比特线流，但局域容量界系直接借用比特线处方，且弦流位形沿已知比特线预设，缺乏弦论自身的基础与物理载体识别。

Method: 在亏格零（genus zero）与两阶导数（two-derivative order）下，利用壳外球面导出的靶空间作用量的锥形变分固定弦框架熵密度；通过引力复制构造与加权流-割对偶导出弦框架范数界；随后在NS-NS支撑的BTZ黑洞中求解规则稳态F1弦解，并以均匀系综（uniform ensemble）构造守恒且范数恒定的投影弦电荷密度；最后推广至NS-NS与RR通量混合的BTZ背景。

Result: 所得稳态F1解的空间部分为对数螺线（logarithmic spirals），其均匀系综给出守恒、常范数的投影弦电荷密度，实现通量等于BTZ熵的极大流；在混合通量背景中，改变通量比例会在固定度规与膨胀子（dilaton）下改变稳态F1位形，而相应守恒流仍复现相同的领头BTZ熵。

Conclusion: 范数界与其物理极大流代表均源于弦论，稳态弦为比特线几何刻画的熵流提供了物理通道，从而弥补了此前处方性假设的不足。

Abstract: Bit threads express holographic entropy as the maximal flux of a divergenceless vector field subject to a local capacity bound, but do not identify a physical carrier of that flux. In our previous work, we identified the projected string charge density with a bit-thread flow, reproduced the entropies of BTZ and ten-dimensional three-charge black holes, and showed that the maximal entropy flux is preserved under S- and T-dualities. However, the norm bound was imported from the bit-thread prescription, while the string-current configurations were prescribed along known bit-thread lines. In this paper, we give a stringy foundation for the local capacity and construct dynamical probe-string representatives of maximal flow. At genus zero and two-derivative order, the conical variation of the target-space action derived from the off-shell sphere fixes the string-frame entropy density. The gravitational replica construction and weighted flow-cut duality then yield the string-frame norm bound. We next find regular stationary F1 solutions in an NS-NS supported BTZ black hole whose spatial parts are logarithmic spirals. A uniform ensemble produces a conserved, constant-norm projected string charge density and realizes a maximal flow whose flux equals the BTZ entropy. We finally extend the construction to BTZ backgrounds with mixed NS-NS and RR flux. Varying the flux fraction changes the stationary F1 configurations at fixed metric and dilaton, while the corresponding conserved flows reproduce the same leading BTZ entropy. Thus, both the norm bound and its physical maximal-flow representative arise from string theory, with stationary strings providing physical channels for the entropic flux represented geometrically by bit threads.

</details>


### [210] [Exotic Pauli spinors and magnetic flux quantization in a superconducting ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03877)
*L. Ibrain Cohen Iskandar,J. M. Hoff da Silva,R. T. Cavalcanti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论框架下发展“奇异泡利方程（exotic Pauli equation）”形式，并建立标准泡利旋量与奇异泡利旋量之间的有效对称性，用以描述超导环中磁通量量子化。该对称性在运动方程、拉格朗日量与流层面严格成立，并在量子层面由复合的物质-规范幺正算符实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在非相对论框架内描述超导环的磁通量量子化（magnetic flux quantization），并厘清“奇异项（exotic term）”与拓扑相位在对称性中如何被一致地处理，避免以往需要截断奇异项或对拓扑相位时间依赖附加条件的限制。

Method: 建立奇异泡利方程的形式体系，利用协变奇异狄拉克方程（covariant exotic Dirac equation）一致地固定空间与时间拓扑项（topological terms）的符号；在运动方程、拉格朗日量与流三个层面证明该对称性严格成立，并推导磁通量量子化条件。

Result: 当拓扑项符号被协变奇异狄拉克方程一致固定后，标准与奇异泡利旋量之间的对称性在运动方程、拉格朗日量与流层面严格精确，且无需截断奇异项、也不对拓扑相位的时间依赖施加条件；由此得到磁通量量子化条件，并显式构造实现该对称性的复合物质-规范幺正算符（composite matter-gauge unitary operator）。

Conclusion: 该对称性可提升至量子层面，由作用于希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）的幺正算符实现，其中规范部分（gauge sector）需采用通常的正规化（regularization）。

Abstract: We develop the formalism of the exotic Pauli equation and establish an effective symmetry between standard and exotic Pauli spinors to describe magnetic flux quantization in a superconducting ring within the nonrelativistic framework. We show that, once the signs of the spatial and temporal topological terms are fixed consistently by the covariant exotic Dirac equation, this symmetry is exact at the level of the equations of motion, Lagrangians and currents, with no truncation of the exotic term and no condition on the time dependence of the topological phase. Upon deriving the flux quantization condition, we explicitly construct the composite matter-gauge unitary operator acting on the Hilbert space, which implements the symmetry at the quantum level (the gauge sector requiring the usual regularization).

</details>


### [211] [Hamiltonian of open string field theory for time-dependent solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03941)
*Vinícius Bernardes,Riccardo Poletti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文基于 $L_\infty$ 理论的守恒荷（conserved charges）表达式，给出 Witten 玻色开弦场论（bosonic open string field theory）哈密顿量的显式形式，并在 Erler-Maccaferri 解析解上求值，从而精确复现 Sen 的边界态（boundary state）能量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Witten 玻色开弦场论的哈密顿量缺乏明确的显式构造，而近年提出的 $L_\infty$ 理论守恒荷表达式为此提供了可行工具；同时需要通过独立计算检验解析解的物理正确性，尤其是滚动快子（rolling tachyon）情形的能量。

Method: 采用 $L_\infty$ 理论守恒荷的近期表达式，构造 Witten 玻色开弦场论哈密顿量的显式形式；将该表达式在 Erler-Maccaferri 解析解（可描述与任一 BCFT 相关联的 D$p$-膜（D$p$-brane）位形）上求值，并据此计算滚动快子解的能量。

Result: 所导出的哈密顿量表达式在 Erler-Maccaferri 解上可有效求值，其给出的滚动快子解能量与 Sen 的边界态结果完全一致（exact agreement）。

Conclusion: 结果支持以 $L_\infty$ 守恒荷构造弦场论哈密顿量的方案，并进一步验证 Erler-Maccaferri 解析解在能量层面与边界态描述相符。

Abstract: Using the recently introduced expression for conserved charges of $L_\infty$ theories, we give an explicit form for the Hamiltonian of Witten's bosonic open string field theory. We evaluate it on the Erler-Maccaferri analytic solution, describing any D$p$-brane configuration associated to a BCFT. We use the expression to compute the energy of the rolling tachyon solution and find exact agreement with the boundary state result by Sen.

</details>


### [212] [Obstruction to local entropy laws on arbitrary black-hole cuts in five-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03970)
*Cendikiawan Suryaatmadja,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在五维爱因斯坦–高斯–博内（Einstein–Gauss–Bonnet）引力中，不存在同时满足"复现稳态黑洞熵"与"任意割之间非递减"的协变局域熵定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验黑洞熵的局域化表述（local entropy laws）在超越爱因斯坦引力的高阶曲率理论中是否仍成立，特别是能否同时复现稳态熵并满足第二定律式的单调性。

Method: 将五维爱因斯坦–高斯–博内引力视为精确理论，在任意事件视界割（cut）上考察协变局域熵；不假设导数阶数的全局上界；通过受引力波扰动的真空黑洞构造反例，并采用强双曲表述分析相关视界区间及其稳态未来。

Result: 剪切（shear）使割的内禀曲率依赖于其弯曲程度；稳态熵固定了该曲率项的系数，而局域修正无法抵消其对割弯曲的响应。在光滑视界上、无并合与拓扑变化的条件下，阻碍在波幅二阶出现。

Conclusion: 该理论中局域熵定律与第二定律式单调性不相容，构成对黑洞熵局域表述的结构性阻碍。

Abstract: We study local entropy laws on arbitrary cuts of event horizons in five-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity, treated as an exact theory. No covariant local entropy can both reproduce the stationary black-hole entropy and be nondecreasing between every ordered pair of cuts, provided its density is smooth on an invariant open set of off-shell metric and cut jets containing a flat planar cut and every cut used in the argument. No global bound on derivative order is assumed. The counterexamples to the second law are vacuum black holes perturbed by a gravitational wave, with a strongly hyperbolic formulation near the relevant horizon interval and its stationary future. Shear makes the intrinsic curvature of a cut depend on its bending. The stationary entropy fixes the coefficient of this curvature term, and local corrections cannot cancel its response to cut bending. The obstruction appears at second order in wave amplitude on a smooth horizon, without a merger or topology change.

</details>


### [213] [Hermitian matrix-valued nonlocal deformation of the SU($N$) Thirring model at large $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03973)
*J. R. Nascimento,P. J. Porfírio,C. Furtado*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 1/N 领头阶（leading order）研究二维 SU(N) Thirring 模型的厄米矩阵值非局域形变（Hermitian matrix-valued nonlocal deformation），并在原始作用量上直接施加 $f(\slashed{\partial})^\ddagger=f(\slashed{\partial})$ 的厄米性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察非局域形变（nonlocal deformation）下二维 SU(N) Thirring 模型的大 N 行为，并厘清所得能隙与谱零点（spectral zeros）究竟属于明确规定的平均场泛函还是原理论的物理谱。

Method: 对实非局域尺度，可接受的指数形式为 $f(\slashed{\partial})=\exp[-i\slashed{\partial}/\Lambda]$；据此构造并分析偶质量（配对，paired）大 N 有效势（effective potential），导出其径向核（radial kernel）、弱耦合量纲嬗变标度（dimensional-transmutation scale）及形式上的 Lambert-W 零点。

Result: 径向能隙积分（radial gap integral）具有条件收敛的无限截断极限（conditionally convergent infinite-cutoff limit）；但配对方案（pairing prescription）与原厄米作用量的非配对行列式（unpaired determinant）不同。

Conclusion: 所报告的能隙与谱零点应理解为该显式定义的平均场泛函（mean-field functional）的性质，而非原理论的物理质量隙；需进一步推导才能从非配对理论获得物理质量隙与费米子极点（fermion poles）。

Abstract: We investigate a Hermitian matrix-valued nonlocal deformation of the two-dimensional $SU(N)$ Thirring model at leading order in $1/N$. We impose $f(\slashed{\partial})^\ddagger=f(\slashed{\partial})$ directly on the original action; for real nonlocality scale the admissible exponential choice is $f(\slashed{\partial})=\exp[-i\slashed{\partial}/Λ]$. We construct and analyze the even-in-mass (paired) large-$N$ effective potential and derive its radial kernel, weak-coupling dimensional-transmutation scale, and formal Lambert-$W$ zeros. Its radial gap integral possesses a conditionally convergent infinite-cutoff limit. The pairing prescription, however, differs from the unpaired determinant of the original Hermitian action, so the reported gap and spectral zeros must be understood as properties of this explicitly defined mean-field functional. We identify the further derivation needed to obtain physical mass gaps and fermion poles from the unpaired theory.

</details>


### [214] [A Ghost-Free dS/AdS Correspondence in $\mathcal{N}=1$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03977)
*Omer Guleryuz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明标量势符号反转（de Sitter↔anti-de Sitter）可通过超共形Kähler锥内对幂零goldstino度规的Z₂反射，在两个无鬼的四维N=1超引力之间逐点实现，且配对真空的稳定性恰由Breitenlohner–Freedman界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量势符号反转可交换dS与AdS真空，并经domain-wall/cosmology对应把平坦畴壁映射为平坦FLRW宇宙，但其超对称版本在宇宙学一侧出现鬼（ghosts），需构造无鬼的实现。

Method: 在单一超共形Kähler锥（superconformal Kähler cone）内，对幂零goldstino Kähler度规绕平坦极限V=0作Z₂反射，逐点反转V↔−V，同时保持可见Kähler度规、超势与幺正规范费米子拉格朗日量不变。

Result: 配对真空满足H=1/L、m²_dS=−m²_AdS、共形权重相等及m_{3/2}>H，并恰在0≤m²_dS≤(9/4)H²窗口内同时稳定，该窗口由Breitenlohner–Freedman界设定。

Conclusion: 该对应将过抬升与欠抬升的反D3膜真空配对，把de Sitter swampland猜想限制在goldstino（反膜）扇区，并将慢滚暴胀映射为满足全息c-定理的健康AdS畴壁。

Abstract: Reversing the sign of the scalar potential exchanges de Sitter and anti-de Sitter vacua and, through the domain-wall/cosmology correspondence, maps flat domain walls onto flat FLRW cosmologies, but its supersymmetric version has ghosts on the cosmological side. We show that this pointwise correspondence is realized between two ordinary, ghost-free four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ supergravities with spontaneously broken supersymmetry. Within a single superconformal Kähler cone, a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ reflection of the nilpotent goldstino Kähler metric through the flat limit $V=0$ reverses $V\leftrightarrow-V$ pointwise while preserving the visible Kähler metric, the superpotential, and the unitary-gauge fermion Lagrangian. The paired vacua obey a sharp dictionary with $H=1/L$, $m^2_{\rm dS}=-m^2_{\rm AdS}$, equal conformal weights, and $m_{3/2}>H$, and are simultaneously stable precisely within the window $0\le m^2_{\rm dS}\le\frac94H^2$ set by the Breitenlohner--Freedman bound. Applied to string compactifications and cosmology, this correspondence pairs over-uplifted with under-uplifted anti-D3-brane vacua, confines the de Sitter swampland conjecture to the goldstino (anti-brane) sector, and maps slow-roll inflation onto a healthy AdS domain wall obeying the holographic $c$-theorem.

</details>


### [215] [Negative Junction Logarithms in Scalar Multipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04220)
*Chen-Te Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在2+1维自由实相对论标量真空（free real relativistic scalar vacuum）中，三区域结（three-region junction）对真三体Rényi多熵（Rényi multi-entropy）给出严格负的对数贡献，且对每个整数n≥3与所有非退化扇区角度成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三区域结几何下真三体Rényi多熵的符号与结构性质，判定其对数贡献是否具有确定符号，并厘清复制减法（replica subtraction）所移除的圈类与剩余圈类的权重特征。

Method: 采用复制技巧（replica method）进行减法以剔除与单一界面（single interface）相关的圈类；以平直页Friedrichs处方（flat-sheet Friedrichs prescription）在非流形复制顶点（nonmanifold replica apex）处固定标量算符，并导出系数的收敛角谱公式（angular spectral formula）。

Result: 幸存圈（surviving loops）具有正且与标度无关（scale-independent）的权重，所得对数的符号由负的Rényi前因子（Rényi prefactor）固定，故贡献严格为负；选择器（selector）在n=2时为空；角谱公式收敛。

Conclusion: 该几何配置下真三体Rényi多熵的对数项符号被严格确定，Friedrichs处方为顶点算符提供了自洽且收敛的谱表述，n=2处的空选择器表明该效应本质上是n≥3的高阶Rényi现象。

Abstract: A three-region junction in a free real relativistic scalar vacuum in $2+1$ dimensions yields a strictly negative logarithmic contribution to genuine tripartite Rényi multi-entropy for every integer $n\geq3$ and all nondegenerate sector angles. The replica subtraction exactly removes loop classes associated with a single interface. Surviving loops have positive, scale-independent weight, yielding a logarithm whose sign is fixed by the negative Rényi prefactor. The selector is empty at $n=2$. The flat-sheet Friedrichs prescription fixes the scalar operator at the nonmanifold replica apex. It yields a convergent angular spectral formula for the coefficient.

</details>


### [216] [On the universality of entanglement entropy in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04238)
*Orr Sela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究量子场论（quantum field theory）中纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）定义对边界条件（boundary conditions）的依赖性，以三维 Chern-Simons 理论为例说明不同边界选择仅影响非普适项（non-universal terms），而普适项保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准纠缠熵定义需正则化（regularizing）纠缠面并对希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）做因子化，这涉及正则面上边界条件的选择，由此产生两个未决问题：何种条件下边界选择能给出酉映射（unitary map），以及多种选择是否给出相同（至少普适）熵。

Method: 以拓扑理论（topological theories）为对象，具体采用三维 Chern-Simons 理论作为示例，在特定假设下比较不同边界条件对熵的影响；并将类似论证推广至二维共形场论（conformal field theories）。

Result: 在指定假设下，不同边界条件的选择只影响熵中的非普适项，其余项在该意义下仍为普适的；而对应非酉映射（non-unitary maps）的边界条件则可改变这些项。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵的普适部分对边界条件选择具有稳健性，但该稳健性依赖于映射的酉性；文中进一步给出适用于一般理论的普适性条件。

Abstract: The standard definition of entanglement entropy in quantum field theory requires regularizing the entangling surface and factorizing the Hilbert space, which in turn involves a choice of boundary conditions at the regularized surface. This raises two natural questions which are open at present. The first is under what conditions there is a choice of boundary conditions that leads to a unitary map between the original Hilbert space and the factorized one, which is a necessary condition for a well-defined entanglement entropy. The second concerns the situation where there are multiple such choices, in which case the question is whether they all yield the same entropy, or at least the same terms usually referred to as universal. Focusing on topological theories and using three-dimensional Chern-Simons theory as a concrete illustration, we show under specified assumptions that different such choices affect only the non-universal terms in the entropy, such that the remaining terms are universal in this sense as well, while we explicitly demonstrate that boundary conditions corresponding to non-unitary maps can change these terms. We then explain how a similar argument applies to two-dimensional conformal field theories and discuss a condition for universality in general theories.

</details>


### [217] [Gauge-invariant thermodynamic description of rotating black strings in 5D minimal supergravity via Kaluza-Klein reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04373)
*Yang Yu,Peng-Ju Hu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过 Kaluza-Klein 约化（Kaluza-Klein reduction）构建五维最小超引力（minimal supergravity）中旋转黑弦（rotating black string）热力学的规范不变（gauge-invariant）表述，并系统计算两导数层次与四导数修正下的热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转黑弦的热力学表述需要规范不变框架，且高阶导数修正下的热力学行为及其与四维黑洞结果的关系尚待澄清。

Method: 一致地约化体作用量与 Gibbons-Hawking-York 边界项（含 Chern-Simons 耦合诱导的表面贡献），得到四维 N=2 超引力耦合单一矢量多重态（prepotential P = X1^3/(3√3 X0)）；用哈密顿形式（Hamiltonian formalism）建立欧氏壳上作用量与吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）的量子统计关系；并用 Reall-Santos 方法计算四导数修正。

Result: 建立了量子统计关系，并给出旋转磁黑弦热力学的主导四导数修正；所得修正与四维 N=2 超引力中 Kerr-Newman 黑洞的直接计算结果精确一致。

Conclusion: 该约化框架为黑弦热力学提供规范不变处理，并表明显式四导数修正可通过四维对偶黑洞结果交叉验证，支持两框架间的一致性。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant formulation of the thermodynamics of rotating black strings in five-dimensional minimal supergravity through Kaluza-Klein reduction. At the two-derivative level, we consistently reduce the bulk action and the Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary term, carefully accounting for the surface contributions induced by the Chern-Simons coupling. The resulting four-dimensional theory is $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity coupled to a single vector multiplet, with prepotential $P=\frac{1}{3\sqrt{3}}\frac{X_1^3}{X_0}$. Employing the Hamiltonian formalism, we establish the quantum statistical relation between the Euclidean on-shell action and the Gibbs free energy. We further compute the leading four-derivative corrections to the thermodynamics of a rotating magnetic black string using the Reall-Santos method. The resulting corrections are in precise agreement with previous results obtained directly for the Kerr-Newman black hole in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity.

</details>


### [218] [Circuit Complexity of Scalar and Tensor Perturbations in Luminal Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04441)
*Tao Li,Hai-Bing Fu,Lei-Hua Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦—高斯—邦内特暴涨（Einstein–Gauss–Bonnet inflation）的光速分支中，标量与张量原初扰动（primordial perturbations）的电路复杂度（circuit complexity）主要由压缩振幅（squeezing amplitude）演化控制；正耦合增强标量复杂度却轻微抑制张量复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察张量速度固定为单位值时，动力学的高斯—邦内特（Gauss–Bonnet, GB）耦合是否仍能在标量与张量扰动的量子信息特征（电路复杂度）上留下可区分的印记。

Method: 从二阶扰动作用量出发构造双模哈密顿量（two-mode Hamiltonians），导出压缩振幅与压缩角的演化方程；追踪从次视界（subhorizon）到超视界（superhorizon）的压缩动力学，并对所得双模压缩态计算 Nielsen 波函数复杂度（Nielsen wave-function complexity）。

Result: GB 修正使压缩振幅出现明显分离，而未缠绕压缩角（unwrapped squeezing angles）保持近乎锁相；电路复杂度因此主要由振幅演化主导。在所考察的基准情形中，正耦合增强标量复杂度、但对张量复杂度略有抑制。

Conclusion: 即便张量传播为光速，标量与张量高斯态仍携带同一修改暴涨背景下的不同信息，二者复杂度呈现相反趋势。

Abstract: We investigate the circuit complexity of primordial scalar and tensor perturbations in the luminal branch of Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet inflation. Starting from the quadratic perturbation actions, we construct the two-mode Hamiltonians and derive the evolution equations for the squeezing amplitudes and angles. Although the tensor speed is fixed to unity, the Gauss--Bonnet (GB) coupling remains dynamical through the scalar and tensor pump fields. We track the squeezing dynamics from the subhorizon to the superhorizon regime and evaluate the Nielsen wave-function complexity of the resulting two-mode squeezed states. The GB correction produces a clear separation in the squeezing amplitudes, while the unwrapped squeezing angles remain nearly phase locked. Consequently, the circuit complexity is controlled mainly by the amplitude evolution. A positive coupling enhances the scalar complexity but slightly suppresses the tensor complexity for the benchmarks considered. These opposite trends show that scalar and tensor Gaussian states retain distinct information about the same modified inflationary background even when tensor propagation is luminal.

</details>


### [219] [Regge bounds and eikonal scaling in a four-dimensional scalar EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04505)
*Ji Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对无质量位移对称标量理论（含 $g_4(\partial\phi)^4/\Lambda^4$ 与 $g_6(\partial\phi)^6/\Lambda^8$ 算符），在固定 $t<0$ 下研究树图、一圈气泡图与三圈香蕉图的大 $s$ 增长行为，据此推导零点起始割线的色散关系并给出正定性约束（positivity bounds）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正定性约束基于二次减除色散关系（twice-subtracted dispersion relations），其前提是振幅增长慢于 $s^2$。既有推导对该前提在含高维算符情形下是否成立缺乏系统检验，故需直接计算各阶振幅的大 $s$ 行为并据此重审色散关系。

Method: 将分波求和（partial-wave sum）在某个过渡自旋（transition spin）处拆分：低自旋逐道用幺正性（unitarity）给出界，高自旋则借助交叉道对数（crossed-channel logarithms）的程函相位（eikonal phase）并用驻相法（stationary phase）评估。同一构造在色散关系所需的前向极限（forward limit）中施行，并利用截断以下的一圈与三圈吸收部分（absorptive parts）约束 $g_4$ 与 $g_6$。

Result: 在所考虑的理论中导出了割线自零能量起始的色散关系，并确定了树图、一圈气泡图与三圈香蕉图在固定 $t<0$ 时的大 $s$ 增长行为；由此在前向极限下得到对 $g_4$ 与 $g_6$ 的约束。

Conclusion: 通过过渡自旋拆分与程函—驻相处理，可在振幅增长可能达到 $s^2$ 量级的情形下一致地建立色散关系，并借助低阶吸收部分对 $g_4$、$g_6$ 施加正定性类型的限制，为高维算符理论的正定性约束（positivity bounds）提供了可操作路径。

Abstract: Positivity bounds rest on twice-subtracted dispersion relations, which assume that the amplitude grows more slowly than $s^2$. For the massless shift-symmetric scalar with $g_4(\partialφ)^4/Λ^4$ and $g_6(\partialφ)^6/Λ^8$, we derive the dispersion relation, whose cuts start at zero energy, and study the large-$s$ growth at fixed $t<0$ of the tree, the one-loop bubble and the three-loop banana. The partial-wave sum is split at a transition spin: low spins are bounded by unitarity, channel by channel, and high spins are evaluated with the eikonal phase of the crossed-channel logarithms by stationary phase. The same construction is applied in the forward limit required by the dispersion relation, and we use the one- and three-loop absorptive parts below the cutoff to constrain $g_4$ and $g_6$.

</details>


### [220] [Modular properties of correlators in generalised Gibbs ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04636)
*Tanmoy Sengupta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将广义吉布斯系综（GGE）配分函数的模变换嵌套算符乘积展开（OPE）描述推广至局部关联函数，在自由费米子与有理手征共形场论中给出两点及 n 点函数的模 S 变换规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前用嵌套 OPE 描述的模变换仅适用于 GGE 配分函数，尚未推广到局部关联函数，故需研究环面关联函数（torus correlators）的模性质。

Method: 对带 KdV 荷的自由费米子计算精确两点函数，并以化学势形式幂级数推导其模 S 变换；据此猜想有理手征共形场论中 n 点函数的类似变换律，借助 Zhu 递推（Zhu's recursion）在最低显式模参数幂次上证明至化学势所有阶，并在化学势三阶内验证完整猜想。

Result: 变换后的关联函数呈因子化结构：有效荷使系综形变，局域修饰算符（dressing operator）作用于外场，二者均由含形变流的嵌套 OPE 决定；猜想在最低模参数阶被确立，并在化学势立方阶得到完整验证。

Conclusion: 结果将 GGE 配分函数模变换的嵌套 OPE 描述扩展到局部关联函数，为一般有理手征共形场论中的模变换律提供依据。

Abstract: We study the modular properties of torus correlators in a generalised Gibbs ensemble (GGE) of a two-dimensional conformal field theory. For the free fermion with KdV charges, we compute the exact two-point function and derive its modular $S$-transformation as a formal power series in the chemical potential. The transformed correlator admits a factorised description: an effective charge deforms the ensemble, while a local dressing operator acts on the external fields. Both are determined by nested operator product expansions involving the deforming current. Motivated by this result, we conjecture an analogous transformation law for $n$-point functions in rational chiral conformal field theories. Using Zhu's recursion, we establish the conjecture at the lowest explicit power of the modular parameter to all orders in the chemical potential, thereby determining the dressing operator. We also verify the full conjectured transformation through cubic order in the chemical potential, retaining all explicit powers of the modular parameter. These results extend the nested-OPE description of modular transformations of GGE partition functions to local correlation functions.

</details>


### [221] [Scalar radiation from circular orbits in D3 brane spacetimes: a Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi-like approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04665)
*Donato Bini,Giorgio Di Russo,Veronica Fantini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究十维D3膜时空中的标量扰动，构建了适用于该情形的高维Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi类方法，并将问题化为含源项的Mathieu方程修正形式，进而计算标量波在无穷远与视界处的能量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机考察十维D3膜时空（D3 brane spacetime）中标量扰动（scalar perturbations）的解析处理，需解决现有Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi方法在高维情形的适用问题。

Method: 方法上发展高维Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi类形式体系（higher-dimensional MST-like formalism），将标量扰动方程约化为带源项的修正Mathieu方程（sourced modification of a Mathieu equation）。

Result: 作为应用，针对标量电荷沿圆形“赤道”测地线（equatorial geodesic）运动的情形，计算了标量波在无穷远处和视界处（infinity and horizon）的能量损失（energy loss）。

Conclusion: 结论为高维MST类形式可有效处理D3膜时空中的标量扰动问题，并给出沿赤道圆轨道运动标量电荷的标量辐射能量损失谱特征。

Abstract: We study scalar perturbations in a ten-dimensional D3 brane spacetime, developing a (higher-dimensional) Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi-like formalism adapted to the present case leading to a sourced modification of a Mathieu equation. As an application, we compute the energy loss both at infinity and at the horizon in terms of scalar waves due to a scalar charge moving along a circular "equatorial" geodesic.

</details>


### [222] [The Stringy Origin of Kerr Multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04702)
*Maurizio Firrotta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作在平直时空中构造了一族新的玻色弦精确解，可在大质量、大角动量极限下逐阶复现Kerr黑洞的多极矩结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于为旋转黑洞（rotating black hole）与量子弦态（quantum string state）之间的对应关系提供明确的经典数据，从而检验二者的联系。

Method: 方法为在平直时空（flat spacetime）中构造新的开弦与闭弦玻色弦（bosonic string）精确解，并考察其在质量与角动量取大极限下的多极展开。

Result: 所得解在大质量和角动量极限下，于每个固定多极阶（multipole order）上复现根-Kerr（root-Kerr）与Kerr多极矩。

Conclusion: 该族解为识别相应量子弦态、检验其与旋转黑洞的对应关系提供了显式的经典数据；摘要未给出更多定量结论。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact open and closed bosonic string solutions in flat spacetime that reproduce the root-Kerr and Kerr multipoles at every fixed multipole order in the large-mass and large-angular-momentum limit. These solutions provide explicit classical data for identifying associated quantum string states and testing their correspondence with rotating black holes.

</details>


### [223] [Fortuitous BMN Operators from Multiple Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04722)
*Robert de Mello Koch,Sarthak Duary,Minkyoo Kim,Sinong Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出从多个低秩构造块系统构建 \(\mathcal N=4\) 超杨–米尔斯理论 BMN 扇区中偶然上同调类的方法，并通过限制映射验证其非恰当性与偶然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望避免暴力计算，系统化地理解有限 \(N\) 下偶然上同调（fortuitous cohomology）的结构，因此需要一种由低秩构件组合出高阶上同调类的构造框架。

Method: 对色空间的任意划分，构造满足相应低秩理论超对称变换的修正有效字母（corrected effective letters），据此构建规范不变且 \(Q\)-闭的多项式算符；再利用限制映射（restriction map）回收组成成分的上同调类。

Result: 限制映射可在不做暴力计算的情况下确立非恰当性（non-exactness）与偶然性；作者以组合两个偶然 \(SU(2)\) 核（cores）构造 \(SU(4)\) 中的偶然类作为显式示例。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限 \(N\) 下偶然上同调的结构提供了系统化研究途径。

Abstract: We develop a systematic construction of fortuitous cohomology classes in the BMN sector of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory from multiple lower-rank building blocks.
