For arbitrary partitions of colour space, we construct corrected effective letters that obey the supersymmetry transformations of the corresponding lower-rank theories. These allow us to build gauge-invariant, $Q$-closed polynomial operators. A restriction map recovers the constituent cohomology classes, establishing non-exactness and fortuity without brute-force computations. As an explicit example, we combine two fortuitous $SU(2)$ cores to construct a fortuitous class in $SU(4)$. Our results provide a systematic approach to the structure of fortuitous cohomology at finite $N$.

</details>


### [224] [Crossover from Schwarzian dynamics to rotational soft modes in near-extremal Lovelock black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04726)
*Ankit Anand,Aditya Singh,Kimet Jusufi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在七维三阶 Lovelock 引力中，六导数相互作用（six-derivative interactions）不改变软模（soft modes）的计数，但使朴素的 $\log T$ 系数计算失效：$S^5$ 的十五个转动模作为量子转子（quantum rotor）仅在 $K_J^{-1}$ 以上标度被激活，导致 $\log T$ 系数从 $3/2$ 跑动到 $9$，始终达不到朴素高斯值 $19/2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端黑洞熵中 $\log T$ 系数由少量软模的计数确定，本文检验真实的六导数相互作用是否会改变这一计数。

Method: 选取七维三阶 Lovelock 引力作为三次 Lovelock 项动力学起作用的最简设定；通过带电慢转解（charged slow-rotation solution）计算转动惯量 $K_J$；并引入试探带电标量（probe charged scalar）推导近视界 Schwinger 阈值的解析式。

Result: 模的重数（multiplicities）保持不变，但朴素计数失效：固定电荷下 Maxwell 模被冻结，转动模仅在 $K_J^{-1}$ 以上开启，$\log T$ 系数由 $3/2$ 跑动至 $9$。Lovelock 耦合只移动交叉标度而不改变重数；仅大 AdS 黑洞具有完整的转动贡献可与经典熵竞争的宽窗口。带电标量分析给出三次耦合中近视界 Schwinger 阈值的解析结果，其大 AdS 极限用于判断宽转动交叉窗口能否与带电标量 Breitenlohner–Freedman（BF）界不被破坏相容共存。

Conclusion: 六导数相互作用不改变软模重数，但通过量子转子的标度激活显著改变 $\log T$ 系数的跑动行为；宽转动交叉窗口与大 AdS 黑洞及带电标量 BF 界条件之间存在相容性约束。

Abstract: Near-extremal black holes are controlled by a small set of soft modes, and counting these modes fixes the coefficient of $\log T$ in the entropy. We ask whether genuine six-derivative interactions change that count, working in seven-dimensional third-order Lovelock gravity, the simplest setting where the cubic Lovelock term is dynamical. The multiplicities stay the same, but the naive count fails. At fixed charge the Maxwell mode is frozen out, and the fifteen rotational modes of the $S^5$ act as a quantum rotor that only switches on above the scale $K_J^{-1}$. As a result, the coefficient of $\log T$ runs from $3/2$ at the lowest temperatures to $9$ above that scale, and never reaches the naive Gaussian value $19/2$. We compute the moment of inertia $K_J$ from the charged slow-rotation solution and find that the Lovelock couplings move the crossover without changing the multiplicities. Only large AdS black holes have a wide window in which the full rotational contribution competes with the classical entropy. Adding a probe charged scalar, we derive an analytic near-horizon Schwinger threshold in the cubic coupling. Its large-AdS limit tells us when the broad rotational crossover window can coexist with the absence of a charged-scalar Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound violation.

</details>


### [225] [Interpolating between 4d $\mathcal{N}= 1$ SCFT supercurrent multiplet anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04967)
*Pantelis Panopoulos,Ioannis Papadimitriou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维一般 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论（SCFT）中，共形多重态（conformal multiplet）与 Ferrara-Zumino 多重态之间的关系；通过构造显式的局域抵消项（local counterterm），说明共形超引力配分函数经适当 Wess-Zumino 作用量提升后与 old-minimal 配分函数相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重整化 SCFT 可与多种离壳背景超引力多重态耦合，从而定义不同的流多重态（current multiplet）与超共形反常（superconformal anomaly），因此有必要厘清不同多重态之间的关系。

Method: 将共形超引力配分函数通过合适的 Wess-Zumino 作用量提升为 old-minimal 背景场（old-minimal background fields）的泛函，并在分量形式上显式确定连接两者的局域抵消项，用以比较两种超共形反常不同的 old-minimal 超引力方案。

Result: 该抵消项部分依赖于 old-minimal 辅助标量场（auxiliary scalar），给出了文献 [1] 超空间抵消项的分量形式；但完整的抵消项还包含与旧辅助标量无关的额外项，这些项在文献 [1] 的超空间抵消项中并不存在。

Conclusion: 这些额外项在两种多重态之间比较物理可观测量（physical observables）时起关键作用，表明仅依赖文献 [1] 的超空间抵消项不足以完整刻画多重态间的转换。

Abstract: A renormalized SCFT can be coupled to a number of background off-shell supergravity multiplets, defining different current multiplets and superconformal anomalies. We focus on the relation between the conformal and Ferrara-Zumino multiplets of generic $\mathcal{N}= 1$ SCFTs in four dimensions, defined respectively by $\mathcal{N}= 1$ conformal and old-minimal supergravity. We show that the conformal super-gravity partition function, when lifted to a functional of the old-minimal background fields through a suitable Wess-Zumino action, is related to the old-minimal partition function by a local counterterm that we determine explicitly in components. This counterterm interpolates between two different old-minimal supergravity schemes with different superconformal anomalies. Part of the counterterm depends on the old-minimal auxiliary scalar and provides the explicit component form of the super-space counterterm constructed in [1]. However, the full counterterm that interpolates between the two multiplets contains additional terms, independent of the old-minimal auxiliary scalar, which are not present in this superspace counterterm. These additional terms play a key role in the comparison of physical observables between the two multiplets.

</details>


### [226] [Weyl Rescalings: A First Order Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04978)
*L. Andrianopoli,J. Zanelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文讨论外尔变换（Weyl transformation）在一阶嘉当引力表述与二阶度规表述之间的表观矛盾，指出该矛盾可通过引入挠率（torsion）的作用加以解决，并为物质场与引力的共形耦合提供线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外尔变换被认为在极高能标是自然界的对称性、在低能下自发破缺；但一阶表述中联络（connection）与外尔标度无关、须为外尔不变，而二阶度规表述中里奇张量（Ricci tensor）作非齐次变换，两种在零挠率极限下本应等价的表述因此产生张力。

Method: 采用嘉当一阶表述（Cartan's first order formulation），将局域标架（vielbein）按 e^a→Ωe^a 缩放，洛伦兹联络（Lorentz connection）一形式 ω^a_b 视为与 e^a 独立，并利用洛伦兹变换与伸缩变换（dilatation）对易的性质，考查挠率在调和两种表述中的作用。

Result: 研究表明，在考虑挠率的作用后，一阶与二阶表述之间关于外尔变换的矛盾可以得到化解。

Conclusion: 挠率是理解外尔变换在两套引力表述中一致性的关键，该结果亦有助于阐明物质场与引力的共形耦合（conformal coupling）。

Abstract: Weyl transformations, --local rescalings in which the metric transforms as $g_{μν}(x)\longrightarrow Ω^2(x)\, g_{μν}(x)$--, are expected to be a symmetry of nature at extremely high energy, that should be (spontaneously) broken at low energy. In Cartan's first order formulation of gravity this translates into the condition that the local frames (vielbein) scale as $e^a(x) \longrightarrow Ω(x) e^a(x)$. In this construction, the Lorentz connection one-form fields $ω^a{}_b$ are independent from $e^a$, and since Lorentz transformations and dilatations commute, $ω$ and $R(ω)$ must be Weyl invariant, while in the second order metric formalism the Ricci tensor undergoes a non-homogeneous transformation. This generates a tension between the two formulations, which should be equivalent in the vanishing torsion limit. Here we show how the puzzle can be resolved taking into account the role of torsion. This also sheds some light on the conformal coupling of matter fields to gravity.

</details>


### [227] [Hecke Relations from Self-Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05002)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出以非对称轨形（asymmetric orbifolds）下的自对偶性（self-duality）来物理解释Hecke算符（Hecke operators）所联系的、中心荷（central charge）不同的有理共形场论（rational conformal field theories, RCFT）特征标之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hecke算符可用于关联中心荷不同的有理共形场论特征标，但此类关系的物理诠释长期不明，构成研究动机。

Method: 作者以非对称轨形下的自对偶性作为解释框架，并将该构造推广至Harvey-Wu构造之外，给出更一般的恒等式并通过实例加以说明。

Result: 非对称轨形自对偶性给出了Hecke算符关系的简单物理解释，并产出超越Harvey-Wu构造的更一般恒等式，附有示例。

Conclusion: Hecke算符所连接的不同中心荷有理共形场论之间的关系可归结为非对称轨形的自对偶性，该视角同时推广了既有构造。

Abstract: Hecke operators can be used to relate characters of rational conformal field theories with different central charges, but it has remained a mystery how to interpret these relations physically. We offer a simple explanation in terms of self-duality under asymmetric orbifolds. Self-dual orbifolds also give more general identities beyond the familiar Harvey-Wu construction, which we illustrate through examples.

</details>


### [228] [Horizon exchange and the universal all-spin response of Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05054)
*Pierre Vanhove*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文导出了径向 Teukolsky 方程在交换外视界与内视界时对应的 MST 近区响应之间的精确单值性（monodromy）关系，并由此给出交换宇称奇响应的通用表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向 Teukolsky 方程在内外视界交换下形式不变，但物理边界条件破坏该对称性；既有工作仅掌握小自旋展开的个别系数，缺少统一刻画视界交换效应的精确关系。

Method: 推导相应 Mano–Suzuki–Takasugi（MST）近区响应之间的精确单值性关系；在频率领头阶、自旋任意阶下展开，对每个多极（multipole）给出交换奇响应表达式，并将引力扰动情形归结为 digamma 函数。

Result: 对引力扰动，该关系确定四极吸收（quadrupolar absorption）中的交换奇贡献，并将交换偶动力学响应组织为 digamma 函数，其展开可解释 Kerr–Compton 计算中出现的奇、偶 zeta 结构。

Conclusion: 核心结果是精确关系本身，而非此前已知的各个小自旋系数；该关系统一并推广了已有的微扰结果。

Abstract: The radial Teukolsky equation is invariant under exchange of the outer and inner horizons, although the physical boundary condition is not. We derive an exact monodromy relation between the corresponding Mano--Suzuki--Takasugi (MST) near-zone responses. At leading order in frequency and to all orders in spin, this relation yields, for every multipole, an expression for the leading horizon-exchange-odd response. For gravitational perturbations, it determines the exchange-odd contribution to quadrupolar absorption and organizes the exchange-even dynamical response into a digamma function whose expansion accounts for the odd- and even-zeta structures found in Kerr-Compton calculations. The exact relation, rather than the individual previously known small-spin coefficients, is the central result.

</details>


### [229] [A Holographic Weyl Semimetal in an Effective Gauss--Bonnet Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05182)
*F. Mousavi,K. Bitaghsir Fadafan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在探针 D3/D7 模型中，热几何的有效 Gauss–Bonnet 形变会改变传导性外尔半金属相与绝缘相之间的竞争；正耦合使共存边界移向更大味质量并扩大外尔半金属相区，但并不增强电导响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究热几何的有效 Gauss–Bonnet 形变（Gauss–Bonnet deformation）如何影响探针 D3/D7 模型中传导性外尔半金属（Weyl-semimetal）相与绝缘（insulating）相之间的相竞争与相变边界。

Method: 用到达视界（horizon-reaching）与平滑封顶（smoothly capped）两类 D7 嵌入（embedding）分别表示两个相，通过自由能比较确定一阶相变（first-order transition）；并结合在壳（on-shell）分析拆解有限温度位移的来源，计算纵向与反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity）。

Result: 在所考察温度下，正 Gauss–Bonnet 耦合使共存移向更大味质量并扩大热力学偏好的外尔半金属区，负耦合则反向移动；归一化零温相变不变。有限温度位移主要由热标度的有效重标度（effective rescaling）解释，并受到固定视界几何贡献的对抗。相区扩大不意味着更强电响应：共存处纵向与反常霍尔电导跳变可减小，纵向跳变的耦合响应随温度反转，纵向直流电导的质量依赖极大值也可能不再位于热力学偏好分支上。

Conclusion: 热几何形变同时改变了哪一相被偏好以及外尔半金属相如何导电，说明相图变化与输运性质变化需分别评估。

Abstract: We study how an effective Gauss--Bonnet deformation of the thermal geometry changes the competition between the conducting Weyl-semimetal and insulating flavour phases of the probe D3/D7 model. The phases are represented by horizon-reaching and smoothly capped D7 embeddings, respectively, whose free energies exhibit a first-order transition. At the temperatures examined, positive Gauss--Bonnet coupling shifts coexistence toward larger flavour mass and expands the thermodynamically preferred Weyl-semimetal region, while negative coupling shifts the boundary in the opposite direction. The normalized zero-temperature transition remains unchanged. An on-shell analysis shows that the leading finite-temperature shift is largely explained by an effective rescaling of the thermal scale, opposed by an intrinsic fixed-horizon geometric contribution. The enlargement of the conducting phase does not imply stronger electrical response: the longitudinal and anomalous Hall conductivity jumps at coexistence can decrease, and the coupling response of the longitudinal jump reverses with temperature. The mass-dependent maximum of the longitudinal DC conductivity can also cease to occur on the thermodynamically preferred branch. Thus the geometric deformation changes both which phase is favored and how the Weyl-semimetal phase conducts.

</details>


### [230] [From Z to all: cyclic sum rules for thermal CFT partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05281)
*Arnav Advant,Harsh Anand,Nathan Benjamin,Abhijit Gadde,Vipul Kumar,Shiraz Minwalla,Jyotirmoy Mukherjee,Sridip Pal,Asikur Rahaman,Pabitra Ray*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文显式求解了 CFT$_d$（$d=2\kappa$）在扭曲 $S^{d-1}\times S^1$ 上配分函数的高温展开所满足的无穷多个普适约束，并将其统一为一个与 $\mathbb{Z}_{\kappa+1}$ 同构的大共形微分同构方程；该方程表明 $\ln Z/\beta$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}_{\kappa+1}$ 不变部分由 a-反常（a-anomaly）固定为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作指出 CFT$_d$ 扭曲 $S^{d-1}\times S^1$ 配分函数的高温展开服从无穷层普适约束，但这些约束的具体形式及其统一结构尚不明确，本文旨在显式确定并整合这些约束。

Method: 假定 CP 不变性（CP-invariance）与热有效作用量（thermal effective action）的局域性，从嵌入空间（embedding space）出发，借助代数形式化（algebraic formalism）约束有效作用量的结构。

Result: 这些无穷约束可打包为单一方程，涉及一个与 $\mathbb{Z}_{\kappa+1}$ 同构的大共形微分同构（large conformal diffeomorphism），它循环置换热圆与旋转角；方程断言 $\ln Z/\beta$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}_{\kappa+1}$ 不变部分是由 a-反常固定的常数。

Conclusion: 扭曲球面配分函数高温展开的普适约束具有统一的 $\mathbb{Z}_{\kappa+1}$ 对称结构，其不变部分完全由 a-反常确定；结论建立在 CP 不变性与热有效作用量局域性假设之上。

Abstract: The high-temperature expansion of twisted $S^{d-1}\times S^1$ partition functions of CFT$_d$, $d = 2κ$, has recently been shown to obey an infinite tower of universal constraints. We determine these constraints explicitly and demonstrate that they can be packaged into a single equation involving a large conformal diffeomorphism isomorphic to $\mathbb{Z}_{κ+1}$, which cyclically permutes the thermal circle and the rotation angles. Our equation asserts that the $\mathbb{Z}_{κ+1}$-invariant part of $\ln Z/β$ is a constant fixed by the a-anomaly. Our derivation assumes CP-invariance and locality of the thermal effective action, whose structure we constrain using an algebraic formalism starting from embedding space.

</details>


### [231] [An Exact QFT/Lattice Map for Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05329)
*Shu-Heng Shao,Nikita Sopenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作构造了1+1维连续费米子场与格点场之间的精确映射，将连续哈密顿量改写为具有最近邻耦合与无限维局域希尔伯特空间的格点模型，并在任意有限格点上精确复现能谱、整体算子代数、手征对称性与反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求一种能精确保持连续量子场论（quantum field theory, QFT）性质、且避免畴壁费米子（domain wall fermions）在截断局域希尔伯特空间时引入额外无质量模式问题的格点表述。

Method: 构建连续费米子场到格点场的精确映射，得到带最近邻耦合与无限维局域希尔伯特空间的格点哈密顿量；对N个马约拉纳费米子（Majorana fermions）显式实现O(N)_L×O(N)_R格点手征对称性，并为Schwinger模型构造精确格点模型。

Result: 格点模型在任意有限格点上精确复现连续QFT的能谱、整体算子代数、手征对称性与反常；截断局域希尔伯特空间不引入额外无质量模式，区间纠缠熵随截断去除而收敛；格点局域代数为I型并位于嵌套空间区域的III型连续代数之间，符合分裂性质（split property）；数值上复现Schwinger模型的质量间隙e/√π。

Conclusion: 该精确映射为保持对称性与反常的格点费米子构造提供了新途径，并借助算子代数（operator algebra）视角与数值验证说明了其一致性与可行性。

Abstract: We construct an exact map between continuum fermion fields and lattice fields in 1+1 dimensions. This map rewrites a continuum Hamiltonian as a lattice Hamiltonian with nearest-neighbor couplings and an infinite-dimensional local Hilbert space. The resulting lattice model reproduces exactly the energy spectrum, global operator algebra, chiral symmetries, and anomalies of the continuum QFT on any finite lattice. Unlike domain wall fermions, truncating the local Hilbert space introduces no extra massless modes, and the entanglement entropy of an interval converges as the truncation is removed. The lattice local algebras are of type I and lie between the type III continuum algebras of nested spatial regions, in accordance with the split property. We demonstrate this construction explicitly for $N$ Majorana fermions, realizing the $\mathrm{O}(N)_\mathrm{L}\times\mathrm{O}(N)_\mathrm{R}$ lattice chiral symmetry. We also construct an exact lattice model for the Schwinger model and numerically reproduce the mass gap $e/\sqrtπ$.

</details>


### [232] [Boundary $c\leq1$ Liouville CFT from the Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05378)
*Panos Betzios,Paul Ghiringhelli,Olga Papadoulaki,Ioannis Tsiares*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了中心电荷（central charge）$c\leq1$ 的边界刘维尔共形场论（boundary Liouville conformal field theory），采用 FZZT 型边界条件（FZZT-type boundary conditions），并证明其在圆盘（disk）与环面（annulus）上满足 Cardy–Lewellen 缝合约束（sewing constraints）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人工作（如 $c\geq25$ 情形及 Ribault–Santachiara 的闭弦谱）已建立部分结果，但 $c\leq1$ 时边界三点函数与体-边结构常数（bulk-to-boundary structure constants）等关键数据缺失，边界理论的一致性尚未被完整验证。

Method: 采用解析自举（analytic bootstrap）方法，推导缺失的边界三点函数、体-边结构常数以及自洽的圆盘一点函数，并将其表示为刘维尔动量（Liouville momenta）的显式亚纯函数（meromorphic functions）。

Result: 所得数据使理论满足圆盘与环面上的 Cardy–Lewellen 缝合约束；与 $c\geq25$ 形成鲜明对比，两个“类时”（timelike）FZZT 边界之间的开弦谱（open-string spectrum）是离散的，但经开-闭对偶（open-closed duality）与 Ribault–Santachiara 的连续闭弦谱（continuous closed-string spectrum）非平凡地保持一致。

Conclusion: 结果建立了 $c\leq1$ 边界刘维尔理论的自洽性，并揭示低中心电荷区开弦离散谱与闭弦连续谱通过开-闭对偶相容的反常谱结构。

Abstract: We construct boundary Liouville conformal field theory at central charge $c\leq1$ with FZZT-type boundary conditions and show that it satisfies the Cardy--Lewellen sewing constraints on the disk and the annulus. Using the analytic bootstrap we derive the so far missing boundary three-point and bulk-to-boundary structure constants and a consistent disk one-point function as explicit meromorphic functions of the Liouville momenta. In sharp contrast to $c\geq25$, the open-string spectrum between two ``timelike'' FZZT boundaries is \emph{discrete}, yet non-trivially consistent through open-closed duality with the \emph{continuous} closed-string spectrum of Ribault and Santachiara.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Stationary distribution of active Brownian particles in a bounded domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03865)
*Alexandra Tzella,Daniel Loghin,David J. Needham,Jacques Vanneste*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particles, ABPs）在边界处的稳态分布，指出其概率密度由正则的体密度与奇异的边界分量组成。作者通过相似解（similarity solution）刻画转向点附近密度行为，并借助匹配渐近分析（matched asymptotics）研究小空间扩散极限下的正则化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动粒子（self-propelled particles）如游动微生物常聚集于边界，ABP 模型可简洁刻画该现象，但其在边界处的概率密度同时包含体（bulk）与边界分量，二者交换对应捕获与再进入，需澄清其稳态分布结构与奇异性。

Method: 先在稳态下求转向点（turning points，取向与边界相切、粒子重新进入体相处）附近的相似解（similarity solution）；再在 ABP 模型中加入空间扩散（spatial diffusion），用匹配渐近分析（matched asymptotics）研究扩散系数 eps 趋于 0 时的正则化效果。数值验证采用格点马尔可夫链（lattice Markov chain）与有限元离散（finite-element discretisation）。

Result: 相似解表明体密度（bulk density）具有奇异性，边界密度（boundary density）不可微。匹配渐近分析给出有限 eps 在转向点附近的正则化描述，并与两类数值模拟结果进行对照评估。

Conclusion: ABP 在边界附近的稳态密度需同时考虑正则体分量与奇异边界分量，转向点是理解二者耦合及小扩散极限奇异行为的关键；有限空间扩散对密度奇异性起正则化作用。

Abstract: Self-propelled particles such as swimming micro-organisms often accumulate at boundaries. Active Brownian particles (ABPs), that is, particles moving at constant speed with an orientation undergoing rotational diffusion, provide a simple model. ABPs have a finite probability to be on the boundary. Their probability density therefore includes both regular bulk and singular boundary components. Exchanges between these represent capture at the boundary and re-entry into the bulk.
  We examine the distribution of ABPs in the stationary regime. We first obtain a similarity solution that describes the probability density near the turning points, where orientation is tangential to the boundary and particles re-enter the bulk. This shows that the bulk density is singular and the boundary density is non-differentiable. We then add spatial diffusion to the ABP model and investigate the limit $\eps \to 0$ of small spatial diffusivity. We use matched asymptotics to describe the regularising effect of a finite $\eps$, focusing on the vicinity of the turning points. We assess our predictions against numerical simulations using both lattice Markov chain and finite-element discretisations.

</details>


### [234] [Effect of Small Particle Interactions on Assembly of Binary Colloidal Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03909)
*Dillip Kumar Mohapatra,Teun W. J. Verouden,Bos Everhardus Sprik,Wouter G. Ellenbroek,Janne-Mieke Meijer*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用临界卡西米尔力（critical Casimir forces）可逆调控二元胶体混合物的粒子间相互作用，揭示小颗粒的动力学冻结与运动性变化如何决定二元团簇在结构优化与动力学阻滞（kinetic arrest）之间的竞争结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体自组装（colloidal self-assembly）中的核心难题在于理解相互作用强度如何影响层级结构形成过程中结构优化与动力学阻滞之间的竞争，尤其当多对相互作用同时存在时这一竞争机制尚不清楚。

Method: 将不同尺寸的二氧化硅胶体分散于近临界水/2,6-二甲基吡啶（2,6-lutidine）溶剂中，借助临界卡西米尔力提供可逆且温度可调吸引；系统调节大-大（L-L）、大-小（L-S）与小-小（S-S）三对有效相互作用，在壁限域的准二维（quasi-two-dimensional）几何中利用共聚焦激光扫描显微镜（confocal laser scanning microscopy）成像并进行粒子分辨的团簇生长与键动力学分析。

Result: L-L 与 L-S 相互作用共同导致高壳层占据率的致密 L-S 核壳团簇（core-shell clusters）；额外引入 S-S 相互作用则抑制壳层形成，转而生成扩展的支化二元网络（branched binary networks）。粒子分辨分析表明，结构转变由小颗粒运动性从高度动态、驱动结构重组，转变为冻结动力学导致动力学捕获（kinetic trapping）所支配。

Conclusion: 相互作用所支配的小颗粒运动性与动力学阻滞决定有限二元团簇的生长路径，为二元晶体及动力学捕获的空间贯穿二元胶体网络与凝胶（gels）的形成提供微观层面认识。

Abstract: A central challenge in colloidal self-assembly is understanding how interaction strength influences the competition between structural optimization and kinetic arrest during the formation of hierarchical structures. Here, we investigate the assembly process of a binary mixture of differently sized silica colloids dispersed in a near-critical water/2,6-lutidine solvent, where critical Casimir forces provide reversible and temperature tunable attractions. By systematically tuning the effective interactions between large-large (L-L), large-small (L-S), and small-small (S-S) particle pairs, we directly observe how the nucleation and growth of the binary colloid assemblies evolve in a wall-confined quasi-two-dimensional geometry when multiple pair-interactions are present. Confocal laser scanning microscopy imaging shows that combined L-L interactions and L-S interactions lead to compact L-S core-shell clusters with high shell occupancy, while the additional presence of S-S interactions suppress the shell formation and lead to extended, branched binary networks. Particle-resolved analyses of cluster growth and bond dynamics reveal that these structural transitions are governed by the motion of the small particles in the clusters changing from highly dynamic leading to structural reorganization to frozen dynamics resulting kinetic trapping. Our findings demonstrate how the interaction-governed mobility and kinetic arrest of small particles influence the growth of finite binary clusters and offers microscopic insight into the formation of binary crystals and kinetically trapped space-spanning binary colloidal networks and gels.

</details>


### [235] [Thermodynamically consistent thixotropic viscoelastic modeling of human blood from a conformational free energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03949)
*Thomas Martinod,Andrés Córdoba,Jay D. Schieber*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出 ROUGE 张量模型，用单一 Helmholtz 自由能统一导出血液应力、三个结构变量弛豫与熵产生，从而保证任意流场下满足热力学第二定律，且仅含五个参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结构触变性黏弹性模型（structural thixotropic viscoelastic models）虽能准确描述人血流变，但其结构动力学为假设性设定，无法保证与热力学第二定律相容。

Method: 以两个构象张量（conformation tensors）描述单个红细胞及缗钱状聚集体（rouleaux）的形变，以标量变量描述聚集度；扩展非平衡热力学（nonequilibrium thermodynamics）的简化表述至多类结构变量描述的多尺度微结构，并由单一假设的 Helmholtz 自由能同时确定应力、三变量弛豫与熵产生；聚集与聚集体形变的耦合基于纤维蛋白原（fibrinogen）的 Langmuir 吸附并加约束，使静息全聚集态为自由能稳定极小。

Result: 对已发表剪切率阶跃升、阶跃降（step-ups/step-downs）数据的拟合表明纤维蛋白原结合位点远未饱和，故自由能无可调参数；聚集体破坏无需经验剪切激活项，结构破坏量与流动对聚集体所做功成正比，单一结构时间即可同时给出数秒内破坏与数十秒的代数式恢复（algebraic recovery）。模型最终含五个良定参数，拟合所得溶剂黏度与血浆黏度一致。

Conclusion: 将该类血液本构模型的动力学建立在自由能基础之上，可在保留描述精度的同时获得热力学自洽性与更少的经验参数，为结构触变性血液建模提供了物理可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Structural thixotropic viscoelastic models describe the rheology of human blood accurately, but their structural kinetics are postulated, so their compatibility with the second law of thermodynamics is not guaranteed. We present ROUGE (ROUleaux model Guided by free Energy), a tensorial model of blood that describes the deformation of individual red blood cells and of rouleaux with two conformation tensors, and the degree of aggregation with a scalar variable. A single postulated Helmholtz free energy determines the stress, the relaxation of all three variables, and the entropy generation, so the model satisfies the second law in any flow. To build it, we extend a simplified formulation of nonequilibrium thermodynamics, which accounts for conformational energy and entropy, to microstructures described by several structural variables of different kinds. The coupling between aggregation and rouleaux deformation is motivated by the Langmuir adsorption of fibrinogen and is restricted so that the quiescent, fully aggregated state is the stable minimum of the free energy. Fits to published data for step-ups and step-downs in shear rate show that the fibrinogen binding sites remain far from saturation, so that the free energy has no adjustable parameters. They also show that rouleaux breakdown requires no empirical shear-activation term: the structure is destroyed in proportion to the work done by the flow on the aggregates, and a single structural time produces both breakdown within seconds and an algebraic recovery over tens of seconds. The resulting model has five well-determined parameters, and its fitted solvent viscosity coincides with that of plasma.

</details>


### [236] [Non-equilibrium orientational dynamics of pinned topological defects in two-dimensional nematic liquid crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04071)
*Petur Bryde,Jörn Dunkel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种线性相位扩散（linear phase-diffusion）表述，可准确预测二维向列液晶中被钉扎拓扑缺陷的取向弛豫动力学，并据此实现瞬态织构图案的反向设计与稀疏测量下的隐藏缺陷构型推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维向列液晶的长波动力学由拓扑缺陷运动主导，其平动可用有效向列弹性粒子间作用力描述，但取向动力学的准静态力矩描述常因时间尺度分离失效而崩溃，严重阻碍织构响应动力学的反向设计。

Method: 在非线性二维Landau-de Gennes理论框架下，引入线性相位扩散（linear phase-diffusion）表述，用于刻画被钉扎拓扑缺陷的取向弛豫动力学。

Result: 该表述能够准确预测被钉扎缺陷的取向弛豫动力学；并展示了其对瞬态向列图案反向设计（inverse design）以及从稀疏测量推断隐藏缺陷构型的能力。

Conclusion: 线性相位扩散表述克服了准静态力矩描述因时间尺度分离失效所带来的障碍，在向列液晶织构动力学的反向设计与缺陷构型推断方面具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: The long-wavelength dynamics of two-dimensional (2D) nematic liquid crystals (LCs) is dominated by the motion of topological defects, which behave like interacting particles endowed with both topological charge and orientation. In experimentally relevant regimes, the translational dynamics of the defect positions can be accurately described by effective nematoelastic particle-particle interaction forces. By contrast, analogous quasistatic torque descriptions for the orientational dynamics typically fail due to a breakdown of time-scale separation, severely hindering the inverse design of texture response dynamics in nematic LCs. To overcome this obstruction, we introduce a linear phase-diffusion formulation that accurately predicts the orientational relaxation dynamics of pinned topological defects in the nonlinear 2D Landau-de Gennes theory. The broad practical potential of this approach is demonstrated by showing how it enables the inverse design of transient nematic patterns as well as the inference of hidden defect configurations from sparse measurements.

</details>


### [237] [Multi-Fidelity Machine Learning for Predicting Startup Stress Growth and Strain Hardening in Wormlike Micellar Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04115)
*Saghar Zolfaghari,Nunzia Lauriello,Ilaria Cusano,Marcello Ferranti,Rossana Pasquino,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出多保真度机器学习（multi-fidelity machine learning）框架，用稀疏实验数据预测CPyCl基蠕虫状胶束（wormlike micelles）的应力增长曲线（stress-growth curves）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蠕虫状胶束溶液的非线性启动响应（stress overshoot、undershoot、strain hardening）随配方变化，跨组分与剪切率预测困难；且盐化学、浓度、剪切率的数据覆盖稀疏且不均匀，难以仅依赖数据密集型神经网络训练。

Method: 框架将描述整体启动响应的低保真度解析模型（low-fidelity analytical model）与捕捉配方特异性非线性特征的高保真度实验数据（high-fidelity experimental data）相结合，构建多保真度学习框架。

Result: 训练后的模型可在未测试剪切率下预测完整的应力增长响应，并能从插值曲线中提取应变硬化特征；经验证可增强稀疏流变测量数据。

Conclusion: 该框架为探索配方变量、线性黏弹性（linear viscoelasticity）与非线性启动行为之间的关系提供基础，并支持蠕虫状胶束溶液更高效的表征。

Abstract: Wormlike micellar solutions exhibit formulation-dependent nonlinear startup responses, including stress overshoot, undershoot, and strain hardening, making prediction across compositions and shear rates challenging. We developed a multi-fidelity machine-learning framework to predict stress-growth curves in cetylpyridinium chloride (CPyCl)-based wormlike micelles from sparse experimental measurements. The framework combines a low-fidelity analytical model describing the overall startup response with high-fidelity experimental data capturing formulation-specific nonlinear features. This approach addresses the limited and uneven coverage of salt chemistry, concentration, and shear rate without relying solely on data-intensive neural-network training. The trained model predicts complete stress-growth responses at untested shear rates and enables extraction of strain-hardening characteristics from interpolated curves. By augmenting sparse rheological measurements, the framework provides a basis for exploring relationships among formulation variables, linear viscoelastic properties, and nonlinear startup behavior, while supporting more efficient characterization of wormlike micellar solutions.

</details>


### [238] [Topology Does Not Determine Defect Mobility in Active Polar Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04428)
*Abhra Puitandy,Shradha Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在二维可压缩Toner–Tu模型中，固定±1缺陷对的电荷与间距、仅改变+1缺陷的内部形态（morphology），发现活性导致形态依赖的迁移率不对称：反转星状体（aster）的径向取向会反转哪个缺陷移动更远，且星状体响应最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷（topological defects）通常仅以电荷（charge）刻画，但同电荷缺陷可具有不同内部形态；该文旨在检验形态是否也是支配缺陷对运动的独立动力学变量。

Method: 采用二维可压缩Toner–Tu模型（two-dimensional compressible Toner–Tu model）研究孤立±1缺陷对的湮灭（annihilation），在保持初始电荷与间距不变的前提下改变+1缺陷的形态，并结合早期时间分析（early-time analysis）考察密度模式与极化的耦合。

Result: 活性产生形态依赖的迁移率不对称（mobility asymmetry）；反转星状体的径向取向会反转哪个缺陷运动更远，星状体响应最强。早期分析显示初始形态选择不同的密度模式，其梯度随后反馈至极化（polarization）；去除密度—梯度耦合会显著抑制该不对称。

Conclusion: 除拓扑电荷外，缺陷形态亦构成活性极性物质（active polar matter）中支配缺陷对运动的动力学变量。

Abstract: Topological defects are commonly characterized by their charge, although defects with the same charge can have different internal morphologies. We study the annihilation of isolated $\pm1$ defect pairs in a two-dimensional compressible Toner--Tu model, varying the morphology of the $+1$ defect while keeping the initial charges and separation fixed. Activity produces a morphology-dependent mobility asymmetry: reversing the radial orientation of an aster reverses which defect travels farther, and asters show the strongest response. An early-time analysis reveals that the initial morphology selects distinct density modes whose gradients subsequently feed back on the polarization. Consistently, removing the density-gradient coupling strongly suppresses the asymmetry. These results establish defect morphology, in addition to topological charge, as a dynamical variable governing defect-pair motion in active polar matter.

</details>


### [239] [Amplified transport variability in granular slope flows near the critical angle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04440)
*Fumiaki Nakai,Yuto Sasaki*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟表明，在接近临界角（critical angle）时，相同宏观条件与相同基底脉冲（basal pulse）下颗粒坡面输运量的变异性显著放大，标准差可超过均值，因此需以分布而非均值描述输运，并需引入含涨落（fluctuations）的本构描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震震动等机械扰动可使颗粒坡面发生运动，理解其停止前的输运（transport）对地形变化预测、滑坡灾害评估与工业粉体流动控制均很重要；实验已显示较小扰动可触发雪崩、接近稳定极限时动员面积更大，但在相同宏观条件与相同强迫下事件间输运量差异有多大仍不清楚。

Method: 采用数值模拟（numerical simulations），对具有相同宏观条件但初始颗粒构型（particle configurations）不同的静态颗粒层施加完全相同的基底脉冲；随后在能量平衡（energy balance）中引入涨落，构建随机模型（stochastic model）。

Result: 靠近临界角时输运变异性被放大：平均输运量大致与距临界角的角距离成反比增加，但标准差（standard deviation）增长更快，甚至可超过均值，即相同强迫可产生差异很大的输运量。

Conclusion: 给定宏观条件赋予唯一响应的确定性本构律（deterministic constitutive laws）无法刻画这种变异性；评估临界角附近的输运需考虑其分布而非仅均值，并推动纳入涨落的本构描述。

Abstract: Mechanical disturbances such as seismic shaking can set a granular slope in motion. Understanding the resulting transport before arrest is important for predicting topographic change, assessing landslide hazards and controlling powder flows in industrial processes. Experiments show that smaller disturbances can trigger avalanches and larger areas are mobilized as slopes approach their stability limit. Yet how much transport varies between events under the same macroscopic conditions and forcing remains unclear. Here we use numerical simulations to show that transport variability is amplified near the critical angle. We apply identical basal pulses to static granular layers with the same macroscopic conditions but different initial particle configurations. Although the mean transport increases approximately in inverse proportion to the angular distance from the critical angle, the standard deviation increases even faster and can exceed the mean. Thus, identical forcing can produce widely different transport amounts. Deterministic constitutive laws that assign a unique response to given macroscopic conditions cannot capture this variability. We introduce fluctuations into the energy balance to develop a stochastic model that captures the main features of the broadening transport distribution. These results show that assessing transport near the critical angle requires considering its distribution, not just its mean, and motivate constitutive descriptions that incorporate fluctuations.

</details>


### [240] [Hydrated porous substrates modulate the spatiotemporal dynamics of bacterial turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04668)
*Zixiang Lin,Jin Zhu,Hongyi Bian,Zijie Qu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究考察多孔水合基底（琼脂）对近界面细菌湍流的影响，发现密集菌液中面内集体动能随琼脂浓度非单调变化，且随时间内集体流动显著衰减、空间关联变短。作者将平均场动理学理论（MFKT）扩展至含界面法向渗流与切向滑移，定性解释了较小流动结构的成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界条件对细菌湍流影响显著，但水合多孔基底如何影响近界面的集体运动尚不清楚，故需明确孔隙边界对细菌集体动力学的作用机制。

Method: 实验上表征不同浓度琼脂基底上方稀薄与密集大肠杆菌（Escherichia coli）悬液的时空动力学，并以玻璃作为非多孔参考；理论上将平均场动理学理论（mean-field kinetic theory, MFKT）扩展，纳入界面法向渗流（normal seepage）与切向滑移（tangential slip），并进行线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）。

Result: 密集悬液加载后短时间内，面内集体动能随琼脂浓度呈非单调变化，而个体游动能与特征空间尺度变化较小；在琼脂上方五分钟内集体流动强烈衰减，空间关联变短、时间关联变长、高波数（wavenumber）相对谱权重增大。理论预测相关长度尺度减小，可定性解释随时间及高琼脂浓度下出现的更小流动结构。

Conclusion: 多孔基底以依赖渗透率的滑移长度与流体层厚度调控近界面集体模态的不稳定性截断，导致细菌湍流在强度、持续性与空间组织上呈现基底依赖的差异化变化。

Abstract: Boundary conditions strongly influence bacterial turbulence, motivating the question of how hydrated porous substrates affect near-interface collective motion. We characterize the spatiotemporal dynamics of dilute and dense Escherichia coli suspensions above agar substrates of varying concentrations, with glass serving as a nonporous reference. In dense suspensions shortly after loading, collective in-plane kinetic energy varies nonmonotonically with agar concentration, while individual swimming energy and characteristic spatial scales show comparatively little variation. Over five minutes above agar, the collective flow attenuates strongly, accompanied by shorter spatial correlations, longer temporal correlations, and a greater relative spectral weight at higher wavenumbers. To explore how the porous boundary may contribute to these changes, we extend mean-field kinetic theory (MFKT) to incorporate normal seepage and tangential slip at the interface. Linear stability analysis distinguishes near-interface collective modes from those in the bulk and identifies how fluid-layer thickness and permeability-dependent slip length modify the instability cutoff. The predicted reduction in the associated length scale provides a qualitative interpretation of the smaller flow structures observed over time and at higher agar concentrations. These findings reveal distinct substrate-dependent changes in the intensity, persistence, and spatial organization of near-interface bacterial turbulence.

</details>


### [241] [Statistical approach of random packing of polydisperse similar hyperspheres: with application to binomial and lognormal size distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04703)
*H. J. H. Brouwers*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文推导了离散二项分布与连续对数正态分布多分散超球（hyperspheres）的堆积分数（packing fraction）闭式表达式，并给出覆盖任意标准差、欧氏维度、颗粒类型与堆积模式的统一公式，与文献数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分散颗粒混合物（polydisperse packings）的堆积分数缺乏涵盖离散与连续粒径分布、任意欧氏维度（Euclidean dimension）的解析表达，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于小粒径比二元堆积（binary packings）解析模型，将其应用于多分散混合中统计上可能的颗粒对，先导出小尺寸差离散颗粒的闭式表达；再对二项数分布（binomial number distribution）取粒径比 u→1 与颗粒数趋于无穷的极限，转化为连续对数正态分布（lognormal distribution）。

Result: 得到由标准差、欧氏维度 D 与单分散堆积分数（monosized packing fraction）控制的闭式表达式；在 D=2、3 及小标准差下与文献数据吻合良好，并发现大标准差时缩放堆积分数趋于与维度无关的极限值。

Conclusion: 提出的闭式表达式同时匹配小标准差解与大标准差渐近线（asymptote），可覆盖任意标准差、欧氏维度、颗粒类型及其堆积模式，具有较强普适性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the packing fraction of polydisperse similar hyperspheres with a discrete binomial and continuous lognormal particle size distribution. First, a closed form expression is derived of a mix consisting of multiple discrete particles in D-dimensional Euclidean space R^D with small size differences. A recently developed analytical model for binary packings with small size ratio u is used, here applying it to the statistically probable particle pairs in the multi-sized mix. The new model is subsequently applied to discretely sized particles with a binomial number distribution and a constant size ratio u between each subsequent particle size. By taking the limit of u -> 1 and an infinite number of particles sizes, the binomial particle distribution is transformed into a continuous lognormal distribution. The resulting expression for the packing fraction can be solved in closed form, resulting in an original expression for the packing fraction of mixtures of hyperparticles with a lognormal distribution. This explicit expression is governed by the standard deviation, the Euclidean dimension D, and the monosized packing fraction of the considered particle shape. For a number of particle shapes and their packing modes (from looser to denser) the scaled expression is thoroughly compared with data reported in the literature. They concern packed particles with small standard deviations and in Euclidean dimensions D = 2 and 3, and good agreement is found. For large standard deviations the scaled packing fraction approaches a limiting value, which appears not to depend on the Euclidean dimension. Based on the insight, a closed form expression is put forward for the packing fraction that matches the small standard deviation solution, and the large standard deviation asymptote, so covering arbitrary standard deviations, Euclidean dimensions, particle types, and their mode of packing.

</details>


### [242] [Long-time Brownian dynamics of hard and sticky colloids by geometric projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05018)
*Rémi Martin,Sandrine Duluard,Céline Merlet*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于过阻尼约束（overdamped constraint-based）的布朗动力学方法，以几何投影替代对刚性近接触力的时间积分，在保持硬球结构与聚集动力学正确的前提下将可达物理时间延长1–2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子分辨的胶体模拟受限于模型中最快的微观时间尺度，对聚集胶体尤为严重：显式近接触势迫使积分步长解析窄吸引阱内成键粒子对的快速位置弛豫。

Method: 在每次自由布朗更新后，通过将粒子位置投影到约束构型集上，以几何投影步强制硬球排斥、粘性键与硬壁相互作用，从而避免时间解析的刚性力积分。

Result: 该方法保持硬球结构与扩散行为，复现粘性硬球（sticky hard spheres）的聚集动力学，并在同等计算成本下将物理时间延长1–2个数量级；由此实现低Péclet数电泳沉积的粒子尺度模拟，揭示悬浮液先致密化、随后形成键合网络的路径。

Conclusion: 该框架以LAMMPS fix形式实现，为稠密受限胶体系统的长时间布朗模拟提供了模块化途径。

Abstract: Particle-resolved colloid simulations are often limited by the fastest microscopic time scale retained in the model, which can be many orders of magnitude shorter than the collective process of interest. This is especially restrictive for aggregating colloids, where explicit near-contact potentials force the time integration to resolve the rapid positional relaxation of bonded pairs within narrow attractive wells. Here we introduce an overdamped constraint-based Brownian dynamics method that replaces the time-resolved integration of stiff near-contact forces by a geometric projection step. After each free Brownian update, hard-sphere exclusion, sticky bonds, and hard-wall interactions are enforced by projecting the particle positions onto the constrained configuration set. The method preserves hard-sphere structure and diffusion, recovers the aggregation kinetics of sticky hard spheres, and extends the physical time reached at fixed computational cost by one to two orders of magnitude relative to the explicit-potential simulations tested here. This acceleration makes Brownian-dominated, low-Péclet electrophoretic deposition accessible to particle-scale simulations, revealing a pathway in which the suspension first densifies and only subsequently develops a bonded network. Implemented as a LAMMPS fix, the framework provides a modular route to long-time Brownian simulations of dense constrained colloidal systems.

</details>


### [243] [Designing tunable hyperuniformity in random organization through nonlocal noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05085)
*Yusheng Lei,Yuhan Peng,Ran Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作表明，由粒子动力学产生的有效驱动噪声可在保持普通扩散与质心守恒的同时选择超均匀指数，并通过两种基于偏置随机组织（biased random organization, BRO）的微观协议实现可调的超均匀标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超均匀流体（hyperuniform fluids）虽能抑制大尺度密度涨落，但用以设计其渐近标度的微观协议仍十分有限，缺乏将目标超均匀指数与具体微观动力学联系起来的物理基础。

Method: 提出两种基于偏置随机组织（BRO）的协议：其一将二维BRO层通过密度依赖的驱动幅度非互易地耦合到三维布朗气体；其二引入长度服从类帕累托分布（Pareto-like distribution）P(ℓ)∼ℓ^(-1-b)的互易驱动。

Result: 第一种协议在绝热极限下产生空间非局域噪声，其功率谱为q^3，对应S(q→0)∼q^1；第二种协议在2<b<4时二阶矩有限而保持普通扩散、四阶矩发散使长波噪声谱变为q^b，涨落密度场理论预测S(q→0)∼q^(b-2)并可连续调节，得到粒子模拟支持。

Conclusion: 结果将可设计的超均匀标度与明确的微观动力学相联系，为非平衡粒子系统中集体密度关联（collective density correlations）的设计提供了物理依据。

Abstract: Hyperuniform fluids suppress density fluctuations at large length scales, but microscopic protocols for designing their asymptotic scaling remain limited. Here we show how effective driving noise generated by particle dynamics can select the hyperuniform exponent while preserving ordinary diffusion and pairwise center-of-mass conservation of the organized particles. We establish this connection through two protocols based on biased random organization (BRO). First, a two-dimensional BRO layer is nonreciprocally coupled to a three-dimensional Brownian gas through density-dependent kick amplitudes. Bulk density fluctuations generate spatially nonlocal noise with a $q^3$ power spectrum in the adiabatic limit, producing $S(q\to0)\sim q^1$. Second, we introduce reciprocal kicks whose lengths follow a Pareto-like distribution, $P(\ell)\sim\ell^{-1-b}$. For $2<b<4$, the finite second moment supports ordinary diffusion, while the divergent fourth moment changes the long-wavelength noise spectrum to $q^b$. The corresponding fluctuating density field theory predicts continuously tunable hyperuniform scaling, $S(q\to0)\sim q^{b-2}$, supported by particle simulations. Together, these results connect targeted hyperuniform scaling to explicit microscopic dynamics and provide a physical basis for designing collective density correlations in nonequilibrium particle systems.

</details>


### [244] [How symmetry and complexity shape three-dimensional programmable self-assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05301)
*Prarthana Agrawal,Joakim Bohlin,Ard A. Louis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文以三维多立方体（polycube）模型研究自组装（self-assembly）规则的概率性，发现结构的组装规则概率随组装复杂度（assembly complexity）指数下降，且最高概率结构来自高度对称结构的极小集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图回答某些结构是否本质上更易于设计，即它们是否允许更多组装规则集合，以及这一性质能否由几何预测，从而为最小化可编程自组装系统的设计提供依据。

Method: 采用带可编程面对面结合（face-to-face binding）的三维多立方体模型，将结构的组装规则概率定义为随机选取的规则集合产生该结构的概率，并用组装复杂度（即指定组装规则所需的最小信息量）进行量化分析，同时考察对称性的作用。

Result: 对多立方体而言，组装规则概率随组装复杂度指数下降，因为低复杂度结构不仅由最小规则集产生，也由大量更复杂的规则集产生；高概率结构来自高度对称结构的一个极小子集；但对称性本身不足以决定组装复杂度，相同对称性的结构在所需组装信息上可以差异显著。

Conclusion: 研究将信息、对称性与组装规则概率联系起来，表明识别允许规则复用（rule reuse）的几何特征对于设计最小可编程自组装系统至关重要。

Abstract: Self-assembly enables complex structures to emerge from simple building blocks through local interaction rules. Are some structures intrinsically easier to design, that is, do they admit more sets of assembly rules than others? And, can this be predicted from their geometry? We address these questions using a three-dimensional polycube model with programmable face-to-face binding, defining a structure's assembly rule probability as the chance that a randomly chosen rule set produces it. For polycubes, this probability decreases exponentially with assembly complexity, the minimum information required to specify a structure's assembly rules. Intuitively, this follows because structures with low assembly complexity are generated not only by minimal rule sets but also by numerous more elaborate ones, and so arise disproportionately often. Symmetry can reduce assembly complexity by allowing the same building blocks and local interactions to be reused across different parts of a structure. Indeed, we find that the highest probability structures come from a tiny subset of highly symmetric structures. However, symmetry alone does not determine assembly complexity. Structures with the same symmetry can differ substantially in the amount of assembly information they require, depending on how effectively their global symmetry can be exploited by local growth rules. Overall, our results link information, symmetry, and assembly rule probability and suggest that identifying which geometric features permit rule reuse is central to designing minimal programmable self-assembling systems.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [A note on the BRST-BV Formalism Explanation of Deformation Quantization and Algebraic Index Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04258)
*Lihao Zhao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文从流形上单个自由粒子的一维场论出发，经BRST形式化与BV量子化并配合几何识别，重新导出辛流形上的形变量子化与代数指标定理，从而阐明这些数学结果的物理来源与机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望揭示形变量子化（deformation quantization）与代数指标定理（algebraic index theorem）这两项数学结果背后的物理起源与内在机制，而非仅作纯数学论证。

Method: 以描述流形（manifold）上单个自由粒子的一维场论为起点，按物理学标准程序对其应用BRST形式化（BRST formalism）与BV量子化（BV quantization）；所进行的计算虽不完全严格，但具有更清晰的物理解释，随后结合几何识别（geometric identifications）推进。

Result: 通过上述计算与几何识别，成功重新得到辛流形（symplectic manifold）上的形变量子化与代数指标定理。

Conclusion: 该工作表明辛流形上形变量子化与代数指标定理可由自由粒子一维场论的BRST/BV处理自然导出，从而阐明了这些数学结果的物理起源与机制。

Abstract: In this note, we begin with considering the simple one-dimensional field theory describing a single free particle on manifold, and apply the BRST formalism and BV quantization to this model following the standard procedures in physics. Through calculations that, while not fully rigorous, have a more transparent physical interpretation, then subsequent geometric identifications, we recover the deformation quantization and algebraic index theorem on symplectic manifold. This note thus elucidates the physical origins and mechanisms of these mathematical results.

</details>


### [246] [Hidden Symmetries of the Kepler Problem and Jacobi's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04791)
*Alejandro Moreno Díaz,Juan B. Sancho de Salas*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 通过位置-速度对偶与广义雅可比定理，将开普勒问题的速端曲线等同于常曲率度量的测地线，从几何上导出隐藏对称性，并自然恢复角动量与Runge-Lenz矢量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开普勒问题（Kepler problem）的隐藏对称性（hidden symmetries）通常以代数或解析方式导出，缺乏统一几何解释，本文旨在给出其几何刻画。

Method: 结合中心力系统的对偶（duality，即位置与速度互换）与广义雅可比定理（Jacobi's theorem），把给定能量的开普勒速端曲线（hodograph）等同于常曲率度量的测地线（geodesic）；补加上一个无穷远点后几何完备，并由其Killing矢量场生成能量超曲面上的有限维李代数。

Result: 无穷远点给出Killing代数的典范分解（两个矢量子空间），其对应的Noether不变量（Noether invariants）分别恢复角动量（angular momentum）与Runge-Lenz矢量。

Conclusion: 开普勒问题的隐藏对称性可由常曲率几何的Killing矢量场统一解释，两种守恒量作为同一几何分解的两个部分得到自然导出。

Abstract: We present a geometric derivation of the hidden symmetries of the Kepler problem, combining a duality for central-force systems, obtained by interchanging positions and velocities, with a generalized form of Jacobi's theorem. Applied to the dual Kepler problem, this theorem identifies Kepler hodographs of prescribed energy with geodesics of a metric of constant curvature. After adjoining one point at infinity, the resulting geometry is complete. Its Killing vector fields induce a finite-dimensional Lie algebra of infinitesimal symmetries on the corresponding energy hypersurface. The distinguished point at infinity determines a canonical decomposition of the Killing algebra into two vector subspaces, whose associated Noether invariants recover angular momentum and the Runge-Lenz vector.

</details>


### [247] [Pure Spinor Factorization of Belinski-Zakharov Solitons and Regular Gravitational Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03750)
*Steffen Aksteiner*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文将 Belinski-Zakharov 孤子构造分解为带符号 Pfaffian tau-函数，给出度规及其正则性条件的纯旋量描述；在此框架下证明渐近平坦平衡问题的唯一性并条件性给出全局存在判据，最终枚举出 356 类杆词渐近平坦正则解与 52 个渐近平直局部平坦孤立解，并探讨解空间的两种组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于为 Belinski-Zakharov 孤子解的正则性提供解析刻画，超越有限数值求解，从而系统理解渐近平坦（asymptotically flat）与渐近平直局部平坦（asymptotically locally flat）正则解的整体结构。

Method: 将 Belinski-Zakharov 孤子构造（soliton construction）分解为带符号 Pfaffian tau-函数，建立度规与正则性条件的纯旋量（pure-spinor）描述；据此推导积分杆数据（integral rod data）的解析限制，证明固定杆结构（rod structure）与周期下的唯一性，并结合穷举枚举与严格存在性证书构建解目录。

Result: 构造出覆盖至十二个转折点（turning points）的 356 个杆词类（rod-word classes）正则渐近平坦解，以及至八个转折点的 52 个孤立正则渐近平直局部平坦解；解析的正性（positivity）与光滑完备化结果将正则性推广至数值解之外；端点复合规则精确复现全部保留的至十二转折点渐近平坦词。

Conclusion: 解空间可由广义秩二根系（generalized rank-two root systems）与较简单端点几何的复合两种方式组织，其中端点复合规则与枚举结果完全一致，表明纯旋量与 tau-函数框架为分类正则孤子解提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We show that the Belinski-Zakharov soliton construction factorizes into signed Pfaffian tau-functions, revealing a pure-spinor description of the metric and its regularity conditions. We derive analytic restrictions on the integral rod data, prove uniqueness for the asymptotically flat balance problem at fixed rod structure and periods, and give a conditional criterion for global existence. Combining these results with exhaustive enumeration of the admissible data and rigorous existence certificates, we construct a catalogue of regular asymptotically flat solutions representing 356 rod-word classes through twelve turning points, and 52 isolated regular asymptotically locally flat solutions through eight turning points. Analytic positivity and smooth-completion results establish regularity beyond the finite numerical solve. Based on this catalogue, we investigate two organizations of the solution space: generalized rank-two root systems and compositions of simpler endpoint geometries. The endpoint composition rules reproduce exactly all retained asymptotically flat words through twelve turning points.

</details>


### [248] [The twistor construction of toric gravitational instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41867)
*Maciej Dunajski,Lionel Mason,Paul Tod*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文以黎曼球（Riemann sphere）上的有理矩阵函数显式构造全部 ALF 与 ALE 环面引力瞬子（toric gravitational instanton），并通过求解黎曼–希尔伯特分裂问题（Riemann–Hilbert splitting problem）实现重构与完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有环面引力瞬子（ALF/ALE）缺乏统一的显式构造与完整分类，本文旨在给出所有此类瞬子的显式描述与分类框架。

Method: 在黎曼球（Riemann sphere）上以单复变有理矩阵函数构造拼接矩阵（patching matrix），对应扭子全纯向量丛（twistor holomorphic vector bundle）；瞬子重构归结为黎曼–希尔伯特分裂问题（Riemann–Hilbert splitting problem）的求解，并借助线性代数方法完成，形式上类似但不完全等价于 Belinski–Zakharov ansatz。

Result: 给出环面引力瞬子（toric gravitational instanton）在锥形奇点（conical singularity）与轨道流形奇点（orbifold singularity）解消意义下的完整分类及显式形式；并通过计算机辅助数值构造展示了两个具有五个节点（five nodes）的新 ALF（非 AF）引力瞬子。

Conclusion: 有理矩阵函数与黎曼–希尔伯特分裂问题的线性代数求解为环面引力瞬子提供了统一、可计算的显式构造与分类工具，并可延伸至新瞬子的数值发现。

Abstract: We give an explicit construction of all ALF and ALE toric gravitational instantons in terms of certain rational matrix functions of one complex variable on the Riemann sphere; these provide patching matrices for their corresponding twistor holomorphic vector bundles. The reconstruction of the instantons arises from the solution to a Riemann--Hilbert splitting problem and we show how this can be solved via linear algebra, resembling but not equivalent to the Belinski--Zakharov ansatz. This construction gives a complete classification together with an explicit form for all toric gravitational instantons up to the resolutions of conical and orbifold singularities. We illustrate the formalism by computer--assisted numerical construction of two new ALF (but not AF) gravitational instantons with five nodes.

</details>


### [249] [Coisotropy-one homogeneous spacetimes I: (3,2)-Kinematical Lie algebras with $d\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00399)
*José Miguel Figueroa-O'Farrill,Juan Manuel Lorenzo-Naveiro*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是"余各向同性为一（coisotropy-one）齐性时空分类"系列论文的第一篇，旨在将运动学李群（kinematical Lie groups）的空间各向同性齐性时空分类推广到非空间各向同性情形，并分类了空间维数至少为4的(3,2)-运动学李代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有齐性时空分类主要局限于空间各向同性（spatially isotropic）情形，即旋转群在每个切空间的方向空间上传递作用；为推广到更广阔的非空间各向同性情境，需要发展新的分类工具，故启动该系列研究。

Method: 聚焦余各向同性为一（coisotropy-one）情形，在空间维数至少为4的条件下，对相关传递李代数（transitive Lie algebras）即(3,2)-运动学李代数（(3,2)-kinematical Lie algebras）进行分类，分类在保持旋转子代数（rotational subalgebra）的同构意义下进行。

Result: 完成了空间维数至少为4时余各向同性为一的(3,2)-运动学李代数的分类；作为副产品，还按适当的等价概念分类了Bianchi李代数的二维实表示（two-dimensional real representations）。

Conclusion: 该工作构成向非空间各向同性齐性时空分类扩展的第一步，为后续系列论文奠定基础；同时所获得的Bianchi李代数二维实表示分类可作为独立结果使用。

Abstract: This is the first of several papers devoted to the classification of coisotropy-one homogeneous spacetimes. It is the first step in extending the classification of spatially isotropic homogeneous spacetimes of kinematical Lie groups to the much vaster context of homogeneous spacetimes that are not spatially isotropic; that is, where the rotations no longer act transitively on the space of directions in each tangent space. In this first paper we classify (up to isomorphisms preserving the rotational subalgebra) a class of the relevant transitive Lie algebras, the so-called (3,2)-kinematical Lie algebras, for the coisotropy-one case in spatial dimension at least 4. As a by-product of this classification, we classify two-dimensional real representations of Bianchi Lie algebras up to a suitable notion of equivalence.

</details>


### [250] [Toric gravitational instantons with four fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05333)
*Lars Andersson,Mattias Dahl,Gustav Nilsson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对环面作用具有四个不动点的单连通环面引力瞬子（toric gravitational instantons）进行分类，证明X_3上给定twist的渐近平坦瞬子（asymptotically flat instanton）在尺度意义下唯一，验证了Li–Sun的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献（如Li–Sun）提出X_3上环面渐近平坦瞬子的唯一性猜想，但缺乏完整分类与证明；本文旨在对四不动点环面瞬子给出系统分类并验证该猜想。

Method: 结合Geroch猜想（Geroch's conjecture）的solitonic形式，将光滑性转化为soliton数据的多项式方程；并采用改编自Aksteiner的凸性原理（convexity principle），证明给定rod structure的锥度量（conical metrics）的cone errors是rod长度的严格凸函数梯度。存在性分别由ALF端的显式解与Li–Sun存在性定理给出。

Result: 分类结果包括：四共线中心的hyperkähler multi-Eguchi–Hanson与multi-Taub–NUT度量；X_3上的渐近平坦瞬子（固定环面定向、无穷远处twist在(0,1)内，每种twist在尺度意义下唯一）；以及一个ALF端、非hyperkähler的瞬子。后两者在任一取向下均非Hermitian。唯一性由严格凸性给出。

Conclusion: 在尺度意义下，X_3上给定twist的环面渐近平坦瞬子是唯一的，证实了Li–Sun猜想；文中还概述了用Aksteiner的chart将此类AF瞬子与Teo发现的瞬子等同起来。

Abstract: We classify the simply connected toric gravitational instantons whose torus action has four fixed points. They are the hyperkähler multi-Eguchi--Hanson and multi-Taub--NUT metrics with four collinear centres, the asymptotically flat instantons on Li and Sun's manifold $X_3$, up to scale one for each twist at infinity in $(0,1)$ for a fixed orientation of the torus, and, up to scale, a single further instanton with an ALF end, which is not hyperkähler. The last two are Hermitian for neither orientation. In particular, the toric asymptotically flat instanton on $X_3$ with a given twist is unique up to scale, as conjectured by Li and Sun. The proof combines the solitonic form of Geroch's conjecture, which turns smoothness into polynomial equations for the soliton data, with a convexity principle adapted from Aksteiner. At a fixed asymptotic parameter, the cone errors of the conical metrics with a given rod structure are the gradient of a strictly convex function of the rod lengths, and the strict convexity gives uniqueness. Existence follows from an explicit solution for the ALF end and, for the AF ends, from Li and Sun's existence theorem. We also outline how a chart of Aksteiner identifies these AF instantons with those found by Teo.

</details>

### [251] [Constraining the Superconducting Gap of Monolayer FeSe/SrTiO$_3$ via Statistical Analysis and Tip-Height-Dependent Tunneling Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03978)
*Dongwon Shin,Xiyou Peng,Kaleb McClure,Paolo Vilmercati,Chris Howard,Petro Maksymovych,Norman Mannella,Hanno H. Weitering,Wonhee Ko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过超低温扫描隧道光谱（scanning tunneling spectroscopy）的温度、针尖-样品间距与空间位置依赖测量，结合统计分析解耦隧穿贡献，将单层 FeSe/SrTiO₃ 的超导能隙约束在 6–13 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层 FeSe/SrTiO₃(001) 具有界面增强超导性，转变温度约为块体 FeSe 的近十倍，但其隧穿谱缺乏可清晰辨认的超导相干峰（coherence peaks），导致能隙大小无法明确确定。

Method: 采用超低温扫描隧道光谱，系统改变温度、针尖-样品间距及横向位置，并通过统计分析解耦不同隧穿贡献；对针尖高度依赖的光谱进行回归提取衰减率（decay rates），并开展空间分辨光谱与相关分析。

Result: 衰减率分析揭示三个能量区间：6 meV 以下以 Andreev 反射主导的隧穿（Andreev-reflection-dominated tunneling），6–13 meV 为 Andreev 与准粒子隧穿（quasiparticle tunneling）混合，13 meV 以上以准粒子隧穿为主；空间上，6 meV 以下隧穿电导主要局域于缺陷附近，更高能量则空间扩展。

Conclusion: 综合结果将超导能隙约束在 6–13 meV 之间；该工作表明，当复杂谱特征妨碍相干峰明确识别时，光谱的统计分析是约束超导能隙结构的有力方法。

Abstract: Monolayer FeSe grown on SrTiO$_3$ (001) exemplifies interface-enhanced superconductivity, exhibiting a transition temperature nearly ten times that of bulk FeSe. Yet, its tunneling spectra lack clearly identifiable superconducting coherence peaks, preventing an unambiguous determination of the gap magnitude. Here, we use ultralow-temperature scanning tunneling spectroscopy as a function of temperature, tip-sample separation, and lateral position to disentangle tunneling contributions by statistical analysis. Decay rates extracted by regression of tip-height-dependent spectroscopy reveal three energy regimes: Andreev-reflection-dominated tunneling below 6 meV, mixed Andreev and quasiparticle tunneling between 6 and 13 meV, and predominantly quasiparticle tunneling above 13 meV. Spatially resolved spectroscopy and correlation analysis further reveal that the tunneling conductance is predominantly localized near defects below 6 meV but becomes spatially extended at higher energies. Together, these results constrain the superconducting gap to lie between 6 and 13 meV. Our findings establish statistical analysis of spectroscopy as a powerful approach to constraining superconducting gap structures when complex spectral features prevent the unambiguous identification of coherence peaks.

</details>


### [252] [Intra-unit-cell Josephson scanning tunneling spectroscopy of the cuprate Bi$_{1.2}$Pb$_{0.8}$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04815)
*Fengyu Yao,Jiong Mei,Wenjin Mao,Runxin Li,Yuchen Huo,Miaomiao Wang,Quanxing Wei,Guihao Jia,Qing Qu,Cui Ding,Lin Zhao,Zhuoyu Chen,Ming-Qiang Ren,Kun Jiang,X. J. Zhou,Jin-Feng Jia,Qi-Kun Xue,Yu-Jie Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用常规 s 波超导铅（Pb）针尖进行约瑟夫森扫描隧道谱（Josephson scanning tunneling spectroscopy），在 Bi₁.₂Pb₀.₈Sr₂CaCu₂O₈₊δ 中观测到零偏压电导增强与原子尺度空间调制，并通过微观计算表明其源于符号变化的 d 波配对序参量在原子尺度隧穿中的干涉效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体（cuprate superconductors）以 d_{x²−y²} 波配对对称性为主已较为确立，但其在局域库珀对隧穿（local Cooper-pair tunneling）中的微观表现仍研究不足，需要原子尺度的直接证据。

Method: 采用 s 波超导 Pb 针尖对 Bi₁.₂Pb₀.₈Sr₂CaCu₂O₈₊δ 开展约瑟夫森扫描隧道谱测量，结合原子分辨电导图与位点分辨谱；并构建纳入双层电子结构（bilayer electronic structure）与真实 Wannier 基隧穿矩阵元的微观计算。

Result: 观测到零偏压电导增强，其对结电导的近似二次依赖与相位扩散型约瑟夫森隧穿（phase-diffusive Josephson tunneling）一致；电导图显示出显著的晶胞内调制（intra-unit-cell modulation），氧位附近约瑟夫森响应增强，铜位及对角方向明显受抑，微观计算结果再现了该空间图样。

Conclusion: 符号变化的 d 波超导序参量（sign-changing d-wave superconducting order parameter）通过相反符号配对贡献之间的干涉在原子尺度约瑟夫森隧穿中显现，为铜氧化物的非常规配对对称性（unconventional pairing symmetry）提供了补充证据。

Abstract: The predominantly $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave pairing symmetry of cuprate superconductors is well established, yet its microscopic manifestation in local Cooper-pair tunneling remains less explored. Here, we investigate Bi$_{1.2}$Pb$_{0.8}$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$, using Josephson scanning tunneling spectroscopy with a conventional $s$-wave superconducting Pb tip. We observe a zero-bias conductance enhancement whose approximately quadratic dependence on junction conductance is consistent with phase-diffusive Josephson tunneling. Atomic-resolution conductance maps and site-resolved spectra reveal a pronounced intra-unit-cell modulation, with enhanced Josephson response near oxygen sites and strong suppression near copper sites and along diagonal directions. Microscopic calculations incorporating the bilayer electronic structure and realistic Wannier-based tunneling matrix elements reproduce the observed spatial pattern through interference between pairing contributions of opposite signs. Our results reveal how the sign-changing $d$-wave superconducting order parameter manifests itself in atomic-scale Josephson tunneling, providing complementary evidence for unconventional pairing symmetry in cuprates.

</details>


### [253] [Nonreciprocal charge transport in quantum materials: From superconductors to magnetic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04821)
*Boning Yu,Yalei Huang,Alltrin Dhanarajagopal,Xiaofeng Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了非互易电荷输运（nonreciprocal charge transport）在量子材料中的研究进展，涵盖块体晶体、人工异质结构、范德华体系、高温超导体与磁性材料中的内禀与外禀机制，并以超导二极管效应为核心应用焦点，讨论对称性破缺、自旋轨道耦合与磁序的作用，指出机制统一性缺失等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易电荷输运（电流 preferentially 单向流动）已成为量子材料领域的核心议题，但相关发现众多却缺乏统一图像，各机制相对重要性仍存争议，亟需平衡梳理已知与不确定之处。

Method: 采用综述（review）方法，跨材料体系（块体晶体、人工异质结构、范德华系统、高温超导体、磁性材料）归纳研究进展，区分内禀起源（如能带结构效应）与外禀起源（涡旋运动、器件几何、热效应伪影），并结合对称性破缺、自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与磁序分析观测响应。

Result: 综述汇总了非互易输运在多种量子材料中的实验进展，重点呈现超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）——正反方向具有不同临界电流——的应用潜力；同时指出众多发现缺乏统一图景、机制相对重要性持续争论。

Conclusion: 综述提炼关键开放问题并提出未来方向，包括建立更完善的实验标准与推进器件集成（device integration），旨在对已知、不确定及可能发展方向给出平衡视角。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport, where current flows preferentially in one direction, has become a key topic across quantum materials. This review covers some recent progress in bulk crystals, artificial heterostructures, van der Waals systems, high-temperature superconductors, and magnetic materials. The review discusses both intrinsic origins, such as band-structure effects, and extrinsic ones, including vortex motion, device geometry, and heating artifacts. A central focus is the superconducting diode effect due to its application potential, which shows different critical currents for opposite directions. The review also examines how symmetry breaking, spin-orbit coupling, and magnetic order contribute to the observed responses. However, there remain challenges when many exciting findings lack a unified picture, and the field continues to debate the relative importance of various mechanisms. The review highlights key open questions and suggests future directions, including better experimental standards and device integration. This review aims to give a balanced view of what is known, what is uncertain, and where the field might go next.

</details>


### [254] [Ta-based S-I-S junctions for integrated superconducting Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04982)
*Trent Borman,Cameron Bullock,Cole Chason,Bradley Christensen,Jonathan Cochran,Emad Din,Evan Donohue,Jason Flagg,A. L. Graninger,Eric Jones,Robert Klaes,Shawn Keebaugh,Erin Kennedy,John Kula,Jason Lapano,Melissa Loving,Christopher Pasco,Devika Pathan,Tam Pham,Timothy Pillsbury,Robert Pownall,Paige Quarterman,Jacob Ruf,Jade Simmons,Nathan Siwak,Joel Strand,Yong Mo Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作制备并测试了以α-Ta为电极、TaO_x为势垒的S-I-S约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction），通过籽晶层工程（seed layer engineering）实现上下电极的α相成核，并在150 mm晶圆工业产线上验证了其电学均匀性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钽（Ta）基约瑟夫森结有望用于经典与量子超导技术，但需证明其可在工业规模上制备并具备良好的结性能一致性，因此研究α-Ta电极结的材料平台可行性。

Method: 采用籽晶层工程生长底电极与顶电极α-Ta，比较Nb与TaN_x籽晶层方案，并指出顶电极在室温下需引入再籽晶层（re-seed layer）才能成核α-Ta；结合室温下不同结面积的电学探测与Ambegaokar-Baratoff理论表征晶圆级临界电流密度（critical current density）。

Result: 在20 × 20 mm芯片上，0.4 μm²结的结参数分散度（junction spread）低至2.0%；全流程基于150 mm晶圆的标准化工业代工（foundry）工艺完成；同时发现籽晶层选择会对结的隧穿电学行为产生细微影响。

Conclusion: 成功制备并测试了α-Ta电极/TaO_x势垒的约瑟夫森结，表明该材料平台值得进一步研究，具备应用于经典与量子超导技术的潜力，并朝着规模化实现迈出重要一步。

Abstract: In this work, we have presented S-I-S Josephson junctions with $\rmα$-Ta electrodes and a ${\rm TaO}_x$ barrier. We grew base and top $α$-Ta electrodes using seed layer engineering, showing both Nb and ${\rm TaN}_x$ are viable seed layer choices, and particularly emphasize that at room temperature a re-seed layer is required to nucleate $\rmα$-Ta for the top electrode. However, we note that the choice of seed layer can have subtle implications on the electrical tunneling behavior of the junctions. We used room-temperature probing of variable junction areas, combined with Ambegaokar-Baratoff physics, to characterize the wafer scale critical current densities. For 0.4 $μ$m$^2$ junctions, we found junction spreads as low as 2.0% across the 20 x 20 mm chip relevant for device performance. The entire process was carried out using standard industry foundry processing using 150 mm wafer tooling, which is an important step towards proving that Ta-based junctions can be implemented at scale. We have successfully fabricated and tested junctions with $\rmα$-Ta electrodes and a ${\rm TaO}_x$ insulating tunnel barrier, demonstrating that this is a material platform which merits further investigation and studies for potential application in classical and quantum superconducting technologies.

</details>
